1a1(kha): bde ba chen po byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i le'u/
2a1(kha): ma na du g+hu dag/'di yin no//dus gsum bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas dang /rdo rje 'chang ba'i rnal 'byor dang //dpal chen khrag 'thung ma mo'i tshogs//gzhan yang rnam pa mang
2a2(kha): 'o li lha dang lha min rlung lha dang //ga shin rjeklu dang lha rnams dang //gnod sbyin srin po 'byung po'i tshogs//dbang med dad pas 'dus pa dang //lha'i tshogs kyis gang ba yi//gzhal med khang
2a3(kha): mdzes de dag ni//me dang 'dra bar 'od gsal zhing //nyi ma zla ba'i gdan dang 'od//zhing rnams gdan chen gsal ba dang //rin chen las byas shin tu snang //skal dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas
2a4(kha): zhing /rab sdang gnod byed rnams kyang spangs//'di la gnod byed gang yin pa//byung po grul bus srin po dang //mi dgas srul pa de dag gis//rmad 'byung 'di ni mi thos te//chang
2b1(kha): san du sa ga bye ga/'jug parnams //na sa pa na dugs//thugs pa
2b2(kha): kyis spong bar 'gyur//chang 'byung byang chub sems 'di ni//dpa' chen bad+ma ru ka ri//brtsub pa gsong bzugs kyi mchog/chos kyi gtam la 'jigs med cing //las kyi sa gos par mi 'gyur te//dpal
2b3(kha): chen 'khor tshogs mang po dang //sangs rgyas rnal 'byor chen po dang //'khor tshogs ye shes rnal 'byor ma//'jigs med rdo rje sems dpa' dang //skal ldan rnams kyi don du gsungs//gzhan gyi don du ma gsu
2b4(kha): so//byang chub kyi sems rmang du byung ba las//chos kyi gleng gni'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das 'og min gyi gnas snying po byang chub na bzhugs pa'i sku//rdo rje sems dpa' la gzigs
2b5(kha): nas 'a chos pa dbyung ba'i byang chub kyi sems//rmang dubyung ba nas bya ba'i ting nge badzin la snyoms par nugs so//de'i tshe 'i dus gsum gyi 'de bar gshegs pa rnams dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen na
3a1(kha): @##//'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa 'di dag thams cad/bde bar gshegs pa thams cad don gcig pa ste/bdag nyid byang chub kyi sems rmad du byung ba las 'byung zhing //de dag thang/
3a2(kha): de las bskyed pa dang /de las byung ba'i dkyil 'khor rmad du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor yin pa'i don/thugs su chud pa'i don/rdo rje sems dpas rig par bya ba'i phyir/rdo rje sems dpa' la gzigs te/yid
3a3(kha): kyi dkyil 'khor sgo phye nas/rang gi rig pa'i don gsal bar byung ste/thams cad kyi don ji lta bu yin pa/so so snang bas gsal bar byas nas/rdo rje sems dpa' nyid dang /bde bar gshegs pa thams ca
3a4(kha): dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas su med pa rnams/byang chub kyi sems rmad du byung ba 'di nyid yin pa dang /byang chub kyi sems rmad du byung ba las bskyed pa'i dkyil 'khor
3a5(kha): sku rmad du byung ba sku'i dkyil 'khor gsang barmang du byung ba dang /gsung thad du byung ba gsung gi dkyil 'khor gsang brjed dbyung ba dang /thugs rmad du byung bathugs kyi dkyil 'khor gsang brda du byung /
3b1(kha): ba dang /chos chen po mdu byung ba nyid yin par snang bar byas nas/de dag thams cad bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis sku gsung thug su bsdus so//de nas rdorje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//'jig rten mkhas pa rdzogs sangs/
3b2(kha): rgyas/sangs rgyas don du mdzad pa yis/thugs kyi smon pa'i don bstan pa/gang blo za tshim za ba dang /gang zhig 'gal bar yid sems dpa'/'jigs pa thams cad de la 'ong /mgon tshal ba bdun du 'gas//rdo rje sems pa
3b3(kha): nyid kyang sdang /sngags 'chang rnams kyis spangs par 'gyur/sngags rnams thams cad gsang ba yi//lta brda du byung ba'i don//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag nyid kyis//mngon chad rtogs par ma gyur pa//thugs kyi sgron ma'i don bstan
3b4(kha): gang la sangs rgyas gus mdzad pa//don de la ni phyag 'tsha'o//sangs rgyas kun la brtse dgongs nas//gang gis don du mdzad pa las//don mdzad de la phyag 'tsha'o//gang gis don du byas pa'i don//de dag bdag la 'grub gyur cig/
3b5(kha): gzhal yas me 'od 'bar ba ltar//don de bdag gis rig gyur na//sangs rgyas kun dang bskal 'a mnyam//sangs rgyas kun pas rgyas gyur cig/dus gsum rnal 'byor gang dag gis//rmang byung byang chub sems 'di ni//kun gyis rin
3b6(kha): bam yin//lha rnams kun gyis mi rig go//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis kyang //'di ni rig par dkara' ba'o//bde gshegs rnams kyi sngon ma rig / zhes gsol to//sems dpa' chen po/thams cad mkhyen pa sgom pa'i mchog
4a1(kha): ngo bo nyid ni dmig su med//nga'i lus su su sbyor ba/byang chub med cing bsgom pa'ang med//lus dang ngag sems mtshan nyid ni//ye shes lnga'i byang chub sems//sems can kun gyi sems chen po//rang bzhin dag pa dri ma med//thams cad sa
4a2(kha): ma skyed zag pa med//byang chub med cing mngon rtog su med//khams kyang med cing 'byung ba med//mtshan nyid mi 'gyur na mkha' bzhin//gnyis med gnyi su 'dzin med cing //byang chub sems 'di rgyas pa'i spyan//zhes bka' stsal to//kye sems dpa'/
4a3(kha): /chen po//ba bar gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa 'dra ste/nga'i byang chub sems rmadu byung ba 'di yin no//mang rig pa las byung ba'i chos can po rna du byung ba nyid yin no//
4a4(kha): rdzu 'phrul chen po rma du byung ba nyid yi no//nga'i 'phrul chen po rma du byung ba nyid do//nga'i rol pa chen po rma du byung ba nyid yino//ngas mdzad pa rma du byung ba nyid yi no//de'i dkyil 'khor chen po rma du byung ba nyid yino//nga'i cho 'phrul chen po rma du byung ba nyid yino//nga'i gas pa/
4a5(kha): /rma du byung ba nyid yino//bde bar gshegs pa rnams dang /rdo rje sems dpa' nyid gsang ba dang /gsang ba chen po dang /gsang ba de kho na nyid dang /lta ba gsang ba rma du byung ba nyid yin no//sku rma du byung ba'i sku'i dkyil 'khor gsang ba rma du byung ba yino//gsu
4a6(kha): mdu byung ba'i dkyil 'khor gsang ba rma du byung ba nyid mi no//thugs rma du byung ba'i thugs kyi dkyil 'khor gsang ba rma du byung ba nyid yino//byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///le'u 'dis chos nyid bstan pa'i phyir sems
4a7(kha): dpa' chen go/byang chub 'dod pa de la byang chub med//sa dang rnam thar byang chub mchog la ring /rgyu 'dra'i chos nyid gang gis shes par 'gyur ba yi//de nyid byang chub sems nyid bdag nyid yin par nges/byang chub snying por bdag 'jug pas//thob pa
4b1(kha): /med cing spang ba'ang med//spangs rgyas rnams kyi byang chub ni/khwa snyad yin te don du med//chos kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dag/bdag med chos las kun du byung /ma skyed pa'i chos rnams la ngo ba med de sgom pa'ang med pa mag pa'i tshul du basbyor bas/dngos so/
4b2(kha): kun tu rab tu grags//chos rnams rang bzhin 'od gsal bas//gdod nas dag pas nam mkha' bzhin//byang chub med cing sems can med//bdag tu med pa'i chos las byung //sangs rgyas byang chub rdzogs byed pa//rnam par mi rtog dmigasu med//spros pa med pa'i
4b3(kha): lceru ste gsum pa'o///le ru 'dis che ba bstan te//sems dpa' chen po/thugs kyi dkyil 'khor rang bzhin las//ye shes rdzogs bzhugs me long bzhin//nga na dus gsum bzhugs pa yi//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes te//byang chub sems kyis
4b4(kha): don rtogs pas//sangs rgyas kun gyis nyid sha can//nga ni sangs rgyas kun gyi ngur//dur khrod chen po nga bdag yin//sems dpa' chen po thams cad dang //sems can thams cad nga las byung //sems can dur nyid nga yin te//skal ldan sems pa rig par gyis
4b5(kha): sangs rgyas thams cad nga'i zhing //sems can thams cad sku gsung gter kha thams cad nga'i longs spyod rgyu ka zhes gsungs pa'i cho ga thos pa dang //sems dpa' skal ldan zhal mthong ba//rmang byung gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor 'dir//byang chub sems nyid bdag
4b6(kha): yin rig thal mo sbyar nas 'di skad gsol//dus gsum sangs rgyas thaMd kyi//thugs kyi thugs te thugs la rol//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//sku'i sku ste sku la rol ka/dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//gsung gi gsung ste gsung la rol//zhes
4b7(kha): gsol pa dang /sems dpa' chen po//bskal ba'i bus kyi sngon rol du//nga nyid gcig pu sngod du byung //byung ba bas kyang nga sngar byung //sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gsung thugs//nga las byung ba de yi phyir//sems dpa' chen po'i thugs nyid ni//ngas byin rlabs rtse
5a1(kha): bo sa//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad dang //rnal 'byor chen po kun 'dus nas//'od zer mngon sum nyi bzhin shar//sems dpa' chen po bskal pa bzang //rnal 'byor gang zhig 'di 'chang ba//de ni sems dpa' nyid kyi sku gsung
5a2(kha): thugs//de nyid yin par rig par gyis//don 'di rig pa'i rnal 'byor pa//sangs rgyas sa la mi gnas te//thams cad nga nyid nga'i thugs//sems can thams cad nga las bskyed//zhes gsungs so//che ba bstan pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o///liT/
5a3(kha): 'dis gsang ba'i zhal gyis bzhes pa bstan te//sems dpa' chen po//da byed pa po nga nyid rang bzhin gyis rnal 'byor dag nas/sangs rgyas ma skyes pa med pas//'dus byas kyi chos thams cad ye nas med de//nga'i phyir chos thams cad ye nas rnam par dag
5a4(kha): pa nyid do//nga nyid chos kyi sku'i rnal 'byor yin pas//nga'i chos thams cad rnam par mi rtog pa'o//sangs rgyas kun las sngar sangs rgyas pa'o//na mkha' med pa'i sngon rol du//na mkha'i chos nyid ye nas med//pA la chags med sngon rol can//chags pa'i 'dod
5a5(kha): chags sa nas med//sring pa meng pa'i sngon rol duka sring pa'i chos nyid ye nas med//'byung ba med pa'i sngon rol na//chen po lnga nyid ye nas med//sangs rgyas med pa'i sngon rol ma/sems can ming ni se nas med//mya ngan 'das pa'i sngon rol na//'khor ba'i chos byi/
5a6(kha): me nas med//de phyir 'byung ba bas ni nga sngar 'byung //thams cad nga yis byis pa yin//thams cad nga las bskyed pa yin//thams cad nga las byung ba yin//zhes bka' stsal to//gsang ba zhal gyis bzhes pa gsang ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///le'u 'dis
5a7(kha): byang chub kyi cho 'phrul chen po bstan te//gzhi rtsa med pa'i sems nyid ni//bkrus pas mi 'dag na mkha' bzhin//skye dang bral ba'i byang chub la//byang chub 'bras bu long gis med//rmad 'byung byang chub sems 'di ni//rang gi rig pa ye shes chen pos mkhyen nas//rol
5b1(kha): thabs chen po rang la rdzogs pas sangs rgyas so//byang chub sems 'phrul chen po'i bstan pa 'di la don rnam pa lnga ste/byang chub sems dpa' rdzu 'phrul chen po rmad du byung ba ste/byang chub sems rdzu 'phrul chen po rmad du byung ba dang /byang chub sems rol pa chen po'i tshe/
5b2(kha): 'phrul chen po rmad du byung ba dang /yong su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul chen po rma du byung ba dang /byang chub gsang ba rma du byung ba ni chos rje su bstan pa'i cho 'phrul rma du byung ba dang /byang chub sems gsang ba chen po'i rgyud gsang ba'i cho 'phrul chen po mngon par bstan pa'i chos rmad du
5b3(kha): byung ba'o//byang chub sems rmad du byung ba las/'phrul chen po bstan pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o////le'u 'di las byang chub sems gsang ba nyid bstan pa byang chub kyi sems gsang ba rma du byung bas 'phrul chen po 'di ni sku gsung blugs nyid du gyur pa sbyin te/sngon
5b4(kha): g+hi swAngs rgyam rnams kyis kyang ba shing ba ma yin//rje sems dpa' chen po//ljang lamo gnas pa rnams ni thugs gsang po chen po rmad du byung ba'o//yang dgas ngas rgyas lam riganas/nas 'i tsho'i lhar chags pa med par kun tu khyim pa'i rang la 'dus pa ni/byang chus
5b5(kha): kyi sems rmad du byung ba nyid do//kye sems dpa' chen po/bkod pa med par kun la mngon par snang ba ni/sku rma du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor yin//rang rig pa las byung ba'i chos rnams ni gang du yang bsam du med pa nyid kyi don du gyur pa'i gnas ni/byang chub kyi sems
5b6(kha): rmadu byung ba nyid do//kye sems dpa' chen po/ma skyes ma spros par sgra skad sna tshogs so sor rang las byung ba ni gsung rmad du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid yin no//sgra las 'das pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi sku gsung thugs nyid du gyur pa ni/byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba
5b7(kha): nyid do//kye sems dpa' chen po/rang rig pa'i sems las byung ba'i chos//chos sku mi mnga' bar kun la khyab par gnas pa'i rnamakha' ste/byang chub kyi sems rmad byung ba nyidado//kye sems dpa' chen po/sku dang gsung thugs dpe byad las 'das pa sku//wa
6a1(kha): //rgyun gyis rdzogs par gsal ba rang las byung ba ni/klu chub kyi sems ra du byung banyid do//me sems papa chen go//cher po lda ni sra lnga lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor byengad do//chen po lnga'i dadus mni/byang chub kyi sems dri med par za/
6a2(kha): byung ba nyid do//dngas ma de bsal ba'i snying pa shin tu bdag dka' ba nyid ye shes te/thugs nyid gyur pa'o//kye sems dpa' chen po//'byung ba chen po so sor ma bcos ma byas bsam pa med pa las sgra skang sna tshogs 'byung ba ni nga rang nyid yin no//kye sem
6a3(kha): dpa' chen po//'di dag ni bdag nyid las byung ba gang gis ngo bor yang 'gyur pas chos kyi sku ste/byang chub sems mdu byung ba'o//'byung ba chen po bzhini skye ba 'byung ba dang /skye ba rnams kyi rgyu nyid du gyur pas/byang chub sems rmad du byung ba nyid do//'byung
6a4(kha): po bzhi las skye zhing 'byung ba dang /skye ba rnam pa bzhi'i sems can rnams/bdag nyid du gyur pa sangs rgyas med pa'i sangs rgyas yin pas/byang chub kyi sems ca du byung ba nyid//kye sems dpa' chen po//de la byang ba ni bdag nyid las byung ba'i chos thaMd la nyon mong/
6a5(kha): pa'i drim yinas med pa'o//chub ces bya ba ni 'das pa dang /ma byung ba dang /da ltar bzugs pa dang /stong gsun gyi snod chen po'i 'ji rten gyi khams mtha' las su med pa thams cad don gcig ste/sku gsung thugs las pa rtogs pa'i chos rdul tsam yang
6a6(kha): la/chos pa 'dzin byer med per thugs chung ba ni/kun chu ba'o//sems can thams cad sku gsung thus su rgyu me pa'i do las gso ba med par gsal pa nyid kyi ting 'dzi byed do//byang chub kyisams nyid bstan pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o//
6a7(kha): 'dis byang chub sems rnam par thar pa'i lam bstan te/bcom ldan 'das rigs lnga'i sangs rgyas la/rnam par thar pa'i lam du mda' gsol pa dang /sems dpa' chen po nyon cig zhes bka' stsal pa/de bzhin gshegs pa tha~Md la rnam par thar pa'i
6b1(kha): mno ga chos byed nas par bar ba cu yod du/'dod nags dang /zhe sdang dang /gaN+DI zug dang /ngad+ham dang /drag drug go//des 'ang nags ni 'di las byung ba'i chos pa 'dag da dus dang rnam pa med par spong bar 'do //chags pa med dang pa da du
6b2(kha): rgya 'o//de sabadag nyid las byung ba'i tshis ni//gzugs dang /ska dang/dri dang /ro dang /reg bya //dag ji rji tsam/med bdag nyid gyur par 'ad ci 'a la chags pa'o//zhe sngang ni bag nyid la yinas gnas pa chos thams kyad 'byung
6b3(kha): sa/sa ma thos/'as rgyu la /na sa pa ga pa da rgya tham sa
6b4(kha): gaN+DI mras ni dag nyid byung ba'i chos rnams cadas/sgrub so sor myed med par thugs chung/so song dbye bar ngas pa mi mang bas gang zug pa dang /chos thams cad ba ba gshegs pa'i thugs nyi chung bas/gsang pa mchog zhing ces pan/tshad pa ga nyid
6b5(kha): cig tu bzhugs te/sa dpasa'i rim par dbye ba'i dgongs pa mi mngar bas/gti mug pa dang /sku gsung thugs kyi ngam nyid du gcig te/phyir med pas thug su chud/mnyam pa nyid kyi skur bzhugs pas na mnyam pa'i don la dgongs pa gti mug pa dang /
6b6(kha): sa ma nga /sa thos ya sa skyesas rgyas/ rgyas skye grus ngas pa dang / gas pa nga chu ba snyams ya gas ga
6b7(kha): gzhan du 'byung ba dang /gzhan du 'gog pa dang /gzhan las skyes pa dang /gzhan las byung ba dang /gzhan du gyur par ston pa mi mngar bas gti mug pa dang ka bdag nyid las byung ba'i sprul pa'i sku rnam pa sna tshogs las/rol pa ci yang mdzad cing sku
7a1(kha): #gcig tu ston pa mi mnga' bas gti mug pa'o//nga rgyal ni bdag nyid las byung ba'i chos thams cad dang /skye ba thams cad lang dang /bdag med par thug su chung de mkhyen pas/de phyir chos thaMd lang dang bdag med par ston par mdzad pa'i/
7a2(kha): ngargyal dang /bdag nyid las byung ba'i chos bla na med pa thams cad thug su chud de/dus gsum du bdag nyid ston pa mdzad cing /bdag nyid che bar ston par mdzad pa'i nga rgyal dang /bdag cing byung ba'i chos rnams la/bdag nyid dus thams cad longs spyod
7a3(kha): @ra mdzad pa'i dbang gis//rol me'i sku ci yang ston par mdzad pa'i nga rgyal dang /chos thams cad dang /srid pa gsum dang /chen po lnga la sogs pa skye 'gro thams cad bdag nyid las gyur par thug su chud de//de dag thams cad bdag las bsdus shing /
7a4(kha): bdag nyid du gyur pa'i che ba'i nga rgyal lo//phrag dog ni chos thams cad bdag gi ngo bo nyid gcig pa las/tha dad par rtogs pa de la phrag dog pa dang /'dod chags gti mug rnams ye nas sku gsung thug su dbyer med pa las/mtshan nyid so sor 'dzin pa la/
7a5(kha): drug dog pa dang /yang dag pa'i don ma nor par gtan la phab pa la/byin ci log tu rtogs pa la drag dog pa dang /chos de rnams thams cad bde bar gshegs pa yin pa la/de ltar ma rtogs pa la phrag dog pa dang /che ba'i lam yin pa la rnam par tharabs
7a6(kha): yong pa sa drug dag par//chom na pon du byung ba las// chub kyi sems thar pa'i las brnyan pa'i le ste gargya 'o//kyis nis byang chub kyi sems ye nas mi mnyam par bgyur pa ste/rdo rje sems dpa' bar khyod//sang dgas sa rgya
7a7(kha): ga khag bu bye sa nga du ma rje mis bdag /rna chog ma pa byang sa byu/nga sa sa byung ga tung yo// gas su ngan rmug sa sa na su kun gyi sgrub gru mas rgyas byang chu phran sod/
7b1(kha): rang las sgrub por sku rnams la ka rang gi longs spyod cir yang mdzad//sprul pa'i rnam pa spyi cir sang ston//khyod las cho 'phrul sna tshogs 'byung //rol pa'i sku yi tsir yang rol ka/sangs rgyas rol pa khyod las byung //sems dpa'i chen po thams cad
7b2(kha): khyod nyid gis byung ba'o//sems dpa' chen po 'di ni//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsod pa rmang du byung ba'o//sems dpa' chen po nyon gcig/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyis thaMd mdzad de/chags pa dang bral ba'i lus las 'das pa
7b3(kha): yang khyod do//nga'i byang chub sems phra zhing zag pa med pa las dang bral ba/rnab med de rdul dang bral bas dri ma med pa//bde bar gshegs pa dang sems can thams cad la gnas pa yin//'di ni sangs rgyas thaMd kyi gsang ba'i byung /
7b4(kha): kyi sems gsang ba dam pa'o//sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa ni 'di ltar rig par bya'o//ye shes lus can de bzhin gshegs pa yin te/de'i sku'i 'od zer la zhugs shing gsung gi 'od zer la rol te/
7b5(kha): rnam par dag pa na mkha' dang 'dra ba'i sku'o//de la yangs pa dang /kun khyab par gnas pa dang /'gag pa med par 'jug pa ni na mkha'o//gsal zhing snang ba thams cad ni sku'i 'od yin zhing /na mkha'i rang bzhin ni ma yin no/
8a1(kha): lnga so sor gsal ba dang /snang ba chen po yod pa ni chen po lnga nyid rigs lnga'i skur gyur pa'i 'od yin te/nag pa'i khams kun la khyab pa'i 'od dang /na mkha'i rang bzhin las byung ba ma yin te/sku mdog ji lta ba bzhin du nam
8a2(kha): mkha'i gdog kyang de bzhin du gyur ba yin no//bar snang la snang bar ston pa/nyi zla gza' skar la sogs pa rang gi snang ba'i 'od 'byung ba dang /zar snang snang bar ston pa yang spu rin chen po che'i 'od dang /gsung chos nyid du spros pa'i
8a3(kha): zer yin yin no//chen po bzhi las byung ba'i kha dog rnams dang /rnam pa sna tshog su smra ba dang /bar snang la sprin gyi kha dog so sor snang ba dang /gzhan tshon gyis kha dog 'byung ba yang /rigs so so'i sku dang /ye shes kyi 'od las byung ba
8a4(kha): yin gyin namakhar dang kha dog kyang chen po bzhi'i mthu las byung ba ni ma yino//su dkar na mkha' yang dkar/sku mdog ji lta ba bzhin du na mkha'i mdog kyang de dang 'dra ba dang /cher po bzhi las byung ba'i skye ba rnams kyi kha dog rnams kyang de dang 'drag
8a5(kha): de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni chen po lnga la rten cing 'byung ba chen po bzhi'i rgya las byung ba 'am/skye ba bzhi las skyes pa ni ma yin no//'byung ba chen po bzhi dang 'byung ba las gyur pa thang dang /skye ba rnam pa bzhi'i gnas la skyes pa'i sems can thams
8a6(kha): /stong gsus kyi snying chen po'i la rten gyi khams sa grus zhus pa med 'arnams ni/ de zhi gshegs kyi sh+'u ga rmugs nyid las byung shib/thug tung thugs kyi po po chen po rnams sbyang yang ros cig nas #
8a7(kha): mas pa de mdzad pa dang /tshas la rol pa chen po rtag tu mdzang ba'i don du de bzhin gshegs pas mdzad do//chen po lnga med pa'i sngon ram na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i skuni rang byung ste/sku'i 'od la zhugs shing gsung gi 'o sa ros nyid /
8b1(kha): ra ra 'byung ba las/rgyang chu gi sems ye nas sanyam pa dang mi mnyam par bstan pa'i 'du ste sa pa'o///le'u 'dis byang chub sems las byangs pa ldan te/sems pa' chen po byangab chu ba sems pas dag mdu byung ba
8b2(kha): de nga pa dang ngagasum gyi stong zer ba'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' las su med pa rnams sa sa rnas kyi mdzang pa gas dpa ma du myong ba'o/yangs rgyas nyid las skyes pas gsang ba nan byung ba 'ongangag nyid kyi ris pa'i ma shes yis sa
8b3(kha): ba dang /ma byung ba dang /rang rig pa'i ye shes nges ma byas pa'i chos gang yang med pas gsang ba rmad byung ba'i chos chen po ste/byang chub sems dpa' chen po nyid dang /byang chub sems rnams kyis shin tu rtogs par dka' ba'i phyir gsang ba mchog gi ngas
8b4(kha): gas ban thu sa gangs sa pa ba pa /ma mas pa'i mas sa/da sa chen pa/ mas ngas pang sa rtsungs /klu su dus
8b5(kha): slong ba ga gags pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs nyid pas byungasas gas sa'o//de bzhi gshegs pa nyid 'arnam pa dag pa'i phung po lnga ma dang //gzugs rnam par dag pas pang dugyur ba'i phung po dang /dag pa nas da nga 'dreb pa 'phru nga/
8b6(kha): blos pa'i tshor ba 'gags pa med pa'i drung ba dang /skye ba med pa'i 'dugs chas nyid du gyur ba'i dung po dang /yin pa med pa 'i 'du byed rnam pa thams cad du thu dang pa'i sgo ba phung po dang /rnam par shes pa namakha' dang 'dra ba'i ye shes 'byung ba'i phung po dang ldan/rje
8b7(kha): sku lngani phung po lnga kun gyi bdag nyid du 'ang ba dang /kun gyi dag nyid myur bas su'o//su rnam par snang bachad du gyur ba ni bu blo thun gyis rdzogs pas yangs skyong rdugs pa'i bu sna rnam par snang ma dang//da la rnam par snang mang ces gsungs pa ni/kun gzhi
9a1(kha): @# par shes pa nyid sku lnga nyid du gyur pa yin te/de nyid las sku rnam pa sna tshogs cir yang snang mdzad do//de'i phyir sems dpa' chen po ji skad gsungs zhe na/'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams dang /dus dang dus kyi so sor gyur pa dang /dus
9a2(kha): gsum gyi bde bar gshegs pa thams cad dang /skye ba thams cad dang /skye bo dang /skye 'gro rnams dang /chen po lnga dang /'byung ba bzhi las byung ba'i chos thams cad kun/sku lnga rnam par dag pa las ma byung ba'i chos gcig kyang med do//de dag thams cad sku lnga/
9a3(kha): las byung ba las thaMd nas thams cad du sku lnga nyi du ma gyur pa gcig kyang med do//de'i phyir thams cad sangs rgyas gyur pa gcig kyang medado//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku ni/phyi dang nang dang /lus dang /ngag du yang snang la kun tu yang snang //kun la snang bar mdzad//
9a4(kha): yod ji skad du gsungs zhe na/nga'i sku rnam par dag pa ni cir yang sprul cing ci yang 'gyur zhing /kun tu 'gyur ba nyid yin te/sku lnga las gyur pa'i chen po lnga nyid la nga rol pa chen po rdo rje rnam par rol par mdzad/rtag par bzhugs shing rtag par rnaraM cir yang rol/rdo rje rnam par
9a5(kha): rol pa chen po cir yang mdzad/chos nyid la mnga' mdzad pa'i rol pa chen pos/sku'i cho 'phrul chen po cir yang snang zhing cir yang ston/cho 'phrul chen po cir yang ston/cho 'phrul chen po cir yang mdzad do//sku cir yang snang ba la cir yang rol pa'i cho 'phrul gyis//rtag par
9a6(kha): longs spyod par mdzad pas so//de'i phyir sems dpa' chen po/phyi'i spyod yul thams cad ni 'di ltar min/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag ni/nga'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzigs mo'i yulo//nga nyid las byung ba'i chos nyid//ma byung ba 'byungs
9a7(kha): byed pa'i yul lo//ma byung ba med par byed pa'i dam tshi gi yu lo//sku gsung thugs mngon par snang ba nyidado//sku gsung thugs mthar mi bzad pa'i mdzod do//ston pa mthar mi zad pa'i don nyid//sku gsung thugs kyi rol pa'i cho 'phrul chen po nyid do//rdzu 'phrul chen po nyid kyi
9b1(kha): rang bzhin//nga'i mthu las byung ba nyid//nga'i stobs las byung ba'i chos so//de'i sgra gsung thugs mi zad pa rgyan gyi 'khor lo nyid //stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad ni/nga nyid kyi yul lo//bzhugs gnas so//gnas yas khang /
9b2(kha): 'di dag thams cad ni ba'i sku'o//nga'i gsung ngo //nga'i thugs so//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thang ni/sangs rgyas kyi rdzogs pa chen po nyid do//byang chub chen por rdzogs pa chen po nyid do//sangs rgyas kyi zhing bde ba chen po nyid do//byang chub kyis
9b3(kha): nyid do//byang chub kyi sems rmad du byung ba thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid mino//skurma du byung ba nyid do//gsung rma du byung ba nyid do//thugs rma du byung ba nyid mi no//chos rma du byung ba nyid yino//gsang ba rmadu byung ba nyid yino//rol pa rma du byung ba nyid yino//mdzad ba du byung ba
9b4(kha): ba nyid yin//rdzu 'phrul rma du byung ba nyid yin//byang chub sems rmad du byung ba 'di nyid yi no//sangs rgyas kyi sa chen po non pa yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yi no//sangs rgyas kyi lta ba mthar phyin pa yang 'di nyid yino//sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyod pa mthar phyin pa yang chos can
9b5(kha): bor na du byung ba 'di yin no//ston pa phun sum tshogs pas bstan pa ya kang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yin no//nyan pa phun sum tshogs pas nyan pa sang ma du byung ba 'di nyid yi no//sangs rgyas rnams kyi bde ba chen po yang 'di nyid yin//sangs rgyas rnams kyi 'dod pa'i
9b6(kha): yon tan lnga yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid mi no//'dod pa'i yon tan lnga'i mtshan nyid kyang mdu byung ba 'di nyid yino//sangs rgyas rnams kyi bde ba chen po mthar thug pa nyid rma du byung ba 'di nyid yino//sangs rgyas rnams kyi sprul pa'i sku'i chos nyid la rol mo'i cho 'phrul gyis
9b7(kha): chos la tshis kyi 'khor lo bskor ba'i chos kyi 'khor lo yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yino//'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug dang /nga rgyal dang /phrag dog dag kyang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yin//chos kyi gsung la ma yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yin//chos kyi
10a1(kha): 'khor lo yang du byung ba 'di nyid yin no//'khor ba yang chos nyid la chos nyid kyis don gyis 'khor zhing /chos sa chos nyid gnas pa yang dradu byung ba 'di nyid yin//sku gsung drugs las ma gtogs pa/spyong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig te/
10a2(kha): gyi khams na/'khor ba dang nyon mongs pa'i ming yang med do//sangs rgyas 'byung ba las ma gtogs pa son du skye ba'i rgyu ming yang modo//u snang pa las ma gtogs pa sems can gyi rang bzhin dang chen po lnga le nas gud na yod do//sangs rgyas kyis bden pa chen po
10a3(kha): las ma gtogs pa/sems can dang sdug bsngal gyi chos ye nas med do//sangs rgyas kyi nges pa'i sa chen po rdzogs chen gcig pu las ma gtogs pa/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na sems can gyi yul dang gnas ni nga nyid kyi gsang ban du byung sa
10a4(kha): las med pas/nga nyid las med pa'i chos nyid las/sems can gyi yul dang gnas ni ming yang medo//sku rmang du byung ba dang /byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i rang bzhin bstan pa dang /nges pa'i man ngag bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o//kalI
10a5(kha): 'dis sku gsung zhugs byang chub kyi sems brtsal du med pas 'grub pa'i lung bstan te/sems dpa' chen gyi/rgyal ba'i rgyal ba rnams kyi sku/sna tshogs cir yang snang ba yang /kun tu rin chen lta bu te/zhes gsungs pa yin no//skul ba dog ci'i yang sdang ning
10a6(kha): cir mdzangs pa ni rin po che dang 'dra ba ste/rin po che ni dgar po yang rin po che/dmang po dang ser po dang ldan pa dang /snga thul sogs pa khe dag ci 'dra ba yang rin gyi che nyid min//'do rin po che yin/'din min pa nis min//de bzhin du rgyal ba'i rgyal ba nas
10a7(kha): sku dang /kha dog thams cad du snang ba dang /dbyings thams cad ji ltar 'dug pa dang /dri cing bro zhing ldan pa dang /ro dro ltar blo ba dang gzugs ji ltar snang ba dang /sgra ji ltar thos pa dang /reg bya ji ltar tshor ba thams cad nyid kyi sku yin na//rgyam ba'i rgyas /
10b1(kha): rnams kyi sku yin gyi/kha dog dang dbyibs ni rgyal ba'i sku yin/'di ni rgyal ba'i sku min bya ba lta bu ni ma yino//de'i phyir sangs rgyas rnams kyis ye shes kyi sku/sna tshogs gzugs brnyan yid las skyes//zhes gsung so//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thugs ni/
10b2(kha): ye shes brgyad so sor dbyer med pa/gang gis mi shig pa'i don/rig pa'i ye shes kyi sku dang gnyisu med par sku las shar ba ni/thugs rma du byung ba'o//sems dpa' chen po/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad ni/de bzhin gshen
10b3(kha): pa rnams kyi thugs las phyung zhing /thugs rma du byung bar ma gyur pa medo//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams la zad pa'i mtha' med pas stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa 'di la zad cing med pa'i dus med do//stong gsum gyi stong chen
10b4(kha): 'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa 'di la zad cing stongs pa'i dus med pa ni/bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi sku gsung thugs nyid yin te/sku gsung thugs nyid zad cing stongs pa'i dus med pa ni/byang chub kyi sems rma de byung ba nyid yin+da te/de bzhin du sh+ye/
10b5(kha): ga spyo pa/sus dus sa rgyub nga /nga do/gas pa mas ni/ga sung byegs seM su su rgya pa di dang /ma nin sngonas snamam gyi gying mo par darigs pa gi kyi nyid du gyu na
10b6(kha): bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyis gsung ngo //gsung las gyur pa'i sgra skad sna tshogs pa cir yang gyur pa dang /ming dang tshig mang po rnams dang /ye shes la sogs pa dang /sgra skad sna tshogs pa mang por 'byung ba rnams la/gsung du ma gyur pa gcig kyang med de/sku
10b7(kha): dang gsung nyid gsung ngo //gsung dang thugs nyid skur te/gsung sprul pa'i sku/ thugs snying po byang chub mchog gi sku/sku longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku'o//sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi rang bzhin no//rmad byung ba 'di nyid yin par rig pa/
11a1(kha): @gyis shig/ye nas yod cir rtag par bzhugs/rang byung che ba bdag nyid sku/se nas dag pa chos kyi sku//bdag nyid ye nas sku gsung lhun gyis grub 'gyur ba med pa bsod nams sku/ye nas rdzogs pa sprul pa'i sku//lhun gyis rdzogs pas
11a2(kha): longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//bya rtsal med pas snying po byang chub sku//sku gsung thugs bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o///le'u 'dis byang chub sems kyi spyod ba dang /phrin las khyad par chen po bstan te/sems dpa' chen po//'dod cha
11a3(kha): ne sdang gti mug nga rgyas phrag dog ni rdo rje theg pa chen po nyid do//snying rje med par gastod pa dang /'jigs pa med par sbyor ba dang /gtsang sbra med par za ba dang /rang gi khu bas rang mchod pa/rdo rje theg pa'i chos nyid yin//sems can brtse ba med pa dang /ma byin
11a4(kha): len dang mi tshangs spyod//gzhan la bslob par mi byed pa//'di ni rdo rje theg pa'i dam tshig ste//'di ni rang byung byang chub sems//bde ba gsal ba'i 'khor lo la//rgyal ba bdag nyid rtags par bzhugs//rdo rje theg pa gsang ba rma du byung ba'o//gang zhig bya
11a5(kha): chub mchog 'dod pa//rtag tu bdag nyid mchod par bya//mchod pa kun gyis rab tu mchod//yid bzhin rin chen la sogs rnams//sangs rgyas 'dod na bdag nyid mchod//sangs rgyas zhing khams thadra//bdag nyid kun gyi lha yin pas//kun gyis mchod
11a6(kha): //mas thams pa sa nan//rgyal kyis kyims pa ste/ gas yo ba sa/dri ni gas 'i byang chub sems/sas rgyas cing du chung meg/danas sa rgyas pom mu yin//ce sems pa ched /
11a7(kha): mam su lu dag gag du byangs pa ga gas du 'i 'as pa pa kyi gyis sos gas kyis pa grol pa sa mdzu na migs nags
11b1(kha): sa chos 'khrugs ngos la gti mug la/'dod pa'i sgying gi khrag chen po yi//kun gzhigatan nas phung mdzang dram mthung/yang ba'i dus na bskal ba'i se bar ga/mi za jigs gzugs pas mams dul mthung //bsal bu pru n+den pa du 'grubapa
11b2(kha): ma// gi d+han mas kun du hU~M 'jig byed pa//des kyang ni thugs kyi mtho ma srug/khrag sems ma sems la phyag 'dril sadrung/rnam papa yang chung klog pa'i rlu chen dang /'jig rtes pams kun tu g.yengs byed pa/dring sasus
11b3(kha): khams gsum thams cad 'thor byed pa/nga'i ba spu'i cha tsam brjod pa'i sgra/zil non tshad sku mnga' ba nga la thud//'jig rten 'di na lha mi 'ga' tsam gsod byed pa/gsod pas 'jig su rung zhes kun skrag kyang /gso dang mi g.yo thams cad kun gsol zhing //
11b4(kha): jigs med pa lnga bu la ji skad byo//sangs rgyas thaMd gsol kyang nga la zhos/dadung 'gro kun gsom byed sgribs shing skyes pa nga la 'thud/rang rig gshegs byed sras mang spel mdzad pa/mad dka' nga lta bu la kun bzang ji ltar bya//tshad med dbris mtshon sku
11b5(kha): mnga' gsol 'dra yang /ma drangs thab su mtshon pa tsam du zad//ji skad smras shing dmigs pa nga'i sku gsung thugs/dngos grub bdag la yod pa'i phyir/yid du 'ong ba bdag nyid bsgom/mkhas pas mchod pa bdag las byung na bdag las byung ba'i mchod pa rnams
11b6(kha): bdag nyid bdag la mchod par bya//mkhas pas bdag gi khu ba dag/sbyor ba'i don gyi rtag tu dbyung /'di ni sangs rgyas bdag nyid yin/sbyong po mkha' dang mtshungs pa'o//'di ni rdo rje theg pa'i spyod pa rmad du byung ba'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o//
11b7(kha): le'u 'dis gsang ba rmad du byung ba bstan pa te/bdag las byung ba'i khu ba yis//bdag gi lus la rab brgyan na//sangs rgyas mthong ba yi du 'ong //'jig rten khams rnams dbang du 'gyur//nga'i lus nyid sngags kyi lha//rtag par mchod na sangs rgyas 'grub/
12a1(kha): /gang gi mchod na dngos grub thob//ngag dang yid kyang phyag rgya yin//spyod pa cir yang sprul pa'i sku//dngos grub mchog rnams bdag la byung //byang chub myur du thob pa yin//lus dang ngag dang gsang ba'i gnas//nam mkha' bzhin du mnyam par gnas//sems la dmi/
12a2(kha): bapab bdag med pas//lha'i ting 'dzin gsang ba'i sngags//ngo bo nyid kyis dmigasu med//kye sems dpa' chen po/nga byang chub kyi snying por zhugs pa'i tshe//stong pa'i chos la stong pa'i yul med mi gnaso//byang chub sems la byang chub bsgrub pa'i rgyu med de mi gnas//srid pa'i
12a3(kha): chos thams cad la srid pa med de mi gnaso//chos nyid la sems med pas/rnam par dag pa la gnyi su med do//sems can tha~Md mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i phyir/yid la byed pa mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas so//sems can thams cad gsang ba
12a4(kha): mchog gi ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor gyi ye shes kyi lha nyid yin pas/sangs rgyas kun gyis gsang bar mdzad pa'o//nga las 'byung ba'i chen po lnga nyid de/ye shes gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha nyid yin pas/dngos grub gzhan nas blang du med pas dus gsum gyi bde bar
12a5(kha): gshegs pa'i gsang ba chen po nyid yi no//sems dpa' chen po/sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems 'di ni/sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs las byung ba nyid yino//de phyir sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems/sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs la gnas/sems dpa' de bzhin
12a6(kha): rig par gyis//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' ya mtshan pa'i ting 'dzin la zhugs nas 'di skad ces gsol to//sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems/sangs rgyas nyid kyi thugs la gnas/rdo rje sems dpa'i shes rab kyis yis pha rol phyin pa'i gnas/dus gsum gcig
12a7(kha): ste rtag par bzhug ng+ge gsol to//sems dpa' chen po de bzhin te/rnam par snang mdzad ye shes te/bde bar gshegs pa'i thugs la gnas//yi ge kun la mi snang yang /sems la gsal bar mngon par snang //skal ldan rnams kyis rang rig pa//don chen yi ge/
12b1(kha): dam pa ste//khong nas 'byung ba skye ba med//sems dpa' chen po/stong gsum gyis stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad ni sangs rgyas kun gyi ye shes dri ma med pa nyid yin//de'i phyir ngas 'di skad bka' stsal to//ye shes 'ba' zhig las byu/
12b2(kha): b.raH sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnyed pa yin//bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi gsang //gsang ba rma du byung ba'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o////le'u 'dis byang chub sems dpa'i lung bstan te//sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/bde bar gshegs
12b3(kha): ba rnams kyi rgyud gsang ba'i don rnam pa gsum ste/don dang lung dang nges pa'i gsang ba gsum//de la don ni bkri ba'i don ma yin te//nges pa'i don gtan la phab pa'o//de la bde bar gshegs pa rnams dang /snod bcud na mkhas bsdus pa'i chos//stong gsum gyi stong chen
12b4(kha): po'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus lus pa med par bsdus pa'i chos thams cad don gcig ste/sku gsung thugasu 'dus so//sku gsung thugs kyi don dbyer med de/gang gis mi shigs gang gis mi brjod/gang gis kyang mi phyed pas rdo rje'o//sku gsung thugasu ma gyur
12b5(kha): pa'i dngos po 'am ma 'dus pa'i chos gcig kyang mede/sku gsung thug su lhun gyis grub par gtan la phab pa'o//de'i phyir rdo rje chos so//de'i phyir rdo rje'i theg pa'o//lung ni sgom par shes par bya'o//sgom pa ni lha dang bdag du don gcig kyang gnyi su med par shes par
12b6(kha): bya'o//shes par bya ba ni chos thams cad kyi don ni ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /bdag nyid kyi don ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /lta ba ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i thugs gsang ba'i rgyud ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i rang
12b7(kha): bzhin ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i yon tan dang /rdo rje rnam par rol pa dang /rnam par rol pa chen po ma nor bar shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /chos nyid la longs spyod chen pos mngar mdzad pa'i don ma nor bar shes par bya/
13a1(kha): @/ba dang /nges pa'i gsang ba'i man dag dang rmadu byung ba'i don no//mdu byung ba'i gsang ba ni chos thams cad bdag nyid las 'byung zhing bdag nyid kyis mdzad/bdag las 'phros shing bdag nyid de la rol/bdag las sprul cing nyid kyi dbyi su bsdus/
13a2(kha): sa nyi la 'byung rnas pa ban cid ba sa sna pag tsang na na las bdag ny-id kyi hU~M/ shing lasazung ba nas pa skye / phyu rjen
13a3(kha): gla ya bdag nyid la bsrung de/bdag las ma byung ba 'am/bdag nyid dus gyur pa'i chos gcig kya med do//chos chen pon du byung ba las/gas ba ma du byung ba'i byang chub kyi sems lung du bstan pa'i lo'u ste bco lnga 'o///me'u 'dis gsang
13a4(kha): byung di kyi sbyin lasas rgyus kun gyi thugs ni gang //da ba gshegs pa thams cad 'byung ba'i gas sems can byinas pa gas pa chags brgya ni gsungs dus /
13a5(kha): nyid do//rnam par shes pa'i tshogs brgyad ni/mchod pa'i lha mo brgyad dang /dngos grub kyi rdzas nyid do//sems dpa' chen po dang /sgo nga'i rnam par shes pa ni/shes 'ab zab mo'i sgo dang /gsang ba'i rgyud mthar phyin mi 'grog pa'i sgo dang /chos nyid kyi 'khon
13a6(kha): lo bskor ba'i sgo dang /sku gsung thugs nyid gyur pa'i sgo dang /'bras bu yod pa'i sgo'o//sems can gyi sgo lnga'i rnam par shes pa la/yid kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar gyur pa ni/yi ge 'khor lo tshogs chen gyi gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor gsang ba nyid de/
13a7(kha): rnam par shes pa'i yul da /dbang po rnams kyi rnam par shes pa'i tshogs te/mtshan dang dpe byad kun las 'das pa ste yid kyi lha'o//de'i phyir dag 'das par sku/lus da ltar 'byung ba'i sku'o//dga'adas pa'i lam/sus dkar bzhugs pa'i lam
13b1(kha): pa'i lam/sems phyis 'byung ba'i lam nyid do//ngag 'das pa'i yul lus da ltar bzhugs pa'i yul ta sems phyis 'byung ba'i yul/sems dpa' chen po de'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyis mkhyen pa'i yul/sangs rgyas nyid kyi gsang ba'i gnas//gsang /
13b2(kha): barma du byung ba'i yul bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu drug pa'o////le'u 'dis rma du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bstan te//sems ni ji bzhin mnyam pa yi//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rnal 'byor//yang dag bdag med de bzhin nyid//yang dag lam de yi ge med//'gro ba
13b3(kha): bA med pas lam yang med//kun spyod yon tan rnam lnga ni/ye shes rol pa'i bdag nyid yin ka 'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rnams//sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i ye shes lam//sku gsung thugs kyi byang chub sems//byang chub sems ni thar pa'i lam//'khor
13b4(kha): ba'i sgyum tsho mtha' ring las//nyon mongs sems can gnas pa'i lam//byang chub sems ni thabs chen gyis//gsang po 'ba' zhig las rung /mchong ba chen po bde ba che//'did tshigs chen po dga' ba'i mchog/mche ba chen po zhe sdang che//zhe sdang chen
13b5(kha): rdzogs sangs rgyas//mchod pa chen po gti mug che//gti mug rdo ste gti mugasel//mchad pa chen po phrag dog ce//khrag dog chen po dga' bas dger//mchod pa chen por rgyal che//nga rgyal chen po rgyal bas rgyus//kye sems dpa' chen go ni/
13b6(kha): gshegs pa rnams kyi thugs rje rol pa mdzad pa'i don du/byang chub sems dpa' rol pa'i yul ma gtogs par/dmyal ba'i yul ye nas med do//dmyal ba'i rgyu med de'i dngos kyang med//kye sems dpa' chen po/ma skyes pa'i chos rnams la/nam
13b7(kha): kyang skye bar mi 'gyuro//sangs rgyas dang sems can gyi gnas ni gnyisu med de gcig pa'o//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing khams yongasu dag pa nyid//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i
14a1(kha): @#/'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa 'dri na/'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa yang med/bde ba chen po'i chos nyid 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid do//de'i phyir byang chub kyi ye shes ni/sangs rgyas thugs la gnas par snang /rnam
14a2(kha): shes dmigs pa'i yul las 'das/gsal ba mchog gi bde ba yis//sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs la gnas//'di ni gsang ba'i byang chub sems//zhes gsungaso//bong ba chen po'i dkyil 'khor rma du byung ba'i le'u ste bcu bdun pa'o//
14a3(kha): le'u 'dis rma du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bstan ste/yang gsungs pa/sems dpa' chen po/yang dag sangs rgyas gnas rig pa/rtag par sangs rgyas nyid la gnas//na mkha' lta bur chags pa med//gnas de nyid ni yang dag pa'o//
14a4(kha): phung po lnga don de nyid shes//ma 'pho ba'i don yang rig/mi 'pho ba'i lam la gnas//rang bzhin med pa'i don shes rig/rang bzhin med pa'i lam la gnas//mo skyes pa'i sems nyid la//sems can skye ba'i rgyu mi gnas//ma rig med pas 'khor ba'ang med/
14a5(kha): mya ngan mi 'da' brtag par bzhugs//mya ngan 'das pa'i las yang med//srid pa'i chos la rang bzhin med//lus dang ngag sems dag pa las//chos rnams rang bzhin dag par zad//sems can med de sangs rgya sku med//sa sar+g+has la mA la mi grAn+ta /
14a6(kha): 'khor ba'i skyon gyis mi gos shing //rnam par thar pa'i sgo las 'das//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad chen po snying po byang chub na bzhugs pa'i sku la/sangs rgyas thad dad pas 'dus nas/tshig 'thun par 'di skad ces gsol to//
14a7(kha): 'jig rten mokhas pa rdzogs sangs rgyas/gang gis theg mar byang chub sems//bla med dkyil 'khor sgom pa rnams//snying po byang chub bzhugs par gsungs/'dzam bu gling du gsungs pa yis//de ltar bzhugs dang 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs sangs rgyas man
14b1(kha): pa rnams//thams cad don du bshad par gsol//de nas skyob pa sangs rgyas rnams//gsungs pa 'di ni yang dag bshad//byang chub sems ni ma yin pa'i//khams gsum gnas pa'i sems can la//phan pa'i sman ni gzhan med de//byang chub sems
14b2(kha): kyi don rig naka 'khor ba'i chos ni rgyu medaka/ myur du byang chub kun gyis thob//mi skye mi 'jigs bde la gnas//sku gsung thugs kyi gsang ba las//yang dag snying por byung ba ni//de ni byang chub sems zhes bya//de las byung bar rig pa ni/rma du byung ba/
14b3(kha): byang chub sems//gsang ba mchog gi rin po che//sangs rgyas rnams kyi dkyil 'khor yin//sangs rgyas rnams kyi gsang ba'o//khams gsum bla na med pa'i lam//sa rnams ngag dang dbyer med pas//sa rnams sangs rgyas nyid kyi sa//rnal 'byor
14b4(kha): dbang phyug chen po yis//gsang ba'i don rnams gcig tu sgom//ye shes rang byung byang chub sems//sangs rgyas thams cad bdag gis byas//ye shes lha rnams rang las byung //'di ni rma du byung ba'i sku//gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa yin//'adz+ra/
14b5(kha): dang bsnyen pa bya mi dgosaka byang chub sems ni skye du med//lha yang gzhan nas bod mo dgos//mchod pa gzhan la mi mchod do//dngos grub gzhon la mi slong ngo //sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems//bla na med par rtog gyur naka rgyal bas gsungs pa'i ye shre/
14b6(kha): gang //de ni dkyil 'khor snying por gsungs/ka/sems dpa' chen po gsang ba mchog gi dkyil 'khor 'di ni//sangs rgyas nyid kyis mkhyen pa yi no//sems can mang por byas pa ni//gsang ba mchog gi don nyid do//sangs rgyas nyid kyis mdzad pa yin//gas/
14b7(kha): brgyadu byung ba'i dkyil 'khor bstan pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o///le'u 'dis byang chub sems sgom pa'i don bstan te/khams gsum rin chen rnyed pa'i mika/ji ltar 'dod pa bzhin du 'grub//khams 'di yid bzhin nyi du 'gyur//sems dpa'
15a1(kha): #//chen po//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di ni/yid bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che nyid yin no//'di skad gsungs pa'i chos thos nas//rdo rje sems dpa' dpa' bo yang //'jigs shing spangs nas stan las langs/mchod
15a2(kha): pasna tshogs byas nas kyang //dga' ba'i tshig gis 'di skad gsol//sdang mdzad rdo rje rgyal mtshan ma//dga' ba'i bsam pas bstod byed pa//sangs rgyas kun pas sngar sangs rgyas//mi shes dri ma yongs nas med//dpal gyi rnam rgyal de bzhin du//lo/
15a3(kha): spyod dbang phyug dpal dang ldan//rdo rje nyi ma gzi brjid dang /mdog sngag shin tu 'bar ba yis//bzhad cing rma du mdzad pa yis//bzhad cing rma du mdzad pa yis//dga' mgu rangs pa rnamasu 'gyur//pad+ma bzhad pas chos kun gyi/rtogs mdzad sangs rgyas
15a4(kha): thams cad kyis//byang chub sems 'dis mnyes gyur nas//skad cig nyid la don rtogs te//snying po byang chub nyid 'gyur//rdo rje gdan la yang sangs rgyas//lam 'di rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems//phyi ma 'byung bar bde ba dang /sangs rgyas nyid la rdzogs 'gyur te//sangs
15a5(kha): rgyas nyid dang skal ba mnyam//zhes gsol to//de nas ston pa bla na med//rab tu dgyes pa mdzad nas 'dzum//de nas thugs gsang ba'i sgron mas 'di skad gsungs so//rmang byung byang chub sems bsgom pa//'gro ba myur du byang chub ste/ma skyes byang chub rmad byung la//nyon mongs las/
15a6(kha): kyi gos mi 'gyur//byang chub 'ams 'di byang chub nyid//tshogs rnams ma bsags cig char rdzogs//rang gi snying por rang sngags kyis//bsgrub pa'i snying por chud mi za//rang gi snying por rang bas nas//bsams na lhun gyis grub pa'i sku//sangs rgyas mi sgom bdag nyid sgom//snying
15a7(kha): bo mi brjod bdag nyid brjod//bdag nyid ma mthong sku gsum mthong //'khor ba ma mthong bde ba nyid//rang gi lhar ni bdag sgom na//bsod nams ye shes cig car rdzogs//rang gi snying por rang sngags rdzogs//bsgrub pa'i snying por chud mi za//phyag rgya mi bskyod yan lag bskyod//
15b1(kha): sangs rgyas mi gshegs bdag nyid gshegs//sangs rgyas mi mchod bdag nyid mchod//ston pa ma mthong bdag nyid mthong //sangs rgyas ma mthong slob dpon mthong //gzugs nyid ma mthong chos sku mthong //dpe byad mi blta rang lus blta//yang dag ye shes rang rig gnas/
15b2(kha): myur du bla na med pa'o//thams cad 'byung ba'i sbyor ba yis//bdag gi lhar ni bdag nyid bya//'di ni sangs rgyas thaM dgal//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes kyi//shes rab sa lus phun sum tshogs//'di 'dra'i dkyil 'khor bla na med//sangs rgyas de bzhin gshegs pa gsung/
15b3(kha): theg pa gsum gyi nges 'byung la//theg pa gcig gi 'bras bur gnas//byang chub sems sgom pa rma du byung ba'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o////le'u 'dis gsang ba mchog gi bde ba rma du byung ba bstan te/sems dpa' chen po//sman mchog nad med bder 'tsho ba//dus
15b4(kha): gsum mchog byang chub sems//'jig rten sman rnams 'di yi no//byang chub sems kyis byas pa rnams//dge tshul dpag bsam ljon shing bzhin//rtag tu byang chub bde ba yis//byang chub mchog gi snying por 'gyur//sems dpa' chen po/phung po lnga rigs lnga/rigs
15b5(kha): lnga rdzogs pa'i lhun gyis grub pa'i gsang ba'i dkyil 'khor yin la//yang dag pa'i dkyil 'khor ni//gzugs kyi mtshan nyid mang po sna tshogs par 'byung ba rnams ni//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi lha dang /lha mo dang /sems dpa' dang sems ma la sogs pa/de bzhin
15b6(kha): gshegs pa'i rigs lhun gyis grub pa'i gnas pa la bya'o//de'i don ni 'dus pa dang /'tshogs pa dang yongasu rdzogs pas//de la tshogs shing 'dus pa ni//dkyilo//mtshan nyid so sor snang ba ni 'khor lo ste/'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnam par snang mdzad/
15b7(kha): mngo par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa dkyil 'khor bye//rams gsus rnam par rgyas pa'i dkyil 'khor //suni snying po byang chub na 'azhugs pa'i ra thung sa shes kyi su'o//byang chub sems ni chos nyid byang chub sem so//phyag rgyani byang chub mchogag byas kyi
16a1(kha): /gas na phyogs panir gnig so//de'i phyir rnam par sdang mdzad kun tu nas zhes bya'o//gas pa'i rgyud las bshad pa yin//sa ni se shes bla ma sa nyid //sems dpa' chen po/gzugs kyi phung po las tshor ba snga tshogs rang byung ba ni/tshor ba'i phung po ste rin po ma dzi
16a2(kha): lhun gyis grub pa'i lha dang lha mo dang /sems dpa' dang sems ma la sogs pa'i rin po che'i lhun gyis rdzogs pa ste/de la gcig tu 'dus te/rang bzhin med pa ni dkyil/mang po 'tshogs pa ni 'khor te/rin chen 'byung ldan mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa ni/rin chen 'byung
16a3(kha): ldan zhes bya ba'i dkyil 'khor chen po ste/dbang chen rol pa'i dkyil 'khor ro//byang chub sems ni byang chub sems thugs rini rin po che'o//phyag rgya ni dbang sbyin pa'i phyag rgya'o//sku ni rin chen 'byung ldan sla'o//sa ni yi ge 'khor lo tshogs chen gyi sa'o//kye sems dpa' chen po/tshor ba'i phung /
16a4(kha): bo las 'du shes rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung bas de nyid ni pad+ma'i rigs te/'du shes rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba nyid/pad+ma'i rigs lhun gyis rdzogs pa ste/pad ma'i rigs kyi lha dang lha mo dang sems dpa' dang sems ma la sogs pa/chos nyid dkyil 'khor rang byung ba yin te/bka' 'khor lo//
16a5(kha): ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid do//thugs rje 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor zhes gsung so//sku ni bsod nams kyi sku'o//byang chub kyi sems kyi ye shes kun tu spros pa nyid do//sa ni rdo rje 'dzin pa'i sa'o//phyag rgya ni chos kyi phyag rgya'o//sems dpa' chen po 'du shes kyi phung po las kA las byed pa sna tshos
16a6(kha): /'byung ba ni las kyi rigs te/'du byed mang po 'byung ba nyid ka las kyi rigs kyi lha dang lha mo dang /sems dpa' sems ma la sogs pa/las kyi rigs lhun gyis rdzogs pa ste/bdag nyid las 'byung bar mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa te/'gro ba 'dul ba'i dkyil 'khor nyid//
16a7(kha): byang chub sems ni rdo rje'o//sku ni longs spyod pa'i sku'o//phyag rgya ni las kyi phyag rgya'o//sa ni kun tu bzang po mchog sa'o//sems dpa' chen po/'du byed kyi phung po las byed pa'i rnam par shes pa sna tshogs par rang byung ba nyid ni/rnam par shes pa'i phung po ste rdo rje'i rig
16b1(kha): rnam par shes bamang po nyid/rdo rje rigs kyi lha dang lha mo dang /sems dpa' dang sems ma la sogs pa/rdo rje rigs lhun gyis rdzogs pa nyid yin te/sngon te mngon par sangs rgyas pa 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor te/thams cad kyi dbyings rnam par dag gapa rdzogs pa chen
16b2(kha): dkyil 'khor nyid//byang chub sems ni spros pa med pa'o//spyan rdo rje'i sku'o//drug rgya ni dam tshig gi phyag rgyar//sani bya ba med pa'i sa ro//sems dpa' chen po//dazan na gshegs pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi nang na/'di ni gsang ba chen po'i gsang ba'i mchog sa
16b3(kha): phung po lngal rigs lnga mang po dang /lha dang lha mo mang po dang /kha dag mang po dang /ye shes mang po dang /sku yang po dang /gsung mang po dang /thugs mang po dang /phyag mtshan mang po dang /phyag rgya dang /ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor mang po 'das shing 'tshogs pas tshogs
16b4(kha): dkyil 'khor nyid do//gsang ba thams cad 'dir 'dus pa/gsang ba 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor ro//khams gsum bla na med pa'i gsang ba mchog gi dkyil 'khor na du byung ba bstan te/dkyil 'khor lnga po 'di dag ni/rims kyis bkong ba ma yin te/lha'i ting 'dzin
16b5(kha): gang bsgom pa'i sku gcig las/sku lnga lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin gyi gnas dang /dkyil 'khor gcig las dkyil 'khor lnga char rdzogs par rang byung ste/bkad cing bskyed mi dgos so//byang chub sems kyang chos nyid ma skyes pa las byang chub sems
16b6(kha): lnga 'byung bas/rang byung mchog gi dkyil 'khor te/phyag rgya yang rang bzhin gyi gsal bar 'byung bas rang byung mchog gi phyag rgya'o//de bzhin du sku dang /ye shes dang /rigs dang /phyag mtshan dang /kha dog thams cad gcig las kun rdzogs par 'byung zhing /
16b7(kha): rang bzhin gyis gdas so//gas ban du byung ba las//khams gsus thun med pa'i sangs rgyas gsang ba sa gong nas sod pa rdzogs par sa ci/ rdong gsus kyi stong chen po'i 'ag rten gyi khams pasas gyis 'i bya ma pas klas te su med pa'i ban
17a1(kha): dkyil 'khor lnga lnga'i rang bzhin gyis yod pa dang /snod kyi 'jig rten chen po lnga gcig gi mtshan nyid la yang /dkyil 'khor lnga lnga rdzogs par yod do//bde bar gshegs pa'i sku bsam gyis mi khyab pa re re la/dkyil 'khor lnga lnga rdzogs par bzhugs pas
17a2(kha): /de'i phyir gsang ba rma du byung ba nyid do//byang chub sems rma du byung ba nyid do//dkyil 'khor ma du byung ba nyid do//rtswa ban du byung ba nyid do//chos chen po na du byung ba nyid do//sangs rgyas na du byung ba nyid do//rol pa dang sprul pan du byung ba nyid//mkhyen pa rma du byung ba nyid do//
17a3(kha): ye shes rma du byung ba nyid do//cho 'phrul chen po dang /rdzu 'phrul chen po rma du byung ba nyid//mdzad pa rma du byung ba nyid//sku gsung thugs rma du byung ba nyid do//dus gsum rma du byung ba nyid do//srid pa gsum rma du byung ba nyid do//khams bla na med pa/gsang ba mchog gi
17a4(kha): dkyil 'khor bla na med pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o///le'u 'dis gsang ba mchog gi bde ba chen po bstan te/kye sems dpa' chen po/de bas sangs rgyas ma yin pa'i/sems can gyi sems kyi rgyu ni gang /byang chub sems ni ma yin/pa'i chos dang chos nyid
17a5(kha): dngos ni gang /dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor gsungs pa nyid/rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bdag la 'dus/dkyil 'khor bdag la 'dus/dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor zhes bya ba/ye shes dkyil 'khor thugs la gnas/rdo rje mi shes mchog nyid ni/bdag gi sbyor ba brdzun/
17a6(kha): pa'o/ thang rang byung dkyil 'khor ro//dkyil 'khor snga ma 'dus pa'i chos de ni/sangs rgyas nyid la'ang mi mnga'o//kye sems dpa' chen po/skye ba'i seMn thams cad kyang /dbyings gsum rnam par dag pa yin/skye ba med pa'i sangs rgyas yin/byang chub sems kying po 'dri/
17a7(kha): sangs rgyas gsang ba nyid las gsungs//gang phyir sangs rgyas nyid las gsungs/byang chub sems ni skra gro'i so//til ma rang brtags ral gro bzhin//byang chub sems ni yongs kyi lam//skyon gyis bad+ma bzhin//bskal bar 'bar ba'i me bzhin du//sdug bsngal sdig pa/
17b1(kha): /nam grol bla med 'bras bu 'grub//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun dang //bla na med pa thams cad kyang //nga'i byang chub sems la gnas//mtshan dang dpe byad kun las 'das//yid bzhin nor bu rin chen ltar//ma bsams lhun gyis grub pa'i sku//khams gsum 'di na gcig/
17b2(kha): bu gnas//gcig pu rab tu che ba'i sa//gsang ba mchog gi dgyes pa na//thams cad bdag nyid gcig pu bzhugs//khams gsum med par gnas med pas//sems dang chos nyid gcig pu 'dus//gsang ba mchog gi bde ba bstan pa'i li'u ste nyi shu gcig pa'o///
17b3(kha): le'u 'dis lta ba rmad du byung ba bstan te//kye grogs po dag/byang chub sems ni mngon sangs rgyas//don 'di de ltar gang rig pa//rnal 'byor rtag tu bde la gnas//dmyal ba'i gnas ni bla med lam//dmyal ba'i gnas na de ltar spyod//gnod sbyin srin po gshin rje'i
17b4(kha): tshogs//sangs rgyas yin par kun gyis mthong //dngos la dmyal ba'i rgyu mi gnas//dmyal ba'i rgyu med kun gyi rig/dmyal ba'i me lce'i phreng ba rnams//byang chub sems yin gnod pa 'jil//de phyir sangs rgyas 'da' zhig rnal 'byor med kau/de la lam mod 'gro ba'ang med//sung+yas
17b5(kha): med de rgyu yang med//byang chub med de ming yang med//byang chub nyid kyis yang med//sems can med srog kyang med//nyon mongs med 'khor ba yang med//bde ba med de 'bras bu yang med//re ba med dogs pa yang med//gnas pa med cing yul yang med//theg pa med cing //
17b6(kha): 'jug pa yang med//smon pa med cing bsgrub pa'ang med//spong ba med cing len pa'ang med//sems kyi gnas kyi yul med pas//sgom du med pas byang chub sems//gsang ba dam pa'i dam pa na du byung ba'i le'u ste nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa'o///le'u 'dis brtsapA ba
17b7(kha): dang bral ba'i rgyu/rol du med pas 'grub pa'i rgyu bstan te/kun rig chos kyi rjes bka' stsal pas/lce chos thams cad kyi chos nyid 'din ngo mtshar gyi ste/de ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i rnal 'byor yin te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa med na/pha rol tu phyin pa dgu'i
18a1(kha): de med ci thar pa'i las yang med de/'asrung ba med pas rnas 'byor dumi chung cing don med pa pan//de nas mngon sum pa'i ting 'dzin grags nas yang gsus pa/de bzhin gshegs pa la chos zad sangs rgyas pa med/nas kyi dbyis zhes byu yang med de
18a2(kha): de nyid chos kyi daskyes so//lta ba thams cad la mkhas par bya'o//bslab ba tha~Md rnam bslab pa/gang med de sgrub pa ma yin te/gang la sgrub pa med pa ni /mkhas pa 'di bslab pa yin//byang chub 'dod pa de la byang chub med//sa dang rnam par byang chub mchog la rig 'di
18a3(kha): chos cing gang gA ga gyur bapa/rnaMs ni byang chub myur du thob par 'gyur/byang chub sems mdu byung ba ltas/brtsam pa dang bral bu'i le'u ste k+Sh+mI kurtsa gsum pa'o///de nas su 'dum nan pa skyes bya ba'i ting 'dzin la zhugs nas/sang 'di spyad gsung so//pha rol

18a6(kha): mkhyen pa'i ye shes thob pa ste ye shes so//lus las byung ba'i chos la kun tu spyod pa'o//sems las kun 'byung ba'i chos la spyod pa'o//ngag las kun 'byung ba'i chos las spyod pa'o//chos las kun 'byung ba'i chos la spyod pa'o//yul bar na se'i gos bzhin du/so

18b1(kha): brtogs ka' ba'i dbyings//sangs rgyas gzhanan song ba ma yin no//yang gsungs pa/bam mnyam pa'i lhag pa'i sems dang che zer so rdzogs pa'i chos kur thugs rung bas khams gsum las rnas par rgyal ba'i byang chub sems rba du byung ba nyid yin no//
18b2(kha): byang chub sems rma du byung ba'i dam tshig brgyad ni/ye nas ma skyes pa dang /ye nas ma byung ba dang /ye nas mi gnas pa dang ka ye nas stong pa dang /med pa dang /phyal ba dang /gcig pu dang /lhun gyis gnas pa dang brgya do//de la ma skyes mi mchi'o//gdod nas ma byung
18b3(kha): bas mi 'gro'o//mi gnas pas mya ngan las 'das pa'o//gzung 'dzin las bral bas ye nas stong pa'o//med pas dam tshi go/gcig bsrungs pas kun thub pas bsrungs pa'o//phyal bas dam tshig la phyogs ris med pa grangs las 'das pa'o//stong spyi phung yin te gong na
18b4(kha): gzhan med pas gcig pu a d+hi so gar dam tshig rtag pa gsung drung du gnas pas mi 'gyur te/lhun gyi gnas pa'o//ma du byung ba'i dam tshig nyi shu rtsa lnga spyod do//bsrung ba med pa gsum thug su chud//thugs las mi mkhyen pa med cing /thams cad thug su chung bas
18b5(kha): dung ba dang /gsung las thams cad ma rdzogs pa med pas bsrung du med pa dang /sgra las thams cad spyan gyis rdzogs shing gdan gyi longs spyod la thogs pa med pas bsrung du meng pa'o//de TIr bsrung ba sred pa'i sku gsung thugs/mkhyen pa med pas thams cad chug
18b6(kha): thams cad bsrung du med pa dang /bsrung ba thams cad rdzogs pas na/sku lnga lhun rdzogs bsrung du med/rgyas gsungs so//byang chub sems rma du byung ba las bsrung du med pa'i dam tshig gi le'u ste/nyi shu rtsa bzhi'o///le'u 'dis mchog tu gsang ba'i man
18b7(kha): ngag gi le'u ba stan te//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chen pos gso la pa/bcom ldan 'das sngon chad mi mthong mo rig pa'i/bcom ldan 'das bdag dang bde bar gshegs pa thams cad kyis sngon ma mthong ba ngo mtshar che ba'i gsang ba chen po'i chos/
19a1(kha): de bzhin gshegs pa'i theg pa gang dang mi mthun pa sngags kyi chos gang ba yang mi mnyam pa/gsang ba gang dang yang sa 'dres pa/theg pa gang dasang blur mo sa mi pa'i chos nyid ming med par sangs rgyas med par sems can ming med par/rol par

19b1(kha): bzhin du sems can rnams kyi don mdzad pa dang /mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du sems can rnams kyi don mdzad pa yin pas/thug su chud de mkhyen cing /snod dang sbyar nas gsungs pa yino//sems dpa' chen po/gsung ni rdo rje sgra mi nyid do//
19b2(kha): ba byang chub sems ni lus ngag yid gsum so sor ma yin tha mi dad pa/gnyi su med par rig la/rig par byed pa ni sbyor ba'o//sgrol ba ni chen po/gsung ni rdo rje sgra mo nyid do//sbyor ba byang chub sems ni lus ngag snyid gsum so sor ma yin pa tha mi dad pa/
19b3(kha): gnyis su med par rig la/rig par byed pa ni sbyor ba'o//sgrol ba ni lus dang ngag dang /sems dbyer med pa ni rdo rje'o//rdo rje ni gang na yang mi gnas pa dang /chos thams cad gdon yang mi gnas pa nyi du gyur pa nyid sgom pa'o//chos can po rma du byung ba'i///

19b5(kha): nyams pa bskang ba'i don bstan te/rdo rje sems dpas ston pa la zhus pa/chos chen po rma du byung ba thugs rje chen po ji lta bu kun rig chos kyi rje ston pas bka' stsal pa/stong ri gnam lcags thog chen gyis/skyon gyi gangs ri'i rtse nas bcom/bdud rtsi rgya mtsho zhu nas
19b6(kha): dkyil/ting 'dzin 'bum phrag yangs pas ni/'dis ni nyams pa med pa'o//yang rdo rje sems dpas ston pa la zhus pa/thugs gsang ba'i rgyud 'di gang las gsungs pa/ri rab sum cu phrag dgu'i bskal pa zhing gi rdul rnyed kun zhes zhus pas/ston pa kun rig ches
19b7(kha): kyi su bka' stsal pa ta snying gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kun dang /rma du byung ba'i don kun gcig ste/ri rab sum cu phrag dgu yang /rmad byung byang chub sems 'di mas//sgom pas rdo rje phyi mar rlag/zhes gsungaso//de phyir byang dul
20a1(kha): gdu byung ba'i sku ni//stong spyi lud ces bya'o//gsung ni ye shes mkha' la rol zhes bya'o//thugs ni mamakha'i spang po la rdo rje'i zong gis dra zhes bya'o//de yang rma du byung ba'i sku ni/snying po byang chub na bzhugs pa'i sku zhes bya'o//rma du byung ba'i gsung ni
20a2(kha): snying po byang chub na bzhugs pa las/dgongs pa nges par 'grel pa zhes bya'o//du byung ba'i thugs ni/mtshan nyid ye shes mi zad pa'i blo gros kyi thugs ste/me shes thugs te thugs kyi mchog/ces bka' stsal to//de yang thugs gsang ba nyid kyi rgyud las
20a3(kha): nam kho'o sku yas mad byung na na mkha'i gsung las las ston pa/nam kha'i thugs ni thugs kyi mchog/chad byung na mkha' gnas kyi mchog/ces 'byung ste/de ltar namakha'i sku gsung thugs bshad nas sa'i sku gsung thugs bshad pa ni sa sku ni'i rag

23a6(kha): gsung drung du ba ldan po dang /byang chub sems mthor thug med pa dang rgyad do//des daphyen byang chub sems kyi 'ong bzhin na bstan pa la yang don gnyis to//snang ni srid pa'i chos thams cad yo nas ma bcas par sangs rgyas par bstan pa dang /de b+hang rig cing /gas na/

23b1(kha): rnam na dang /bya chub sebs pas pa gartags pa c+doMs 'dzin na gnag sa tham du me bar ldan pa'o/bya chu sa pa sgyu lus kyi dang ldan pa la 'di ste/sa par 'gyu / su bar mo'i gyis/gong sa
23b2(kha): ling 'bras bu byang chub sems kun dbang por shar bar bstan pa dang /mi rtog par mi shes kyi rig pa rang las 'char bar bstan pa dang /chos skye gag nga bral pa'i ngang las kyang /zugs rje thabs mkhas pa'i bye brag sna chog su bstan pa dang /phung po dgas
23b3(kha): skye mched lha'i sku phyag rgya chen por bsgoms pas/dus gsum du 'dus ma byas pa'i snying po//ye shes kyi rig pa cid+ha'i dkyil du 'char bstan pa'o//byang chub sems mthar thug pa med pa ltar bstan pa la/yang don lnga ste/thabs dang shes rabasgom pas/rigs
23b4(kha): sde'i sangs rgyas kyi sku mdog dang /bzugs tshul bstan pa dang /dbyings dang se shesu gsal bas/sku'i dngos po dang bral ste/rigs pa'i ting 'dzin spangs pas/thams cad 'od kyi dkyil 'khor du bstan pa dang /shes pa dab shes bya rtogs pas/sas
23b5(kha): rgyas kyi dgongs pa ma 'dres par thug su chud par bstan pa dang /na mkha' ltar kun la khyab cing rero dogs dang 'khor ba'i chos med par bstan pa dang /rang rig pa byang chub kyi sems kyi rtogs pa'i 'bras bu dus gsum du mi 'gyur bar bstan pa'o//byang chub sems
23b6(kha): khyab gdal chen por bstan pa la yang don drug ste/gsang ba mchog gi dkyil 'khor du ni/don rtogs pas/ne ra sa ha'i dbyings bgrod par bstan pa dang /'du 'bral med pa'i don btsal dang bral bar bstan pa dang /lung gi sha thang phye nas man ngag gi dbyings
23b7(kha): bgrong par bstan pa dang /dam tshig sdom pa la gnas shing sku gsung thugs kyi 'bras bu thob par bstan pa dang /rgyal ba thams cad kyi thugs kyi gter byang chub sems rmad du byung ba yin par bstan pa dang /rgyu 'bras chos thams cad ji ltar bstan pa
24b1(kha): dang 'a ba rnams cad kyi chung du 'gyur ste rgyud gyis gnas pa'i chos nyi kun gyi snying po'o//sgra dang shes kyi 'byung gan su gyu pas chung la/sems ga sa gyu bas//kun gzhigs pa dbyis rnams baspyang ba lal du med panam /

24b6(kha): na no sum mas du byung b.las/'a mi chog la snyen pa'i 'a du ste sus bu 'dus lo//su 'is byang chub sems ca du byu ba'i yod tan na te byo nu sams rlom tha di tan pa/gsungs pa'i mas gsus ni ni nyi/
25a1(kha): @pa'i mi zhes 'byung ba ni /ka dper na byang chub sems rmad byung ba'i don rtogs pa ni/mchod dang 'ching bu nor bu'i ri/nor che ba snang bai DUR+Ya lug shig tsam zhig gi 'od kyis/ri di ril kyang nor bu'i ri de kun bai DUrmar sngon no//de dang 'dra bar byang chub sems kyi
25a2(kha): sems stong spyi phud kyi ye shes mkha' la rol pa 'di ni rmang du byung ba ste/khams gsum ril yang rin po cher mthong la/srid pa gsum yang rin po cher gyur pa la bya'o//de bzhin du ming bzhin gyi nor bu rin po che la ni/ci yang 'byung ba lta bu zhes 'byung ngo //byang chub sems/
25a3(kha): @rmad du byung ba los/rma du byung ba'i yon tan dang /sgom pa'i lung bstan pa'i le'u ste sum cu brgyad pa'o///le'u 'dis byang chub sems kyi man ngag bstan te/byung ba las thams cad rnam par dag ste/byas zin pas byung /don thams cad dbyingasu gcig ste/
25a4(kha): ma btsal bar dbyings su med pas chub po//thig le chen pos kun la khyab ste/gcig pas sems kun tu bzang po'i ngang du gsal tI/mi 'byed pas bde/rgyu rkyen kun dang bral te rag gzhan la mya lus pa chen po/rags par stong pa'i rtsol ba mi bas mo ste bye ba byed pa
25a5(kha): med pas byung /mi dmigs pa'i ngang la mi g.yo ba'i dran pa kun dang bral bas chub/skye ba med pa'i ting 'dzin la yid bzhin du mi yengs pa'i tshe na sems//dran pa rnal du phab pa la gnyen po gzhan med pas bde/dbang dang dngos grub kyi rig gang du snang mo lu so ti/
25a6(kha): gyis bsgrub mi dgos pas dgongs pa lhun gyis rgya bas chen po ni byang chub sems ca du myong ba las/byang chub sems man ngag tu yin pa'i len ste sum cu dgub 'o///da ni le ru 'dis slob ma sdong bdang po'i slob ma la/lobs par bya ba'i gtan tshogs gtan te/
25a7(kha): byang chub sems ni kun tu bzang po yang yin no//rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs te rigs lnga yang yin no//rigs kyi sum rnams kyang yin no//byang chub sems dpa' dang sems ma yang yin no//sku gsung thugs kyang min//rgyu 'byung chen po lnga yang yino//rgyu yang yin no//'bras bu/
25b1(kha): yang yid/rdo rje yang yin no//ye shes kyang yin//chos nyid kyang yin no//chos kyi dbyis kyang min//rnaMs kyi su bde ba chen po meng mino//chos can sa sa no//'khor ba yang yin//mya ngan las 'das pa yang yin no//gnyis med pa sang yino//rgyu bras kyang mi
25b2(kha): rnam par snang mdzad kyang min//yang dag pa'i ye shes kyang yin no//sems dpa' bcu song yin no//de'i phyir sna tshogs kun tu bzang po rdo rje sems/de gnyis ma skyes rgyal ba sum gyo mkha'/sab sum dbyer med pa ni sku gsung thugs//nas lung gi rgyal po
25b3(kha): gsungs pa'i mnA dag bstan pa'i le'u ste bzhi bcu pa'o//leru 'dis na du byung ba'i slob ma dang /snod kyi sbyin dang yon tan bstan te/de yang gzhung las theg pa man pa la zhugs pa dang /rang gi sloba'i bangs dma' bya dang /zlos pa nyung b+h+yo/
25b4(kha): sbrasgram dang /bslang ba'i tshig gis 'chal ba dang /spus ngan dang pa chung ba dang /pho dregs che la ring phyogs can/gcad kyang khrag med ser snas bcings//de la rmad byung don mi bshad//bshad kyang blo rgyud mi khrol bas//bla med theg rgyal rdzogs pa'i lung //a ti sr-i/
25b5(kha): rim pang //byang chub sems ma byung ba las//slob ma snong na pa la mi bskrun par 'du skye pa'i bcu gcig pa'o//da ni le 'u sa slobs snong babang ba'i sleb phrul longs par byu gtan cig dbulas thun mo sa ma yin pa'i do brgyung kyi bstan to//
25b6(kha): da yang gzhung las glang pos theg tsong khur bskur na/dung 'gro ba la dang stobs mi mnyam//rgyal stabs de yang thun mong min//gangs kyisang gi'i gangs byung na/sbe chags pas dang gdangangos mi mnyam//gangs gang 'di sa thun mong min/rgyam michen po'i rnangs ma
25b7(kha): naT chu ban phal dang rlabs mi mnyam//chu klung de yang thun mong min//ri rab lhun po'i rtser phyin na/ri phran pal dang 'phang mi mnyam//rtse rgyal 'di yang thun mong min//mkha' gsal nyi zla'i 'od mthong na//gzar skar gal dang 'od mi mnyam//'od 'di kun dang thun mong min/
26a1(kha): khyud chen nam 'phang gcod mthong na//'dab chags phal dang shugs mi mnyam//rtsal shugs 'di yang thun mong min//rdo rje theg pa'i gting mthong na//theg gzhan lal dang gting mi mnyam//gting bag 'di yang thun mong min//shes rgyud sbyangs pa'i mkhas
26a2(kha): dang /thong ba chung ba'i blo sbram gnyis//'gro ba'i don la don mi mnyam//don mchog 'di yang thun mong min//zhes gsungaso//chos chen po rma du byung ba las//slob ma snod bzang po la lobs par bya ba'i yon tan thun mong ma yin pa'i le'u ste zhi gnyis pa'o//
26a3(kha): rdzogs so////rgya gar gyi mkhan po shrI sid+ha dang /bod kyi lo ts+tsha ba bai ro tsa nas bsgyur ba'o///
27a1(kha): rgya bar gang du po d+hi hU~Ms pa shi ka/khong smang du /byang chub kyi sems rbad du byung ba byang chub sems sa du byung ba 'du bcom ldan 'das dpas kyi ga/sas rdo rje sems dpa la byug man po//chang srung byang chub sems 'diganas//
27a2(kha): de yis gzhal yas yon tan 'dis//don 'di bstan pa 'di yin no//dus gsum bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas dang //rdo rje 'chang ba'i rnal 'byor dang //dbang tshan khrag 'thung ma mo'i tshogs//gzhan yang rnam pa mang po yi/ lha dang lha min rlung lha dang ka/gnod sbyin srin po
27a3(kha): 'byung po'i tshogs//ga shin rje klu dang lha rnams dang //dbang med dad pas 'dus pa dang //lha yi tshogs kyis gang ba yi//gzhal med khang mdzes de dag gis//me dang 'dra ba'i 'od gsal de ka/nyi ma zla ba'i gdan dang 'od /ka zhing rnams gdan chen gsal ba dang /
27a4(kha): rin chen pos byas shin tu snang //skal dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas yin//rab sdang snod byed rnams kyang spang /'di la gnod byang gang yin pa//'byung po grul bum srin po dang /yi dgas srul po de dag gis//rmad byung 'di ni mi thos te//rmad byung byang chub ste//
27a5(kha): jis 'jim nyin man pas grag sa mig/'di mas gang tsug pags byed pa//'a gos pa thams cad de la 'ong //sngags 'acang rnams kyis lu sa pa 'gyur// klung byung chub sems 'dis/sa pa da //zlu pags u
27a6(kha): mchog/chos kyi gtam la 'jigs med cing ka las kyi gos par mi 'gyur ba'i//dpal chen 'khor tshogs mang po dang //sangs rgyas rnal 'byor mang po dang //'jigs ming rdo rje sems dpa' dang ka skal ldan rnams kyi don du gsungs ka gzhan gyi don du
27a7(kha): sa pa/bya du gyi du bya sa/ma gros ni sa das pa ldas ras yin phyog gis bya rgya tha u ma pagacinas dag la sa
27b1(kha): sems na du byung ba zhes ba'i ting nge 'dzin la sgoms par bzhug so//de sa dus gsum kyi bde bar gshegs pa rnams dang /skang gsus kyi sto chen po'i 'ag rten gyi khams mapa' las su med pa 'di ga thams cad dag 'ad ba gshegs
27b2(kha): pa thams cad den gcig ste/bdag nyid kyi byang chub kyi sems tsha du byung ba las byung zhing /de dag thams cad de las bskyed pa dang /de las byung ba'i dkyil 'khor yang rma du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor yin pa'i phyir/thug su chung ba'i don rdo rje sems dpas
27b3(kha): rigs par bya ba'i phyir/rdo rje sems dpa' la gzigs te yid kyi dkyil 'khor sgo phye nas/rang gi rig pa'i don gsal bar byung ste/thams cad kyi don ji lta bu yin pa/so so sdang bar byas nas//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid dang /bde bar gshegs pa
27b4(kha): thams cad de dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas mu med pa rnams/byang chub kyi sems rmad du byung ba nyid yin pa dang /byang chub sems rmadadu byung ba las bskyed pa'i dkyil 'khor/sku rma du byung ba sku'i dkyil 'khor rmad
27b5(kha): byung ba dang /gsung rma du byung ba gsung gi dkyil 'khor rma du byung ba dang /thugs rma du byung ba thugs cad kyi dkyil 'khor rma du byung ba dang /chos chen po rma du byung ba nyid yin par snang bar byas nas/de dag thams cad bcom ldan 'das nyid kyis sku gsung
27b6(kha): thugs yin pa dang /de napa rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//'jig rten mkhas pa rdzogs sangs rgyas/sangs kyis don du mdzad pa yi//thugs kyi sgrol ma'i don bstan pa/gang bro th+wI chom na da dang /yang zhig 'gal bar yid sems dpa
27b7(kha): 'jigs pa thad de la yod/mgo ni tshal ba bdun du 'gas/rdo rje sems dpa' nyid kyang sdang /sngags 'chang rnams kyis spang bar 'gyur/sngags rnams thams cad gsang ga yi//b+ha ba rma du byung ba'i don/rdo rje sems dpa' bdag nyid kyis/sngon chad rtogs
28a1(kha): bar 'gyur gzugs kyi sgron ma'i don bstan pa/gang las sangs rgyas kus mdzad pa'i/don mdzad de la phyag 'tshal lo//sangs rgyas kun la brtse dgongs nas/gang gi don du mdzad pa yi//don mdzad de la phyag 'tshalo//gang gi don du byas
28a2(kha): den 'di dag bdag la grub gyur cig/gzhal yas me long 'bar ba ltar//don de bdag gis rig gyur nas/sangs rgyas kun dang skal ba mnyam//sangs rgyas kun pas rgyas gyur cig/dus gsum rnal 'byor gang dag gisaka/skal ldan gang zhig 'byung ba yi//
28a3(kha): don du bdag gis bshang bar 'tshal ka rmad byung byang chub don rtogs nas//rdo rje sems dpa' bdag nyid kyang //rdo rje gdan la yang sangs rgyas//rmad byung byang chub sems 'di ni//kun gyis rig pa ma yin no//lha rnams kun gyis mi rig go//byang chub sems/
28a4(kha): rnams kyis kyang //'di ni rig par dka' ba'o//bde gshegs rnams kyis sngon ma rig ces gsol to//sems dpa' chen po/thams cad mkhyen pa'i bsgom pa'i mchog/ngo bo nyid ni dmig su med//nga si lu su sbyor ba//byang chub med cing bsgom pa'ang mang //lus dang ngag sems
28a5(kha): mtshan nyid med//ye shes lnga yi byang chub sems/sems can kun gyi sems chen po//rang bzhin dag pas dri ma med//thams cad ma skyes zag pa med//byang chub med cing mngon rtogs med//khams kyang med cing 'byung ba'ang med//mtshan med mi 'gyur na mkha' bzhin///
28a6(kha): /gnyis med gnyisu med min zhing ka/byang chub sems 'di rgyas pa'i spyan zhes bka' stsal to//kye sems dpa' chen po /khang par gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' las mu med pa 'dra ste/de'i byang chub sems nas/
28a7(kha): du byung ba nyid yin no//rang rig pa las byung ba'i chos chen po ra du byung ba nyid mino//nga'i tshe 'phrul chen po du byung ba nyid min//pi chu 'phrub nadu byung ba nyid mino//ri ros pa chen po da byung ba nyid yido//'di mdzad pa mad byung ba nyid yin/pa dkyil 'khor chen po ma du byed
28b1(kha): /na ba nga na/ gshegs pas dang / sems sa gsas dag ga pa dag ga pa ci dang /rnams gyu nas su/ rgyub chu'i ma la sa sa rgyam sa ma
28b2(kha): rgya sa gsung shi rjo pa du tu macang sa'o//zugs mthu ci la 'a'o gas sa byu bcad man//bya chung kyisangs du byung las//du byung 'dzin pa'i ma gas pa/'bu ri chos nyid na pa'i phyi
28b3(kha): chen po//byang chub 'dod pa de la byang chub medo//sa dang rnam par byang chub mchog la ring //sgyu 'dra'i chos nyid gang gis shes gyur pa//de nyid byang chub sems yin bdag nyid yin par desarba/byang chub snying por bdag 'jug pas/thob pa med cing spang ba med//sangs rgyas rnams kyi byang chub ni/
28b4(kha): bsnyad yin te don du med//chos kyi mtshan nyid rnam par dag/bdag med chos las kun tu 'byung ka ma skyes pa'i chos rnams la//kho bo med sgom pa'ang med//namakha'i tshul du rab sbyor ba//ngos po kun tu rab tu sngags//chos rnams rang bzhin 'ong gsal bas//gdong nas dag pas na/
28b5(kha): mkha' bzhin//byang chub med cing sems can med//bdag med pa'i chos las byung //sangs rgyas byang chub rdzogs byed pa//rnam par mi rtog dmig su med//byang chub sems rma byung ba las//chos nyid bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///le'u 'dis che ba bstan te//
28b6(kha): sems dpa' chen po//nga yi dus gsum bzhugs pa yi//thugs kyi dkyil 'khor rang las byung //ye shes rdzogs bzhugs me long bzhin//sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes te//byang chub sems kyi don rtogs pas//sangs rgyas kun gyi snying sha can//nga ni sangs rgyas kun gyi dur//dur
28b7(kha): khrod chen po'i nga bdag yin//sems dpa' chen po thams cad dang /sems can thams cad nga las byung //sems can dur nyid nga yin te//skal ldan sems pas rig par gyis//sangs rgyas thams cad nga'i zhing //sems can thams cad sku gsum gtar//thabs cad nga'i longs s+ho/
29a1(kha): Tha byung /sangs rgyas kun gyi sku gsung drugs//babs 'byung ba de'i phyi/sems da 'chen po'i thugs cid ni//ngas byin bdabs chen thos pas//dus gsus sangs rgyas thams cad dang //nal 'argya chen po kun 'dus nas//'od zer mngon sum nyi zhan 'acar//sems
29a2(kha): dpa' chen po bskal ba bzang //rnal 'byur gang zhig de 'chang ba//sems dpa' nyid kyi sku gsung thugs//de nying yin par shes par gyis//don 'di rig pa'i rnam 'byor ba//sangs rgyas sa la mi gnas te//thams cadas nyid nga mi thugs//sems can thams cad 'ayas skyes
