50a1(kha): nag phyag skang du kho N+Di ci ltar tsa nu ga n+d+ha/khong sang du byang chub kyi sems ma du byung ba'i rgyud bcom ldan 'das dang la kun tu bzang po la phyag 'tsha lo//'di skad bdag gi thos pa'i dus gcig na/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs
50a2(kha): sangs rgyas thams cad dbyer meng ba'i bdag nyid/gzugs can ma yin pa'i snying po/yon tan thams cad kyi 'byung gnas/zhes bya thams cad kyi rgyal po de bzhin nyid rnam par dag pa la longs spyod pa/longs spyod pa'i mngon par byang chub pa'i sku dang /sprul pa'i
50a3(kha): sku la sogs pa kun gyis ba/ye shes rnam par dag pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid/gang dang yang gnyi su med pa/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i kun tu bzang po'o//bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen po no//byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor ni//chos thams
50a4(kha): ti 'dzin la longs spyod chen po ma lus par myong bar mdzad pa'i longs spyod rnam par dag pa'i sku ste/mtshan dang rab byad thams cad dang ldan pa'o//mngon par byang chub pa'i sku ni snying po las dkyil 'khor 'byin pa'i sku yin te/'dod khams kyi lha'i rgyal 'od
50a5(kha): gzugs khams kyi lha'i rgyal po'i dkyil 'khor na/rdo rje dang nor bu la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor 'byin pa'o//sprul pa'i spyani thabs mkhas pas gnas thams ca du/dmigs pa ji bzhin du rgyun mi chang par chos kyi 'khor lo bskong ba/sangs rgyas drang sems can du ky-i
50a6(kha): spyod sna ga/rtag pa dang mi rtag gnyis kyi gzhi'am ngo bor gyur pa'i /mtshan dang dpe byad kyis brgyan pa/sku yongasu rdzogs pa'i zhal gyis/chos thams cad mos pa dang mthun par gsung//gsung bar 'gyuraro//rnam par dag pa'i sku de ni/de bzhin nyid kyi dkyil 'kho
50a7(kha): po byang chub na dus rtag tu gnyisu med do//rtag pa dang mi rtag pa'i rgyal po thugs rje chen po//de ni gzhi tshad med pa'i/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor rang byung gi rgyan dang rol pa la sogs pa dkrigs pa'i rang 'byung ba nags lo//byang chub pa chen po'i sku ni ri rab ri'i rtse mo ni
50b1(kha): nor bu rin po che'i khang pa na/zla ba rgyas pa lta bu'i tshul du zhal rab tu rgyus so//thabs mkhas pa spyod pa thams cad kyi bdag nyid ni/bya rgod phung po'i ri la ljags kyi pad+mar bkram nas/khyim pa dang rab tu byung ba'i cha lug su bzhug so//de nas byang chub sems dpa'
50b2(kha): chen po rdo rje 'dzin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po pad+ma'i yu bo 'dzin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po snying po rnam par grol ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po yul rnam par 'joms pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chen po 'jam dpal la sogs
50b3(kha): pas/snying po byang chub shin tu 'ong ba'i gnas ga na bar/gzugs kun du ston pa'i gsung la mnga' brnyas pa bzhugs pa'i gnas de mthong zhing /longs spyod pa'i sku zhal thal bar gsal bar snang bas/rdo rje thogs pa la sogs pas/phyi'i mchod pa dang nang gi
50b4(kha): mchod pa la sogs pas mchod de/g.yas phyogs nas lan gsum bskor ba byas nas//phyag btsal te 'dus pas bcom ldan 'das chen po thugs rje chen po'i snang ba'i gzugs mtha' yas pa mnga' bded /zhal 'dzum pa mdzad nas/cang mi gsung bar bzhugaso//de nas byang
50b5(kha): byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje can la sogs pas/bcom ldan 'das kyis spyan sngon 'dug pa las langs te 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das dus gsum gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi mkhyen pa'i//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnablo
50b6(kha): kyis spyod yul/theg pa thams cad dang thun mong ma lags pa/yang dag par rtog ma thag thams cad mkhyen pa'i go 'phangs thob par 'gyur ba'i don bshadu gsol/de skad ces gsol ba btab pa dang //zhal shin tu yang 'dzum pa mdzad nas 'di skad ces gsungaso//sems dpa'
50b7(kha): chen po nyon cig/yang dag pa ni don dam pa'i bden pa dang /kun rdzob kyi bden pa gnyis dbyer med pa'o//rgyud bskyed nas 'bras bu 'byung ba med la/'bras bu la 'bras bu 'byung ba ni/'jig rten gyi sa bon gyi tshul du/'bras bu las sa bon gzhan du mi 'byung ba'o//
51a1(kha): /de bzhin du sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis spyod yul ni yang dge ba 'bras bu la 'bras bu rdzogs par spyod pa'o//yang dag bden pa 'bras bu te/'bras bu las ni 'bras bu 'byung /de bzhin nyid la gnas bcas te/phyi nang snod bcud rnam par dag/
51a2(kha): byed spyod bya byed bde ba'i lhun/rmad 'byung byang chub sems 'di gnas//de'i yon tan bsam yas pa'i/don 'di bstan pa 'di yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje can la sogs pas yang 'di skad ces gsol to//rang bzhin lha dang ting 'dzin/ye shes rmad
51a3(kha): byung dkyil 'khor nyid//bcom ldan snying po kun gyi gtso//thabs la mkhas pas bsha du gsol//de skad ces gsol pas/bcom ldan 'das kyis ngo mtshar ba'i spyan bgrang nas/'di skad ces bka' stsal to//rmad 'bru byang chub sems 'di ni/rdo rje sems dpa'/
51a4(kha): gsal zhing /rdo rje sems dpa' nyid la gzigs/'di la pha rol phyin bcu rdzogs//sa bcu rims kyis bgrangs pa'ang /mnyam nyid rig pa'i ye shes kyis//sa rnams mnyam nyid thig ler sbyor//chos sku ye nas mi dmigs phyir//rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzim
51a5(kha): kun gyi snying po mi 'dra bas//dngos dang dngos min tshang tshing nyid//skad cig nyid la bsreg pa yi//rnam par mi rtog triting 'dzin rlung //de bzhin mnyam nyid dkyil 'khor 'di//sangs rgyas kun gyi nying sha can//sangs rgyas kun pas sngar sangs rgyas/rma du 'byung ba'i
51a6(kha): sems 'di ni//sku dang gsung thugs dag pas na//kun gyis reg pa ma yin te//khams gsum ltas kyang ma reg cing //nyan thos la sogs smos ci dgos//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis kyang //rmad 'byung 'di ni rig par dka'//gzhi rtse med pa'i//
51a7(kha): don gyis mchog/cis kyang mtshan du med pas na//sangs rgyas kyis kyang rtogs par dka'//kun gyi rang bzhin zhi ba 'di//bzham zhing bla med lam mchog ste/ma byung mi skye'i snying po 'di//mtshan ma na mkha' bsngo bral ste//gdod nas byang chub byang chub
51b1(kha): kyang 'grub pa med phyir dgongs mi 'gyur//stong gsum stong gi 'jig rten khams//mthar yas mu med gangal ba yang //srad 'byung byang chub dkyil 'khor yin//nye lam pade rdzogs bla na med pa'i lam//rtogs ma thag tu go 'phangs chen po yang //thang /
51b2(kha): dor med pa'i chos sku dpag med thob//byang chub kyi sems mdu blang ba'i rgyud las/gleng gzhi'i la'u ste dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das thabs la mkhas pa'i thugs rje chen po'i bdag nyid kyis/zab mo ba mo zhes bya bar ting badzin la snyoms par zhugs
51b3(kha): nas ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//sangs rgyas sprul pa 'byung ba rnyed par dka'//yang dag rmad 'byung rtogs pa de'i char mi phod//dal ba 'byor pa rnyed par rab tu dka'//de bzhin sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor nyid//gsang ba rmad 'byung nyid kyis rab tu bsdus//chos kyi/
51b4(kha): mtshan nyid ye nas rnam par dag/bdag med ngo bo med pas rtogs par dka'//rnam par rtogs pa'i mtshan nyid dad pa dang //byang chub sems bskyed sems can ni//de dag shin tu rnyed par dka' ba ste//sems can sangs rgyas can ming med pa'i//sngon rol 'byung ba'i rmad
51b5(kha): 'byung byang chub sems//sems can sangs rgyas gang yin rtogs par dka'//byang chub chos ni rmad 'byung rnam mi rtog/mya ngan 'das dang 'khor ba ye nas med//rmad 'byung byang chub sems kyi myu gu la//'byung ba lnga yang 'bras bu'i tshul du byung //sems can rnams
51b6(kha): la snying rje che ba yis//sems can dag la rnyed par ragu dka'//sems can gaun rtsa med pa'i chos nyid 'di//skye dang 'jig bral byang chub sems nyid de//ye nas dag ste sgyu 'bras song gi med//kun rol rang la rdzogs pa'i sku nyid ni//dug gsum dug lnga'i rtsig
51b7(kha): le chen por 'khyil//zhe sdang la sogs ming med pas//rmad 'byung la sogs chos kyi mchog/khyab cing gardal ba rtogs par dka'//bar du gcod pa'i chos rnams kyi//sdig sogs bye brag bsam mi khyab//de bzhin rmad 'byung sems nyid kyis//dug lnga gsang dang 'khor ba
52a1(kha): @/gas//sangs rgyas la sogs kun gsang pa'i//sdig pa'i mchog ni gos ma srid//gosu sdig med sdig kyang gsung //rmang 'byung byang chub sdig po che//sangs rgyas kun gyi nyid sha can//rang pa'i stobs kyis bsod nams cher 'phags pa'i//don 'di lud
52a2(kha): tsam rtogs pa khyad par 'phags//so so ma yin mnyam pa'i snying po la//bsod nams dpag tu med pa kun rdzogs te//nga la nga med kun la rnam par rol//rmad 'byung byang chub sems 'di dngos po dag/ye nas sangs rgyas sems can ming ma grags//cir yang 'phrul
52a3(kha): chen ston pa'i 'phrul po che//khams gsum kun rdzob khams gsum rin po che//chos 'di brtag par dka' phyir thams cad bdud kyi las//sku lnga lhun gyis rdzogs pa'i dkyil 'khor te/'phra zhing zab mo kun gyi sa bon gyi/lus 'di sgyu ma yin pas gzhig nas de nyid
52a4(kha): la/brtags na rang bzhin shes pa byang chub rmad//de phyir lus 'di brtags la rmad 'byung rtsal bya'o//gcig gi rang bzhin dges na kun gyi rang bzhin shes//kun gyi rang bzhin rang bzhin nyi du gcig/so so ma yin tha dad ma ni de rmad 'byung ni//byang chub rmad 'byung de bzhin
52a5(kha): gshegs pa'i sku//'od dpag med pa'i chos la byang chub sems//che ba lnga'i snying po kha dog rdzogs//chos rnams thams cad rtag pa nyid kyis rol//chen po lnga'i sngon du rma du byung //yang dag snying po 'du shes dag pa'i//lcags kyus bzung bas gtan du bzung bar rung //de
52a6(kha): brlabs de la brtan pas de rnyed 'gyur//rtag par dka' la bstan par dka' phyir de ltar bstan//ye nas thar lam sgro skur zhi ba'i rmang po che//sangs rgyas kun gyi lta ba'i dngos po ste//dri med sa bcu rdzogs pa'i 'od po che//'khor ba'i 'khor lo bskor ba'i rtsibs dang ldan//nam pa
52a7(kha): du pa gyug du gyi pa can bya tsu yo ga/nga kyig ga sa skyeg la la/ na sas su ga la
52b1(kha): tshe du brjong pa 'di brjod do//zhi ba bla med byang chub 'di la sgro skur med//tha stad dral bas tshang pas mi gnod lung //ye nas zhi bas de bzhin nyid mi gnas//'dzin dang rtog pa zhes bya ting 'dzin//kun rdzob rang bzhin gnas pas gdod nas bral//mchod cing brjo med pa'i dbyis/
52b2(kha): las 'a khatam cing mimanyam sang bas pad pa'i klung /bya byed na dang bral bas chos kyi sku//de sag/med rgyu rdzogs pad pa'i sang//des de bzhin kun 'byin mo gru 'bras/byang chub sngob cing pas byang chub nying mo 'dod//senas rnam par dag pasam
52b3(kha): mar bsgom dang bral//bya bral lta ba'i klung las ma nyams 'jug sgo med//theg le chen pos btab pas mchod pa'i klung //snying po'i tshig bral dam pa nyid las 'dar bya med//dkyil 'khor gsum gyi snying po nyid de bsngo dang bral//'byung ba lnga'i rtsa ba nyid de mtshar du/
52b4(kha): med na sangs rgyas thams cad 'byung ba'i rgyud de sa bon tshul//'byung ba nyid mamakha' las 'jar byung chub dbyer yang med/'gro rnams rgyam cho mu bran kun 'dus dbyer med tshul du gcig/thig le gcig tu bsdus pa'i bin chags na dang bral//glang med do /
52b5(kha): med pa rgyu 'angs 'khor med //khams gsum yi sangs rgyas pas rigs med gcig gi dang ka yang dag nyid la yang dag nyid kyang rdzogs pas gti mug nyid/dmigs byang migs byed med pas blun pa'i sku//drig li bde chen sku ni thams cangs sa rgyas pas//sa ku
52b6(kha): gsang thog le nyid du bzlums//chos sku gsal ba'i klung du kun gcig pas/mtshams med lnga spyad kun bzangs smram la rol//sangs rgyas nyid las gzhan med kun sangs rgyas//de bzhin rgyus phyir dmigs spyad thos pa sangs rgyas nyid la rol//rlud 'byung bya/
52b7(kha): tshugs nyid ni sangs rgyas zos pa'i lto//rigs gcig rigs brgya sku gsum sku lnga la sogs//mkha' las nyi zla gza' skar tshul du dang gis 'char//snying po'i snying po rdo rje snying po 'di yis sna tshogs brgyus/de phyir rmad 'byung 'di ni snying po'i mda'od/
53a1(kha): ye nas dag pa'i klung du dbyer med kun bsdus pas//rmad 'byung byang chub sems kyi rgya mtsho yin//'khor ba ming med mya ngan las 'das pas//rmad 'byung byang chub sems 'di yang dag rmad/byar mad de bzhin nyid 'das ma nyams pas/rmad
53a2(kha): 'byung byang chub sems ni sa 'das pha rol phyin//kha dog dpe med na mkha'i tshig 'das pas/rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni rang bzhin med//phyi dang nang med dus gcig rol mdzad pa'i//rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni kun shong lto bo chaka kha dog dpe med na mkha'i
53a3(kha): tshig 'das pas//rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni rang bzhin rang bzhin med//stong nyid de bzhin mi dmigs las 'das pas/dge sdig gnyis med byar med nyid kyi ngang /gcig kyang bsnyad cing dpag tu med pas bde ba'i mchog/rmad 'byung byang chub sems de ltas
53a4(kha): rdzogs pas//dpe med byang chub sems la kun bla na phyir//khams gsum ma lus gsod pa rgyal sras mchog/rmad byung byang chub semasu kun gcig phyir//mchod pa'i sprin gyis bdag mchod rgyal ba mnyes//bden gnyis mi 'byed yang dag yang dag pas//seMn/
53a5(kha): bsad pa kun bzang mkha' la rol//mthong dang thos las 'das pa'i 'das pa la/gzhan du mi 'jog de bzhin de gnas pas/rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni kun sbyor sku/de bzhin klung las rgyal dang sems can ma mthong phyir/sgrib gnyis bsal du med de
53a6(kha): lam la sbyang mi dgos//rmad 'byung de bzhin ma banos bde chen rlung //yang dag yang dag nyid kyang ma mthong rmad 'byung ste/'bras bu'i 'bras bu nyid 'bras las 'das/de phyir bde ba chen po'i ting 'dzin ces bya'o//byang chub sems rmang du byung ba'i rgyud la
53a7(kha): rma du byung ba'i rang bzhin bde ba'i mchog yin pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa' rdo rje 'dzin pa chen pos/bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang po la shin tu dgyes pa'i spyan gdangs nas
53b1(kha): ye shes chen po'i mchod pa rgyas par mchod nas bcom ldan 'das yangs pa chen po byang chub ces bgyi ba'i tshig gi don ni gang lags/khyod kyi byang chub gang lags/khyod kyis byang chub gang las thob nas byang chub brnyed lags/chos rnams
53b2(kha): gang las nges par brnyes par bgyis na/ma rtogs pa rnams kyis ji ltar byang chub rtogs par 'gyur ba bka' stsal tu gsol/de nas yangs pa chen pos zhus pa'i don thugs su chud nas/byang chub chen po'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/
53b3(kha): brjod par bya ba brjod ga rmad 'byung byang chub sems dgying mtshan nyid 'di yi ge med pa'i don du grags pa'i phyir/ngo bo nyid med byang chub dang bral ba/lhun rdzogs rnam par dag pa'i byang chub mchog/brjod med pa'i yi ge tshig dang bral/rgya
53b4(kha): bas mtshon du med de sngags kyi klung /mi mnyam mnyam las 'das pa mchog gi lam//mnyam nyid 'dod pa grol bar dka'/de nyid rmad 'byung sgrib pa'i skyon can yin//mnyam nyid byang chub 'dod la byang chub bral ka phung po skye mched khams/
53b5(kha): bshig byang chub 'dod/byang chub yang dag de la ring ste byang chub nyams/sku gsung thugs bral de bzhin byang chub 'das/phung po rgyu sogs dpe bshig byang chub du/gang 'dod pa de mtshan ma nyid la 'dzin/mtshan med zhi ba de ni snying po ste/chos
53b6(kha): mu ye shes stong pa'i mtshan 'das pa/'das pa'i 'das pa nyid de rgyal po'i tshul/ye shes snying po mchog tu gyur pas tshad ma'i lung /dbyer med dam par gcig pas tshad kun ldan//de bzhin nyid de yi sangs rgyas pa yin/'phags pa'i ye shes
53b7(kha): rab spyod pa'i shes rab can/gang la'ang chags pa med pa de bzhin du/rmad 'byung byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin 'di/brjod med pas dmigs pa'i yul dang bral/bsam pas rnyed pa'i lus med bsgom pas nad /ye sangs rgyas pa kun gyi rang
54a1(kha): bzhin yin//de phyir gang yang ma spangs byang chub snying po ste/gcig pu de bzhin lhun gyis grub pas na/de bzhin ting 'dzin chos dbyings la sogs pa/gang yang ma yin lhan cig mi gnas rdzogs/rmad 'byung byang chub sems 'di kun rdzo/
54a2(kha): su/'phags pa'i ye shes rab sbyong ba shes rab kyang //gzhan gyi mi bskyod 'jigs med de bzhin du//kun rdzob snying po rmad 'byung byang chub sems/namakha'i dbus kyang mi bskyod 'khor bar kyang //mi 'jigs dbyer med 'dres nas ye rgyas btab
54a3(kha): dgras 'jigs bde dpa' bos med bgyis la//nyon mongs rnams kyi 'jigs bams par min//de bzhin rmad 'byung byang chub sems nyid 'di/kun 'dus thig le rnam par dag pa'i sku de bzhin dbyings 'das yi sangs rgyas min na/rdzogs chen ma
54a4(kha): yin zin chags nad dang bral/byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin 'od gsal te/snying po'i snying po lta ba'i lam po che//btsal ba'i nad med tshad mas gzhig mi rung /mngon sum la sogs 'jig rten tshad mas pa zhig pa min/pha rol phyin rdzogs pas ni
54a5(kha): yongs rdzogs ltar/de bzhin rdzogs chen bya bra la snying po 'di//kun gyi ngo bo'i rang bzhin de bzhin du//chos rnams ming med de bzhin nyid du rdzogs//'dres shing dbyer med don dam kun rdzob dang /sangs rgyas sems can rang bzhin nyid du gcig med pa'i//
54a6(kha): snying po nyid las yod pa'i snying po 'byung /snying po snying po nyid mad snying po can/thabs dang thabs mkhas sgo la chags pa med//sgo bral sgo zhes thabs la mkhas pa bzhin/rang bzhin kun rdzogs zhi ba'i rang bzhin nyid//sgro med phugs ming thabs kyi rgya
54a7(kha): bo yin//ye sangs rgyas pas bgrod pa'i lam dang bral/thig ler zlum pas yang dag nyid du gcig/ma bsdus de bzhin nyi du 'dus pas thabs kyi mchog bsam pa dag pas rab tu byung ba'i mchog/bsam pa sgon khur ba ni rang 'byung min/de bzhin rmad
54b1(kha): 'byung chos nyid bde chen 'di//bsam dang bral zhing bsam pa las 'das pas/'khor ba'i rtsa ba dregs pa'i rab mchog yin/zin rtsol nad dang ldan pa ma yin pas/yang dag yang dag rab kyi yang dag yin/'du ba'i ting 'dzin 'du las
54b2(kha): 'das pas na/ting 'dzin rgyal po zhesu rab tu grags/nye lam bder rdzogs lam nad bral ba yin/'di la 'jug lha mi dgos ye nas zhugs/bsrung dang dam tshig mi btsal stong las 'das/rdo rje sems dpa'i thugs kyi dkyil 'khor 'di//
54b3(kha): gnyis med sangs rgyas med pa'i che ba yin/nyon mongs lnga'i rang bzhin gyis kyang ni/byang chub yongasu tshol bar byed pa bzhin//rdzogs mchog rmad 'byung byang chub sems nyid 'di/rang bzhin nyid kyis rang bzhin nyid kyang tshol//bstsal kyang rnyed pa'i
54b4(kha): dus med rang bzhin nyid las ma nyams pas//lha dang spyod pa ting 'dzin dgongs pa'i rlung //de la mi dgongs dgongs pa dgongs pa'i nang //gang la lta ba med pa de la lta ba gnas//spyod med ting 'dzin mang pa'ang de bzhin//bsgom pa'i don ma rnyed pa'i drang srong
54b5(kha): rnams //bsgom chags nang dang bral ba bsgom pa'i rlung //mi 'gyur zhe nye'i gzong brdab bzhin du gnas//rmad 'byung byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin 'di//drang srong rgyal ba rnams kyi dgongs pa mchog/'dus gsum rdzogs spangs rgyas khwa 'di sdug
54b6(kha): mig byang chub kyi sems mdu byung ba'i rgyud las//lung gi dpes mtshon pa dang /lha spyod ting 'dzin bstan pa'i lo'u sgo bzhi pa'o///de nas rdo rje 'dzin pa chen pos bcom ldan 'das dpa pa kun du bzang po la phyag btsal ti shin
54b7(kha): chu 'dzum pa'i medangs dang tsas pas sbyin gangs nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi dang chi ba nyid ji lta lags chos rnam padu bshad/chos shes bgyi bdi ltar lags paga' stsal du gsol de sad ces gsapa pa dang /sangs gcan po
55a1(kha): ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/skye shi tha dad ma yin gzhung gcig ste/'khor ba med par mya ngan yongs mi 'dar/mya ngan 'das dang 'khor ba ba mi dang /de bzhin skye gi gnyis ni ming nyid pa dang kyang /rang bzhin gcig las ming gartags
55a2(kha): zla dang snang ka /de bzhin snying po sgo thugs mi 'byed pa/theg pa rnam rtog grangasu tha dad kyang //rang bzhin nyid las ma nyams rang bzhin nyid//skye dang shi dang mya ngan 'das pa yang /ngo bo nyid gcig pas 'khor ba nyid/mi spong mi g.yo bag la gnas med de/gnas
55a3(kha): med pa la gnas pa med gnas mi mtshungs rdo rje sems dpa'i thugs nyid ni/thams cad ye rgyas btab pas khams gsum 'ub/tshig gi mtha' las 'das pas cang ma mthong //skye ba zhes pa chos rnams 'byung ba'i gzhi//mya ngan 'das 'khor ldan
55a4(kha): pas med pa yin/mi spong gnas med gnas pa las kyang 'das/spyod las 'das pa'i ye shes rgyal pos 'phags/don dam kun rdzob mi 'byed don dam med//don dam 'dod pas dum pa'i don mi rnyed//don dam mong ba de la don yang gnas//sgro dang skur
55a5(kha): zhing rdo rje bsra pa'i mchog/mi g.yo mi 'gyur thun po'i tshul du gnas//di la gnas pa'i rgyal po de bzhin gshegs/ma dang sring mo'i gzigs pa de bzhin du shrIs//dkyis dang bying rol mnyas nyid lhun gnas pas /ma dang srid mo sbyar ba ma kyi mchog
55a6(kha): dge dang sdig tu ma mthong ye rgyas pas/sdig pa spyad pas dkyil 'khor dkyil 'khor ldan//sangs rgyas rnam pa bzhi'i chos bshad yid pas/thog med 'du shes mi bden tshor ba bral ka sgrub pas pha rol phyin drug gsung bar 'gyur//tshig tu ba shad pas rmad
55a7(kha): 'byung don mi rnyed//mi ge'i mchog nyid yang dag gsal ba nyid//yi ges thig bzhir brtags pa rmad 'byung nya mas ka ston pas bshad pa don rtogs 'khrul pa'i lam ka nad med byi na spun kyang mi dgos na/nad kyi don phyir sman dgos ji bzhin duka/
55b1(kha): rgyud byed blan du byed de//snyun srung snyi de bzhin bzhi gnas na//bla med cig kyang tha ma dag smra swA //na na mo sangs pa na la pos kyang //ma hA srungs gsung du da sted// gdus srung rgyum pa'i rgya
55b2(kha): dzo po tshu sa tsam tshad nas/mngags pa pa'i tshig pa la gzang 'byung byang chub sems skyed ma mthong na/chom macag pacas kyi lam la gnas//khyim pa'i lam pazhin drus klu ba ///rgyul pa'i gas skyes skye drung
55b3(kha): ming ba'i sems la gnas pas ma spangs rgyas sangs rgyas zhesurab tu grags/ku thub ga' bas rgyas gdung phyug 'tshal klog//'chang dang badzin pas shes shing brnyed min te/rmang babyung byang chub sems nyid rnyed pa ni/sems mi rang bzhin ma no rags pana'o//
55b4(kha): dka' thub la sogs byang chub chen po'i gegs/ngo bo nyid ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i tshul//ji ltar las skyong ci yod mi dgos na/de 'drar spya du med pa thob par grags/rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni nges mi skyed//rtad 'byung 'di ni bde gshegs las kyang 'das/rgyu la
55b5(kha): bas mi gzigs du khrod chen po'i klong ka/ye shes ting 'dzin rdzogs pa'i mang po che ka gzhan gyis mi'i byed de bzhin nyid kyi sku//thams cid med pa'i sngon du ye sangs rgyas//dus gsum sangs rgyas kun gyi mes po che ka dmigs gnas bsgom du dngos po'i jo ban/
55b6(kha): byag rgyar bskyed du 'ang bzhin se nas zhe/ bsnyen bkur bsngod du nam dag yon tan med//mi dmigs dag pa'i jo bo thaMng zug//ma khe rgya dag pas mnyam dang mi mnyam rnams//suM'i sku ste sangs rgyas kun gyi sru//'dzin pa pra mo yod par shes pa'ang de//de sgrib chen/
56a1(kha): @chen po snang du mi btub ltar ka/gzung 'dzin rtog pa phyag rgya'i skur bsgoms pas/thams cad sangs rgyas rmad 'byung ngo bor yang //chos su ma lus bsdus pa mthong mi 'gyur/snang srid ltar bsgom pa nyid rmad 'byung skyon//rmang 'byung byang chub sems kyi
56a2(kha): sa bon las ka phyin pa snod bcud 'bras bur rnam par smin//sa bon 'bras bur smon pa de yang ni//slar son gan su dag pa'i gcig chu la ni//rgya mtsho che las chu bran kun 'byung ba/ka slar 'dus lan tshwa'i chu ro kun gcig 'dra'o//byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i rgyud las/
56a3(kha): pe las 'das pa'i chos thams cad kyi de kho na nyid kyi le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos/nyid rma du byung ba la gzigs nas brjod par bya ba brjod pa/e ma ho//byang chub sems dpa' chen pos kyang //chos gcig
56a4(kha): ma gzigs chos lam zad mtha' med ka longs spyod rdzogs dang sprul pa'i sku dang nika/mngon par byang chub skus kyang chos gcig pa'o//ma gzigs gzigs pa med pa rgyan dang ldan//mtshan dpes brgyan pa yang dag sangs rgyas min//mtshan sku de nyid don gyi 'bras bu
56a5(kha): yang ka su 'dod de la yang dag sangs rgyas ring ka rmer 'byung byang chub dag pa yang sangs rgyasaka/kha dog dbyibs med mtshon pa las 'das te//gzhi med pa'i rtsa gzhi kun gyi mchog/lo ma'i rtsa brtsa ba'i lo ma'ang yin//mkha' ltar mi 'dod de yang 'das pa nyid//rmad
56a6(kha): @'byung byang chub sems kyi chos gcig po//rnam par dag pa'i chos sku de nyid las ka gzhan gyis reg cing myong ba ma yin phyir//sangs rgyas sras bcas mtshan ma'i mtshan dpes brgyan/mngon sum gnyisu de nyid su rnal pa/de la rmad 'byung 'di mi gnas te zin ma//
56a7(kha): nad na snang stong ngo po dben pa'ang 'di'i skyon ga/rgyal ba'i rgyal ba zhes bya brjod du med//dbyings dang ye shes gnyis kyi tshig kyang zad//mtha' dang dbus la mi gnas bde ba'i klung ka khyab brdal byang chub mtha' ltar dpag tu med ka/'di nyid skur 'dod sgo skur
56b1(kha): ga/ khyogasas sring ga shid kyi spru la rtags/seM sa rgyu rdzogs po rnam par dag/rigs mchog cad kyi sbrul su gnas pa//jig pa snang ba dang ngas pa pa'i mnag sems can gsang pa nyid kyi gal bdag kyi
56b2(kha): 'kho gas la dang phyag rgya'i bdag nyid yin//rig sngags gsang sngags snying po dag bri'ang yin//dbang bskur ma thob rgyal bar gyur pa ste//lha klu mthu chen rnams kyang bsrung bar 'gyur//mi 'dud dpang med nyid kyi sku la 'dud//bsgom dang ting 'dzin 'bras bu grus/
56b3(kha): ba'ang min//dkrol 'khor kun gyi bdag po nyid kyang yin//gsung ba'i yum rnams 'di la yongs kyis 'khril//de bzhin gshegs bkun gyi da bar 'gyur/sku gsum rnga rjer bstan pa'i dkyil 'khor phyin//kun tu bzang po sna yum 'khril pa'i bya'o//'din/
56b4(kha): su rnam pa la sangs rgyas rdzogs/mang 'byung zhesu 'di la brjod pa yin khyang chub kyi sems cad byung ba'i rgyud las gsangasargal gyi ba zhes pa'i la'u ste drug pa'o///de nas yang bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems pas yang bu med /
56b5(kha): don zhu ba'i phyir/thal mo gdong du zung nas/dgyes pa'i spyan gdangs te/bcom ldan 'das don dam pa'i sems gang lags//de bzhin nyid ces bgyi ba dang ka khyad par ci mchis bka' stsal du gsol/don de thug su chud nas bcom ldan 'das chen thos
57a1(kha): sdug ga byang chub sems na semasas spyan gyisems sdang dang /khrus gso med pa das 'anyid//da dang sems la mi dang ma nas med/'dzi mi gas tsha ma ming bang ga/deni pa dag legs de bshe nyi ces kya dadas
57a2(kha): can pa dang dbyer med gcig/de nas bcom ldan 'das chen pa nyid kyis brjod par bya ba'i dran brjod pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes mdzod chen po//mi bden mtshan ma'am tshig dang bshad bral ba/theg pa chen po'i rnam par dog pa'i//zag pa chen po chos
57a3(kha): ya 'dug cid //tshas las pa dam pa mi bzhin la 'byur spyod//byang chub nyid byed mi 'ad rtswa ba'i mchog/sangs rgyas sems canas samang ba ba gul/se sgyung u bar dag sa rgyal ba /zung dab du brab yang pa'i spyod drug/r+dz+dza
57a4(kha): med stong pa dam pa dang //de bzhin nyi du 'ang ma mthong sgom pa'i mchog/chad 'byung rdzogs chen ngo bo nyid gsungs//ye shes rgyal po mkha' 'dra mtha' las 'dus/drang srong nge 'dzin pa la sgom zhes bya//dmigs pa'i don la 'jug pas dgos bgol//dmigs
57a5(kha): pa'i gza' gtad 'ja' ba gtan nas nyams//stong nyid de bzhin nyid lta lta ba lkugs//bde ba'i shul lam 'dod pa ma thag ring //bsrung dang mi bsrung gnyis kyis dam tshig nyams//spyad dang mi spyad 'dzin pas thar lam 'gagyes//khar+dz+dza dang mi ltar mthong bas
57a6(kha): babarlag/bsgom dang mi sgom gnyis kyis rtogs mtha' med//rmad 'byung 'di las 'di rnams spyir gyur pas//rmad 'byung mdzod chen ngo bo nyid las nyams//rmad 'byung byung chub sems kyi rang bzhin ni/chos lam ni mi 'dzin yod med mtha' yas 'das//
57a7(kha): 'as ma'i ma dun sumakyil la sa na pa mi nga /ga'adangs rnabs pazungs bad 'i mi dun ny-id zi pas lus pas
57b1(kha): /ji ltar bshad kyang rmad 'byung don mi rnyed//zla ba tshes gcig mkha' la shar ba la//'dzub mos mtshon pas mthong tshul tsam du 'dzin//drang srong chen po rnams kyi don spyad pa//tshig tu brjod pas ngo bo nyid las nyams//yid la bsams pas chad pa'i mthar snang 'gro//
57b2(kha): me pa'i snying po las mi yod pa'i snying /de bzhin de bzhin de bzhin gyi nar bzhag/mang 'thung byang chub smra'i rang bzhin to//'di lta'i don la sangs rgyas rtag tu bzhugs//byang chub kyi sems ca du byung ba'i rgyud las//don dam pa'i sems kyi rang bzhin dang /mdo sde'i
57b3(kha): mdzod dang /theg pa chen po'i mdzod lha spyong dang gol sa sbyang ba dang /nyid kyi rang bzhin bstan pa'i le'u ste bdun pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos/skyon gyis mi gos pa'i don nyid kyis brjod par byas pa/rmad 'byung byang chub
57b4(kha): sems la mnyam 'jog tshe/lus sems sangs rgyas yin par bya dgos shing //phyi rol don la mi brtag sangs rgyas nyid //mtshan dpas brgyan pa ma yin na mkha'i la yan pa mi ltung ba//gang yang min pa'i rang bzhin de tsam la//lus sems mi gcod klong de rang bzhin/
57b5(kha): gzhag/de 'dri'i klong la rmad 'byung nyid gnas pas//mtha' gnyis skyon gyis gos par long mi 'gyur/snga phyi'i rgyal ba sems can lus gnas tshe//'chi ba'i dus tshe bag chags 'di ldan pas//de mthus sems can snying po rgyal bar 'gyur//rmad 'byung byang chub sems dpang
57b6(kha): ldan na//rigs drug nyan thos dag tu mi mthong bas//rigs drug nyan thos dag tu bsad pa med//sems can nyan thos dag tu su mthong ba//de la sgron gas sdig kyang yod par gsungs//dpe na mi lam na ni mi bsad tshog sang pa'i tshe na sdig gis mi 'jigs gzang
57b7(kha): 'byung byang chub sems kyi don shes na/don de bdag la ldan par 'gyur ba'i tshe//rmi lam sang 'dra sdig pas gos mi 'gyur/rmad 'byung byang chub sems kyi mthu ldan ni//ma dang sring mo bu mo la sogs rnams//rmad 'byung nyid las gzhan du mthong ba min/de dra sbyar
58a1(kha): gapa ngam pa nyid la sbyar/kha sring bu mor mthong ba 'ching ba'i rgyu/dper na rmi lam na ni ma sring sbyar byas pas gsang pa'i tshe na lan gyis mi 'jig so//yang dag don shes mi lam sang 'dra da//yul du skyon gyis gos par 'gyur mi srid//nga 'byung byang chu
58a2(kha): sems kyi ngan ldan na//dkyil 'khor phyag rgya bzhir ldan mthong mi 'gyur//dkyil 'khor phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor su mthong ba/de la phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor ldan pas na//rmang 'byung de la mtshungs par yongs ma gsungs//dper na mi las na ni de bzhin gshegs su
58a3(kha): /mthong //sad pas bdag tu snang las gzhan med de/de bzhin phyag rgya sgom pa'i dkyil 'khor bas//mtshan ma nyid las byang chub gting ma rnyed//dkyil 'khor ma yin rmad 'byung byang chub la/ka dkyil 'khor mtshan mi'i skyon gos mi 'gyur//rmad 'byung byang chub sems
58a4(kha): dadang su ldan pa//dge rtsar mi spyod dge rtsar mthong mi 'gyur//dge rtsar mthong ba de la skyon yod 'gyur//dge rtsar ma mthong ba nyid byang chub mchog/mi lam na ni dgra dang 'grogs par mthong ka sad pa'i dus na de dang 'grog mi 'gyur//dge rtsas 'jig rten 'di'i snyan dngags
58a5(kha): mang byung byang chub semos kyis ma bin na/sdug bsngal skye gzhi 'bras bu rmi lam 'dra/sems can rnams la snying rje brgya med cing / mtshams med lnga skyong zhe lnga ma tshangs spyod/'dod son lnga 'i ma gzhan lnga slong mi byid //rtag tu bdagra mchong gzhan du mi rigs pa/
58a6(kha): nga 'byung byang chub theg chen dam tshig yin//dug rdzas 'khor ba'i rgyun gcod byed pa yang /chad 'byung byung chub nyid du yang dag gsungs 'dod cing chags pa'ang mang 'thung byang chub nga//gzhan la sdang ba'i zhe sdang mar 'byung ngab//de bzhin 'khor ba gti mug zad cu
58a7(kha): ga ces 'dag da nga rgya ma ni byung ba/gang drin pan pa gad 'phrag nga / ga rjo da pa ga ma ma ca pas dul ba bya 'a gra las ga ba tsu legs pa rgyas kyi /
58b1(kha): nga drug ga rje sems rabang nyid kyi thugs la gnas/bskal ba 'dzum gyi mes kyang bsreg tu med/khring ba gsum gyi chus kyang bkru su med/'di gas bstan du bde gshegs rnams la med /khrid ba 'byung ba'i sngon du gangs rgyas te/thams pa cang
58b2(kha): byang byang chub sems nyid kyang ka skyon dang sgro zhing gdod nas rnam par dag/byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i rgyud las/skyon gdod ma nas rnam par dag pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o///de nas sems dpa' chen po rdo rje thogs pa la sogs pas/
58b3(kha): mongs pa chen po la bcom ldan 'das mtshan ma nyid gsal bar gzhag nas/mtshan ma yas rnam par grol ba'i thabs bko' stsal du gsol//zhes gsol bas//den mo zhes te brjod pa/ mtshan can mtshan dpes brgyan pa sgom pa paM/yang dag pad pa'i dkyil
58b4(kha): nga ba nyid /mthang ba nyid du de bzhin gshegs gsungs//rang 'byung shi kyi sku la lnga 'dod pa'i//blo pan byung chub sems rapas bslad du rug//mtshan dang macan ma med pa'i tsheg las 'das/nyid kyis son par bya ba 'dod pa med 'grus dgas
58b5(kha): ma nyid sa ste /gas pa'i gsang la garlu chub sems nyid 'di/rang byung dag da'i dkyil 'khor na 'i//sprul pa'i khyung 'dug nyi du la tshul du 'phrug 'ang thung bachag gi byag ma rgyab pa//khrong bar byas po rnams kyis pas du ru
58b6(kha): thugs sngags bsgom pa'i tshig rnams las//rmad 'byung ye nas sangs rgyas bstan pa 'di//skye med sangs rgyas kun gyis mes chen po/bskal pa'i me bzhin gcig pu gnas pa las/chos rnams ma lus chos so cog pa brdzun//thad brdzun par shes na 'jigs dang bral
58b7(kha): da kyil 'ams mthas kyi gas nyi ni kyis snyem ngas thas gi phyin gigab pa chem gyi slod kyang /ya 'a sras bu na pa snyid du byug pas nyid kyis ga ches nyid gamizigs mi ga pa gad kyags ga'ang
59a1(kha): 'byung byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin no//rdza ba nyid 'das las rdzogs rnam par dag sangs rgyam mi 'do sangs rgyas sa las 'das sangs rgyas smos las dag sa phyag rgya gsam//chag nams kun gyi bzhin gnyis med ma
59a2(kha): rang bzhin gcig tu zad pa yin//rang bzhin gcig pa nyid kyang yod min te/rang bzhin nyid kyis gnas shing chags pa med//mtshan ma can gyi chos ni 'jig rten gyi//lam dang 'bras bu yin te zang yod phyir//bla na med pa'i 'bras bu thob mi 'gyur//ma 'gyu
59a3(kha): 'di ni chos kyi rang bzhin dang /gcig pa nyid kyang mi 'dod dbyings las 'das/sangs rgyas phyag rgya mi 'dod ye byAta ba ka khams gsum ye nas sangs rgyas chos skur zhi//mya ngan 'das pa 'das pa nyid kyang 'das//yon tan nyid kyi phung po rdo rje'i skuka ci yang 'du 'bras
59a4(kha): /med pa'i brtson 'grus can//ngo bo nyid sku zag med rang bzhin bral//gzhal yas me 'od 'bar ba'i dkyil 'khor can//nyi ma 'bum gyi gzi ltar 'phro ba'i bdag/chos kyi lam la 'jug pa'i mi zhig gis//lus dang sems la bslab pa cher byas te//phyi rol sangs
59a5(kha): rgyas mi btsal rnyed mi 'gyur//sems can nyid ni kun tu bzang por gsungs//nang gi nang la de bzhin lta bar bya'o//byang chub kyi sems rma du byung ba'i rgyud las/mtshan ma med par grub pa dang /theg pa chen po la khyad par 'phags pa'i le'u ste dgu pa'o//
59a6(kha): de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos nyid brjod par nyid la byas pa/kun tu bzang po bde ba chen po'i sku//sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor chen por ni/'khor ba chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor//byang chub sems la sbyor ba ma yin par//'khor ba
59a7(kha): 'khor lo bskor ba rngub yang med/yang dag ye sangs rgyas pa ma rtogs par//dmyal ba'i ming med ma rig pa'ang ming //dus gsum bde bar gshegs pa thad dang /rigs drug 'jig rten 'byung ba lnga sogs rnams//byang chub sems kyi rin chen sgrom du
59b1(kha): drug mi nga sadus spyong glang phyug se ma sas nga shAn pa chos byung chub sems kyi rlos ga sa gtags pa gargyas da sa gnan med pa sa tsha me sems kying bzhin gdas med pa med pa dus gas
59b2(kha): sangs rgyas chags pa ngo mtshar che/rang rig byang chub sems las ma gtogs pa'i/kun bzang gzhan nas rnyed byar mi 'gyur bas//sngon chad ma rig ma mthong ngo mtshar che bden gnyis rgyu 'bras yong gis med pa /bslu ba'i rmi lam bzhin gyis kun rdzob
59b3(kha): spyod//don dam pangan pa ma gtogs ngo mtshar tsho//'ching byed dmyal rgyu drug lnga gzhan rtogs pas /byang chub chen po'i byang chub chen po ru ka dug lnga rgyas par gyur pa ngo mtshar che ka sdug bsngal sdig pa mi dge ci dpyad kyang //rnam grol bla meng sangs rgya
59b4(kha): yin pas//pang bzhis skyon gyis ma gos ngo mtshar che ka /dmyal rgyu mi dge sna tshogs spyad pa dang /mi gtsang zas zos bshang bkun sbyar yang ka kun tu bzang po'i rol pa bdag nyid las/rgyu'i 'bras bur mi smin ngo mtshur che//nyams dmanar mi
59b5(kha): dmyal ba'i gnas rnams kyang /kun tu bzang mo b+ha ga'i mkha' gling las/dmyal ba'i gnas med byang chub lam/bde ldan de lnga gnas pa ngo mtshar che/gnod sbyin srin pa la sogs 'bum gyis kyang //bla med byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin la ShaH
59b6(kha): 'joms dang thob par mi 'gyur gyi nam bsdus rang bzhin bsdus pa ngo mtshar che//e ma rab tu gsang ba'i chos chen po//de bzhin gshegs pas gsungs pa gong yang med//theg le yongs kyi theg pa mi mnyam ste/gzhan dang thun mongs ma yin
59b7(kha): mtshar che//sngags kyi phreng ba yu gu rgyud tshul du/bskyed dang spro dang bsdu dang sbyar drangs dang /mchod dang 'dod rtsol don gyi phyir gzhag na/re smon thub chod rtsol bral las 'das pa'i /theg pa'i rgyal po rgyu 'bras ye dag pas/de bzhin de bzhin de la mnyam
60a1(kha): @/gnas pas /chog phreng bar gyur pa mtshar che//ming gzugs mnyam gzhag lengs sngags tshig la 'gags sku dang bral ba'i byang chub sems legs dge ga sangs pa'i dkyil 'khor nyid byung chub rgyu 'bras yongs kyi nga /
60a2(kha): y-i lam sung byang chub bdag nyid can ldan//sa pa sgra gags pa kun gyi thugs ci dgams rnams kyis pas yin//la sam byas kyu 'dras pa samed/rang bzhin tsha dag par tshum ma
60a3(kha): can pa gas pa kyi thug sbyin rgyas/nasams sa / da nyid ya ga du la ba las la mas ku su gas
60a4(kha): du//na bu byas ma shing rin chen sgros rnams las//'ongs skyong sna tshogs 'byung ba'i sa bon byed//myu phrul mkhan zhes thams cad 'phrul bar mdzad//skye meng las thams cad kun gyis shing smras pa nyid ni tsa med 'gags pa
60a5(kha): ga pa kun mom can byas 'phrul po Ni kA kAn bzang bdag nyid sku la ngo mtshar che//e ma'o ra mdzes thig mchog khyad par car//byar med bya bral bya byed chos dang brag/ji ltar gnas kyang rdzogros po chen po'i dang //rgyu 'dra'i dkyil 'khor lus
60a6(kha): dang mthun pa yin//dang sna ma nga bo nyid kyi zhi ba lus//sgra waM'i dkyil 'khor gsal ba ngom tshar che yenas rnam par dag pa'i rang bzhin las/oM shes 'ong du 'bar ba ngo mtshar che/bskal pa'i me bzhin thad bsreg pa l-i thams cad ma lus /
60a7(kha): pa ma cher che//ral gri bzhin du thugs gcod ba la/thams cad ma lus 'dung ba po cha che//'tshang ba mtshang ba bzhin du ci yang ma bskor ga thams cad ma lus bsrubs pa ngomatshar che/thams padam lus kun pabang nyid kyi dang / /
60b1(kha): chub kyi sems mdu byung ba'i rgyud las/rgyud kyi bdag po nyid kyis rgyud bshad pa las dam pa'i 'jus bcu bya'o///da nas bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la sogs pa rnams kyis phyag btsal bstong pa/bslab bya dzad/
60b2(kha): rnam par 'jig mdzad pa mgon po mchog 'dzin mi g.yo bde chen gu ka/do mtshar thabs chen ston pa'i dpa' bo laka/dus gsum rtag par 'dud pa'i bzhin phyag 'tshal/che ba lnga rdzogs sangs rgyas snying po la/sangs rgyas ma lus thams cad
60b3(kha): 'dus par bgyi//lta ba mi spangs sku la ngoms par blta//'du 'bre la med pa'i spyod pa lhag par 'dzin//'di la mos pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad laka su la bstod pa bzhin du byug kyang 'tshalal/byang chub sems dpa' 'di la su mos
60b4(kha): pa'i sems can de la'ang rtag par bzhin phyag 'tshal//ngo mtshar rmad kyi cho 'phrul ston pa yi//khams gsum yongs sgrol la seng ge'i rgyal po la//yang nas yang du 'dud cing phyag kyang 'tshal ka/mkha' ltar dgos pa sru las
60b5(kha): ba'i sku ka dbye med mkha' 'dra'i brdal ba chen po la//yang nas yang du 'dud cing phyag kyang btsal ka rnam grol ye grol 'khor ba kun grol ba'i//grol ba'i rgyal po mi gtong 'dung bar bgyid//dngas snyigs blang dor med pa gcig pu ga/rgyu 'bras
60b6(kha): mi 'byed rnam par dag pa'i chos//rnam thar lam dang mi mthun rnam grol chos//zab mo zab mo dam pa 'bras bu'i chos ka 'di 'chang klog dang gus dang sems pa lnga//dang mos kha bsags ma yin rnal ma la /'dud cing 'dud cing bzhin phyag/
60b7(kha): 'tshal bar bgyid//ces rgyad dang rgyud kyi bdag po la//bstod pas/rgyud kyi bdag po rdo rje sems dpa' nyid la gzigs te/spyan bgrang zhal gdangs nas/rdo rje sems dpa' khyod las lhag med//byang chub rnyeng dka' de nyid khyod kyi
61a1(kha): dus gsum sangs rgyas rnams kyis rtogs dka' ba'i//gnyis med rang bzhin gnas pa'i chos 'di la//'di las sangs rgyas gzhan na yod pa ma yin//pal ba rtsol ba'i don chen ye min pa'i//mi gso rang bzhin gas/
61a2(kha): pa'i min chen 'dis/sangs rgyas sems can ma lus thams cad kyang //mthong ba tsam gyis rnam dag grol bya'i phyir//rmad 'byung byang chub sems ni gta gtang //thugs kyi thugs te ye shes nyid ma yis/'khor lo nyid las 'od
61a3(kha): /kyi zer bskyod pa//thugs kyi dkyil du stim la 'khor los bskor//snying po mtha' med rnam par dag pa'i rgyud//sna'i rtse mo las byung sgron ma 'bar ba dag/ye shes dag pa'i sgrom du sba bar bya ka byang chub kyi sems rma du byung gi'i rgyud
61a4(kha): las//rgyud kyi bdag po la bstod pa dang /rgyud las bstod pa dang /bdag pos rgyud gtad pa dang /gdam ngag la zla ba'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o///rgyud kyi rgyal po chos thams cad kyi rmad du byung ba//rdzog so///byang chub kyi sems
61a5(kha): rmad du byung phyi ma ta ma nyid dang gsum bzhugs pa las/zab mo po ti'i lha khang du bu gnyis lus so//slob dpon bud d+ha kup+ta sa bsnams par bzhed nas/lan gcig gzigs pas/gnod sbyin kha rtsal gyi 'gram du thal lcag gcig rgyab nas rgyal bar gyur
61a6(kha): to brgyal sangs pa dang /srin mo ste bu'i tshul du 'dug pas smras pa/gnas khang rgyal bar gyur pa 'di nyid kyi/rgyal po mi 'od khyod kyi ma khyer ro//gal te khyer na khyod nyid bsang par bya//zhes smras pas/sred cing dngas nas/ma ni gang du spyan gzhan sa
61a7(kha): dudrangs/bu gnyis de lus so//gang du de nyid rnam par 'grel ba la/dgos don gsal tshad don la 'char ba ni/ma phu gsum nas drangs nas gtan lo ngago byi nas blo'i zor la spyod nas bya bzod na/'phags thugs 'ag ba dang 'bral du
61b1(kha): gnod sbyin mos//bar chad byed phyir blo'i gzer la skyong/las stong mkhas pa mgon po la/myang stong lce nag bdud rtsis zhus/sarba mdz+wa laM/bkris/
62a1(kha): @byag du/ ti med dmak+Sha ga sa cig tI wAn yo ga du rdzogs padzin po 'am ca nas bzod pa/chos su bye med rig pa'i rgyu ces phyag/bcos ldan 'das da la byung ma nyid leshes kyi bu dang phrul sa bya/
62a2(kha): ma go//di skad gsungs pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das 'og min gyi gnas/byang chub kyi snying po na bzhugs pas/rigs lnga'i ni sku gsung thugs gzigs nas/se shes 'khor lo dgongs pa gag gsos pa med par snyoms par gnaso//de tshe dus
62a3(kha): gsum sangs rgyas dang /rdo rje 'chang ba'i rigs 'dzin dang //phyi nang dam gdags chos skyong dang //gzhan yang lha mi la sogs pa//dbang med dang bas 'dus pa rnams//rang sems rtogs par 'dod pa'i phyir//dad pas yid kyis sems med par//thabs gcig rig pas
62a4(kha): 'khor 'gyur//de nas thugs kyis gzigs pa'i don//rdo rje sems dpa'i don rigs te//bskul don gsol btab ba rtse dgongs nas//ting 'dzin gyis gsol ba btab/'jig rten mkhas pa rdzogs sangs rgyas//ye shes thugs che chen po sdes//zhi gnas la ni 'bral med
62a5(kha): /don//chos nyid gnas lugs ji lta ba ka 'khrul pa'i blo yis rtogs pa'i thabs//nges don chos sku rang chas pa//dus 'dir bdag la bsha du gsol//rang byung ngo bo nyid sku de ka/don gsan chos rjes bka' stsal pa//sangs rgyas dgongs pa ji lta ba//'khor 'das ma rig snying /
62a6(kha): po'i don//yang mes nga yis don bshad du ka rdzogs chen skye med don zab kyang //snod ngan rnams la sngog mor che //lan cig deng sang mi rnams ni//dam chos spong ba'i rgyu bdun ni ka 'jig rten drug gi rkyen byas ste/shes rab 'chal ba'i skye bzhi la//ma go ba'i rtogs gsum gyis
62a7(kha): lhagades gams pa'i skyon bdun 'byung //de ma don sngon skad cig la/rgyud lungasangs rgyas sgeg cing spangs//nyan thos mdo sde brgyal cing 'bog//lha dab mini srin pa'i tshogs//sgo sgor 'jigs pa'i gang zag rnams//ngag tu dbyal ba chen po bcung ka
62b1(kha): mi'i bar chad dang //'jigs pa sna tshogs kun kyang 'ong //mgo lus tshal ba bdun du 'gas//rig 'dzin rnams kyi spong bar 'gyur//de phyir snod ldan shin tu dgon//de skad bka' stsal don go nas//sems kyi gdung ba bdun ston ste//'gal sems rdul ma med
62b2(kha): bar ni//dang bas byang chub don la mos//nges shing dang pas yid spraso//de tsheng ba nyid skye ngas yis 'khor lde'i dgongs pa yaka thugs kyis mngon par bzhengs nas kyang //kun bzang chos sku'i rtogs don deka don sbrus rtsa ba bka' stsal pa/leg so dang ldan 'khor rnams
62b3(kha): tse//lhag mthong gsal bar ngas ba sad kyis//gdeng du go 'gyur rnal 'byor bas /de lta'i shes pa 'di nyid la/sul med pang yan btang nas 'byung //gal mad mi shes shul las 'charo//de skad gsungs pas drin go nas//'khor rnams dga' mgu mi ngas te//
62b4(kha): ngan panal sgonas pastod byas nas//sdo dang na dang bas skom pa dang //skal ldan rtogs pas chod pa dang //bye brag bstan pa pa gdu gsol//don gas na 'khor la bka' stsal pakam rtogs dang bas kyi snyoms kyang ka rang sems dmu nor stong khams lan /mangs kyi skab logs
62b5(kha): Sha k+tang srung gam dgas rnams pas mi sgros cing //nga yang 'dus pa'i ston pa nyin//nga gdon gyis des don 'di//'khor 'das tshig du yong dag ga/chos surnam par lta du de//kun pa khya kyang rtogs pa gar//u dum pa'i'i ma tog bzhin gyu ma nga
62b6(kha): 'an sa 'ag zhig/'byung srid kun gyi thun mong min//de phyir snod lngas byung gyur na//thu sa rdo rje slob dpon la//zhan pa gang bsgrub pa dang nas nas//mched la sprid ltar gdung byed cing //thos bas ma smom par 'dod bar byir//byang chub sgrog dang sngog pa/des donapa
62b7(kha): dam na di ma rtso gong sa spyang dgas dang ldan pa na ga ge ba'i rjes //bsnyen steg pa das par chog dbyi lung badzin pazhin du ston /daya de cis mas 'i sngon/ya cig dus kyi nga sang na/jogs ca na des na sang snying pa ga dang ldan na dzin
63a1(kha): @'di la 'jug/de chen rdzogs chen bstan pa 'byung ka/de dus nges don 'chang ba la ka lta dman la sogs dgra lnga 'byung //nyes pa bzhi'i gnod bsams dang //tsha mtsho ma med par 'chang nus na//bde gshegs mkha' 'gros byin gyis brlobs//gnod pas
63a2(kha): mi tshugs bsod nams 'phel//de tshe mu stegs la sogs rnams//rang rang brkam chags rtsod gyur nas//bkra mi shis shing rang brlag nas//'og mi bstan pa 'jig par 'gyur//de lta bu'i dus byung tsho//gal te tshogs la chos 'chang na//nyams dang lkugs
63a3(kha): pa zhe 'khon rnams ka yod med gsher la bshag su bzhug/nyam su len par dam bcas la//gdam ngag yang snying tshang bar bstan//de ltar ma lus pa la 'di bstan na/ka/gnam ljags sa la bying ba bzhin ka mkha' 'gros srog gcad nges par ltung //de phyir ma rtags
63a4(kha): chos mi bshad//rdzogs pa chen po 'khor brtsad nas gcod pa skye med rigs pa'i rgyud las//don gyis gling bzhi bstan pa'i le'u ste dang po'o///da yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyonaka nga'i gsung gi 'od zer gyis ka ma rig sems kyi mun gseb du

68b5(kha): bo 'khor ba kyang nas gcod pa/chos sku skye med rig pa'i rgyud las/man ngag gi dbye ba'i sgo nas//'khrul pa ldog pa yon tan bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu bdun pa'o///kun bzang nga'i byin brlabs kyis//cig du ma bkod thugs la rgyud//don gyi gting phyin/
68b6(kha): bstan pa'i phyir//tshig nyung don du rdzogs pa yis//thig le la shan dbye ba ni//mtshan nyid brtags pa'i thig le dang //gsang sngags bzhi'i thig le dang //rdzogs chen spyi'i thig le dang //yang ti'i thig le nyag gcig la//re re la ni bzhi bzhi ste//ngo bo dpe dang gtan
68b7(kha): /tshigs kyis//'gro sa la shan dbye ba yang //rtsal gyi sgo nas gsum bstan pa//snang ba chos dbyings la sogs pa//dbye ba'i sgo nas bzhi ru snang //da yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//nang gi rig pa chos sku las//sgra mthun don dpag gtan tshigs kyis
69a1(kha): /thams pa thig le yin mod kyang //grangs ni bzhi'i mtshan nyid la/ngo ba la sogs pa'i bzhi'o//skyenas gtan pas rtogs pa'i tshe//don gyi ngo bo gnyisu 'dus//nga yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//phyi nang yul la sogs rnams kyis//bcom parten
69a2(kha): /rnams bsal ba'i phyir//zung lnga ya bcu bdun gyis ni//mi 'gal shes pa'i rnal 'byor de//nges pa'i gdeng ldan smon slob dpon po mchog/rdzogs pa chen po 'khor ba rtsad nas gcod pa//chos sku rig pa skye med kyi rgyud las//the tsom gags bsal ba'i phyir//dngos
69a3(kha): bstan pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o///dayang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//de nyid rtogs pa'i yon tan ni//sems bskyed gong 'og rnam pa dang //zhi gnas lha mthong rdzogs pa yis//pha rol phyin bcu dbang la sogs//sngags kyi mdzad skyong thams
69a4(kha): cang rdzogs//de yang 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams nyon//rang sems rtogs pa'i ye shes la//gol sgrib med par bstan pa yang ko bcu gnyis gsum dang bdun bstan pa//snga phyi'i rtog pa gsum du 'dus//gang yang yid la ma byas na//gol med chos sku Sh+Ta
69a5(kha): la gnas//nga yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//yang dag don gyi nying khu'i don//ye nas chos sku 'bral med don//nyams kyi rim pa lnga ldan pas//rtags chen gsum gnyis shugs las 'byung //de tshe bdud kyi spyod yul no//bzhi gsum drug 'byung zil
69a6(kha): gnon na//nges pa don gyi rtags yin pas//de phyir bar ngo'i sangs rgyas 'grub ka rdzogs pa chen po 'khor brtsad nas gcod pa//chos sku skye med rig pa'i rgyud las//gegs sel ba'i yon tan bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o//
69a7(kha): sems dpa' chen pos yang gsol pa//'bras bu yon tan 'dul bya yi/blta dam nyams pa bskang thabs dang /rtsa ba gcig pa'i don rtogs na//sangs rgyas mdzad spyod ji ltar spyad//kun rig rgyal pos bshad du gsol//de skad zhus pa'i don
69b1(kha): gsan nas/'khor rnams la ni bka' stsal pa/ lta spyod la sogs thams bskang ba//nyes 'gyod yid la byas nas su//bla ma mkhas pa'i don ldan na//dbang rnams rim par mnos nas ni//rang sems dbang bcugs don zhus nas//rgyun du mi spong gus pa'i chog
69b2(kha): de la rtag tu gnas par bya//de bskongs gdeng chen bdun ldan pas//da lta nyid du sangs rgyas thugs//gser sbrul mig gi 'dod yon lnga//dus gcig nyam su myong ba bzhin//ci spyod sangs rgyas ye shes thugs//sems can 'gro den btsal mi dgos//mi
69b3(kha): lus ldan pa 'di nyid la//lha mi phyag 'tshal mchod pa dbul//rdzogs pa chen po 'khor brtsad nas gcod pa//chos sku skye med rig pa'i rgyud las//lha spyod nyams pa bskong ba tshad bstan pa'i le'u ste nyi shu pa'o///da yang 'dus pa'i 'khon
69b4(kha): rnams nyon//'khor 'das 'byung ba'i rtsa ba ni//pha ma med pa'i bu bzhin yin//de yang gdod ma gcig las gnyis//gsum la 'brel yod gcig la med//tshar ba bzhi'i don shes pas//skal ldan rnams kyi rang rig bya//bden mthong snang med rtogs dus 'di/
69b5(kha): tshig du mtshon par mi nus kyang //sems la gsal por yod pa'i phyir//rig pa'i ye shes chos nyid mthong //dus gsum dag du skye 'gag med//mtshan mar mthong ba ci yang med//rin chen 'dam khung gtor gyur kyang //de'i yon tan mkha' la 'byung //de/
69b6(kha): bzhin rang sems ngo bo nyid//mngal du rang sems bying gyur kyang //rang byung gdod nas rang gsal phyir//ye shes chen po rgyun mi 'chad//da yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon/ka/lus kyi nang na chos sku gsal ka/nang sgo de'i 'od shar bas//snang ba grogasu 'ong ba rnams
69b7(kha): de tshe lus dang 'brel ba med//cang shes reg 'jam na mkha' lding //rgya mtsho'i gser dang mchog rab bzhin//sems kyi gar phyin chos bskur gsal//yul gyi bzung 'dzin skyon mi dgos//rig pa zo thub pag zon ldan//'khor bar gnas kyang
70a1(kha): smrahAgo da/ rkyen sa da ste gyung na //DU segs kyis/bras tha gi da/ zed rgyu gas pas gas kyu sa zhes bya/rdzogs pa chen po la yong cad nas ga dag cos sa
70a2(kha): rig ba'i rgyud las//snang med sems kyi rig ba rtogs dus bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gcig pa'o///da mang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//nga'i mdzad skyong chen po na//sku med gzugs skur 'thung ba ni//thams cad zil gyis gnan par byed/
70a3(kha): med rig pa'i gsung gi ni//sangs rgyas bstan pa rgyas par sgrogs//rnam par rtog med ye shes thugs//cir yangs la le tha~Md mkhyen//stong gsum 'jig rten gsum po yang //nga'i bzhugs gnas gzigs mo 'os//sku gsung blugs kyi rol pa ste/
70a4(kha): rims ni ga sum gsas dang //smye rnam pa sus ni///ces phra samig can/sras dgral ba'i skang cig la/sangs rgyas sngal shes su//gzugs su rnams gnyis zhugs pa 'jar/sems can pasas kyi'i khyad pa mes/ying na
70a5(kha): bo gang 'thang ston//rdzogs pa chen po 'khor brtsad nas gcod pa/chos sku skye med rig pa'i rgyud las/yon tan gyi gdul bya bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gnyis pa'o///de nas kun rig rgyal po yis//'dus pa'i 'khor ni rnam bzhi la//nges pa'i bzhos
70a6(kha): byu bkar stsal pa//rang rang rtags pa 'dir smos cig /khyi sang gsus po'i don gsams//rnam par gzigs la sogos pa yis//sngon byung sangs rgyas dun gyis/ra rtogs pa'i don brjod pa/rig pa 'jig med chos kyi gru//rtogs pa 'irjid med
70a7(kha): yid ches//ma btsal rang sar 'gyur ba med//dran pa skyon bral dang du bzhag/snang med rang sems chos sku gsal//rang lus da lta nyams pa med//dngos med rig pa stong par gsal//log par dang sel la sogs pa'i//da lta sangs rgyas rnam bdun gyis//rang
70b1(kha): rtogs pa yang gsungs pa/rang byung kun khyab ye shes gsal gas la le mi rtog pa lta nying //nang gsal phral la mA yengs bka/dngos med gsal ba skom mi dgos//ra lugs rangs chos nyid don/ngo bo 'gyur med me bcos pa/tha ma la snying po rtogs pa hra/
70b2(kha): byams pa las ni stsogs pa yis//ma byon sangs rgyas rnam pa bdun//rang rang rtog pa yang gsungs pa//shes pa 'dzin med ye shes 'grub//cir snang dgag sgrub med par zab//snga phyi yengs med ye shes rgyun//rnal du rig pa 'gyur ba med//skye med rtogs
70b3(kha): pa byang chub sku//brjed med ting 'dzin 'byung 'jug med//rang sems ma bcos don la zhogan dus gsum sangs rgyas de dag gis//dgongs pa gcig phyir ye shes mthun//rdo rje yengs med la sogs pa//rigs 'dzin sems dpa' yab sum bcu//rang rang
70b4(kha): rtogs pa'i don brjod pa//ye shes 'dzin med reg pa stong //stong pa'i dran pa 'gyur ba med//spyod bral lta ba yengs pa med//ye shes 'chi med zhe sdang bral//rang sems ci spyong snying po'i don//ma skyes du rgyal ngo bo nyid//dngos med ye shes ke la yod//'dod chags
70b5(kha): gos med ye shes rgyun//lta ba rtsol ba meng ba'i klong //gsal ba zhen med nyams kyi mchog/de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//rgyal sras sprul pa de nyon cig/rang sems 'gyur med rig pa gsalo//re dogs med pa lam du rtogs//de skad ces ni gsol pa
70b6(kha): dang 'thugs la dgongs nas bka' stsal pa/shes pa blo'i khyad par las//tshig du mtshon pa sna tshogs dang //don la dbyer med ngo bo gcig/sangs rgyas dgongs pa khyod kyis rtogs//rdzogs pa chen po 'khor brtsad nas gcod pa//chos sku skye med rig pa'i
70b7(kha): rgyud las//'khor gyis rtogs pa brjod pa'i le'u ste nyer gsum pa'o///de skad ces ni gsungs pa dang //de tshe 'dus pa rnam bzhi yang //rang sems rtogs pa mngon gyur nas//dga' ba'i sems kyi yi rangs ste//rdo rje sems dpas don
71a1(kha): gsol pa//rtogs pa brjod pa'i don mthun pa//'bras bu sangs rgyas da lta myong //rab sems ma dang stug gis phrag//de phyir nga lha kun bzang nyid//rdo rje sems dpa' dga' ba che//'dren mdzad khyod la phyag 'tshalo//de skad gsol ba'i don go nas//
71a2(kha): /rdo rje sems dpa' yang gsungs pa//sangs rgyas thugs kyi byin brlabs las//dgos pa ma lus bcud sdus pa//dus gsum rgyal ba'i thugs kyi don//gdam ngag tshegs med nyid khu ni//dang ldan khyod la ngas bshad pa//don 'di rgyud la shin tu spros//yang dag
71a3(kha): nyinyi bu sems kyi bcung //thig le nyag gcig mngon phyung ba//chos kyi gter gyi 'byung gnas 'di//snong ldan rnal 'byor rnams kyis kyang //gal te phyi rabs don ston na//sangs rgyas chos skyong dbang du btsugs//gzhan la mi sprul mnas bor nas//snod
71a4(kha): ldanamam ngag tshang bar bstan//tshogs kyi mchod pa ci nus bya//de ltar ma bcos chos bstan na//slob dpon tshe thung bar chad 'ong //bka' chu bla ma dmod pa la//mi tshe 'di nyid da lha'i dus//nang dang kha logs sna tshogs 'ong //de phyir man ngag sang snying 'di
71a5(kha): dzis rnamags panas yong phyir//na 'di ma spel sems la chos//rdo rje sems dpas de ga gsus//rdzogs pa cher po la ba bkyang nas gcong ba/chos kyu skye ba med pa'i rgyud sa//rin po ga gyi 'byung gnas nas rlu ba rtogs so//
71a6(kha): rgya gar gyi mkhan po bi ma la mi tra dang a tsar+ya dran pa ye shes kyi rang 'gyur byas te gtan la phab pa'o//kris ///rgyu gar skad du/sin+d+hu ma hA bud d+ha sarba sa ma ti/d+har+m+ma kA sa oM ka ta d+ya la kad+h+wi
71a7(kha): ka rarA b+da 'gu na mu ta la tan+t+ra mam/bod skad du/rdzogs pa chen po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku cig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don/rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud ces bya ba//dpal sku cig chos kyi sku de kho na nyid mi
71b1(kha): ga pa phrus rgya /da gsus sa byu pa som //'an slong gi sa ca na/nyi sas migasasems pa mams #
71b2(kha): mngon 'gyuraro//de tshe chos kyi snying po de//khazhengs pa med pa'i dpal bzhengs ka rang rig ye shes 'gyur med don//brjod med snying po bka' stsal pa//rang byung ngo bo'i gdam ngag de//'dzin pa'i yul med mtha' yas grol//blo yis ma bcos don dang ldan//
71b3(kha): rtogs med mnyam nyid chos nyid nyan//de skad don gyi bka' stsal pa//ji bzhin sems dpa' don rig nas//rang gi yid kyis rtogs gsal phyir//ting 'dzin dang las 'di skad gsol//don chen smra ba'i seng ge khyod//chos kyi sgron ma bsam las kyang /
71b4(kha): btsal med thug med brjod du med//de don bdag la bshad par mdzod//de skad zhus pas don gnas nas//mnyam pa'i don la rab bzhengs nas//brjod med bstan pa'i phyir//don gyi byin rlabs bka' stsal pa//brjod bya tshig don dgongs pa'i//nyi gos
71b5(kha): dril bcas don ldan pa//rgyud rnams kun la tshang ba gces//de phyir des don bshad pa yin//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//ye nas gnas pa'i ngo bo la//yin ma ni lhag pa'i the tsom mod//rtog bral shes pa rang gnas phyir//re dogs med pa'i don mthong gis//
71b6(kha): de don gyi gsungs pa dang //'dus pa'i snying po sems dga' nas//ji bzhin las kyi rgyud dag du//rang gi rtogs pa mngon gyur nas//kun bzang rig pa'i ston pa la//bden pa'i dgongs don 'di skang gsol//rtsol bral yang ti'i rtogs tshul ni//yul med/
71b7(kha): rang byung ye shes la ka zhen pa med pa'i rig pa'i shar//gza' gtad med pa'i ngo bor rtogs//de skad ces ni zhus pa dang //de'i bsam pa thugs chud de//sku cig ston pas bka' stsal pa//de ltar rtogs pa'i byin rlabs kyis//phyogs bcu'i dus ma lus ba
72a1(kha): sangs rgyas ji snyeng gang bzhugs pa/chos nyid chos skur dgongs pa gcig/gzhanan sangs rgyas rdul tsam med//de skang gsungs ma thag du ni//da mo dus gsum sangs rgyas dang //rdo rje 'chang gi rig 'dzin dang /rgyal sras sprub gi/
72a2(kha): na po dang //das btags mkha' 'gro sde lnga dang //dang ldan lha mi las can kun//khab len la ni lcags 'du bzhin//mos grus dang pas 'dus nas ni//lus dang thugs rjes 'joms nas 'khod//rtogs pa chen po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs
72a3(kha): chos sku bka' cig du 'dus pa'i bka' bgrong ba'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gis rgyud las/des don cheng du gsungs pa'i gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///des ngan snang ba bang 'dugs loka/zhus pa'i don gson bka'/
72a4(kha): de nas kun rig rgyal po las//'dus pa 'khor gyi don che du ka/rdo rje sems dpas don zhus pa//sems can ma rigs gsal ba'i phyir//gdul bya'i bsam pa'i khyad par las//ci rigs par ni bka' bgros pa//nges don snying po bsha du gsol
72a5(kha): zhus pa'i don gsan bka' stsal pa//nga yis byin gyis rlabs pa yis//sangs rgyas ma lus 'dir 'dus pa//sku med gzugs skur mngon bzhengs la//gsung med rigs pa'i don sgrogs pas//rang sems snying po rtogs pa'i thabs//chos nyid
72a6(kha): gnas lugs gang yin pa//rtogs med thugs rjes bka' bgros pa//sems can don du byin gyis brlabs//de skad ces ni gsungs pa'i don//sangs rgyas sems dpa' kun gyis kyang //gang zhig zhus pa ma spongs na//mnga' bdag rdo rje
72a7(kha): sems dpa' yis//phyi rabs dang ldan don chad du//nges pa'i dgongs don 'di skad gsol//dus gsum sangs rgyas ma lus kun//nges don mnga' bdag khyed yin pas//rtsol bcas khyod ni mtha' ston pas//rtsol med theg pa mi gsungs
72b1(kha): ga sngon gyi thugs dam yal lam ci//ma nor chos nyid bsha du gsol//de skad ces ni zhus pa yis//sangs rgyas 'ub chub 'dus kun nas//rang rang dgongs pa'i don bdur nas//bka'i 'gro ba mod du bgyis//zhus pa'i don gsan bka' stsal pa
72b2(kha): gzhi nyid rtogs pa lam gyis mchog/de nyid ma bral mngon gyur pas//'bras bu sangs rgyas da lta nyid//skye gzhan du btsal mi dgos//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//ma nor sangs rgyas dgongs pa nyid//nga yis nga nyid grol ba las//
72b3(kha): sangs rgyas sems can ma lus pa//nga'i bka' ni na gro nus med pa'i phyir//ma gsungs rgya cher bshad pa 'dra/ka/de yangs 'dus pa'i snying po gsol//rgya cher ma gsungs don 'di ni//yul la zhen pa med pa'i phyir ka shes pa rang gsal
72b4(kha): rigs pa las/yid la dran dang ma dran pa//gang gi phyogs lhung med pa'i phyir//ma bsgom ye nas 'bral ba med//gang yang yin phyir gol sgrib med//de'i yon tan brjod mi lang //de skad ces ni gsungs pa dang //rdo rje sems dpa' don go nas//ma rig
72b5(kha): spangs pas chos sU rmas//de spyir rtags pa mngon du gyur//rang bzhin lta ba gdeng ldan pas//'bras bu sangs rgyas ngas 'byung //sangs rgyas thaMd kyi dgongs 'chos sku gcig tu 'dus par bka' 'gros pa'i don//rin ma mi min tan kun 'thung
72b6(kha): gi rgyud las//bka' bgros pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de nas kun rig ston pa yis//phyi rab rnal 'byor don chen du//bcung dang snod kyi rim pa ni//'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa/bla ma don ldan nges pa'i bcud //phyi nang dbang
72b7(kha): rnams rdzogs nas ni/thun mongs ma yin khyad 'phags pa'i//rang rig ye shes dang gsum ni//dang pas mnos pas bden pa mthong //thal da yin tan ni/chos rgya che lagadas dag zag/bka' sangs snyi ma tu rdzan/mi nga bar gsus
73a1(kha): /ni thob//nges pa don gyi gdengs yod phyir//gcig pur gnas kyi skyo ba med//lta ba rtogs pa bdud mi tshugs//ting nge 'dzin yengs med khams mi rtog/tshul 'chos med cing drang por mdzad//chags chud ci byed chos dang mthun//do dgar mi
73a2(kha): gtong snod la rtogs//dad pa can la gdam ngag yang //sangs rgyas dgongs pa thugs la 'chang //da yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//slob ma'i snod kyis mtshan nyid ni//dang ldan khyad 'phags shes rab can//'og ma'i rjesu mi 'brang ba dang //gsang sngags
73a3(kha): bka' ba rtogs pa mkhas//man ngag chos la shin tu mos//bla ma la gus phyi thag ring //chags med 'bul nus dad pa'i brtan//chos kyi gzhungs 'dzin spyod pa mkhas//phag du bstod cing yon tan brjod//gzhan la sgros skur zil gyis gnon//thabs la
73a4(kha): mkhas pas mi mi sdang //gtang ba dang len chud mi bzon//de ldan blo ma byung srid na//'bral med ye shes rang shar don//rgyud drug yang dag yin pa'i phyir//bstan ma thag du rtogs pa'i chos//snod ldan gsang thub dga' la bstan ka/sangs rgyas thaMd kyi dgo
73a5(kha): pa chos sku cig du 'dus par dka' 'gros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//snod bcud bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la ka rtsa ba mdor bsdus bka' stsal pa//chos rnams
73a6(kha): kun gyi rtsa ba ni//'khor 'das bzhi'i ngo bo nyid//de ma shes pas lung ma bstan//de las 'khrul pas 'khor bar smin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//phyi ba mu stag rnam gnyis dang //nang pa sangs rgyas mkhas mches pa'i//nyan thos rang rgyud yan chad kyis//gzhi nyid ma
73a7(kha): shes 'a khrul ba'i phyir/b+hi gol bdud bslus par dor la khyams//de'i mdog chos shes pa dgos//'khor 'das ma rig nges pa'i don//bka' rtags pa'i don bsdus pa'i//dang ldan 'khor la ngas bshad kyis//rang gi sems la nges par 'tshod //de yang 'dus
73b1(kha): snying po son//rig 'dzin dngos grub bka' rtags ni//sras ya rgyal sring gtang ba bzhin//lung rdzad nges dgi dam dag ni//slob ma dang ldan 'ga' la gtad //rig pa byin brlabs bka rtags ni//rin chen gser 'gyur de bzhin du//tha ma la shes pa 'di nyid la/'ang rig chos su/
73b2(kha): byin gyis brlabs//nyam su myong ba'i bka' rtag ni//bde ba snying la zhen pa bzhin ka rang rig chos skar ngo sprad pas//zhen med nyams dang 'bral mi 'phod//lo zlo spyod pa'i bka' rtags ni ka mar gyi 'o ma mthung ba bzhin//ci spyod 'brel med chos skur shes//gol med la zlos
73b3(kha): don mi 'bral//'bras bu mthar byin bka' rtags ni//nor bu rin chen rnyed pa bzhin//chos rnams chos skur cig shes pas//'khor stong sangs rgyas smos ci dgos//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//bka' rtags lnga ni cig shes nas//rang sems rtogs pas bzhi dang drang/
73b4(kha): de phyir chos sku 'bral med lam//'bras bu 'gyur med sangs rgyas yin//de yis de nyid mtshon pa'i dge//'dzum mo la rten zla ba mthong //gdam ngag la rten lhag mthong shar//don rtogs klong nas yis 'phyo/rig pa chos sku rnam pa gnyis//tha dad med de nyi zer tshul//don la
73b5(kha): dbyer med chos kyi skug/rgyu 'bras ma bskyed ye nas yin//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus pa'i bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyu de las//gdam ngag rtsa ba gcig pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o///de nas rdo
73b6(kha): sems dpa' yis//'dus pa'i tshogs la phan gdugs phyir//kun rig chos kyi rgyal po la//bden pa'i dgongs don 'di skad gsol//'khor 'das gzhi ni gang gi bgyi/ ma rig bzhi ni gang gi btsal//de las 'khrul pa ji ltar byung //de skad zhus pa'i don gsan nas//'dus
73b7(kha): pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa/ka/e nas gnas pa'i gzhi nyid ni//skye ba ma blang dus na yang //rgyu rkyen ma skyes me yod kyang //sgo skur bral dang ma bral ba'i//rtogs dang 'khrul pa gang yang med//rig dang ma rig shan ma byed//sangs dang ma sangs ngos ma zin//rang rig hata dade/
74a1(kha): @/tsam yod kyang //gang gi 'du shes ma zhugs pas//bya ba btang ba'i mi bzhin du ka/'dzin byed don gyis ma zin pra//rang bzhin lu su mi btags te//gzhi nas ma rig med pa'i phyir//bag chags ma gos 'khrul ma myong //rtogs pa ma gzhag sangs ma rgyas/
74a2(kha): phyi'i 'byung ba ma chags phyir//rgyud dang rkyen gyis 'dus ma byas//de yang 'dus pa'i sems dpa' nyon//rang ba ni'i mtshan nyid ni//rtogs dang mi rtog cha med phyir//'khor ba myang 'das gnyis kyi gzhi//kun la yod pas gzhi zhes bya//sangs rgyas thams cad kyis dgongs la
74a3(kha): chos sku cig du 'dus pa'i bka' 'bros pa'i don//rin po tsho kun 'byung gi rgyud las//la khor 'das kyi bzhi bstan pa'i le'u ste lbu pa'o///dongs kun rig rgyal po yis//ji ltar 'khrus pa bka' stsal gdang po 'khor 'das par ngol//ji zhig ltar la sangs tshub
74a4(kha): gyis//'khrul be ni med las 'khrul pa 'byung //med las byung ba ma mtshon che//dbang 'dus pa'i snying po gas ni//sgyu tsam ci zhig dran pa skabs//ji zhig dran dngam dran b+h+r-i//gang blo ma 'jug don mi shes//ma rig gzhi ni lung ma bstan/'khrul ba ni med pa'i 'khrul pa di/rang

74b1(kha): ga bzhin//lhan cig ma rig gsun du smin//de nas ma rig sbubs nang nas//pho mo ma ning ma yin pa'i//rgyu 'dra gzugs nyid rdzus skyes 'byung //'jig rten mes dang ma rig rgyal//zla drug lon dang pho mo smin//de sbyar 'bru drug dug sngar skyed//de la skye ba rnam bzhi yis//'gro drug/
74b2(kha): sems can tshangs par byas//'khru ba zhi thog ma med nas byung //de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//meng pa las ni yod 'dzin pas//'khrul bzhi las ni 'khrul 'byung //dper na mtho yor mi mthong tshe//mtho mor mi ni ma yin ti//ji zhig lta rol mi ru mthong //de bzhin
74b3(kha): gzhi la ji zhig ltar//blo bur rkyen gyis skyes pa yis//sems dang bag chags kun gzhi byung //de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//med pa la ni yod pa rtogs/ka/'khrul pa rnams kyis shes pa las//rgyal ba kun gyi mi mkhyen phyir//des don gzhi las 'khrul pa med//blo
74b4(kha): bur sems las 'khrul pa 'byung ka thag pa sbrul du 'dzin pa bzhin//de las skyes par 'khor bar 'khyams//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//da lta gang na gnas pa'i tshe//'khrul gzhi yod las kun btags gsum//byang chub ma shes gti mug gis//de phyir thog/

75a1(kha): @ba bzhin//dbye ba drug gi don mtshon de//ngo bo mi 'gyur ga ni byas nas ka kha dog dbyibs can dag du 'khrul//log pa'i dka' thub dmyal bar ltung //rig pas brtags pas bdag ma grub//de nyid la blos chos sku ying ka/de las gzhan pa'i sangs rgyas med/

79a5(kha): rkyen ni bag chags gnyis kyi gol//grol sar bar dor lus gsum len//sdug bsngal sna tshogs nyam su myong //kye rgas sdug bsngal zangad dus med//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de las thar cing ma grol bas//kun brtags dag rtsa 'di byas
79a6(kha): bas/ dge bcu sbyin sogs dad pas spyad//phyi ma 'bras bu 'dod pas sems//rgyu mthun bag chags mthu rtas pas ka/lha dang me'i go 'phang tsam//smon lam btab ba'i dbang btsan yang //dge ba'i ste ga zad 'khor bar 'khyams//de yang 'dus pa'i
79a7(kha): snying po nyan//gnos 'gyur lha'i bar de li//rtogs dang lha gnyis dus snyams pas// dus gsum cig la migi tshang na//'bras bu sangs rgyas dus de nyid//ma rtogs lha sgom ru 'drang s+ya//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//rnam shes rlung non par dom
79b1(kha): phyi nang rlung gis bde snyoms pas//phag mo la sogs 'pho ba bdun//'od gsal ye shes sku 'dod pas//drang don yin phyir nges don med//sangs rgyas tha~Md kyi gongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus pa'i bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po
79b2(kha): yon tan kun 'byung gi spyad las/srid pa par dos la byams pa bstan pa'i yaru ste bac+doM snad pa'o///danas rdo rje sems dpa' yis//bsgru pa'i la hU~Mr gyi don tshad du//kAn+da rI/smom pa nos d+he ga//
79b3(kha): dgongs don 'di skad gsol //lung ldan nges pa'i gdam ngag gis//bdung bde dpe'i zlog chos dupe don gang //ma rig rgyab de mdud ji ltar bzlog/phyi nang la bzlog gtan tshig gang //snang ba yul gyi gol sa gang //mnyam pa'i rgyal
79b4(kha): pos bshad du gsol//de skad zhus pas don gsan nas//de'i bzlog chos bka' stsal pa//dpe la 'khrul med bzlog chos dang //don gyi 'khrul med zlog chos dang //rtag kyis 'khrul med zlog chos dang /nga yis bshad kyis legs par nyon/
79b5(kha): del dpope'i bzlog chos dang //zag pa sbrul du 'khrul srid kyang //mtho yir sbrul du 'khrul mi srid//lung ma bstan pas 'khrul srid kyang //rang bzhin bzhi la 'i khrul pa med//zag pa sbrul du mi 'khrul bzhin//rtogs pa'i gzhi la 'khrul pa med//
79b6(kha): de 'ang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//dza mo rto ru 'phrul srid kyang //bya nirta ru 'khrul mi srid//yul ni sems su 'khrul srid kyang //rig pa bems por 'khrul mi srid//'byung ba lnga la rig pa med//de phyir yul sems 'brel ba med//de lang rdo rje sema?/
79b7(kha): dpa' nyon//don gyi ldog chos bstan pa ni//shes pa lung ma bstan pa la//'gro drug sems can 'khrul pa 'byung //lhun grub sangs rgyas 'khrul ma myong //de phyir gzhi la 'khrul pa med//ri bong rA ni med shes bzhin//do go log rtog 'khrul
80a1(kha): /ma~M srid//'dra ba las ni yod 'khrul pa//sems ni yul du 'khrul pA yin//gzug su 'dzin pa sna tshogs 'byung //de tsam nyid na rlon lnga med//de tsam don gyi bzlog chos yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//rtags kyi bzlog rnon
80a2(kha): /sa sbyi chung rdzun na ky-i //khams larbu byung mi spyod//sangs rgyas mtshan dang byed rjen/kha ma'i sras su khrul mo srid /khams cad mkhyen pa'i ma shes 'di/ma 'i rmongs par 'phrul mi srid/rba ldan
80a3(kha): pa'i gdam ngag 'di//khams gsum 'khor bar 'khyams mi srid//gzhi dang 'thun par nyams myongs phyir//skye ba phyi ma len mi srid//gnyis kyi klong thim don shes nas//bar do mun pa 'byung mi srid//ma btsa la gzugs sku byung ba sa//
80a4(kha): sngon du dge ba'i rtsa ba bya mi dgos//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan 'byung gi rgyud las//des don rtogs kyi ldog chos bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o//
80a5(kha): de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//mi rig bzlog pa'i ye shes ni//dgag bsgrub med pa'i rig pa la//gzhi la phyogasu zhen pa bzlog/snyam byed the tsom za ba ni//lto ba'i gdeng gis zil pas bzlog/gdam ngag rang grol dran pa yis//snga phyi
80a6(kha): dus gsum 'khrul pa bzlog/chos su gcig du rdzogs pa yis//rgyu bras gnyis 'dzin tha ma bzlog/dgongs pa'i dngal blo gnas pas//kun brtags phra mA'i chos la bzlog/de nga 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//sang ti thig le nyag cig g-i/
80a7(kha): theg dgu'i 'dod pa'i chos la bzlog/rgyun chad phyogs lhung med pa yis//rang don nga rgyal mtha' las bzlog/'dzin med rang byung sman chen gyis//'khor ba'i btsod rong nad las bzlog/de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//'khrul med dbya
80b1(kha): sa brgyad kyis//ken+d+ha 'khrul thams cad gzhi nas dgra/de phyir chos nyid cig shes pas//'khrul mongs thams cad dus gcig stongs//byang rna thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus pa'i bka' bgros pa'i don/rin po
80b2(kha): yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//ma rig pa bdud 'dzin 'khrul pa bzlog pa bstan pa'i le'u ste nya shu pa'o////de nas 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//thun mongs phyi rtags gtan tshigs ni kA na mkha' rang bzhin skye med phyir//gdod nas ra
80b3(kha): byung stong pa nyid//rkyen gyi gzhig cing zhom du med//de phyir rang byung skye med yin//de'i ngang las 'byung chen bzhi//rol pa ma 'gag cir yang snang //las dang rkyen dang bag cha gos ltas//phan gnod dge sdig gnyi su mthong //'byung ba 'gags na mkha'
80b4(kha): bzhin//snang stong gnyis med dbyer med pa'i//de phyir mtshan nyid gnyi su med//ye nas gnyis med ngang gi stong //de ltar rtogs pa rang gi blo//yul gyi 'byung ba rig pa med//de phyir gzung 'dzin rang sar grol//ngo bo nyid kyis dmigasu med//de yang rdo rje sems dpa/
80b5(kha): nyon//thun mongs ma yin nang bltas pa'i//rang sems skye med stong pa la//kha dog dbyibs kyi mtshan ma med//de phyir rang byung skye ba med//rang byung ma skyes ye nas yin//de la dran rig ma 'gags pa'i//rig pa gsal ba rgyun mi 'chad//gang dran rol pa ma 'gags
80b6(kha): shes / de tsam nyid na chos skure shes//sems nyid stong dang rig pa gnyis//so so ma yin gcig gis dang //de phyir mtshan nyid gnyi su med//de ya thA dad dbyer med cig/ye shes rang byung ngo bo nyid//sems dang rol pa rang sor grol//gang yang gzhi las med pa'i
80b7(kha): phyir Ta/rang gi blo gros ngo bor gnas//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//khyad 'phags la bzlos gtan tshigs na//nyi ma dang ni zer bzhin du//phyi nang gnyis ka gzugs dang sku//yang dag gnyis na skye ba med//ma skyes rig pa dngos med gsal//gang yang yis
81a1(kha): nags sa gnas nyid dgag sa sru gu kyim ga rnas med/ dam su du pas kyi namaca cadan pa cos //masas pa
81a2(kha): sa bya ba rgyas byi/sa kyi pa pasnyenapa dag phyi 'a gyu sa pa byung //dgung sogs 'ang grol gsus gsangs rgyas thams cad kyi dngas pa cos srug sa du dus pa 'thos pa'i da/nab mason tan bur 'thung gi rgya sa
81a3(kha): phyi nang mdog tan tshig bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gcig pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun rig ston pas bka' stsal bA//sna ba med pa'i gzhi nyid la//spong sdud zhen pas 'a khrul pa'i phyir//med pa la ni yod 'dzin pa/rmongs pakun
81a4(kha): gyi shes pa las ka sangs rgyas kun gyis mi mkhyen no//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//lus sems par na rang dbugs rgyu//dbugs ni sems kyi rta yin phyir//dbugs la brten nas sems rgyus pas/ka/sde phyi'i zhon pa sna tshogs byas//be ma rig ma phyed shin tu
81a5(kha): gol/spong sdud zhen pa sna tshogs kyang //sku yis mi 'dus gcig mi srid//spang gi mi spongs bral bar dka' /zhen pas mi zhen 'dre mi srid ka/gzhi la med par ma shes pas ka/'khrul pa'i chos la bden par 'dzin//snang ba chos dbyings gol bar gsungs//bud d+ha thaMd
81a6(kha): kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyu las/snang ba'i yul gyi gol ser bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gnyis pa'o////de nas kun rig ston pa yis//de nas 'dus pa'i snying po la//rigs 'dzin
81a7(kha): grub pa thob pa yis to/bka' brtags ched du bka' stsal pa/sangs rgyas kun gyi nges pa'i don//don gyi khyab dang rtags pa yis//byin gyis khyab dang brda' tshig don//snod ldan blo dang ldan pa ya/ma nor nges par ngas pa shad kyis//brjod mang dang ming yi kyis
81b1(kha): tsan sangs rgyas snying po ches su ni ka 'gro drug sangs rgyas man chad la/rang 'byung se shes rab yod//kun la khrub bas nam par 'dra/ des pa rnaMs pa'i snying po con/de tshe dog cin gnas mchog du//sngon gyi sangs rgyas rig wa la/cig dus /
81b2(kha): dang drug pa/rang gi rig pa rdog ngas rgyud//dbal kyi pal gyur kun bzang gis//rgyud bdag rdo rje sems dpa' la//des don gdas gdod pas nangs//de pyir byin kyis brlangs zhes bya//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//spu u bdang yis brtan pa las
81b3(kha): tshigs don lus dang rtags kyi brda' //rigs 'ji sprul pa gnang stsal gyi//lha gnas mi'i yul du byung //de tshe nges don dam tshig ni//lag na rdo rje'i sprul pa las//yang dag gangam ngag sngon byung nas//rdo rje mi 'gyur rtsal la ni//sngabs sbyor legs
81b4(kha): pa'i tshig gis ni ka don dang mthun par bkrol nas bshad//de tshe nges don gangam dag ni//rnam gzigs sprul pa bsgrub mchog la//tshig nyung don 'dril gdas dag zab//go sla gdeng du gyur bar bstan//de yis byams mgor su sprul pa/mi rjed bzung thob
81b5(kha): grogs btsal la//bstan pa tsam gyis sangs rgyas pa'i/ka/rang byung chos sku mngon du go///do dus sgron ma'i sku sprul pa'i//rin chen rtsal la mngon bstan pas//dug lnga grol mngon gyur nas//bsgrib gnyis ma sbyangs mngon sangs rgyas//de
81b6(kha): yang 'dus pa'i snying po gon//sum rgyud ma nor gang ma ngag ni//sras la rgyal srid gtong ba bzhin//yang ti gdam ngag ma nor pa//bar mchod cing ma smad don//shes rab blo ldan snod la yin//don nyid sngon du phyung ba bstan ka bud d+ha thams cad kyis dgongs
81b7(kha): pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par dka' bgros pa'i don//rin po tshe yon tan kun 'byung gis rgyud las//rigs 'dzin grub pa thob pa'i bka' brtags dngos bstan pa'i le'u ste nyir gsum pa'o// /de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs ma/
82a1(kha): /la Ta kun rigs sems kyi rgyal po yis//lam lnga rdzogs par bka' stsal pa/gcig char rdzogs pa rim pa ni//ngang ba yis la lung ldan lag/ying 'thang 'bul nus tshogs kyi lam//bsam pa bzang pos gser 'dra 'grub/bla ma gnang

82a3(kha): bden pa'i lam zhes bya//don de go nas rgyun du ni/ka 'bral mod dbang thob des bstan phyir//chu zhen bzhin du gnas 'gyur med//bden pa bye ba'i lam zhes bya//stan rgyud nges pa'i gdam ngag 'di ka rang gi thugs la don rdzogs phyir//dbul pos nor chen brnyed pa 'dra// phyi/
82a4(kha): mthar phyin lam zhes bya//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de dag lam lngar bstan pa yang //gang zag drang phyir bstan pa las//don la rim par dbye ru med//snod bcud 'dzom dus kun dga' rdzogs//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' 'gro//
82a5(kha): par don//rin po che son tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//lam lnga dus gcig du rdzogs par bstan pa'i le'u sgo nyer bzhi pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//gdam ngag mngon du bstan pa yis /ka/rig 'dzin sems dpas don rigs nas ka tha ma la rang
82a6(kha): rang rgyu shes pa ni//lung bstan gdam ngag 'di bstan pas ka/rtsal med dang la rig pa 'phro//zhen med rang gsal sman chen gyis//'khor ba 'dzin pa'i nang mad sa la//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gA zhi dung 'dug tshul snang chu lA dang ka lus gnas ngo bo gradas dag dang //gnas skaH
82a7(kha): mtha'i thugs 'bras bu rnams//nga yis bstan gyis legs par nyon//ye nas gnas po'i gzhi nyid ni//gdod nas ma bcos rang byung ste//de'i don gyis kun la khyab//ye shes 'gyur med kun gyi gzhi//de'i 'dug tshul ring le rig/sus kyang ma byas ye nas yod kas le ba la
82b1(kha): kha dog med//rang bzhin gsal ba'i 'dug tshul ni ka rang byung rig pa'i sngang tshul 'di//phyogs zhen gang yang med pa'i phyir/byi nang med pa chos kyi sku gzang thal 'gyur med le nas med//gdong nus rang gyu dag pa'i gru ka rang gi lus la gnas pa'i tshe ka rang gi gzugs
82b2(kha): gi nang na ni//rang byung rig pa gzhi ldan yod//de'i ngo bo gcig du 'dus//mi mngon gsal ba'i bdag nyid can//de nyid lha de lhan pa//rdo rje rang byung 'gyur ba med//de don nges pa'i gdam dag 'di//tshig dang yi ge yul las 'das//rang byung rigs pa skye 'gag med//ston da'i
82b3(kha): /namakha' sbyin med bka gnas skabs dgongs pa mthar phyin pas ka ma btsal ma bsgrubs mya ngan 'das//las 'ang thugs rje da ltar 'dzom//de phyir 'bras bu mngon du gyur//mthar thug phyi rab rnal 'byor kun//des don gdam dgra 'di rnyed pas ka nyams kyi dgongs pa
82b4(kha): gdeng ldan phyir//shos rab grong du mngon sangs rgyas//de nas 'dus pa'i snying po nyon //de dag rtsa ba mdor bsdu na//khyung chen namakha' lding ba bzhin//chos su rig pa sgo snga med//blo yis ma spyad ri dag bral ga yeng shes sapar byin lhun gyis grub khung byathams ca/
82b5(kha): gongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus pa dka' bathos pa'i don//rin po tshe yon tam kun byung gi rgyung meg//sangs rgyas stong dka hwags kyi ga sa drug mngon du bsgyur pa'i yu s+ye kye ba'o// su hU~MhU~M sems dpa' mis ga na
82b6(kha): 'khor gyis don ched du//kun rig chos kyi ston pa la//bde ba'i dgongs don 'di skad gsol ka/rtogs pa'i yis rang gnas//rigs pa byin gyis rlabs pa'i phyir//ma rigs yeng shes ji ltar dbye//phyed pa'i yon tan ji ltar 'byung ka rtogs gzhi mya ngan 'das pa gang //
82b7(kha): de la sangs rgyas ji ltar 'byung //gzhi gsum 'brel pa yod dam med//kun rig ston pas pa gdugasol//de gang zhus pas don gas na nas//'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa//ma rig yi shes dbye ba ni//sems ni mu 'dra thams cad bsdud//ye shes nyi /

83a3(kha): sogs cis// ngosas sus cad nas 'jom /kru mis myur sru skyes mi rug//ji lung sa kyo'i che mo la//ljang bu 'du ba gas nus//nga bzhin sems kyi sangs sa rgya//rgan tho ga thogs pa las//nyan mongs pag chags gnod sa drug
83a4(kha): bral ba'i chos nyid la//ye shes zhes ni de la byaka sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' dgros pa'i don//rin po me yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las kam rig ye shes dbye ba'i le'u ste nyer drug pa'o///de yang rdo rje sems
83a5(kha): sh+tsa na//shar skye ma ni yon tan ni//sangs rgyas dmyal ba man chad druk+ta mA bcos rdzun grub rgyal du med//rang byung ste shes bdag med smrag/gas pan mi sbyin mo gos/rang ni chos smra des pa'i dad+mo//mi gnas shes rab pa ro byin//skye 'gag med pa'i snong du jus/

83a7(kha): 'das dang 'dri ste rkyen las byung //sang ma 'dra ste gos pa med//de bzhin yang ti'i chos 'di la/'a khor 'das dge skyon mgos//nam pa'i dang la g.yos med bzhin//chos nyid rtog pa'i ngang la ni//dus gsum 'gyur med kun tu bzang //skye dang bral ba'i byang chub la/las kyi/
83b1(kha): rgyu 'bras med pa'i phyir ka/ma byung ma skyes chos la skuka tshig gi rkyen gyi mthar mi gnas//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//yang dag don la bdag med pas//rnam par mi rtog lhun gyis grub ka bya ba tsa la mi mnga' rtogs pa'i lam ka ston pa phyogs lhung yul dang bra la//sod par
