84a5(kha): de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//da lta gang na gnas pa ni ka/lung ldan nges don rtogs par bya//rgyu 'bras ltos pas zhen pa med/ka/ma tshor yid la byed pa med//btang snyoms steng po'i mun pa sangs//gol med na mkha' lta bu la/ka/zhen med rang byung chos skyugs/
84a6(kha): de 'dus rig pa'i snying po gsal//de rtogs rnam pa bcu gcig ldan/dngos po med po'i bya ba bting ka/de nyid rtogs pa mngon gyur yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//bA sa~M s+ye shi'i dus byung tshe//ye nas dag pa'i rang bzhin phyir//de cha skang tsog pa nyid mo//
84a7(kha): gzhi ga nas 'khrul med bya ba btang //dbang pos gzugs ni yul med phyir//rtogs tshu la rang bzhin dag pa la/ka/grub mtha'i blo bya ba btang //de ci rjes dran drug dang ni//'bros bu yon tan lnga ldan phyir//de tshe skad cig de nyid la/sangs rgyas kha nges par 'gro/
84b1(kha): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las/sangs rgyas kyi byung tshul bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer dgu pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//gzhi gsuM
84b2(kha): 'brel ba yod med kyis//'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa//nges don chos kyi sku dang ni ka 'khor gzhi lung ma bstan dang ni//ge nas skabs mthar thugs gnyi ka yis//'brel pa med pa'i 'khor ba stongs//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyin//rang bzhin lus dang chos sku
84b3(kha): gnyis/ka/gnas skabs 'brel pa mthar bug yod//de phyir lung ldan phrang dus na//thig le ye shes rtog phyiro//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gnyis ka'i gzhi dang lung ma bstan//gnas skabs yod la mthar thug med//de phyir gti mug sang dus na//chos skur cig
84b4(kha): du shes pas mchog/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' 'gros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//gzhi gsum 'brel pa sod med bstan dri'i leru s+thA sum+b+ha smra ba'o///de nas 'dus /
84b5(kha): snying po yis//kun tu bzang la 'di skad gsol//gol med chos dbyings gang gis bya//yul du ma bral rig baM gang ka/de gnyis dus mnyam lta ba gang //lta ba mdor ni ji ltar bsdu//rigs pa'i rgyal pos bdasha du gsol//de skad zhus pas don gsan nas//snang ba tshas phyi/
84b6(kha): bka' stsal pa/yul gyi rtogs pa gsum bral ba'i//snang ba ma bcos chos nyid la//rig pa ma smad se shes ni//bes rig byed na sku dang dbyings//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//steng gi chos dbyings chos skur cig rnakhar du ye shes sgyu ma'i dbyings//'og na snang brdzun/
84b7(kha): pa'i skye //gcigs pa dang sa 'dzin na//gal ba med de kun gyi dbyis/de ga sdang ba'i chos rnams la/mi shegs mi chung bral sa//rdo rdzam 'gags rang ba yim pa/rig pa la dran dgyis na 'phyog /gang ra 'dru sa gcig la yang kam-i du
85a1(kha): @kha chos dbyings yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//yul 'di med pa'i don shes kyang kangad dung bag chags cha 'dra yis//chu zla smig sgyu du ba bzhin//'od zer mun ne me long tsam//don las shes med rig pa de//snang yang snod med rtogs pa yis//chos sku
85a2(kha): 'byo ba namakha'i klong //srini dang men gyis ci zhig bya//chos kyi dbyingasu de la gtags//'khrul pa'i rtog pas gzhi mi 'gyur//btsal ba'i bsam pas dbyings mi spyod//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po/
85a3(kha): /tshe yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//snang ba ma bcos chos kyi dbyingasaka/gtan la phab pa'i le'u ste so gcig pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun rig chos kyi rgyal po yis//yul meng rig pa bka' stsal pa/lung ldan bla ma'i
85a4(kha): gdam ngag 'di ka ma rigs 'khrul pa phyi yod//rang rig ye shes nang na yod//phyi nang bar du na mkha' dbyed//sems rigs phyed pas rang byung rtogs//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//sprin med na mkha' nyi shar 'bra zhi na//yul med dran pa'i rig pa de ka zhen med rang
85a5(kha): rig gA mthong ngo //rig pa shes pas ma rigri sa ngas kA rig pa'i klong du chos nyid dran//rang byung klong na yeng shes shar//rig pa'i ngang la chos nyid zhog/de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de tshe kun bzang chos drug gis//gzhi gnas ma rigs drug bcom ste//nyug tsam
85a6(kha): nyid ni tshig tsam stongs//rig tsam byung na ribs 'gribs brlags//sal gyis byung bas mun song sangs//khal gyi byung bas 'thibs pa med//sgro skur bral bsam ma rig dag/'gyur ba med pas na mkha' 'dra//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//'khrul pa'i ma
85a7(kha): rig log bcom nas//lung ma bstan par rang rlags stongs//bem rig phyed pas gzhi dang drad//de phyir ma rig mun pa sangs//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud lnga
85b1(kha): sral dang breb med kyi rig pa ngos bzung bar bsnun pa'i le ru ste so gnyis pa'o//de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la/dus sanyam ba bka' stsal pa//rang byung na nyid kyi stsu/mya rkyenas skyes yang dag yod//yin min rloms
85b2(kha): ma bcos pa//'dus ma byas pa'i nam mkha' 'dra//de'i rtogs tshul yis de//kun bzang gzhi nyid gdod nas ni//rang rig chos sku yin par rtogs//ri bo ta la'i rtse mo 'dra//de yis de nyid mtshon pa'i dpe//mtha' dbus med pas na mkha' 'dra//rnam rtog
85b3(kha): 'joms pa rdo rje 'draka/skye ba med pa a dang 'dra//'dra ba'i phyir na don ma yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gtan tshig nges pa gang yin pa ka rang byung 'khor bar 'khyams ma myong //de rtogs da lta'i dus 'di ru//mtha' bral mngon gyur ba den pa
85b4(kha): @mthong //de'i bzlog chos nges pa dang //mthun pa yin thun gnyis logs pa//gol sgrib skyon gis ma gos pa//sems las ma byung chos skur gsungs//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//rang gi sems nyid nges pa de ka gang dang 'dra med yin pa med//yin ni
85b5(kha): da lta'i shes pa la//de don kun la gnas pa'i phyir kau 'char nub bral ba med pa'i mchog/la bzlos slang med do shes pas//skyon med thad med par dag/de phyir dus 'dir 'bras bu 'grub//phyi mar sangs rgyas smos ci dgos//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po
85b6(kha): nyon Ta chos dbyings chos sku gcig gi ngang //rang byung rig pa'i ye shes de ka nyi zla gzas zin dpe bzhin du//don kun dus gcig yon tan rdzogasaka/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po
86a1(kha): /g.yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//chos kyi sku dang dbyings dus mnyam par bstan pa'i le'u ste so gsum pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun rig rgyal pos bka' stsal pa/rig pa chos skur lta shes na//kun pa
86a2(kha): khyakhya pa'i rig pa yis//'dus ma byas pa'i don rtogs nas//mtha' bral rigapa gcig yin te//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//kun gyis mnyam nyid rgyal ba'i thugs//ma lus sems can kun la gnas//chos sku khyab pas brdal ba'i sku//mtha' dadus med
86a3(kha): pas namakha' 'dra//rig pa sa smad gnyug sar gnas//dmigs 'dzin med la ngang gis gsal//dngos por med la shes pa yod//namakha' nyi ma sha ba 'dra//'dus ma byas pa'i ye shes kyis//rten 'chang med pa'i nyon mongs salaka/'byung ba snga la dag ma
86a4(kha): lus su mi 'gyur 'jom pas rdo rje 'dra/ka/bla mas don bstan rnal 'byor pas//the tsom med par rang sems rtogs//thams cad cig du rdzogs par shes//ma rig log pa'i mig 'byed 'dra ka ye shes rig pa 'di nyid ni//yod med dbus kyi mtha' bral bas//sang de
86a5(kha): sa kho 'pho 'gyur med/ka/ka shi'i ras kyi rin chen 'dra//de sang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de dag ngo bo gcig 'dus pa//da lta'i shes pa 'di nyid la/ka/yid ches gdengs ldan ma rig sangs//rang byung klong dang ye shes rgyas//rig pa'i ngangal chos sku zhog/thaMd
86a6(kha): kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don/rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las/lta ba mdor bsdus te bstan pa'i le'u ste so bzhi pa'o///de nas rdo rje sems dpa' yis//dang ldan 'khor gyi don ched ngu k+Sha kun rig chos kyi
86a7(kha): /rgyal lo lag bden pa'i dgongs don 'di skad gsol/rang gi rig pa ros zin dang //gos su myong po'i bka' brtags kyis ni an dag rin gyi dbye bo gang //chos su'i ngos 'di'i ltar spad/chos sku smad pa'i yon tan gang ka drug gnyis mtshan po'i nyams pa ni
86b1(kha): man ngag gol ba'i 'ad la zlos dang //nyams myong bka' brtags sdus pa ni//kun rig ston pas bsha du gsol//de skad zhus pas don gsan nas//'dus pa'i tshogs la bka' stsal pa//'khor 'das par de'i gzhi nyid ni//skye shi gnyis kyi bar do la//chos sku
86b2(kha): gtan la dbab pa'i phyir//spyi'i kun gzhi'i gzhi cha la//sbyor ba rkyen gyis snga phyir sgribs//lung ldan nges pa'i gdam ngag gis//don gyi kun gzhi ngos bzung phyir //ra shan phye kha ni ngo spyod de//zung bzhi ya bdun don shes bya/ de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//snga phyi/
86b3(kha): bag chags zhes bya ba//bsam pa ma 'khrul dngos po 'khrul//sngar gyi dge sdig ci byas dran//mi shi'i kha dog dran pa 'dra ka/phyis sis nyon mongs blo bur bkang sdod ci bya mno ba ni//dngos po ma 'khrul bsam pa 'khrul//thag pa sbrul du 'khrul pa 'dra//
86b4(kha): sul sems 'dzin pa'i rtog pa ni//bsam dngos gnyis ka 'khrul pa'i phyir//med las yod rtog snga phyi 'dres//ri bong rA la rtog spyod 'dra ka skye bo 'phral gyi bya ba kunaka/gnyis ka ma 'khrul don la rtogs//gang byed lta bas ma zin phyir//zla ba dkar
86b5(kha): kyang mun yod 'dra//zung bzhi zhes ni de la bya/ka/de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//bla ma des lung ldan pa dang //dad ldan shes rab can phrad dus//dngos po ma 'khrul rig pa la//bsam pa ma 'khrul ye shes te//gnyis ka ma 'khrul rang rig sku//lta ba gdengs
86b6(kha): zin lhun gyis grub//de don cig shes mngon sangs rgyas//yab bdun zhes ni de la bya//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//don gyi dbye ba bstan pa'i le'u ste/
86b7(kha): so lnga pa'o///de nas kun rig ston pa yis//rang byung rig pa'i ye shes ni//chos sku ngo sprod bka' stsal pa//shan dbye ngos bzung la bzlas te//rnal 'byor pa'i shin phye ba//sngar gyi bgo chags zhes bya ba//sngon byas da ltar gang
87a1(kha): /dran pa//yul la bzung 'dzin bltas pa la ka dam par zhen nas kun tu btag/de phyir 'khor ba'i sa bon yin//byis kyis nyon mongs zhes bya ba ka ma rig rgyud la rgyur byung sems//de ltas kun bzhi smin pa las//de skyed tshogs brgyad bde sdug
87a2(kha): byung snga phyi gnyis 'dus sgo lnga'i yul//sna tshogs spros pa'i rtog pa da//dwe la zhen phyir chos brgyad byung //de'i nyes pas 'khor ba smin//lung ldan bla'i gdam ngag gis//rang rig ye shes ngos bzung na//da lta'i lus sems 'di nyid la//rten bcas
87a3(kha): gzhi ldan yod pa'i phyir//'byung ba lnga'i dngas ma ni//snying ni chos kyi 'khor lo la//rten bcas gzugs kyi nang na ni//tsit+ta rin chen gzhal yas khang //gur phub 'dra ba'i nang shes naka chos sku gzhi ldan yod pa deka yid kyi khwa dang dung ka yis/
87a4(kha): ma rig snga phyi'i ma rgyas sgribs//de nyid mthong bar bya ba'i phyir//bla ma nges pa'i gdam ngag gis//kun bzang rig pa'i mtshon rnon des//ma rig pa brgya rtsa nas mchod//snga phyi ngan rtog med par ni//rig gi sang de le wa la/ka/da lta'i shes pa 'di nyid la//chos
87a5(kha): sku zhi ldan ngos bzung ngo //de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon ka dbor na mig gi 'bras bu bzhin//rig ge ba la dran pas baM zin ka ye re ba la shes pa dal//rang rig zhen med gdeng gis gsal ka sprin med ston ka'i na mkha' bzhin ka sang de ba la mtha' dbus med/
87a6(kha): gang gi mno bsam ma gtang phyir//khyid rmugs spros pa gang yang med//nyi ma gza' bral lhang de bzhin//sa le ba la 'gyur ba med//bde ba mi rtog rgyun du gnas ka de phyir rang byung 'gyur ba med//g.ya' med me long byang lha bzhin//wa ye ba la dngos po med//de
87a7(kha): tsam nyid na chos kyi dbyings ka gzhi ya yul med rtogs pas cho ga/de dag dbye ba rnams bzhi yang //ma rig ye shes chos sku gcig/rang rig rnyog ma med pa'i phyir//nor bu chu dang dpe bzhin no//rang byung chos sku gsal ba yin//rang rig ye shes sgra
87b1(kha): /lpags pa/dpe mi til gyis mang me 'dra//shes pa ma sgrings don la brjod//sdang ba ma bcos chos dbyings dang //'bras bu longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sduka spangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par blag tu thos pa'i

87b6(kha): gyur kyang //gol med ye shes chos skur gnas//u dpal rnyed pas lha mig rjong //de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//sangs rgyas dgongs pa mi g.yo ba//dus gsum mi g.yo 'phel 'grib med//shes pa mi g.yo rgya mtsho 'dra//mnyam nyid don la rkyen gyi
87b7(kha): tshugs//gsal dang mi rtogs dus mnyam pa//'bras bu rang byung chos sku yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de dag rtsa ba mdor bsdu ba//yid la chos sku wal gyis dran//de'i ngang la cog gis zhog/yul la 'dzin med rtog pa spang ka rang rig

96a1(kha): /nyid stong pa'i ngang du sangs//brjed dran nor ni brnyed pa 'dra//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//mi mngal du gnas dus na//'chi kar dran pa mun song dang ka kha rgya la dang ma dran brjed pa'i dus//gangam ngag dran pa med dang ni//gzhan gyi bar ni mchod par//de tshe dran rnyed/
96a2(kha): lhag mthong sha ra/de ltar nyam su myong ba'i phyir//bar dor mngon sangs rgyas par nges//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//gnyid yeng shes klong thims du bstan pa'i le'u/
96a3(kha): steng bzhi pa ///kun rig stor pas yang gsungs pa//gti mug lang grom rkyen thub pa//ma rig shes bzhin lung ma bstad//gang sa mi sems gti mug sems//lung bstaran sgrol mi don bstan pas//rang rig chos sku yid la bstar//ga ni dang brang bas mun/
96a4(kha): brlag/'bras bu chos sku thams cad mkhyen//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//zhe sdang 'ang grol rkyen thub pa//yul ngan kha dog sla ba la//zhe sdang sems 'khrugs bzod pa nyams //mi bsam yul gyi rjes mi bcad//de dus rtag chad mtha' bral nyams//bdag gzhan gzhi/
96a5(kha): la grub pa med//'bras bu rang rig sprul par shar//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//nga rgyal reng byed rkyen thub pa//gzhan dag ldems pas smra ba la//nga bden mongs pa ched du byed//gang yang don med don dran pas//de nyid rig pa'i shos pa nyams//tshig gi tshe chung gzhi la med//
96a6(kha): /don ldan nga rgyal chos skur 'char//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//phra dog rang grol rkyen thub pa//gzhan gyi phun sum tshogs pa la//g.yo rgyu thabs kyis gzhan la smod//bdag gzhan med shes chos kyi sku//phra dog ye shes nyam su myong //de tsam nyid ni bdag gzhan med//g.yo
96a7(kha): rgyu'i don gis longs spyod brgyan//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//dngos po ma 'khrug 'ang lugs la//rkyen gyis nyon mongs gang skyes kyang //da dus chos sku nyam su myong //de ma yul dang 'brel ba meng//dpyir rkyen thub rtsi mi dgos//sangs rgyas tha~Md kyi dgo sa
96b1(kha): chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' 'gros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//dug lnga rang grol du bstan pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun rig ston pas yang gsungs pa//rig 'dzin sems dpa' sprub
96b2(kha): sku//sangs rgyas dgongs pa nges pa'i don//thugs nas san+d+ha rgyud pa yis//snod ldan gangam dag ston brtan kyang // 'dzin bstan par gyur kyang //swa mi rtog cam bsgol ba//che 'bras mi lus tsam thob nas//'khor ba mi ldog bden pa mthong //de bas mig
96b3(kha): gtan goms na//yus kyi 'byung ba zhig pa'i dus//lha'i rig 'dzin mngon thob nas//dus de nyid du mngon sangs rgyas//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//theg le nyag cig mngon rtogs pa'i//rnam smin lus dang ma bral bar//thog ma med par sangs rgyas tshe//skad cig
96b4(kha): tsam la ma rigs su//lus med rig pa sa shes rgyas//rang byung thugs la skye tsA me //khams cad mkhyen pa'i le gas ldan//dag par nyi sa'i snying po 'dra//de sang dus pa'i snying po con//khe nas dag zer gzhi nyi ni/chos sra ba sbed don shes na//chos su gzugs
96b5(kha): sunam pa gnyisas so sor snang ba ma yin te//chos kyi skul gzugs su 'am/pe na smras dang dam bzhin no//rin chen lus nang chos srul/sdang ba gzung ma yongs spyod sku//rdzang gas bcu sangs pas mthong /las dag tu mi kud dang ga/gzugs can du
96b6(kha): sa phyogs// gi tshangs lenas song//gas nga seM po dzi me //de da rdo rje sems dpa' nyon/gas smras sa sa rgyas la//sems can med byi don ma gos/ song rdzogs kyis/ rtsom pas brdzus sa dgo ga
96b7(kha): ga tan la gyu ga pa ma thu ga/dus gsum grul das po 'gyu /maung ga sa na ming //da med nga byung gi mra/migs / ga zusas na ga na cwa cug
97a1(kha): ston//thabs la mi rten yon tan rdzogs//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las/sring ba bar ro la skye ba phyis ma yin par sangs rgyas bstan pa'i le'u ste pa
97a2(kha): drugs///dem na ris son pa la//rtswa ba brgya byin don sa pa/ nyid rdzog pa nas 'gyu bas//sa ngos pa sbyi dka' stsal pa/rgyo snong grag su gas mi dgos//dus pa'i dan la ka skal pa/sa su gdan sa dag smrag// spyod chu
97a3(kha): yis sangs mi rgya/ma btsal lhun gyis grub pa la//yon tan bcu yis bsgrub du med//kun bzang chos pas nga sde'i phyir//mdo rgyur kun gyi phyi mor ba shang //thos pag+h+nAM zhes rab skyed du med//sangs rgyas sems can las ma byung byi//rgyud lung nan drag grog tu snyi das/ n+doH
97a4(kha): gzhi nyi nga nyid yin kang la dang po rgyu med pas ka tha ma rkyen gyis gzhig du med ke nga la bsgrubs pa'i thabs med pas//dngos grub gzhan nas ro bo med//nga la rten pa'i tshigs med pos//rtogs pa'i spro bsam yul las 'das//ngo la gong na rjes med pas//ye shes kun les
97a5(kha): khyad par 'phags//nga la 'og na 'bangs med phyir//snying rje chen po spyir mi mdzad//nga nyid yis rang bzhin las//thabs kyi ngo bo dbyings na gsal//nyi ma'i snying po rang shar bas//'byung ba lnga la khyab pa bzhin//chos kun rigs pa dngos med pas//btsal ba med par ye gnas phyir
97a6(kha): namakha' lta bu rang bsal bas//bag chags skyon gyis bsgyur du med ka rin chen lta bu'i yon tan la//chos sku'i gzugs brnyan gzugs sku 'byung //phyi nang med par yas sku//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' 'gros pa'i don//rin
97a7(kha): po ma yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//lha dbang gis the tsomo gyis gags pasal ba'i le'u steng bdun pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i tshogs rnams la//kun rig sten pas bka' stsal pa/lung ldan nges pa'i rgyud chen ni//dang ldan kun la 'di yod na/ smin
97b1(kha): /ga mas sos sa/kAn sug ga ga la ga/3/ skyed da/ smra so/ nas khrag du sa rgyas /
97b2(kha): mas pa nga kun/'a byung tshus klu 'phrul sa shes//des don 'di tshigs ras spyo / sa pabyu nga sang byed/ byed ba sgra spyod/ sa nas rtson/ga gyu la na sems pam g+sa/ m+b+hi sa sa rgyas ga /

97b6(kha): rlung ba'i skyon du ni yA //sa sku sems can ston khya pa//thugs rje'i rgyun motshol 'char//yo gas seng song ma yin pas//mang pa'i bskur ba gang la gang ga/don la sangs rgyas sems can med//gzhi shes 'drim med cig pus tshog//de ying 'dus pa'i skye
97b7(kha): ma ga sa la bya kyim da pa/ smin pa kyis nga go la ga nga 'i ga yo pa gal tshan ni yong sa gras/yum dag nyin pan gyis/du byas chung ba dog gapas ldang na
98a1(kha): gyi nang drung khams skye mched kun//nam gyis gzugs skur blos bsgyur kyang //yul gyi 'brel ba mchod phyir/sangs rgyas mi 'byung gin tu go la//ru sbal sbrul gos byeng badra//hrI snod mi 'gyur bcung 'gyur zer//gzhi la 'byung ba med pa'i phyim//2dzan/
98a2(kha): lnga dang 'od lnga ni//gzhi mi shes la snang ba las//don ldan kun la gdod nas med//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gcig lo yon tan kun snang te//gang yang mi 'gal chos sku ni//rang rang mthongs snang so so la//bzang rtog gzugs sku rnam gnyis 'byung //ngan rtog 'gro drug/
98a3(kha): ma rtswa ga gni la gnyig med las byung /byung yang ben gnang gnyig med/de phyir 'khor 'das ma rig chos//dengang 'dus pa'i snyi mo nyon//'khor gyis bcu'i sems dpa' yis//rang la sod pa ma rtogs pa/'gro dan ched du rtsol ba byed// shes sgyu mang stsas myo
98a4(kha): 'dod/glen pa lnga khang lta ba bzhin//snyems gongs b+he dang sems bskyed dang //sku dog 'od dang dpe byad dang //zhing khams brgyan pa sngon du 'dod//sgyu ma'i longs sku bzang por mthong //chos sku ma shes yul du bzung ka de phyir shes bya'i sgrings pas sgribs//gnas lugs so/
98a5(kha): /sku'i sangs rgyas min//de yang 'khor rnams nga nyon cig/ye nas gnas pa'i sems nyid de//nyan thos rang rgyal las dag 'khor//shes rab mnan la tshogs bsags pas//me longs bzhi la rang byed 'dzin//de ni sbrul skur mthong ba'i tshe//chos sku'i gzugs men ma shes phyir/
98a6(kha): ngan rtogs sbyangs phyir mos gus byed//chos skur ma shes thub drug mthong /bag nya la nyon mongs sgrib pa yod//de phyir ye yod bzhi ma shes//mig bsgyur chu mthong snyegs pa 'dra/ka/de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//'gro drug sems can ma lus kun//dug lnga 'ang las skyes tsam na na
98a7(kha): chos ska mdogs gti mug pas//med pa'i yul las chags sdang skyes//'khor ba'i mtha' la zad pa med//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//lha'i bu sems kyi rtog pa
98b1(kha): bsal ba'i le'u steng dgu pa'o///de nas kun rig rgyal po yig//myud bdag rdo rje sems dpa' la/'ang rigi rtog don 'di skad gsol//kun babang gang ma dag 'di logs pas//bdag don rtogs pa 'di lta shar//yul dang sems ni cir snang yang //rtsub pa
98b2(kha): yid kyis btags pa las//don la med par rang yid ches//yung ldan gdam ngag zin tsa ma na//gang yang mi 'gal chos skur shes//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//ji bzhin pa la don gyis zin//ma bcos pa la dran pa gsal//yid ches don la 'gyur ba med ka ma 'gags pa la/
98b3(kha): gyu /thal mang kya sa gnas/shes pags pa la sam byu/ na/ ba sa 'i nga c+doMs na kyi shA k+sha sa ga'i sa d+hA ste/

98b5(kha): nyid las//bzang por song ba med pa'i phyir ka sgro mo dang mi sgom tha snyad bral//phyi rgod gnyis kyang rang 'byung klong ka/ma rig med pas yo shes go//de phyir rang byung shes rab gsal//gzhan dang langs pa med pa'i phyir//de dang mi bad thams cad kun//don la'ang rigis chos sku
98b6(kha): rig/gsal dang mi gsal gang yang rung //'dzin med rang byung chos sku la//dran dang ma dran gang shar yang //gang gis gnyen po brten mi dgos//gang yang chos skur shes pa'i phyir//yengs dang ma g.yengs gdod nas med//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//de lta bu'i sems can

99a6(kha): ga chags dran mi srid//'jig rten mchod do nyan ba bral//sa ni lnga bskyangs dga' la//rten ni bsam gtan rdo rje 'dra//rgyal ni bying rgod rkyen mi 'byung //dgongs pa'i don la 'byung 'jug med//sa ni drug pa 'on gyur la//rten ni shes rab sran dang 'dra//skyed ni med pa'i lam sa tsun
99a7(kha): da rig pa chos skur mkhyen//sa ni bdun pa ring song la//rten ni dge bi'i'o gshed gnyen 'dra//rtsal ni ba sdug bsngal sman du 'gyur//'khor ba'i skyon gyis gos pa med//sa ni brgyad pa mi gsol//rten ni smon lam rin chen 'dra//shes byi'i grib pa rtsa nas dag/dam par lta ba gang la/
99b1(kha): /med dbyu ba legs pa'i blo gros la//rten ni stobs me nyi ma 'dra//rgya la ni log pa dpyis sgrol//sangs rgyas sa la nges par 'gro/rang sams sa ni bcu pa la//rten ni se shes dgyes dang 'dra//rgyal ni sgrib dag thaMd mkhyen//de las bgrod med sangs rgyas nyid//de yang 'du smra//

99b3(kha): rgyud las//sa bcu rdzogs par bstan pa'i le'u ste re gcig pa'o///'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa'i don//des don ma nor 'di rtogs pas//gdas ngag khyad par can du 'dus//dpe don rtag dang sum kar 'dus
99b4(kha): de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//lta ba 'pho 'gyur med pa la//skye 'gag med pa'i rig pa shar//ma bcos rang lugs spyod pa dang kau/re dogs med pa'i 'bras bur gnas//kun tu bzang po'i dgongs par gnas//dpe ni nyi ma'i snying po la//'dzam gling bye brag phrag rgya
99b5(kha): 'char nub ldog par grangs yang mang //nyi ma 'di nyid cig pur 'dus//de bzhin sangs rgyas thams cad kyang //mtshan dang sku mdog phyag mtshan sogaso//che ba'i yon tan bsam las kyang //rigs lnga'i sangs rgyas dgongs par 'dus//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon
99b6(kha): rang byung rig pa skye med 'di//mi 'gyur 'gag med rang gsal ba//ye nas gnas pa'i don shes phyir//de yang rgyun du mi 'bral phyir//de ni rigs kyi dgongs pa yin ka yang dag sangs rgyas dgongs pa'i don//mi 'gyur mi shig rdo rje'i sku ka chos nyid ma bcos ma bskrad

100a1(kha): sun khyab pas//ma byung ma skyes da lta nyid//'phel 'grib med par rang gsal ba//pad+ma rigs kyi dgongs par 'dus ka lus kyi nang na rang rig pa//chos sku gzhi ldan rig pa las//de ru ma 'dus gang yang med//de phyir mes rgyun mi 'chad//las kyi rigs

100b1(kha): ming yang med//rtog pa lha ru gnas gyur phyir//'khrul pa'i snang ba rdul tsam med//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//nang gi sprul pa rnam gsum ni//gsung gi sbrus su 'byung ba ni//mdzad pa bcu gnyis tshul du ne//rashA kya thub pa lha mi'i ston pa de//mdo sde du//

100b3(kha): bo la Ta byi rgyud tan+t+ra mang du gsungs//de ltar tshogs bsags sgrib sbyangs phyir//dam tshig ye shes mchod la sogs//gzhung du byas nas dngos grub re ka thun mongs sangs rgyas 'byung bar 'dod //nga'i thugs kyi sgrol pa las//gzhi nyid rtogs pa'i
100b4(kha): sangs rgyas ni ka sku ni rang rig chos kyi sku//gsung ni brjod med tshangs pa'i gsungs//thugs ni thig le nyag cig go//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//la zlos sprul sku rnams gsum ni//sku'i sprul pa rang lugs la//rtogs ldan lus su ye shes yod//
100b5(kha): rin chen ga 'ur bcug pa bzhin//lus kyi nang na chos sku gnas//gsang ba'i gsung ni brjod pa yin//don ldan tshig du smra mi shes//de don brjod pas brjod med phyir//smras kyang rang byung nyid kyi gsung //gsang ba'i thugs ni rang byung la//bem rig shan byed//
100b6(kha): chos kyi sku//de yang yid ches gdeng gis zin//de don rgyun du skye 'gag med//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//phyi nang dgu ru phye ba yang //gdul bya'i 'khor la tha dad pas//don la dbyer med cig gi dang ka rtsa ba chos skur rtog par gyis//sangs rgyas thad kyi dgos
100b7(kha): pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bkar 'gros pa'i don//rin 'i tshe son tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//chos kyi sku'i sprul pa gzugs su rnam gnyis kyi gzhan don byed par bstan pa'i le'u ste re gsum pa'o////de nas 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams yang
101a1(kha): 'bras bu lhun grub bka' stsal pa//be na sa'i shing thog bzhin//rgyu rkyen 'bras bu tha dang med//dA ni bzhi lam 'bras bu yang //dza ba cig phyir dbyer med dukA/ sbyi rin chen lhun rdzogs yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyan//lung ldan dus
101a2(kha): pa'i gdam ngag gri/rtog pa gzhag pas mngon sangs rgyas ka ma rig med pa'i ye shes la ka/skye ba'i phung po len mi srid//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gong du bstan pa'i gzhi nyid dang //nyam su blang ba'i nyams dang ni ka 'bras bu mthar thugs yin pa'i
101a3(kha): phyi/lam nyid lam la lam nyid gzhi//de nyid cig shes 'bras bu mchog/de nyid rgyu 'bras ro gcig pa'i//dman pa'i lus la sangs rgyas yin//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//de ltar rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor bas//dus 'dir chos nyid bden pa ma
101a4(kha): spyan sgra rims kyis cig par du//sngar ma mthong ba'i zhing khams gsum//ye shes mig gis dus cig mthong //lus dang mya bral rig pa la//'char nub med pa'i ye shes shar//ma bsam pa'i lhag mthong 'a/ma brjod pa'i gzugs kyis zin//ma thos pa'i bdung
101a5(kha): tse myong ka ma bsgrub pa'i 'bras bu thob//de yang rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//rang nyid gar 'dug chos kyi dbyings//su dang 'grogs kyang rgyal ba'i sras//gang du 'gro ba byang chub lamaka /thun mongs dngos grub ci bsam 'grub//phyi mar sangs rgyas smos ci dgos//
101a6(kha): rdzogs pa chen po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//gzhi lam gcig pa'i 'bras bu bstan pa'i le'u ste re bzhi pa'o///de nas kun rig
101a7(kha): rgyal po yis//'dus pa'i 'khor la rgyud gtad pa//mthar thug chos nyid lhun rdzogs 'dika/thog ma'i chos sku rtog pa ni//rig 'dzin grub pa thob pa la ka bka' rtags gdam ngag ma tshad don// lta mi brjed gzung thob pa//'khor 'das ma rig lte ba yis
101b1(kha): se nas chos nyid mang chas la//nges pa'i byin brlabs rig pa yis//bstan pa chos sku bani ldan pas//kun gzhi gzhi cha rang gas la la//rang rig chos su ngo spyang pas/ma rig kun rtags mun pa sangs//rang gsal chos su 'gyur ba med//byi nang rang ga
101b2(kha): lha spyod bstan//yul dang 'brel chod nyid gol sgrib med//rkyen gyis mi tshugs chos nyid grol//de phyir 'bral med rgyun du gnas//'bras bu mngon gyur par do la//khar ma chod par sangs rgya ba ka sku gsum dus gcig rang la rdzogs//de phyir zhi rtogs
101b3(kha): 'gyur ba med//yang dag rig pa'i 'khor don ni//skal ldan 'dus pa'i khyed kyis kyang //des don rgyud 'di yid la chongs//de yang 'khor gyi snying po nyon//tshul chos byed cing slob dpon slong//nor 'phangs chos 'dod thabs//sa bsgrub//nges don
101b4(kha): bka' sgros rgyud chen 'di//nam yang bstan par ma gsung so//bstan pa'i 'jig ka'i dus 'di ruka yang ti snying po'i chos 'di 'byung //de tshe skal ldan 'ga' 'ga' la//des don rgyud 'di gtad pA lusa?//dang med sna tshogs spyod yul min//
101b5(kha): kun dang nges pa de ltar zhog/de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gang gi dang ldan rgyu du 'di la//mos shing gus pa byed pa'i mo//bka' dgu sngos nas 'di 'tshol ba/khal srim bya chos 'di bstan//snying po'i nang na yang snying 'di//gdam ngag ma chad
101b6(kha): des lung rgyud//bde gshegs rnams kyi thugs dam yin//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//gcig dang gnyis la ston srid na//bden pa'i 'tshams las mi 'da' bar//mkha' 'gro chos skyong mchod byas na//gsang thub shes rab ldan pa la/
101b7(kha): tshig tu po don 'drim gdam dag bstan//de dang mi ldan blo ngan ni ka bla ma lung ldan skyon gyis 'brid//thabs kyi chos 'phel bzod du 'tshong //bla ma smod cing rang che 'byin//bka tshun byas nas mkhas su re//slob dpon gsang bas
102a1(kha): chos sna tshogs byed//babs kyis no 'chol skyon sna tshogs//ga ba dag med pa gsang brlung //gdu rgyun gyis dan chung rnams//rang gi rgyas la skyon song d+hi ma/gzhan la grung 'ams suthu tshug byed/byo chang ma 'das
102a2(kha): chung byer//daphyir ngogs pos mi rnams bsdu//byim dadamyal banges par gro/de 'dra'i dam nyams mthong thos nas//demi sang dra par mi spyong//de sang 'dus pa'i stong po nyin//dasti'i ngas nyams me ngan la/slob ma byas nas chos bstanam/ smye
102a3(kha): ma /ma sa pan a sbya //snang ga da rlos/cug/so kyi lta drug/rgyasod na sa napa da/sems pa grus pa med/mas sus kyang ma /
102a4(kha): nga mi la dru/'jig rten nod tshal dang brdag stongs//slobs sa shing mi rnams 'bya//tse kun la ni sdang 'byung //ngas 'dus pa'i snying po con//gzhan gyi yan pa ma yin te//thugs kyi dam tshig thams pa yin//cibs ma ma krung ga/sunas
102a5(kha): grob na gyi sa 'gyur/khyimardo rje dmyal byu /gas kye 'gyong par 'ang no//ga rgyal pas pa rnams grangs nas// med ni thum la//khos zlas dpas ston su nas/sa s+hos dus bci bas byu/sangs rgyas pa
102a6(kha): kyi dgongs pa chos sku gcig du 'dus par bka' bgros pa'i don//rin po tshe yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las//rgyud gtad pa'i 'a'u ster lnga //rdzogs pa chen po sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa chos sku cig tu 'dus par bsal
102b1(kha): pa'i don//rin po che yon tan kun 'byung gi rgyud las/d+har+m+ma kA yi sad+ha ba ya/gu ru te tsu dz+nyA na ke tu tsi ta sa ma ya gu h+ya rdzogs so///rgya gar gyi mkhan po bi la mi tra dang /zhu chen gyi lo ts+tsha ba dran pa ye shes kyis bsgyur cing gtan la phab nas/s+yAng ting/
102b2(kha): nge 'dzin la gdams//des sbas/mo rgyal lo'i tsho la gdams//ris sbas blo gros seng ge la gdams/des 'brom ye shes bla ma la gdams//des sras rgyal ba'i shes rab la gdams//des gang dar ma ye shes la gdams//des shangs pa lce chung ba ye shes rgyal
102b3(kha): mtshan la gangs so//snyan rmyang nges pa gdams ngag gi lde mig/po ti sI ru mthing bshog can gyi rgyud rdzogs s+ho///rgya gar skad du//dz+nyA na gu N+ye ri ba ma rat+na u pa de shwa tan+t+ra/nA mu
102b4(kha): skang du/migs gas bsgron ma rin po maman dag kyi rgyud ces byi'o//bcom ldan 'das dpas rdo rje sa chos da la phyanatshan mo//di skang bdag gis thos 'dus cig ma re bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi suM dang /gsung dngagi/thugs men po
102b5(kha): chom ldan 'das rdo rje 'chang gsang sa mchog gi bdag po //sa 'i skang rag min gyi gnas/chos kyi dbyis kyi po drang mapa' 'ongs yangs pa'i gangs mchog ma/ga/nga gnag nang pa'i tsha 'dzin ma snyoms par bzhugs pa'i dus na/de'i chen ga'o sa/
102b6(kha): /rdzad ma dang ltam yin dang// bdun/srin /garang sbyin pa sogs pa rnams kyin //da rdza sems pas sum mogs pa'i ltungs ga cugs ma da kyi'i sems kyis 'acom ldan 'das kyi drung ma bas rgyam pa na
102b7(kha): gsol to//kye bcom ldan 'das rdo rje gsang ba'i bdag po/chos nyid byang chub sems kyi don/bdag cag rnams la ba sha du gsol//ces gsol to//rdo rje 'chang gsang ba'i bdag po des gsungs pa/kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/chos nyid do//
103a1(kha): gyi rnal mi de/ma bcos ma spang rang byung la/tshig dang yi ge brjod pa las 'das/yid la byas mtshon dang bral/brdi'i thabs kyi bstan pa lag/ngag du brjod pa chos nyid men//don dam de la brjod med kyang //bla ma'i thabs kyis ma mchog na//gnas pa de ni rtog/
103a2(kha): mi 'gyur//chos rnams thams cad brda' yis bstan//ye shes gsang ba'i sgron ma rin po che'i rgyud las//gleng ba ni'i le'u ste dang po'o////de nas yang rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags/chos nyid mtshon pas rtogs 'dod
103a3(kha): bas/slob dpon slob ma'i mtshan nyid cig/gsang ba'i bdag pos bsha du gsol//zhes gsol to//gsang ba'i bdag pos bka' stsal pa//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/slob dpon slob ma'i snod bcud des//legs par bshad kyi yid la bzung //bka' ba ma sbyang skyongs
103a4(kha): chung rgyud la mkhas shing man ngag che//thugs rje che la 'dod pa chung //rgyud par ldan la mtshon pa mkhas//dbang rnams thob la ting 'dzin gsal//de rnams ldan pa'i bla ma la//bcu dang ldan par ngas bshad do//bla ma la gus gtong phod che//dad par ldan la brtson 'grus
103a5(kha): chetshe/bla ma mi gsang shes rab ldan//sems bzang go cha 'thug pa dang //rgyud 'jam zhe sdang chung ba dang //'jig rten 'brel pas skyabs chen min//bla ma 'khor ba'i gnyen gcig po//skye shi la sogs sdug bsngal gyis//mthong nas de las grol 'dod pa//de rnams ldan
103a6(kha): pa'i blo ma la//snod dang ldan par shes par gyis//ye shes gsang ba'i sgron ma man ngag gis rgyud las slob dpon slob ma'i mtshan nyid bstan pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de yang rdo rje sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdag zer/
103a7(kha): /dang 'i rgya bye/nga gi sa du mas jigs gas gsus kyi mas dang che gag ga zung sa 'das tu byas pakra bya rnams#
103b1(kha): /khag papas lha'i dbang bskur na//thun mongs dngos grub pa mo 'byung //'jig rten chos la dbang bsgyur ba//rang lus lnga su phyag rgyar shes//skye med cig pa'i dbang bskur bas//rang lus ma skyes dngos gzhi med//sems kyi dkyil 'khor 'od gas pangs//rdul chen
103b2(kha): 'dzab la rag ma lus//dbang thob byin rlabs rtags rnams 'byung //rang sems mthong bas ting 'dzin gsal//byin rlabs dgug gzhug bya mi dgos//thabs dang shes rab bzung du 'brel//bde chen chos skur shes pa na//mtshan ma'i blo yang rang grol//gzung 'dzin thag pa
103b3(kha): pa de ru chod//rig pa stong gsal dbyer med la//gab pa mngon du phyung ba na//rang byung sems dpa' ngo bo nyid//rigs pa 'dzin med thang la phebs//rig ma la sogs rtsol mi dgos//bdag dang bdag gir 'dzin pa zad//'dzin med sems la bcos slad med//ye shes gas
103b4(kha): bsgren ma'i rgyud las//rig pa rtsal gyi dbang gi le'u sku gsum pa'o///de nas yang sems dpa' 'di skad ces gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//rang byung rig pa'i dbang bskur nas//bsgom pa'i rig pa bsha du gsol//gsang ba'i bdag
103b5(kha): sa bka' stsal pa//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/nges pa'i don la mos pa'i//rnal 'byor ba'i don ched du/sems kyi gnag thabs 'di ltar lags/dpe don rtogs dang ting 'dzin nyams//rgyud la btsal thabs bcos pa'i babs//rnam pa bdun du rig
103b6(kha): bya dab yis mtshon pa 'di ltar yidan//bram ze gtsang ma khrus gsol te//rgyal srid kun la dbang bsgyur zhing //lus de sems can thams cad la//byang chub snying po rang bzhin gnas//de don rtogs par bya thabs ni//dmigs pa dag la brten nas su//mi dmigs
103b7(kha): pa ni rab tu skye//ma zin zin phyir gnang bani bsgoms//zin pa brtan la goms par bya//'dzin rangags la brten mi 'cha'//mang bal rig pa rjen pa gzhago//lta ba'i bogs dbyung la zlas pas//rang sem so dag pa chos sku yin//rang byung legs las 'das pa//
104a1(kha): @/gi gnas lhag mthong zung du 'brel//rig pa yul gyis mi gnod pas//dper na dngul chu bo ba 'dra//rtags ni lus ngag yid gsum la//lus ni 'dar zhing sdug pa dang //'phags dang rgyug pa'i las gnyis byed//ga ni rgod cing ngu ba dang //kha nas sgra skad snga
104a2(kha): tshogs 'don//sems ni 'dzin med btang dka' bas//phyi nas shes pa mi rgyu zhing //dper na sprin por mdzub btsugs bzhin//nyams ni dri ma thogs zhing //shes pa khong nas dga' ba 'byung //snga ma thos pa'i chos skad 'byung //rtog nas yul la ma zhen na//'dzin med sems
104a3(kha): das brab bas//snod lam bzhig byed kyang //sems kyi chi yis sor bam/'dzi ni nag thangs dam pade//dgangs pa sangs rgyas thugs dang mnyam//da don rgyad la btsal pa ra//rnyis ga pa 'dzin pag pa med//dbe na 'a bya tshar 'jug bzhin/sa 'angs lam du
104a4(kha): dgos pa//dper na mu rgod rta 'dul zhing //ma yengs par ni gzhag par bya//de la sems ni 'phros 'gyur na//dper na na mkha'i sprin bzhin du/byung yang gzhi la gnod med ltar//de bzhin byang sems stong pa la//byung yang rang bzhin gnod ma med//rtogs pas mo sgra
104a5(kha): ceches mi dgos//gsal la mi rtog kye ma ho//mi spyod rgya mtsho'i rdzing dang 'dra//byang chub sems kyi rig pa ni//gzhug mar gnas pa chos kyi sku//snga phyir rjes 'brangs 'khrul pa'i lam//shes pa mi 'gyur bkrir ma btug/shes pa dgod par gyur pa'i tshe//'gyur dang /
104a6(kha): bang sa//rnaMs nas 'jug par rang la ldog//rlo bapon chun mo tsho ma thag/sa slong rang bzhin rang ba bzhin//sems kyi nang bzhin chos nyid la//rang bzhin gsal par gro nog/da ltar rtogs pa'i rnas 'byor nyams//shing bal lta bu'i nyams 'byung bas//dbugs
104a7(kha): ni byin rgya mchog/chos nyid dgas pa rten pa'i gams//mtha' dus med pa dam 'dra/'jig 'dras med pas do 'dra//rang byung mi gas dzam nyid/ gas gsang ba smon pa'i rgyud pas//sems kyi gnag sangs 'dan pa'i seng sa bzhi 'o/
104b1(kha): de nas yang rdo rje sems dpas zhus pa/kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//skyon mo mnga' la yon tan rdzogs//bdud dang cho su dbye ba'i phyir//bdud kyi ngos bzung bstar du gsol//gsang ba'i bdag pos bka' stsal pa/kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/'dud dang
104b2(kha): chos su dbye ba la//rnam pa bco brgyad 'dir bshad do//gzugs ni phung po'i bdud du gsungs//bu sogs lha'i bdud sbrul//de la 'dzin pa nyon mongs bdud//'khrul pa'i sems ni 'chi bdag bdud//dgongs pa'i nor bas bdud yin pas//gnod sbyin lha yang de dang 'dra///
104b3(kha): byang chad lta ba'i badrung //rkang po bogs med sgom pa'i bdud//stong med rang kha spyod pa'i bdud//thugs med 'dod pa 'bras bu'i bdung //phra mo chog shes thos pa'i bdud//rtsa rlung 'khrug pa rlung gi bdud//grub mtha' log pa chos kyi bdud//oM thub dam bca' sdom pa'i/
104b4(kha): 'drung /'du 'di longs spyod g.yeng ba'i 'drung /lam na 'brel ba grogs kyi bdud//the chom skyes pa nyams kyi bdud //zangs kyi ma zin shes rab bdung//bar chod 'gegs rnams sems shes pas//bdud kyi gags sel nyams la phan//se shes gsang ba rjod/
104b5(kha): ma'i rgyud las//bdud ngos bzung ba'i le'u ste lnga pa'o///yang rdo rje sems dpas zhus pa//kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//bdud kyi 'jigs pa de sel ba'i phyir//'jigs med skyab cig pagadu gsol//rdo rje 'chang gi bka'
104b6(kha): baT sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang sems ma rtog bdud du shar//yod pa 'o shes skyabs yin pas//de mtshon ngo sgod 'di ltar lags//sdang ba semasu shes par gyis//sems kyi rang bzhin stong pa yin//stong pa'i rang bzhin bsam du med//sa ma med
104b7(kha): mtha' bral chos sku yin//zin pas rtsol ba'i nad dang grol//lhun gyis gnas pa rtogs pas na//de dang ma bral rnal 'byor rgyan//gzhan nas btsal bas skyabs ma rnyed//rang byung ye shes kha drag skyal ma 'dra//rang sems rtogs pas 'jigs pa'i nang dang phralan
105a1(kha): / sngags padas gams pas+ho/ n+dan gas rji byin pa nga// rdo chang !/ tid gsol la mad / gdos mamin//chags dang ti c-in nga gis na//god cing gyi mabyu sa//byin nas ba sa ma
105a2(kha): sems pa ldan na par 'gyur//yas de sun ring po pani slo ste// d+ha dang /pa'i ma zlo pa smra chu sa / rlung myo na sags/ na sa
105a3(kha): gcig/sems ni nas pa'i mtshan nyid ltar/mtshan ma med par nog cig pas//skye med rnams ros bdud rtsi'i mas/rjes po bdud snyom sa bas//byang chun sems kyi las chen //rjes pa stong pa nyid las grol//gangag su meng pa'i chos nyid ma ma
105a4(kha): brtags na tho yin pas//chos nyid blo las 'das pa yin//blo nas blo gros ngo mtshan cho//den tho grol das pa'i rgyud// phyir yod med gnyis minam/sang sa bya chu ba sems nyid ma//mang pa'i smos mi 'dogs pa dang //seng par byung ba mo 'bas pas//gas dam
105a5(kha): ma sa sa//'argya ga'i rgyu'i mig da//rlungs sa pa' yin na/phyin byu med pa zhin/'i snga san du phyub sa /sa na rlo zhi du//d+hI tshigs da sa na ga /ma sems dag 'chos la ma/mas sa thun gyi/

105b4(kha): rang sems sangs rgyas rtse bstan pas//dngos grub chen po 'dir thogs med thob//ma bsam lhun gyis grub pa'o//de ltar ma shes sems can rnams//rang re'i blo yis bcing ba la//thabs rnams du ma gsung gyur kyang //yang dag gdams ngag rgyal po 'di//bka'
105b5(kha): rgyud spu gri lta bu ste//gang zag 'bum las 'ga'i don//'byung ba sems la gnas par bya//gang la mi ltos man dag rgyud//zung du 'jug pa'i ye shes ni//rang lugs bzhon du shes par bya//rgyud dang rkyen las rag ma lus//thug phrad shugs kyi lhug pa'o//sgra gsum
105b6(kha): 'bras bu rang chas pas//ye shes rang chas zang ka ma//'di ni rang la byin gyis brlabs//rnam shes 'du ba'i gnas dag du//bag phab lus sems skrod pa 'du bya//rtags pas blo'i zhen pa log/mi gnas pa ni mnyam pa nyid//dge ba 'phel ba'i gnas dag du//phra rags//
105b7(kha): yul la blo mi bzung //rang sems thabs kyis mthong bya ste//dngos po lta ba yul las 'das//yang dag shes rab mig phyenas//rang sems dri meng chen por mthong //thang la phebs pa ting 'dzin yin//de ltar khong du chud pa ma/s+ye bu phun sum tshogs pa yin/'chor
106a1(kha): gapa'i chos rlomi gzhag/stong pa nyid kyang de bzhin no//sems nyid drung por bathung du med/nges par rang nan pa 'ching /'di sa chags pa 'ana' mngan/rang sems bcing ba'i srin po bzhin/'bras bu dag brlag par 'gyur/dbas sangs rgyas
106a2(kha): 'ad ga yang sas kyid kyi de//bras pa med shes sa rdzogs san//desan kyis su pa d-i/mams /mags gsang basnron pa'i chud pas//mu kri sa zung 'i skye brgyad pa'o//danas sangs
106a3(kha): dpa' rdo rjes 'di skad gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//'gro ba'i skyabs mdzad byugs rje can//bdag cag mongs par gyur pa la//9 nas ngos gzung ba gdu gsol//gsang ba'i bdag pos gsungs pa/kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/de nyid zung du
106a4(kha): thugs pa dang //tshogs drug lcags dang zug blang dang //gnas pa thabs dang wa ru ba shad//nga por ye shes ros gzung ni//sman pa mkhas pa lta bu ste//de yis nang dang dang po'i nyams//rtsa dang chu'i sgo nas rtog/nang ngos byin nas ming 'dogs bzhin//de bzhin nyid/
106a5(kha): bar gsags pa'i rgyu//zla ma mkhas pa'i man ga dang dang //rang gi nyams kyi bye brag gis//tshad ma gsum la rte nas su//sa nas nas ltar khya ba la/dzas zin na ni de dang drang //mi shes sna thugs dgos pa ni//phrul gyi mi yongs su ma thug
106a6(kha): na kunas thugs rtog pa'i mi//'di da m+b+ha gnasu med//da nyid don la gan sa pa'o//tshogs drug thug pa'i man dag ni//rgyas tham gyis gan ba snang /ma srang dza ma ga med pazhin//de bzhin sems nyid rtags par mi/ dag gzhan dzin pa
106a7(kha): nas la du tshum pa zu gra gas / kyi na na/rig bye/ zhi la 'du sa rim ma na lum pas sa ma ju gas das
106b1(kha): ngag ni//rus bral mkhar gzhong gzhag pa bzhin//yan lag 'khums pas gzung med ltar//de bzhin rnal 'byor rnam rtog chud//rnal 'byor sems la dbang bsgyur bas//'khrul pa'i shes pas khug mi nus//ye shes gsang ba sgron ma'i rgyud las/ye shes
106b2(kha): ngos zung ba'i le'u ste dgu pa'o///yang sems dpa' rdo rjes zhus pa//kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//rang byung ye shes ngo bo ste//rang la 'char lugs ji ltar lags//bdag cag rnams la bshad du gsol//gsang ba'i bdag pos gsungs
106b3(kha): sha kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/rang byung ye shes chen po ste//'char ba'i thabs ni 'di lta ste//rang sems gnyug mar gnas pa na//bla ma'i gdam ngag thabs kyis ni ka gnas pa'i don de ma shes pas//de las 'das pa'i gong ma gcad//bla ma yod pa chen po yin//de ltar
106b4(kha): sa blo chung bas//dang po dben par lus dang nas//bya rgod mkha' 'phur ba bzhin//mig gtad bcas pa'i rim pa gtad//rlung sems gcig tu 'dus byas te//bla mas gsungs pa ji bzhin du//rtsol ba drag la thun du gzhug/mnga' mkhan skyug ma spong ba bzhin/
106b5(kha): dmigs pa rtsol ba drag sgribasam//thun tshad rab 'bring gsum du gcad//khams bde mthun la brtson 'grus skyed//zhan na rtsol ba glong la zhag/nyams kyi 'char lugs rnam pa gsum//thol rgal gcig char rim gyis dang ka/bye brag mang por shes par bya//zin pa'i
106b6(kha): rtag ni byi chu byung //lus 'dar yan lag sdug pa dang //mkha' la 'phar zhing rgyug pa dang /bkrag dang ldan la 'byung ba snyoms//brten zhing mgul du mi btub dang //bzung ba sbal btang bka' ba'o//shes pa de dang 'grogs 'did na//khams dang 'thun pa'i spyod pas
106b7(kha): byang /thun chen gom pa'i rnal 'byor bya//slob mi'i ting 'dzin zhan gyur na//bar du sgrims la tha mar slodang//dang po slong la tha mar sgrims//ting 'dzin rab du gyur pa na//ma bcos so ma lhug par gzhag/'dzin rab sbyin phor 'dzub btsugs 'dra//'dzin 'bring bde ba chad
107a1(kha): /khrang ma 'a gyur mtha' dus sems la med//lus ni yod du mi mtshor dang //shing bral 'dab ma yang 'altar//dbugra ni phyin sgyu ma tshor//sems ni mkhar ltar phyogs ris med//ye shes khong shar bad chen 'bar//me she sags pa ljonas de rgyud
107a2(kha): rang byung ye shes gtan la 'bengs pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///de nas yang rdo rje sems dpas zhus pas/kye gsang ba'i bdag po lags//byang chub sems ni bzung dka' bas//sems kyi gzhag thabs gsungs du gsol//gsang ba'i khadag pos

107a4(kha): bzhin/mkha' la lding ltar gzhag pa ni//gshog pa thang gcad lding ba dang //rtsol bsgrub med par lding ba dang //'og ma mthong bar lding ba dang //yon tan rdzogs par lding ba dang //nang du gshog gru rgyas pa ltar//rnal 'byor mi rtag sgom pa'i tshe//shes pa thag bcad

107a7(kha): mig gzhan las ma ga ngas/gnan gyi choms rkun mi tshugs bzhin//de bzhin rnam 'byer sgom pa'i tshe//gang ma dag kyis yin zin pa ni//rnam par rtog pas mi tshugs pa'o//btsun mo lta bur bca' ba ni//rgyal po ya pa dang btsun mo ltar/gan /
107b1(kha): gnod pas mi tshugs ltar//de bzhin gdams ngag ldan pa'i mi//chos rnams semasu shes pa la//dngos po'i dug gis tshug mi srid//bcos thabs rnam pa gsum du bstan//na mkha'i sprin ltar bcos pa dang //khang stong rkun po lta bu dang //dug rtsa sngags kyis//
107b2(kha): rjin/da la rnam pa gsum du bstan//gsal ba'i tshang ni mar me ltar//mi gso ba ni rgyam mo gyur/yang shing 'am pa shing la wAr//dzad rtags rnam kyi gsum du san//byi rigs lus ni mang ma chor//nang rtagis dbugs 'i rgya ma tshor gas
107b3(kha): dngos po'i zhen pa chod//skyed tshul rnam pa gsum yin te//rnam par rtogs dang ye shes skye//stong pa nyid las snying rje skye//zhi gnas sgoms pas lhag mthong ste//'bras bu sku dang ye shes 'char//ye shes gsang ba sgron ma'i rgyud las/byang chub sems gtan
107b4(kha): la dbeb pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o///de nas yang rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdug po lags//lha sgom spyod pa ji ltar lags//'dus pa'i 'khor la bstan du gsol//gsang ba'i bdag pos bkar stsal
107b5(kha): sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/dang po ltabs thag bcad la//'du sgom pas nyas su thang //tha ma myo ngas la dor ro//sems nyid tu rkyen gnyis las 'das//mtshan mer rnam rtog spyon dang bral//rgya me gang la khyab nyid du gnas//mapa' dang dbus su me tog nang
107b6(kha): b+ha'i gos par mi ltung ngo //yin pa gsum gyis thag gcad la//su gsung thugs la yid ches skyes//rin po che'i gser gling du//byin pas thams cad gser du srung //'khor 'das gzang ngan med pal shes//skye sbyang a ni'i mig bzhin du//gnyis
107b7(kha): med par thag gcad do//bsgom pa nyam su blang ba ni//'das pa'i shes pa rjes mi gcad//ma 'ong ba'i sngon mi bsu//da ltar shes pa ma smad pa//dngos po'i rjes ni 'bring na//gnyug ma'i sems ni yin par nges//dbang po sgo lnga'i rjes 'brengs nas//seMn rnams ni

108a2(kha): mi rtogs na//'gro bar 'dod pa lam gyi mchog/ci yang ma skyes ma bcos pas//shes pa so ma gcig du spong //de bzhin gshegs pa'i dgongs pa yin//tha ma spyod pas la dor ni//nya mo chu las mi 'da' bzhin//dkar po'i chos dang 'bral thabs med//dngos
108a3(kha): po'i chos la zhen nas 'khor//yang na ri dwags sgra rnyan ltar//yang na glang po mtsho phrad ltar//na mkha'i mdung skor 'dra ba ste//gang la'ang thogs pa med par bya//ral gri so mangs lta bu ste//gdong du ci byung gcod par byed//ye shes gsang ba sgron ma'i rgyud las/
108a4(kha): lha bsgom spyod pa ma 'dres yongasu rdzogs pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o///de nas yang rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsol to//kye gsang ba'i bdag po laT//thams cad mkhyen pa'i gsang sgo nas//kye sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/kun
108a5(kha): gzhi skye med ma bcos stong pa nyid//de rtogs rnal 'byor gis mi gnod pa//skyes bu goms pas gar kyang ltung med ltar//skye med rtogs pas 'dris pa'i rnal 'byor pas//gang ltar spyad kyang mnyam pa'i thig le'i ngang /ched du med mi slong ngang la gzhag pas
108a6(kha): 'bral//ma spangs lhun grub byang chub chen po spyod//kun gzhi ma bcos gdal ba chen po nyid//chos kun ma 'dres byang chub semasu gcig/kun gzhi ma bcos dang la bde spyod pa//chos rnams kun la ma spyad gang yang med//gang la mi gnas rtag mi spyod na//skyo/
108a7(kha): pa gnas grags nyig nga'i spyod pa yin/chos nyid spyod pa'i rtag ni 'di ltar lags//gang sa zhed pa med nga chags 'akrung //man dang sa kun gni 'gyur med gas pa/ga cin yod gzhan gyi ra gyung /'dzi na gas sto bu rdzu 'phrul phung stong tshur
108b1(kha): del chos sems med pas nga rgyal ribo skad//de yis spong len mi byed mnyam pa nyid drugs la//thig le gcig du rdzogs pas tha dad 'dzin dang bral//gzhan nas ma btsal rang la spyod pa ngo mtshar che//rang bzhin med par shes pas 'bras bu 'byin mi nus//
108b2(kha): mnor thabs kyis nyon mongs rtsa ba bral//dper na snga ma'i snying po zin dang batshungs//skyes bu de la bya btsal med pa ste//dper na sems pa'i 'bras bu srin pa la//smin par bya 'i rtsol ba mi byed dang //byas pa'i rtsom pa de ni smin pa'i grog su 'gyur//
108b3(kha): gagazhi mnyam pa'i rang la gnas pa ni//'khor ba'i chos kun mi spong de dag la//'phags pa'i bden pa'i lam yang de la rdzogs//rang rgyal rten 'brel lam yang de la rdzogs//byang chub skyon 'dug lam yang de la rdzogs//byang chub sum cu rtsa bdun de la
108b4(kha): @rdzogs//bcu yis bgrod pa'i lam yang de la rdzogs//dkyil 'khor rim pa'i dbang yang de la rdzogs//lha dang 'das pa'i phrin las phyag rgya de la rdzogs//'phags pa mnyes byed mchod pa de la rdzogs//sdom pa gsum gyi bsrung bya de la rdzogs/nongs
108b5(kha): zhing masngon pa'i bshags pa de la rdzogs//legs pa'i smon lam bde la rdzogs//thams cad mkhyen pa'i yon tan de la rdzogs//kun gzhi ma bcos thig le nyag gcig de la rdzogs//yon tan thad rdzogs pa'i zhing sa bzhin//thig ni mi 'gyur rang sams
108b6(kha): med yin//le ni bdal ba mnyam pa bde ba nyid du gsal//mnyam pa'i tshul gyis go la sgrib ji ltar spong //mi lha mi sgom mi spyod dbyer med na//'og ma'i lha spyod rnam gsum gol mi 'gyur//gar yang mi 'gro 'dug pa'i mi dang 'ang //mnyam pa'i tshul
108b7(kha): gyis bdud rnams ji ltar 'dul//mnyam pa'i gdeng la yid ches mi 'grel na//bdud kyi bar du sprul pa ci byung yang //dzad sem su gas pas bdud kyis glags mi tshugs//khyim bdag dpa' bos bsrung ba'i bud med 'dra//gza' gtad med 'i'i
109a1(kha): /lta ba mthar dang bral//'dzin pa med pa'i sgom pa dang gi gsal//brang dor med pa'i spyod pa gang la'ang babang ngan med//'bras bu spang byabs med pas sku dang ye shes gsal//de ltar rtog pa'i rnal 'byor ga la 'ardzi med//ye shes gas /

109a3(kha): ga ba na dugas+ho//gas pa'i bdag pos ka' sbrul bya/skyesems dpa chen po nyon cig//mkha' skyo gan su 'ong 'dzin/dang po thos pa'i shes ra cis//rkyenang chos la sgo 'dags tshong /khar du sas pa'i gas rab kyis// don rgyung la goms

110b4(kha): dam mi mdzad dam//sems can lha mi rnams ni shes pa rtsing //dbang po yul la yengs pa'i gzung 'dzin gyis//'tsho ba dkon la tshe thung nad mang bas//thar lam mthob skye shing la chi la rtags ka de phyir lus dag 'dus byas chos spyod kyang ka long med mtha' ring sgrubapa
