112b1(kha): so sor sems//gcig na dge sdig rnam smin 'bras bu yang //sems can ma spyad kun gyis myong bar rig/las kyi rnam smin thun mong mi 'gyur phyir//bag chags brtas pas 'khrul pa'i sdug bsngal yin//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//snang ba//
112b2(kha): 'bo gyur sna tshogs pa'i dun las mi 'gal bar bdan pa'i laru ste brgya pa ///de nas kun rig ston pa sdang mdzad kyis//'dus pa'i dang pan 'khong la paka'aphrul pa//ma rnams dang po rang san panag gyur yang //phyi ros sub gyis tha snyad ga dgas pa 'o//
112b3(kha): 'khrul pa yin phyir shes phyir zhen pa med//chos brgyad rang sar grol bas mnyam pa'i phyir//skye bo nyid kyang ma sbyangs chos kyi sku//dper na a shu ka nyid bdud rtsir shes pa ltar//de bzhin skye bo rang sems 'di nyid ni//sgyu ma'i don don ldan thar lam byang chub sems//bdag
112b4(kha): 'gas rlom sa/ dus pa'i sems dzin tsan/ying kyi rtogs pas so so //de ny-i lu na mdang gdon dgas kyis//ra sa sa rtogs pas kyi med par phyin/sa rnams sems ma pa mas pa tso gsus/
112b5(kha): rtag chad med pa'i phyir//sems der rang gsal don zin mi brjed pas//phyal ba lung ma bstan du gol sa med//dper na gser 'gyur rtsis gang thebs pa rnams//thaMd gser las gzhan du mi 'gyur bzhin//de bzhin mu stegs skye bo sems nyid kyang //man ngag gser rtsis thibs
112b6(kha): pa'i sems na/mu stegs rnams kyang ye shes bde ba'i ngang //yul la mi zhen phyir ni sgom du med//rdzogs sangs rgyas zhes bya ba'i don nyid kyis//sna tshogs zhen las grol phyir sgom mi dgos//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//ma sgoms kyang
112b7(kha): skye bo dang mu stegs kyi golas med par bstan pa'i le'u sta dgu pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i snying po 'khor rnams la//kun rig ston pa chos rjes bka' stsal pa//sems can ma 'khrul sangs rgyas med pa'i gong //ngo bo nyid kyi sku la rang byung sems/
113a1(kha): mang bsgoms kyang skyon med chos dbyings na mkha' 'dra ka skal med de ni kun gyi re ba skongs//kun la khyab cing ma rdzogs med mod kyi/kun gyi gdams ngag ma 'phrod rtogs par dka'//rdzogs pa chen po'i lta ba'i ye shes gting rdzogs
113a2(kha): kyi rgyud las//ma bsgoms 'phrul ba med pa'i le'u ste bcu gcig pa'o///de nas kun rig rgyal po chos rjes bka' stsal pa/'dus pa'i tshogs rnams yid kyis dkon cig 'ang sems ngo bo nyid kyi chos sku ni/dbang po mngon gsum mi gnas/
113a3(kha): kun ma khyab//chos su tshig tu 'di zhes bstan par ni//rdo rje sems dpas kyang ni mtshon du med//de sang rdo rje sems dpa' yid kyis nyon//'khor 'das tshig las 'das pa'i se sa te/ka/dbyings dang kha dog dbang po'i spyod yul du kasangs rgyas nyid kyis bad+man+d+ha/
113a4(kha): cing mtshon du med//mtshon cing bstan par dka' ba'i rang sems de/ka/yod med kun las 'das pa'i byang chub sems//dngos med yid la dran pa'i ye shes ni//kun bzang nyid kyi mi gzigs lta ba'i mchog/gzung 'dzin mthu rtas rnams smin lus 'di la//dbang po//
113a5(kha): rnams ming du brjod byed de//don la phan pa rdul tsam mtshon mi nus//dper na bum pa nang gyi mar me la//dngos med brjod med na mkha' chen po min//de bzhin tshe gcig mi rtog don bsgoms kyang //ye shes rang shar chen po'i lta ba min//bu ma chags nas khwang
113a6(kha): chen por thims pa bzhin//lus bral 'ang sems ngo bo nyid kyi sku//de nyid rigs pas gsal btab bde ba klong //de tsam nyid las kun tu bzang pos kyang //ngo bo nyid kyi sku la mtshon du med//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las/yas
113a7(kha): kyi don la mtshon du med par bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gnyis pa'o///de nas dad pas 'dus pas 'khor rnams la//kun rig ston pa chos rjes bka' stsal baM//sangs rgyas rnams kyi dgongs pa ji lta bu//rang gi mtshan nyid ma
113b1(kha): /yos de bzhin cing /chos sku skye med rtogs nas 'gro don du/ji ra snang ba'i gzugs can skul ba'i mu//mang da ba bcu gnyis 'gro dang mthar byin gzugs ces sgrub pas 'gro bkun 'dug yang ka/se shes blo ldan rlom sems rdul tshab sa'o//
113b2(kha): bdag dang gzhan du rtog pa med pa'i phyig//se nas gnyis meng cig ni chos kyi sku//mthun 'jug 'tshams pa'i spyod pa rgyamatsho ni//thugs rjes chen pos gro don rdzogs par mdzad/nga byung me shes dbyings las ma gsos kyang //gro
113b3(kha): na// ga zun drug la mas par spyong pa rgya tsho //gas rapas gro don rdzogs pa sa ma/nga chung sems kyis lag sa g.yos kyang /'gro bar dpal mgon ku tu yang ba ni bdag ga 'a sa
113b4(kha): blang dor med pa'i phyir/spyod pa rgya mab mo sna tshogs ci bder spyod//padz+ra skye ba'i rgyu ni 'dam yin te//baN+Dal ni 'das kyi skyon gyis gos pa med//chos nyid blang dor med par rtogs gyur na//ye shes rtogs med nyams pa rdul tsam meng //thun 'tshams
113b5(kha): mi sgom rgyun gyi rnal 'byor la//'ching grol spyod pa med phyir grol ba med//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//spyod pa 'ching grol med par bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu gsum pa'o///de nas kun rig ston
113b6(kha): sa kASh+Ta sA / sa pa'i lakhyo rnams spyod bsned tsun cig/sa smos su rdzogs rten sdigs pa//yang dag mi 'gros theg ster cibs//gros me sems nyid segs can gshel gag/ga sa chos ma
113b7(kha): mas pa 'da'i 'i bo can cig pa ni nga mi gas rdzog cig/kyang phyi ma cha na mi ni gsas/ga sod du rigs sod kyi na pa
114a1(kha): de nying senas panas grags yin pa byi/ rtogs chos su mya na 'das pa'i chos/kung tu bang po nga byung la shes drugs/'ang sems dadags gas rgyas nal sems pa sa rgyas glo gu sems cas //kun sa kau ban sa smra
114a2(kha): lags pa//dum ldan labar par sgrib pa'i lus cid yin/dung tshang blo ma'i gang mas dgadod bston pas/ga ni yas srung byung ba nyid dang /dang po'i sems cing nga ba nyid kyi bru//su phyim srad chos sung rig pa'i phyir//blo ngas tshig pa'i dan la sgom ma ngos
114a3(kha): pa mas /sa gas lugs dang sapa//khre sa pa sa y-i su
114a4(kha): rdzul hU~M gsogs dang / nga shes pa pa'i dang /yong das gnyis garba ba ga di byi //rgyu gyis grub pa /du sa gcig khas nyid da byo rdzogs nyid kyi nga bsam 'das dang 'i phyi
114a5(kha): sa ya / rkyen gyi 'jug pa dag 'i gros
114a6(kha): rdzus su d+hA sags pa rgyas / gzugs nyi pos dag tu spyod kyi dang //ngas rjo ma dza sa/su das mdun kyis chu 'a
114b1(kha): /dpa' rdo rje snying po sas//dang pas 'jug pa'i 'khor la ra gdags phyir/bden pa'i don gyis 'di skad yid kyis gsol//rang sems snying bdon rtogs ltab la//ma bsgoms 'phral med don ni ji ltar 'grub//ye shes don ni mtshon du ji ltar meng/skyong ba 'ching grong
114b2(kha): med pa'i don ni gang /sa la sbyangs du med pa'i don ni cig rtogs dam tshig bsrungs nyams ji ltar med//bsgoms na go la sa'i skyon ni ji ltar lags//ya bad+ma'i don gyi ye shes gang la bgyi ka de skad gsol ba'i yid kyis donaH nas//kun rig ston pa chos rjes bka'
114b3(kha): su la gA rang sems snying po'i don rtogs chos sku las//bsgoms bya'i ye shes chos sku gud na med//snga phyi sems kyi ye shes mtha' bral bas//ma nor sgom pa po yang ga la yod//de la bsgom mkhan sems la dngos med naka mi gnas mi rtogs 'joms la rnam
114b4(kha): 'byor min//sems dpa' rdo rje snying po nyon cig/a ti rdzogs chen chos sku bden gnyis bral//ngo bo nyid kyis rang shar ye shes ni//yon med dbus kyi ye shes mtha' gsum bral//yul la spongs sdud gnyis ka'i gdong nas med//dper na namapar rang du shes pa
114b5(kha): @bzhin//ji ltar ma nor rang sems rtogs pa yis ka/lus ngag ma skyes rtogs pa'i gzhi med pa'o//dkyil drung 'dug smra bcad kha na med//de bzhin sems dang rtag chang spros bral bas//rtogs yang mi rtage sgom pa'i 'khor mi 'ong //ye shes dalta bu'i shugs lnga byung
114b6(kha): sa na//rnal 'byor dmigs pa'i shes pas mi bsgom te//rtogs pas 'ang sems ma skyes yis phyir//rnal 'byor ched sgom pa brten mi dgos //sems dpa' 'dus pa'i snying po dgon cig/yul gyi snang ba spyod yul ma grub bying ka na mkha' lta bur ye shes rang
114b7(kha): gsal bas//rang sems chos sku rang gis de rtogs pas /ka/rnal 'byor ci yang yid la mi bsgom no//yang dag lta ba'i don dang mthun par 'grug/sems dpa' rdo rje snying po de nyon cig/rdzogs chen golas gcang phyir sgom med de//ye shes sgom med dag tu rab bto//
115a1(kha): bgrong med snying po lam gyi don ston pa//gzhan khas btsal bas rnyed par mi 'gyur te//ye shes snying po kun tu khyab gnas pas//rang byung ye nas rang rig 'od gsal bas//sa lam btsal du med de snying po'i don//rnam par snang mdzad
115a2(kha): che mchog kun tu bzang //da yang sems dpa' rdo rje snying po nyon//dper na rgyal po gzhan du mi 'gro bzhin ka rang sems yul la mi spyod don la gnas//de tsam nyid na chos sku bde ba'i klong ka lam la ma bgrod chos nyid ye shes skukas dang mi/
115a3(kha): la 'grong na byang chub 'gegs//rdzogs pa chen po'i lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las/sa la sbyang du med pa'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o///de nas dang pas 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams la//kun rig ston pa chos rjes dka' sprul
115a4(kha): ma tshig bsrungs nyams med pa'i ye shes ni//'og ma bzhin du dam du ma bcas pas//nyams pa med phyir ye bsrungs chen po yin//gdengs ldan lta ba'i dam tshig rgyun du dran//cheng du ma yin rang gis don gsal bas//me bsrungs chen po'i nga
115a5(kha): tshig lta ba yin//de yang 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams yid kyis kon//theg pa'i khyad par ji snyed grags pa dang /tshul khrims sdom dang phyi nang dam tshig kyang //'gro ba gdul bya ji ltar 'dod la bstan//tha snyad tsam du gdul bya zhes brjod kyang //dam du
115a6(kha): bca' bar bya ba rdul tsam med//dam bca' med pa'i don gyi ngeng rtogs na//dam du bcas kyang sgron med na mkha' 'dra//'dus pa'i snying po rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//chos sku mo gso thugs kyi dam tshig ste//glang dor med par dam tshig chen po yin//rang seM
115a7(kha): mi spos so gas chen por gnas//dus gsum don dam ma bral nyams pa med//rdzogs pa chen po zhes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las gdam tshig bsrung nyams med par bstan pa'i le'u ste bco lnga pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i
115b1(kha): 'khor la bka' stsal pa/bsgoms na gol sar gyur pa 'khrul pa'i phyir//rang gi sems nyid ma rtogs rtsol ba sta ka phyi'i yul gyi rtsol ba sems bsdus nas//'og ma'i lta ba 'dod lugs rnams bzhi la//gnyis med snang stong khas mi
115b2(kha): len pa rnams//so sor rtogs pa'i rig pa gzhal byas nas ka gsal bde stong pa'i mnyam gzhag don bsgom pas//dug lnga sgom pa'i gang zag yo//khyid rgod mtha' dag shes phyir byar byed//de nyid lta ba mi shes gti mug ste//gsal
115b3(kha): la mi rtogs 'dod chags sgom pa rnams ka khar lo mi rtog zhen pa'i nga rgyal can//'khor ba mad dang ltung du dgod pa'i zhe sdang gis//mi dmigs don la gza' gtad byas nas sgom//rtsol bas snyoms kya yul la 'gyur med phyirak+Sha/mi rtog
115b4(kha): 'dod phyir ma nor don dang bral ka rtog pa bzang ngan ci skyes sems nyid du ka dgag rgyu med pa tsam du blos gzung nas//gsal ba de mi dmigs ched du sgoms pas nor//grol la gtan tshigs bzhigs pas smos ci dgos//de yang 'dus pa'i snyi
115b5(kha): po yid kyis nyon//grib ma gzugasu sbyar ba ji bzhin du ka/lha dang rtsa ba'i thig le thabs sgoms rnams//mtshan ma yul la dmigs phyir sgoms pa po//de nyid semasu blos byas phyir na gol rkyen ma thub phyir gnas lugs chos sku yin//
115b6(kha): de phyir sgoms na 'khor ba'i nang chen yin//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//sgrom na gol sar bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu drug pa'o///de nas yang kun rig ston pa kun bzang gyis//'dus pa'i 'khor
115b7(kha): brag stsal pa/dga' da gus pa'i sems kyi yid kyis nyon//le bad+hU ba yi mes don chen no//kun gni bzhi ma mkha' la nyi gar 'drag/thams cad kun 'dus rang snang le gas gas pa// nas pa tsal med 'bras bu mang ni sangs rgyas/
116a1(kha): / sman gyi yang ces chen po ste//gos me ga nas dag pas rgyas kyi su rang thung 'grugs pa med pas nas grong /ma rdza sa 'arjod seshes snga mchag cis kyang dmig su med pas rang sems legs su//gsang ma na
116a2(kha): du med par rang la rdzogs//chos sku 'di zhes gzhan nas mi rnyed pas//rtogs par 'dod na rang gi sems la tshol//sems la don rnyed rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pa//'khrul pa med phyir sems can 'khor ba med//rang sems rtogs pa'i don las ma gtogs
116a3(kha): pa/bde zhing skyed pa'i sangs rgyas log na med//de phyir rgyu la mi rtogs 'bras bu med//med la rtsol ba rgya mtsho chu bor nas//smin rgyu ri dgas chur mthong snyegs pa bzhin//sgom med don nyid bor na su sgom pa//byang chub med par rang gis sdug bsngal/
116a4(kha): de yang sems dpa' rdo rje yid kyis nyon//rang sems de nyid rtog pa rnal 'byor pa//ye shes bcud nyid chos kyi gter du smin ka na mkha' go yangs 'byung ba'i lam du bde//rtogs pa'i go yangs sangs rgyas lam du bde//sems nyid go yangs sgom med lam
116a5(kha): du bde//dus gsum 'gyur med rang rigs lta ba'i dbyings//'dod dgu rang la yon tan 'chang ba'i phyir//rgyu 'bras gzhan la rag pa ma lus pa'i zab//dper na ma dris pa'i mchod dkyil me 'bar ba//na mkha'i dkyil nas bab pa'i chu yis ni//bskal par de la
116a6(kha): gInang par mi nus bzhin//rang sems chos nyid rang byung ye shes la//'khor bar 'a byams kyang sgrib pas gos pa med//rim rten khod na mkhar 'thon pa'i tshe//rgyam mo nyid kyang bdud rtsir gyur ba bzhin//rang gi byang chub sems nyid rtog tsaman//dug
116a7(kha): nyid kyang ye shis chen por 'char//rdzogs pa chen po sto ba ye shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//so shes kyi don las bzla ba'i le'u ste bcu bdun pa'o///de nas gsang ba'i 'dag po rdo rje sems dpa' yis//'dus pa'i tshogs la nges don
116b1(kha): phan gdags phyir//ston pa kun rig sems kyi rgyal po la//bden pa'i yid kyis 'di skad gsol to//dug lnga ma spangs rang grol ji ltar byung //che ba'i yon tan mi 'byung gang gis bsgribs tshogs ma bsags pa'i gzugs sku gang las bsgribs//god 'og
116b2(kha): the tshom gags ni gang ltar spyod//the tshom chod pa'i nyon mongs rtags ni gang //mnyam pa'i rgyal pos thugs kyis bstan du gsol//de skad ces gsol ba'i don gsan thugs kyis bka' stsal pa//dug lnga ma spangs rang grol ye shes ni//rin chen 'od kyis mun pa med
116b3(kha): pa ltar//'khor ba thog ma rtsad chod don gyi phyir//ngo bo nyid kyi sku ni rang rtogs pas//gdod nas ma rig dag pas gti mug bral//de yang 'dus pa'i sems dpa' nyon//ma rig med phyir zhe sdang yul mi dmigs//de phyir rang sems snying po chos sku la//snga phyi zhen pa'i dbang gi/
116b4(kha): @zhe sdang na//kha dog yul la mnar sems byed pa'i phyir//ba mo la ni nyi ma shar ba bzhin//phye 'brel gcad pa'i man dag d+hAn par bya//du yang dang ldan 'khor rnams yid kyis nyon//'dod chags skye ba'i yul ni sgro lha 'byung ba'i sems//phyi rol bang zing nor la ba m+b+ha
116b5(kha): chags nas//bung bas me tog gzhibs nas mi zhen ltar//chags pa nyid na zhen med rang gi sems/sems la byed pa med phyir sems nyid don du shes//sems can pho mo las kyi chags pa la//rang sems chos nyid rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor pas//mig sdug gnyen pos sgom
116b6(kha): pa mi dgas par//yul la po mo mo byi chags pa spyod gyur kyang //sems la tha ma med pas nyes pa med//de nyid dus na rig bas gas pa zigs pas/dus gsum ngo mo b+ha \u0f7f nyams pa med//dasang 'dus pa'i dang ldan med kyis nyon stong rgyam chen pa'ing sems cag /
116b7(kha): byin mas kyis la bya ba sa/tan pa mi rgos la grong byang chung senas nga rgyas ga sa kyi sprul pa /sa can na ni ga las rnam nga 'i gro sa gcig pa shegs pa yi/nonam du bang cags par ning rgya byed/
117a1(kha): /'dus pa'i snying po 'khor rnams da nyon cig / lta ba na mkha' lta bur 'phang chod rnal 'byor pas//mtho' la phrag dog mnyam la smang gyur kyang //mi len mi spong nyams pa chen po ste//ma ki ta yis thams cad sman du gyur pa bzhin//don drang ma nor man ngag
117a2(kha): gdengs gis 'phangs//bla ma'i bka' nod ye shes rnal 'byor la//dug lnga spangs kyang rang grol ye shes thugs//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las ka nyon mongs ma spangs rang grol du bstan pa'i le'u ste bco brgyad pa'o//
117a3(kha): de nas yang 'dus pa'i 'khor la bka' stsal pa/sangs rgyas yon tan mtshan dang pe byang te/stobs dang mi 'jigs chos ni bco brgyad dang //dus ni nga lta byung ba lus kyis sgribs//dper na khyad ched slong de'i nang na gshog rgyas kyang //slong ngas chags 'thung
117a4(kha): mi nus pa bzhin//rang sems chos skur rtogs pa'i rnal 'byor kyang ka khrus 'di che ba'i yon tan mi 'byung phyir//thun mongs sman gyi sbyor ba'i thabs kyis na//rtsi sbyor sman dang ri mu bcud len gyis//rang lus 'di nyid gsos 'gyur lus rgyas tshe//dug dung mtshon sogs mi
117a5(kha): ga gyu gyu papar bya lugs nis rmos kya dmas kyi na bya rkyen po mdzad pa ga byasas zu /rgyas 'o//khas kyi mams na
117a6(kha): /grong khyer du/rang thu po daphyir bams par smos ci dgog ka phyir bcom ldan 'das kyang rje rje ga thog tu/sangs dang rgyas bas mdzan dung sa byang ldan/ ba'i yon tan ma lus rgyun gyis rdzog gardzogs pa chen po lta ba le shes gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las/du 'dings /
117b1(kha): /rgyas kyi yon tan mi 'byung bar brtan pa'i le'u ste bcu dgu pa'o/ /de nas yang 'dus pa'i 'khobs tre drag su pa da/'ang sems bro bu nyid dugs 'gyur na/ye shes tshogs chen rdzogs pas chos kyi skug per na rgya ma tsho rnyog sa dngas gyu na nas pa /
117b2(kha): ldan byung ba mi 'gag bzhin//chos nyid nga las longs spyod rig pa'i rgyun//de mi sgrub pa sna tshogs sprul pa'i bru//dayang rdo rje sems dpa' yid kyis nyon//mu nyid chags kyis btsags kyang gzugs parnyan med //med pa'i rna sems kyi ba nyid la ni//kun zadas sa
117b3(kha): /rgyas nyid kyis gzugs btsal kyang //gdos pas lus la sangs rgyas rmad mi srid//bsod nams sa tshogs kyis gzugs sku 'byung ba men//rim dgu ston pa'i sangs rgyas gzugs kyi sku//mos pa so so 'dul phyir snang mod kyang ka rgyud nas chos sku rtogs pa'i thab/
117b4(kha): su bstan//smon lam dbang gis 'gro ba'i don byed pa//'og ma'i blo bzhag sangs rgyas chos sku mid//de phyir rang sems ma rtogs dge ba yis//rgyu med 'bras bu mi 'byung dpe bzhin du//bskal pa stong du tshogs ni bsags byas kyang ka 'bras bu gzugs sku'i sangs rgyas
117b5(kha): 'byung mi 'gyur//de yang 'dus pa'i dang ldan 'khor rnams nyon//gser sbrul mig gis 'dod po'i yon tan lnga//dus gcig nyams myong dpe bzhin rnal 'byor pas//ngo bo nyid kyis sku rtogs chos kyis sku//de la rig pas longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//sna tshogs nyid ni gsal thebs
117b6(kha): sgyum pa'i skuka las med rtog par dkar phyir gsang ba'i sku//blo dngos gcig phyir mngon par byang chub ste//de rnams gci la ka gis yon tan lngar 'byung bas//sangs rgyas 'gro don brtsal mi dgos pa'i phyir//sems tshon rang sems ches kyi sku/ka/rdzogs pa chen po rgyu
117b7(kha): migs nyitid rdzogs kyi rgyud las//chos sku rtogs na gzugs sku 'byung ba'i le'u ste nyi shu ba'o///de nas kun rig ston pas 'dus pa'i gtor rnams la//me tshom gags ni gsal mthos bka' stsal pa//'khor 'das spyi'i mtshan nyid sems yin//
118a1(kha): rtogs na kun kyang yin phyir ma rtogs min//a ti thig chen snying po rdzogs pa'i phyir//yod med gang nges rtag chung skyon yon phyir//ma nges 'bras bu log pa'i kha rje kha//sangs rgyas 'bras bu mthong snying rje'i gnas//de yang 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogs yid kyis nyon/
118a2(kha): 'jig rten pa dang 'das pa'i chos rnams kun//gnyi ga'i gzhi ni ngo bo chos skur nges//yul gyi snang ba ma nges sna tshogs pa ka blang dor med phyir chos rnams lhun gyis grub//'dus pa'i gtso bos snying po'i don gsol ba//yul nyid sems la sems nyid yul yin
118a3(kha): laT/yul nyid yin phyir yul la sdug bsngal rigs//sems nyid yul na rang nyid ben por thal//spong sdud mi shes zhen pa skye bo'i blo//de phyir rdzogs chen chos sku bla med gang //de yang 'khor gyi snying po yid kyis nyon//yul nyid spong sdud zhen pa rnams kyi skyon/
118a4(kha): blang dor med phyir gzhon pos btsal mo dgos//de tsam nyid na bsam gtan mchog gi lam//de la gnyen po med phyir bla med chos kyi sku//de yang 'dus pa'i 'khor rnams nga nyon cig/skom du yod med sgro skur mthong ltung phyir//sgom pa'i gzhi gnas mtha' la zhen pa
118a5(kha): steka ma rtogs sgom med don gyi gdeng dang ldan//de phyir mtha'i lta ba nor ba'i lam//de yang dang ldan 'khor rnams yid kyis nyon//a ti man ngag chos sku'i snying po ni//bsgoms kyang 'gal med rang sems na mkha' 'dra//ma sgoms pas kyang chos sku gtor dral med//byang
118a6(kha): /chu ba lam du sems nyid rtogs pa che//sems dpa' rdo rje yid kyis don zhus pa//sangs rgyas sems can mig tsam tha dad lam//tha dad 'brel med thugs rjes phan mi nus//gcig na 'gro drug sems can ci phyir byung //kun rig ston pas bka' stsal yid kyis nyon///
118a7(kha): gnyis so sor snang ba ma yin te//rtog na ngo bo cig phyir thugs rje 'byung //ring sems ma rig rnam rtog sdug bsngal yin//don la dbyer med sems nyid lhun gyi grub//'dus pa'i tshogs kyis sems don yid kyis zhus//sangs rgyas sems can rgyu 'bras yin gyur na//
118b1(kha): sangs rgyas las byung nas gsus don med 'gyur/sems can las byung tshogs gnyis dus me rtag/kun rig ston pas 'khor la ga' phrul pa/sangs rgyas sems pas byu dus rag cig byu//smos bsgra gcag la gu b+h+ya sa si k+Sha ti ga ngo nga sa/
118b2(kha): chos skur gcig/gnyis ka'i ngo bo rang sems chos skur gcig/sangs rgyas sems can skyon yon rnam gnyis kyis//chos nyid 'khrul pa gang yang gzhi bzhag nas//gnyis ka ltar yang rtsod na mi 'gal phyir//ma rig ye shes gang rung phyogs gzung na//kun kyang yin la
118b3(kha): thams cad men pa'i phyir//rgyu yod dang ni rgyu med gang 'khrul pa//gzhir bzhag ma nor shin tu gces par gsungs//sems dpa' rdo rje 'khor gyi don nus pa//rang la yod pa'i mkhas pas ji skad ston//ye nas yod na man ngag bstan mi dgos//med na ri bong rwa bzhin rgyu mi dmin
118b4(kha): kun rig ston pas 'khor la bka' stsal pa//ye nas rang la yod pa'i chos sku ni//bla mas mtshon nas rang sems don bstan pas//ma rig rnam rtog chos skur shes pa las//logs na btsal nas bstan pa ma yino//dang ldan 'khor gyis don ched yid kyis zhus//bla ma'i man
118b5(kha): ngag yul la mi rtog pas//tsam du nyams myong zhi gnas skyon yin phyir//rtog na rang rgyud yin phyir man ngag men//ma dran 'du shes med pa nyid du thal//de yang sems dbang rdo rje snying po nyod//su la la mi rtog de nyid bsam gtan deka/rtog dang mi dran gnyi ga'i gzhi nyid ni//thog mtha'
118b6(kha): tshad mchos nyid byang chub sems//da yang rang byungs shos don rtogs na//yum nying mi rtog mi sgom don la gnas//de ya man dag gis 'dis do dang 'gas ka ma bcos man dag bka' la dgos med 'gyur// yang 'dus pang gzo tshogs yid kyis nyon//khrum ma la
118b7(kha): pad ga dod pa me /kun gni bzhin kyis du ma ngab nyid/ su me las rang sems nam 'al dza//rdo lnga chub nyid 'grub phyib grel bcod/di bltar bstan pas nyon mos grog/ phyi na 'dan shin tu sa par gsungs //rdzogs cher po rtam pos
119a1(kha): na sa/ Sh+tsa k+Sha r+dz+dza sa k+ta ts+tsha sa m+b+ham+b+ha b+h+yas/
119a2(kha): mang sems dbang rdo rje nyon cig/kun tu bzang po 'od dang bcas nas ni//dbang po'i mngon sum byon nas chos bstan kyang //bzang po yin pa'i sems kyis dang rgyu ni//gshin rje'i 'jigs pa byung yang nyam mi nga /gnyis pa 'khrul par rtogs phyir spong len med//ts+tsha
119a3(kha): ba gdang ldan kams myong yin pa'i rtags//theg pa'i bye brag chos rnams ci gsungs kyang //don la bzang ngan med rtogs phyir na sgro skur bral//chos la bzod pa thob pa'i zhum myong rtags//lus ngag gsum sdug pade ci byung yang //skyon yon mi k+ta rang sems 'khrul ba'i/
119a4(kha): phyir na snga phyi med par lta ba'i gdong ldan na//chos nyid rang gsal nyamasu myongs pa'i rtags//lus la mi mthun pa'i nad dang 'gegs//byung yang bag chags dang ni nyon mongs pa//rtog brtas pa nyid las ma rtogs pa//zhen pa med phyir chos sku dad pa yis//mi gtong
119a5(kha): dran par byed pa'i rnab 'byor pa/dngos grub rtsol med dgongs pa gams myong rtags//de yang 'dus pa'i snying po nyon cig/byang chub sems le snga phyi med pa'i phyir//'jig rten mthon dam na gar skyes lus nyid kyang //skye bgag cang pa'i sems la sems gnas na//'du
119a6(kha): 'bral med pa'i dgongs pa nyams myong rtags//rtags chen de ltan rnal 'byor rnams kyis ni//ci spyod thams cad ye shes sangs rgyas thugs//mi'i lus la sgribs g.yogs 'di nyid la//lha mi rnams kyis phyag 'tshal mchod par 'gyur//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba yis/
119b1(kha): /gting rdzogs rgyal po'i rgyud las//rtags bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gnyis pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rje snying po yis//dang ldan 'khor la phan gdags phyir//mnyam pa'i rgyal po kun bzang la/yid kyis dran pas 'di skad don gsol pa/wA/
119b2(kha): mas pa'i rnab 'byor sbyin ni gang /de sa bangs jing+ge bstan par gyi//gongs par 'bras bu 'i/ byung //'khor lasas 'jams pag tshigs gang gis gzang //do mig mas dag rnam byor la gtang //mnyam zi me shes rgyal pos byung ba
119b3(kha): gsog/dgang zhus po do gas na yid kyis bka' stsal pa/dod med nga rgyal drags par gakhas 'dre bas/rang gi ska bas sa rabs mtho bzhed nas//tshig la mags sbyar mkhas pa'i bla med kyang //sa tshig phyal por byang chub sems spangs nas//'og 'dzin/
119b4(kha): khong gsog bden tshig ci smras kyang ka 'khrul pa'i sems nyid log 'dren dbang du 'gyur//gnam lcags mkha' las 'dam du bying ba bzhin/myal bar nges 'gro gtan du snying re rje//de yang 'dus pa'i rdo rje sems dpa' nyon//gsang sngags gsang ba'i lta ba'i sgor/
119b5(kha): rmugasanas//sang dag 'ang sems don pa mi dung bar//ma chos zang ci jug dag las byung bas//ri sa srog rtags 'og tug du gyur/Ni zhes rang gis dargyal ba skams byed ga/ran yang sangs rgyas chub thob mi srid/de sang sams dpa'i snying byong
119b6(kha): nyon cig/rgya mtsho dbyings kyi pha bod dkyis skams sbyar//theg pa 'og ma'i sde snod chos mo nyan kyang //yang dag don gyi lta ba'i gdeng mang bA/tshig tsam lus las chos kyi khung phyung nas//bya med chos kyi nga rgyal blo bstan nas//lus ngag rtsing chos baT hral spyod bye//
120a1(kha): /rnal 'byor pa'i lta ba nyam su myong ba'i bstan pa'i le'u ste nyer gsum pa'o///de nas yang kun rig rnam par snang mdzad kyis//rnal 'byor lta pa nyams na skang ba'i thangs//'dus pa'i tshogs la che du dkanga' stsal pa//rang gi byang chub sems
120a2(kha): nyid ma rtogs na//ma hA yo ga'i chos kyi tshogs mchod phyir//bkang bshags phrin las gzung du byas gyur kyang //rnal 'byor lnga pa dam nyams skongs mi 'gyur//de sang rdo rje sems dpa' yid kyis nyon//bla mas rgyud pa'i gdam ngag don ldam pa//rang sems dbang du bcun
120a3(kha): nas don zhus nas//rgyun du mi gtong lta ba dran byas nas//snying po mchog gi sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//bden chen thugs la gsal ba'i dam tshig ste//yan lag dam tshig nyams kyang sgrib mi nus//rgyun du mi gtong ting 'dzin don bstan na//mtshams med la sogs klus
120a4(kha): kyang chos kyi sku//'gro ba drag pos yid byas lta ba ni//mi brjed bzungasu don la gus pa'i chos//sems la gsal bas rnam rtog ming yang med//skyabs dang mgon du lta ba'i mchog tu gsungs//rdzogs pa chen po'i lta ba ye shes gting rdzogs rgyal po'i rgyud las//rnam
120a5(kha): 'byor ba la lta brnams na bskangs pa'i thabs bstan pa'i le'u ste pa bzhi pa'o///de nas 'dus pa'i snying po 'khor rnams la//kun bzang ston pa tshes rjes bka' stsal pa lta ba nyams pa bslangs pa'i 'bras bu ni//rang sams sangs rgyas ye shes
120a6(kha): snying po byung /dag+sa pa tsas hrIs sangs rgyas sa la gnas//'bras bu gzhan du tshal mi dgos pa'i mchog/da byung gangs nas dag pa'i ngos nyid /sa su 'gyur ba med pas rang sems don//'bras bu dus ma mi ltos nga lta nyid //ye shes dbang ba sangs rgyas ku
120a7(kha): dang mnyam//rnas smin lus 'di bral bas kun bzang thugs//byang chub chos nyid rang byung mthar phyin pas//mi tshe 'di yis byang chub mthog thob nas//rgyu la mi ltos 'bras bu ye nas tshogs//da lta nyid du sems 'di kun tu bzang ka/lus 'di bral bas sangs rgyas chos kyi skur/
120b1(kha): skye ba mi len 'bras bu btsal mi dgos//dngos po lus med zhing po byang chub sems//sprin med namakha' gza' gar 'char bzhin/rang sems rtogs pas 'gro don bcul mi dgos /rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ses gting rdzogs rgyal po'i rgyud las/lta ba nyams pa spongs pa'i 'bras bu
120b2(kha): ston pa'i la'u ste nyi lnga la'o///da nas kun rig rgyal pos 'khor rnams la//'khor 'das sa mtshams bka' stsal pa/thog mar 'khrul pa'i gzhi gnas pagachags te//gnyid dang 'dod chags 'jigs dang rtogs parnams//sngar gyi mngal gyi sgrib g.yogs
120b3(kha): bag chags rtas//sems dpa' rdo rje 'dus pa'i snying po nyon//skye ba rang sems ma rtogs par do nas//dper na srin dang ling tog chu bur mig/rdzas rnams mi mthong bsgribs pa'i mun pa bzhin//ma rig dbang gis sgribs pa'i bag chags kyis//rang la yod pa'i chos sku don sa
120b4(kha): /bsgribs//de tshe sngon gyi rdzas rnams rjes dran nas//gos dang gnyen 'dun dran pa byung nas kyang //nga nyid nges par shi 'am gar 'gro ba'i//sgrib g.yogs lus kyi chu bur dmigs pa'i phyir//dper na mig phyung bzhin du bag chags blos//dug lnga las la kun spyod yid dran 'gyur
120b5(kha): shes rab lta ba'i mig med gti mug blos//ming shes byed pa med phyir 'dus byas kyis//dge ba ma lus ngag yid kyis ci spyad kyang //phan pa rdul tsam mi nus don med 'khyams//de tsho gzugs bral yid kyi dbang po ni ka kun tshang thogs med 'gro ba'i phyir ni 'khru la//dang rin
120b6(kha): dab chags mkhanga' la 'gro sangs ni//'dod pa'i dbang gis sa khung ste yis 'dzin//de bzhin gzung 'dzin dbang gos lus su 'jug/de tshe gcig gis mtha' dgang len pa/char tshogs sdug bsngal nyen gyur skyangs tshol ba//gnal ba pong spyis sgribs na dag d+hA/
120b7(kha): mdang zhu sa sa chung ba stag/cho la 'du khrod rkyen kyim ma du jug/rnam gzhig ngal ma bya ba gyi/yon gan las la 'das med ga/das das pa'i kho mas den cig nas phyog la rtogs y-i/
121a1(kha): ngag skal med bsam gtan zhi gnas kyi//ye shes bag chags cha mnyam ting 'dzin gyis//rtog pa mtha' mnyam su myong ba'i phyir/ka/'dod khams lha mi gar skyes rang dbang yod//rtogs pa 'bring gi rnal 'byor lta ba ni//dran pa ma yengs tsam du don gas
121a2(kha): ni TA rnam rtog chags pa de nyid zil gyis gnon//dper na mi gang rtsal ldan de shugs kyis//rtsal med non bzhin rje su gsal bas na//dper na khyud sgong nas bya bton bzhin//rnam smin lus dang bral nas don bral te//gtsang ma ris kyi ltar ni skyes nas kyang //mngon par
121a3(kha): 'bras bu sangs rgyas ye shes 'byung //de yang sems dpa' rdo rje snying po nyon//rtogs pa rab kyis blo dngos gcig pa'i phyir//lta ba'i rgyun la yengs pa/mi 'byung phyir//lus kyi sgrib g.yogs bral ba'i skad cig la//'bras bu sangs rgyas rgyal po chos kyi sku//dab med
121a4(kha): /kun bzang ye shes thugs kyi thugs//nam mkha'i mthongasu nyi ma shar mun pa med//de bzhin rnal 'byor lus bral sems nyid l-i//ma rig med par chos nyid gsal ba'i phyir//sangs rgyas ye shes thugs la 'char nub med//rdzogs pa chen po lta ba ye shos gting rdzogs kyi
121a5(kha): rgyud las//'khor 'das gnyis kyis mtshams bstan po'i le'u ste nyer drug pa'o///de nas yang kun rig rgyal pos don bstan pa//theg pa'i rgyal pos chos nyid lhun rdzogs 'di//skal dang ldan pa'i 'khor rnams khyed kyis kyang ka rgyud 'di yid la
121a6(kha): gnas cing 'chang bar gyis//de yang 'dus pa'i rdo rje snying po nyon//spyod pa tshul 'chos byed cing dpob dpon smod//nor pangs chos 'dod thabs kyis 'bru ba la//ye shes gting rdzogs rgyud chen lta ba ni//snod ngan rnams la nam yang bstan par ma gsungs so//bka' bzhin
121a7(kha): ma byas don gyi snying po chos//'jig rten ner phyir don ston rnal 'byor laka/sangs rgyas dkon mchog bka' chad 'byung nges phyir//'jig rten na mkha' 'gro dam can smod ci dgos//gnod sbyin srin pos srog gcad dmar med ltung //da yang 'khor gyis sems dpa'
121b1(kha): kyis cos/brtan pa 'jig pa'i dus 'dir chos kyims kyi//snang po rdzogs car 'di 'byung 'gyur bas//sdod ldan nal 'byor 'gar la gtang bu las/nga meng drang ba rgyums kyi spyod su N+Din/'khor rnams kun sa byang ba glog/khan ga /
121b2(kha): @'byor khas 'tshe chos pa kun//don med tshig mkhas nyam su len pa med//'dus byas chos spangs don la mi 'dus par//bsod nams 'dod phyi grong du chos 'chad 'byung //de yang rdo rje sems dpa' snying po nyon//rnal 'byor gang zhig slong ldan chos//gzhung bzhin
121b3(kha): @don las mi 'da' dad pa can//bka' dgu rdos nas man ngag rgyud 'di tshol//de tshe rnal 'byor las ma don gyi phyi ro//sgra skyibs mi bya rgyud 'di bstan par bgyi//gal te snod ldan ma phrang dus nye na//chos skyod yod pa'i btsug lag khang chen nam//dregs pa'i lha chen
121b4(kha): gnas pa'i brag dkyil du ka/sa phyogs brtags la dang ldan phyi rabs la//phan phyir smon lam btab pa sbas te bzhag/rdzogs pa chen po lta ba yis gting rdzogs kyi rgyud las//'khor la rgyud gtod pa'i le'u ste nyer bdun pa'o///rdzogs pa
121b5(kha): na po b+hA ba le shes gti rdzogs kyi rgyud 'di//u rgyan gyi yul bye ma gsung dru nas//sings la slob dpon d+harma ga bas//u rgyan gyi slob dpon sa hA bo d+hi ma thashad//ngas sa rba r+ya dra po'i ye shes la//ngos pod yul du bsnams nas byon pa'o//sbyin ci
121b6(kha): thugs dem lags s+ho//rdzogs so//dge'o///phyag skad du/badza gu h+ya ga d+has twa ma hA d+h+ye rmAtwAn+tu/da skad du/rdo rje sems dpa' rnamakha' tshe gas pa'i snying po rnal ma'idon ma rgyud/
121b7(kha): dpal rdo rje sems dpa' la phyag 'tsha lo//'di skad bdag gis bshad pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das rang bzhin rdzogs pa chen po//ye shes lhun gyis grub pa//rang byung rig pa'i rgyal po/'od gsal thig le bde ba chen po de//rig 'pho 'gyur med pa'i gnaso sa/
122a1(kha): khang na/dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas dang /khams gsum gyi sems can dang ga lam la rje su lta ba rnams 'byed pa mi mnga' bar thabs gcig tu bzhug so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rig pa'i rgyal po la/ka/rig pa'i rdo rjes 'di skad ces zhus so//e
122a2(kha): mamo'o dgongs pa rnams kyi gsang //stong nyid zhi ba thugs rje'i dang //snying po'i snying po don gyi mchog/rdzogs chen don 'di legs par gsungs//zhes gsol to//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rig pa'i rgyal po ngas//btsal du med pa thig le chen po'i 'don 'di gsungs pa
122a3(kha): @rang byung gsang ba theg le'i ngang //chos kun 'dus pa lus pa med//bgrod med btsal bral lhun gyis grub//sbyangs thob ma yin rdzogs pa'i klong //rig pa'i rgyal po kun gsal ba//'dod pa'i don kun rdzogs pa'i bdag/ye shes rgyamatsho rnam grol lam//thegs/
122a4(kha): / rdzogs mkha' ldang sangs//rang rig legs pa'i snying po nyid//kun gyi rang bzhin chos kyi dbyings//sgro 'dogs skur 'dregs kun gyis spyod//TI b+h+yAM ma hA sau sa kyi srid//khrig tu cig yA kAn rdzogs pas//s+ye m+b+has d+hi s+yA u p+'u g+h+ra/'adz+ra b+hag+h+ra
122a5(kha): ma shar//khyab pa chen po chos kyi khru//stong pa chen po rig pa'i rgya//ye nas dag pas 'dara' ba medo//ye shes gsarab byang chub sems//na mkha' chen po tsog pu nyid//thig le chen po yi bstan paso//sgo bsdu med pa'i dbyings la gnas//sgo skur dmigs par
122a6(kha): yul med pas//rang rig gsal bar bus mtha' med//kun gyi rang bzhin gsang ba'i phyir//rtog dpyod 'dzin pa'i yul gyis stongs//ma yengs rig pa mnyam gnas ba ka ting nge 'dzin 'khor lo bstan mi dgos//rang byung dgongs par kun rdzogs pas//ma brtsa
122a7(kha): sbyin grub 'ang las 'byung //phyag rgya chen po bdag gnas phyir//ma bsgom 'da' ba med pa'i ngang //dngos bral rig pa'i phyag rgya 'di//ye shes snying por 'od 'bar ba//kun gyi snying por bdag gnas pa//'di rang bzhin gting dpog dkar//thig gcig snying po rdzogs
122b1(kha): pa las chos kun rdzogs te ngo bor gnas//don kun yongs rdzogs thig le la//rang bzhin gnas pas rim pa med//snying po rig pa don gyi rgyud//de rgyud theg pa'i yang snying 'dig ye shes phyag rgya rdzogs pa'i mchog/mi 'da' de nyid kho na'i don//'du shes skye/
122b2(kha): ba ma lus zang //rgyu med sems la rkyen bral bas//skye 'jig 'gyur ba ga la yod//ye nas zin pas bya ba med//lhun gyis rdzogs pas bsam pa med//rang las rdzogs pas bsgrub du med/ka/dmigs med don la bsgom du med//dus cig rdzogs pas bgrod/
122b3(kha): dumed//ye shes rig pa'i phyag rgyal//rnam grol 'bras bu kun rdzogs ldan//gsang b.rang byung rig pa'i sgra/brjod pa ma yin don gyis shes ka/'di ltar cig du gnas pa la//tha dang mthing ba 'khrul pa'i lam//snying po sems nyid rdzogs pa la//dran dang rtsi
122b4(kha): po yid byed med//lus dang ngag sems btsal gnas med//ci bder gnas pa dgag sgrub med//ces gsungs so//snying po rnal ma ngon gyi rgyud las gleng bzhi'i le'u ste dang po'o///de nas yang bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa btsas
122b5(kha): med rig pa'i rgyal pos/lhun gyis grub pa'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces gsungs so//ma bcol lhun grub ma bcos chos kyi dbyings//ma btsal ma byas ye nas ji bzhin nyid//rang byung gsang ba'i dbyings 'di brjod bral te//
122b6(kha): mtshon dang brtag cing spyad pa'i yul las 'das//dus gsum rnam drug rig pa dbyer med pas//rig pa dbyer med cig du lhun gyis rdzogs//ma bsags ma sbyangs ye sangs rgyas pa'i klong //mtha' bral rig pa'i klong la bsam zhing brjod du med//se nas
122b7(kha): kun rdzogs bdag nyid lhun rdzogs bkas la mi bgrod lam la mi sbyor zhing //ma rtogs ting 'dzin sbyor ba sgyu ma'i nad//mi btsal rang bzhin gzhag pa grub pa yi ni//ma yengs klong du zhugs na bsgom pa yin//rig pa cig la rdzogs pas 'dus pa yin//
123a1(kha): rab bzhin 'du ba med pas dam tshig yin//ma bcos lhun gyis grub pa 'bras bu yin/gnyis med dang las gnyis gzung thig les bsgribs//thig le gcig la gnyis 'dzin rtog pas bcings//bden pa gcig la nor ba'i lung gis
123a2(kha): lags//khas ma med gracig la log rtog drang bas bsams//8 byung dzin la spyad da gye du 'tshogs//kha ltar yod don la log mthong stobs kyi bltas//bgrong med lam la rnaMs rtog zhen pas 'grong //gzung med mnyam nyid byin ci log tu gzung //ma dag
123a3(kha): dag par bltas pas dag pa rnyog ma langs//rnam rtog phyag rgyas bcings pas ye shes phyag rgya ba shig/ma brjod ye shes gsung mchog brjod pas nyams//mtshan ma'i dkyil 'khor bsgoms pas ye shes dkyil 'khor spangs//btang snyoms
123a4(kha): stong nyid bsgoms pas bden mchog phyag rgya bral//mtshan ma tshig sa zhen pas mtha'i lta bar ltung ka/gang yang mi 'byed pa mchog dbyings la rol//snying po'i snying po rig pa'i ye shes nyid//don gyi don te kun don sems la zug rgyud kyi rgyud lnga kun
123a5(kha): 'brel rang bzhin rdzogs//khyu bral rdzogs lhung bde mchog phyag rgyas gdag//se nas mi 'da' ltar gyis grub par gsal//rang byung cig la bltas dgur rang snang ba//rnam pa lhun grub rol pa ngo mtshar che//rang bzhin gcig la yon tan kun tshangs pas//ma btsal
123a6(kha): gnag pas yon tan rang bzhin rdzogs//me bsags lhun grub rang 'byung 'bras bu la//rag les ma yin gdod nas lhun rdzogs yin//sbyangs thob ma si ni dag pa brdal phrang seMn//dmigs bral 'di nyid mkha' ltar khya par gnas//bsam med brjod bral cho
123a7(kha): dgyis sgras brjod pas rig pa'i yeng shes rnyed mi 'gyur//sa btsal rang gzhag bde chen rgyas thebs na//thams cad ye shes pade mchog phyag rgyar gsal//stong min ma stong mi yin dbu ma yin//gang yin de yin bdag gzhan gang yang min//bsam pa kun
123b1(kha): /nga spyod ba 'dugs yang //ltung zhing rang gas sa rang rig bde mchog byis gargya gsus dbyis la thebs pas 'dar mi sring //sku gsum dbyings la 'dang brang bral du med pas rnyogs dang mang gas pa pas sgrib pa med/ma yengs rang bzhin gsal pas
123b2(kha): rgyurgyas yin//yengs med 'khor lo sgrib pa rnam par gcod//rnyog dang ma rnyog rtog pas ma rnyogs te//ma rnyogs rnyogs dus bzhi yis ma phye la//dus chod ngos 'dzin pade mchog gdam ngag bzhin//rnyog dang ma rnyog gnyis kar gsal gyur na//dam pa'i dgongs pa lcu/
123b3(kha): gyis grub nges pas//rnyog ma gsal zhing dngas ma blang du med//sdig gsum bde bar gcig pas 'khor ba'i 'jigs pa med//lhun grub nyam su myong bas 'bras bu re dogs med//rig pa'i cho 'phrul nyid la rtsol sgrub bya mi dgos/zhes gsungs so//
123b4(kha): snying po don gyi rgyud las ka ma btsal lhun gyis grub pa'i khung phog pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de nas yang bcom ldan 'das rig pa'i rgyal po des ka thams cad gtan la phebs pa'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa 'di skad gsungs so//kye 'dus pa chen/
123b5(kha): po'i rnal 'byor pa//rang sems rdzogs pas rang bzhin gsal//sgra dang ma gtan tshigs kyis//brjod bral gtan la phabs pa med//ma bcos rig pa'i gtan tshigs kyis//ma brjod don gyi ngo bo mtshon//tshul rang bzhin gsher ba ltar//rig pa nyam su shes par bya/
123b6(kha): ji bzhin rtogs pa'i nyams med na//tshig gi brda' tshig gang brjod kyang //brda' sgo rtog pa'i rjesu 'brang //bden bra la rnam rtog lcags kyis bsdams//rang byung rig pa'i thig le yisaka ma khyab pa'i chos med pas ka spro bsdu bral lo dbyings la rdzogs/bde chen rig pa rdo rje'i lung
123b7(kha): mamabacos 'ja gas la dbying su rdzogs//de la der gzhag de gzhag na/'bad pa med pa lhun gyis 'grub//ma btsal lhun gyis grub pa'i phyir//dper na nyi ma'i 'od dang mtshungs//ma bcos sems kyi rang bzhin dang //ci bzhin chog pa'i gtan tshigs kyis//de nyid ma yin gnan
124a1(kha): min te//gnyis med rig pa dus gcig rdzogs/kun rdzogs bde chen yangs dog med//rang sems lhun grub snying po ni ka nyams myong rig pa dpe bzhin ltar//'od gsal 'du 'ashes rnam bral bas//rang gsal rig pa dbyings la rdzogs//rgyud pa thabs kyi
124a2(kha): 'grub cig gis/rang byung 'od gas pa 'a med la//gnyis med don pa mi gso sang /ma bca' lhun gyis grub par mtshon//thams cad rtogs pa'i thobs yin pas/gang ltar da ba'i nal 'byed//mthun 'jug spyod pa rgyam tsho ni//'dul ba'i bangs la bsten pa yin
124a3(kha): dzam dus gcig khyad par ni//rtog pu'i khyad pa pa che chung gis//ngos po cig la dbye ba yin//gnyis med dugs la bar gcig/'dul thabs theg pa bsam yas bka/rang byung zag pa'i snying por gcig/rang byung rig pa mthun pa med//bstan pa rgyam tsho thugs la rdzogs/
124a4(kha): /de thabs kyis ma mtshon na//rang byung gcig gis khyab gnas kyang //rgyal po'i phrug go 'khyams pa ltar//rang gi mtshan nyid ma rtog so//'di ltar rang bzhin gcig pa la//bsam pa'i stobs kyis so sor phye/don dang 'brel ba'i pra khrid kyis//brjod bral sems kyi phyag rgya rtogs/
124a5(kha): /nges pa'i don lnga de ma thag/skal ldan 'bad pa med pas grol//bde mchog don las ma 'das pas//ma btsal mthar phyin sems kyi lam/rim pa med par dus cig 'jug/rig pa sems kyi btsal rdzogs pas//ma btsal lhun grub phyogs ris med//gang la phyogs ris

124a7(kha): dri da rnam dag la//ma lus bya zin lhun gyis grub//ma bcos ma btsal lhun gyis grub//'jug stod mi gnas bsam pas stong //ma zhi rang gzhag grol ba'i lung //gang bskad rnam rtog rtsol bas bcings//rgyub rigs pa'i sems dang pral//ma batshal ma bsgrub zangs
124b1(kha): kyigyikyis gnas//yengs pa med do ci dgar zhog/mi spro mi sku spu yan du khye//ma bsgom yal bar gzhag pas rtogs//mi bsam mi spyod yul mtshol//rgyu 'o chags bral byang chub sems//ma bcos snying po don gyi rgyud//gsal dang mi gsal dam pa dang //tha mal 'du shes ra
124b2(kha): sa//zhes gsungs so//snying po don gyi rgyud las//lung gsum ma bcos pa gtan la phab pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das rang byung rig pa'i rgyul po ngas//rnam dag gsal ba'i ting 'dzi la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces gsu nga /
124b3(kha): rang bzhin mi gnas byang chub sems//mog rtogs sum bcu drug cu yis//med las sod pa'i tshul 'gyur bas//zhen pa'i 'jig den theg pa gnas/bskyed pa meng pa'i byang chub sems//'jig pa med pa nam kha' 'dra//gang ma ji mi rtog rtag spyod 'das//ma brjod rdo
124b4(kha): pa'i theg par gsungs//gtan la phebs pa'i lung bcu yis ka rang bzhin gcig du gsal bar ston ka lung gsum 'dus pa'i snying po 'dika rtse gsum kun 'dus gsang ba'i gter//rang byung sems nyid klong brdal bas//rnam par dbye ba'i chos med pas//rol pa'i ston
124b5(kha): khyebyed par gyis//klong dgu sems nyid bstan par 'chad//lta ba rang byung rdzogs pa'i lung //ma lus kun 'dus rnam par dag/thig le gcig tu kun rdzogs pas//rang byung snying po gcig la rol//gsang ba'i thig la kun gyi dbyings ka mu mtha' med par rang bzhin gnas/
124b6(kha): /sems par gsang ba byungasasapa//'ang rig pa gi rnam par dag/ 'jug rig pa du shes tho so//rig pa ti yin gyi lung//rigs mang pa'i phyis 'jog pa 'i na sum dgyis lam me dgros//kung bang stong nyid sa 'acos pas sung ba zangs kyi khyang /
124b7(kha): kun zhum 'dres gsal bar rdzogs pa'i phyir//mkha' la gza' skar bkram pa 'dra//thig le gcig tu kun rdzogs pas//bya btsal med pa'i gdeng chen bcu//rang bzhin bcu la btsal med pas//'khor lo sgyur rgyal sa 'dzin ltar//sadza ngas rgyal sras skal ldan lan/thabs ni/
125a1(kha): bstan pas go ba yin//ma bcos pabs kyi gdeng gsum gyis//se shes gsal bar mtshams sbyor nas/kun kyang de nyid gsus 'dus pas//'dis kyang brda don dgrol ba yin//thams cad sems nyid lta 'angang bayadang//rom yi'i khyad par cing bye ba/
125a2(kha): rtogs log pa'i grong khyer ba/rang byung rdzogs pa'i yon tan te//rtsol bsgrub med pa'i thabs chen gyis//yengs pa med pa'i dgongs pa gtugs ka rig gsum mchon pa'i tshul sbyor bas//ma bcos rdzogs pa'i rang bzhin nyid //ji srid rtog pargyur bar//de srid bsam gyis mi
125a3(kha): khyab pa//bsam pa'i dbang du bstan pa yin//de dag legs par shes bya ba//btsal med rang bzhin lhun grub pa//kun rdzogs ye khyab rma du grags//ma bcos ma sbyangs dus cig rdzogs//rnam pa'i rtsol ba blo la gang snang ba//ma bcos lhun gyis grub pa'i rang bzhin yin//su
125a4(kha): zhig rig pa 'phrul gyi lde mig gis//rang byung thabs kyi dkyil 'khor sgo phye nas//ma btsal rang byung lhun grub pade mchog mthong //dus gsum 'du 'bral med pa rig pa'i gter//ma bcos lhun gyis grub pa btsal du med//dmigs pa'i 'du shes chad pas seng su med//8 cu/
125a5(kha): tsig sa rnam pa gang du med//gnyis med thig ler gnas pas bgrod du med//gro ngan sem su gsal bas byas pa zin//ma lus lhun gyis rdzogs pa 'bras mi smrab//ma skyes rnam par dag pas sgrib kyi spyod ka mu b+h+yas rang gzhin rdzogs pas 'dri las bral//di nyid rtags pa
125a6(kha): kun sgru ba kun rdzogs pas//rdzogs chen thig le gcig tu gnas par bshad//zhes gsungs so//snying po don gyi rgyud las//byang chub sems rtogs par bya ba'i rlabs dang rtogs pa'i tshul bsnyen po'i le'u sgo bzhi pa'o///de nas yang bcom ldan 'das rang
125a7(kha): byung rig pa'i rgyal po des//bde ba chen po'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//'di skad ces gsungs so//bde ba chen po rang rig gsal ba'i thugs//gnyis med ngang la ma lus kun rdzogs pas//snying po snying po rang bzhin stong pa'i lam ka bde chen na mapa' mtha' dang mnya
125b1(kha): thar ro//snying po mchog te ngan la rdzogs par gnas//ma skyes 'od gsal bde ba nyid rtogs na//bde mchog nam pa'i kham su mi shong ngo //yangs tsho'o thig le gcig gi dang //snying po gcig la rtogs tshul mtha' yas pas/'bras bu khyang par grangs las 'das pas
125b2(kha): gsungs//ma skyes snying po sgro skur med pa'i dbyings//sangs rgyas sems can ma skyes snying por mthong //snying po ma mthong rdzogs sems nyid mthong //mthar thug med cing dbyings la bri ba med/rang byung bde chen don 'di khyad par 'phags//sgra brjod tshig la dga' bas
125b3(kha): /don mi rtogs//snying po'i snying po bsam brjod cir mi dmigs//ma bsams bsam pa gnyis med gcig gis mtha'//mtha' dang dbus la mi gnas mngon par rtog dpyod bral//ma rtogs mi bsams de bzhin nyid la gnas//bde chen rang rig mi rtog pa//btsal bral rig
125b4(kha): pa dbyings la gnas//bde mchog gsang ba'i thig le nyid//na mkha' khyab pa'i tshul du gnas//ma skyes snying po byang chub sems//'du 'bral med pa'i ngang la bzhugs//rang byung byang chub snying po la//ma btsal ma bsgrubs rang bzhin gzhag/gang dag ye shes dang yin
125b5(kha): yang //bsam pa'i ngos la phyin ci log/smig rgyu la ni chur blo bzhag/don la gzhag blang med na yang //brtags pa'i 'du shes bsam mi khyab//byang chub sems kyi rang bzhin ni//kun dang rig pa dus mnyam pas//gang dag rig pa med par gnas//'di dag 'di ltar
125b6(kha): mchog yin//ma bcos snying po mchog gi don//me btsal rang byung mchog gis shes//rang 'bar btsal med rig pa'i sems//gsal ba'i mar mo'i 'du shes med//rgyu ba med pa'i tshul gyis dbye//phyag rgya chen po kun thebs pas//mtshan ma'i rtog pa gso mi 'gyur//
125b7(kha): /rang bzhin 'khor lor ye brtan pas//rigs pa'i dkyil 'khor bskyed med//ye shes 'bar ba'i bzhugs gnas la//rang byung sgrib med gsal bas brgyan//dus gsum rgyal ba'i rang bzhin pas//gnyis med ngang la don kun rdzogs//rang byung rig pa'i rgyal po la/
126a1(kha): #dran 'byung rig pa'i 'du shes med//sngags dang phyag rgya mchod pa dang //kar spyod cher byas ma lus paka/btsal med log pa'i ri bo 'dis//bdag nyid 'ching 'gyur ci zhig bya//bya ba ma lus kun gtang la//sa byas ma btsal cing mi 'dzin//'du shes med

134b6(kha): 'di ltar rig par bya'o//ye shes lus can de bzhin gshegs pa de ni sku'i 'od la bzhugs shing /gsung gi zer las rol te/de'i phyir rnam par dag pa na mkha' dang 'dra ba'i sku'o//de yang yangs pa dang /kun la khyab par gnas pa dang /'gog pa med par snyoms par
134b7(kha): gyur pa ni na mkha'o//gsal zhing snang ba thams cad ni sku'i 'od yin zhing //na mkha'i rang bzhin ma yino//chen po lnga la so sor gsal ba dang /snang ba chen po yod pa ni/chen po lnga nyid rigs lnga'i skur gyur pa'i 'od yin gyi//na mkha'i khams kun la khyab pa'i 'od
135a1(kha): @dam na mkha'i rang bzhin las byung ba ni ma yin ma yin te/sku mdog ji lta ba bzhin na mkha'i mdog kyang de bzhin gyur pa yin no//bar snang la snang ba ston pa nyi zla dang skar ma sogs pa/rang gi snang ba'i 'od 'byung ba dang /bar snang snang bar ston pa yang //
135a2(kha): sku rin po che'i 'od dang /gsung gi 'od zer spros pa'i zer nyid yin no//chen po bzhi las byung ba'i kha dog rnams dang /rnam pa sna tshogs su spros pa dang /bar snang la sprin gyi kha dog so sor snang ba//'ja' tshon gyi kha dog 'byung ba yang rigs so so'i sku dang gsung las
135a3(kha): /byung ba yin gyi//nam mkha' dang kha dog kyang chen po bzhi las byung ba ni ma yin no//sku mdog dkar na nam mkha' yang dkar//sku mdog ji lta ba bzhin du na mkha'i mdog kyang de dang 'dra ba dang chen po bzhi las byung ba'i skye bo rnams kyi kha dog kyang de dang 'dra'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i
135a4(kha): sku ni chen po lnga las bstan zhing //'byung ba chen po bzhi'i rgyud las skyes pa ma yin no//'byung ba ba ni dang 'byung ba las gyur pa thams cad dang /skye ba bzhi las skyes pa'i sems can thams cad dang /stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus su/
135a5(kha): ba med pa rnams ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gsung thugs nyid las byung zhing //sku gsung thugs kyi rol pa chen po rnam pa cir yang rol cing chos thams cad lo mngal mdzad pa dang /chos thams cad la rol pa rtag tu mdzad pa'i don du de bzhin gshegs pas mdzad//
135a6(kha): chen po lnga med pa'i sngon rol na//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni de bzhin du rang byung ba ste/sku'i 'od la bzhugs shing gsung gi phrer la rol lo//chos chen po na du byung ba las/byang chub sems se nas mi mnyam pa'i le'u ste brgyad pa'o//sams/
135a7(kha): ga kyis kyi nga da ga i man ma na ma la masas cas nyi ga sa du bya la/mas rgyas sa lags pa tan rgyas
135b1(kha): rgyas cang ye shas skyes pas gsang ba na du byung ba'o//bdag nyid kyis rig pa'i ye shes las ma skyes pa dang ma byung ba dang /rang rig pa'i se shes kyis ma khyab pa'i chos gang yang med pas gsang ba na du byung ba'i chos chen po ste/sems dpa' chen po nyid dang //byang chub sa/
135b2(kha): dab rnams kyis rtogs par dka'i phyir gsang ba mchog gi dam pa'o//chos chen po rmad du byung ba las gsang ba bstan pa'i le'u ste dgu pa'o////sems dpa' chen po/rdo rje sems dpe'i thugs gsang ba'i rgyud ni/sku gsung thug su ma lus pa gcig
135b3(kha): kyang med do//bde bar gshegs pa'i sku gsung thugs nyid du gyur pas gsang ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa nyid la rnam par dag pa'i phung po lnga yod de/gzugs rnam par dag pa thams cad nyid du gyur pa'i phung po dang /rnam par grol ba'i tshor ba 'gag pa med pa'i
135b4(kha): phung po dang //skye ba med pa'i 'du shes chos nyid du gyur pa'i phung po dang /len pa med pa'i 'du byed thams cad tha dad pa'i sgo'i phung po dang //rnam par dag pa na mkha' dang 'dra ba ye shes byung ba'i phung po lnga kun gyi bdag nyid du yod pa dang /kun gyi bdag nyid du gyur pas
135b5(kha): sku'i sku rnam por snang mdzad ces gsungs pa ni sku lnga lhun gyis rdzogs pas longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku ste/rnam par snang mdzad do//de la rnam par snang mdzad ces gsungs pa ni/kun ga ni rnam par shes pa ni sku lnga nyid du gyur pa yin te/de nyid la sku rnam pa sna tshogs
135b6(kha): yang sngang nas steng mdzad da/dar phyi semam dpa' chen badzin gsungs nano//das pa dang /ma 'das pa dang /nga wAr byung ba rnams dang /dusna dang dus kyi so sor gyur pa dang /dus gsus kyi ba ba gshegs pa rnams cad dung /skye nga
135b7(kha): skye bo dang /skye gri rnams dang /chen po lnga dang /'byung ba bzhi las byung ba'i chos thams cad dang sku la rnam par dag pa la sa pa byung ba gcig kyang med de//de dag thaMd su wa las byung bas zad nas thams cad du su wa nyi dus gyur pa gcig sa med do//den
136a1(kha): sargyas su su gyur bgacig kyang me /bzhi gshegs pa na m+b+hi nga sa d+hAm smra //sa shAm pa mas sa/blan pa sa mang //la nga/ma nang gsus shes u
136a2(kha): dag pa ni cir yang sprul cing sang 'gyur ning kun tu 'gyur ba nyid yin te//sku wa las gyur pa'i chos nyid la//nga rol pa chen po rdo rje rnam par rol pa mdzad/rtag par bzhugs shing rtag par rnam pa cir yang rol lo//rdo rje rnam par rol pa chen po ci'i yang mdzad do//
136a3(kha): /chos nyid las mnga' mdzad pa'i rol pa chen pos sku'i cho 'phrul cir yang snang zhing cir yang ston cho 'phrul chen po cir yang mdzad do//sku cir yang rol pa'i cho 'phrul gyis//rtag par longs spyod mdzad pas so//de'i phyir sems dpa' chen po phyi'i spyod yul thad
136a4(kha): 'di ltar yin no//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzigs mo'i//yul lo//nga nyid las byung ba'i chos nyid do//ye nas ma byung ba 'byung bar byed pa'i yul lo//ma byung ba 'byung bar byed
136a5(kha): pa'i dam tshig gi yul lo//sku gsung thugs mngon par bstan pa nyid do//sku gsung thugs mthar mi zad pa'i mdzad do//ston pa mthar mi zad pa'i don nyid do//sku gsung thugs rol pa'i cho 'phrul chen po rol pa nyid do//rdzu 'phrul chen po nyid kyi rang bzhin
136a6(kha): ni nga'i mthu las byung ba nyid do//nga'i stobs las byung ba'i chos so//sku gsung thugs mi zad pa'i rgyan gyi 'khor lo nyid do//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad ni nga nyid kyi yul lo//bzhugs gnas so//gzhal yas
136a7(kha): khangango //'di dag thams cad ni nga yi sku'o//nga'i gsung ngo //nga'i thugs so//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag zad ni/sangs rgyas kyis rdzogs pa chen po nyid do//sangs rgyas nyid do//byang chub chen por rdzogs pa nyid do//sangs
136b1(kha): kyi zhing bde ba chen po nyid do//byang chub kyi sa nyid do//byang chub sems rlud du byung ba thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid yin no//sku rmang du byung ba nyid yin no//gsung chad du byung ba nyid min//thugs na du byung ba nyid yino//chos rmad du byung ba nyid yin no//gsang ba mdu/
136b2(kha): byung ba nyid yin no//rol pa rmad du byung ba nyid mi no//mdzod pa rma du byung ba nyid yin no//rdzu 'phrul rma du byung ba nyid yino//byang chub sems rmad du byung ba yang 'di nyid yin no//sangs rgyas kyi sa chen po rma du byung ba yang 'di nyid yin no//sangs rgyas rnams
136b3(kha): b+ha bzang du byung ba yang 'di nyid min no//sangs rgyas rnams kyi longs spyod mamar phyin pa yang 'di nyid yodo//skyon pa phun sum tshogs pas bstan pa sang 'di nyid min no//ban bathun su mchags pas gnapa sang 'di nyid man//sangs rgyas rnams
136b4(kha): bde ba chen po yang 'di nyid do//sangs rgyas rnams kyi 'dod pa'i yon tan lnga yang 'di nyid yin no//'dod yon lnga'i mtshan nyid kyang na du byung ba 'di nyid yi no//sangs rgyas rnams kyi thugs mthar thug pa yang 'di nyid yin no//sku'i sprul pa'i chos
136b5(kha): nyid la rol pa'i cho 'phrul gyi chos kyi nyid 'khor lo bskor ba'i chos nyid kyi 'khor yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yin no//'khor ba chos nyid ma rig pas 'khor zhing chos nyid la chos nyid gnas pa yang rma du byung ba 'di nyid yin no//sku gsung thugs las ma
136b6(kha): gtogs pa stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams na/'khor ba dang nyon mongs pa'i ming yang med do//sangs rgyas 'byung ba las ma gtogs pa'i sems can du skye ba'i ming yang med do//sku snang ba las ma gtogs pa sems can gyi rang bzhin
136b7(kha): rje na ma de/pas rgyas pa chen po las gtonas pa mams panang mug cwom ma med do//sa sa rgyas ma ngas ca ma rdzogs pa ga zu las sa rtenas kyipa stong gsus kyi myong chen pa 'dzin
137a1(kha): //mas na sems dan gyi yul dang gnas nyid las med pas dralas byung ba'i chos las sems can gyi yul dang gnas med do//un du byung ba dang /byang chub kyi sems cangad du byung ba med pa'i rang bzhin bstan pas nges 'ar
137a2(kha): gsang ba'i man ngag bstan pa'o//sems dpa' chen po rgyal ba rnams kyi sku//sde tshogs cir yang snang ba ni//kun du rin chen lta bu ste//zhes gsungs pa yin no//sku'i kha dog ji ltar snang zhing sna tshogs cir yang rol pa ni rin po che dang 'dra ste/
137a3(kha): ste/rin po che ni dkar po yang rin po che'o//dmar po dang ser po dang sngon po dang /ljang gu la sogs pas ni kha dog ci 'dra yang rin po che nyid do//'di ni rin po che yin la/'di ni rin po che min pa ni yi no//de bzhin du rgyal ba rgyal ba rnams
137a4(kha): sku yang kha dog thams cad du snang ba dang /dbyibs thams cad ji ltar bur 'dug pa dang /dri ci mnan pa dang /ro ci ltar bro ba dang /gzugs ji ltar snang ba dang /sgra ji ltar thos pa dang /reg bya ji ltar thos pa dang /ba thams cad nyid kyi sku yin te/
137a5(kha): rgyal ba'i rgyal ba rnams kyi sku yin gyi kha dog dang dbyibs ni rgyal ba'i sku yin/'di ni ma yin pa lta bu ni ma yino//de'i phyir sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes sku//sna tshogs gzugs can yid las skyes//zhes gsungs so//de bzhin gshegs pa
137a6(kha): rnams kyi thugs ni/ye shes brgyad so sor dbyer med pa gang gi mi chod pa'i don rig pa'i yis kyi sku dang gnyisu med par shar ba ni thug so//sems dpa' chen po/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di dag thams cad de bzhin
137a7(kha): gshegs pa rnams kyi thugs las byung zhing thugs nyid las ma gyur pa nyid do//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams zad pa'i mtha' med pas//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' las mu med pa yang zad cing med pa'i dus med/
137b1(kha): stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas pa mu med pa la zad cing stongs pa'i dus med pa ni bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs nyid yin te/sku gsung thugs zad cing stongs pa'i dus med pa nyid/
137b2(kha): mdu byung ba yin no//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs las ma gtogs par stong gsum gzhan na med do//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi gsung ni sku gsung thugs so sor dbyer med pa'i don dang /rang bzhin kun la sna
137b3(kha): bar byed cing so so'i don go ba nyid du ston pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi gsung ngo //gsung las gyur pa rnams ni sgra skad sna tshogs cir yang 'byung ba dang /ming dang tshig mang po rnams la sogs pa dang /sgra skad mang po 'byung ba rnams la
137b4(kha): gsung ma gyur pa gcig kyang med de/sku dang thugs 'di nyid gsung ngo //gsung thugs nyid sku yin te/gsung sprul pa'i sku/thugs snying po byang chub mchog gi sku/sku longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku//sems dpa' chen po/de bzhin gshegs pa rnam
137b5(kha): kyi rang bzhin ni//su rmad du byung ba 'di nyid yin par rig par bya'o//ye nas yod cing rtag par bzhugs/rang byung tsho ba'i bdag nyid sku/bdag nyid ye nas dag pa chos kyi sku//bodag nyid sku gsum lhun gyis grub//'gyur ba med pas bsod nams
137b6(kha): sU tu ye nas rdzogs pas sprul pa'i sku//lhan gyis grub rdzogs pas longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku/bya rtsal med pa'i snying po byang chub sku//chos chen po rmad du byung ba las sku gsung thugs bstan pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///sems
137b7(kha): dpa' chen po//'dod chags dang ni zhe sdang dang /gti mug nga rgyal phrag dog ni/rdo rje theg pa chen po nyid//snying rje med par gsod pa dang /'jigs pa med par sbyor ba dang //gtsang che med par za ba dang /rang gi khu bas rang mchod pa/rdo rje theg pa'i chos
138a1(kha): nyid yin//sems can brtse ba med pa dang sham byin len dang mi tshangs spyod//gzhan la slong bar mi byed pa//rdo rje theg pa'i dam tshig ste//ji snyed rang byung byang chub sems//bde ba gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor lo//rgyal ba bdag nyid
138a2(kha): rtag par bzhugs//rdo rje theg pa gsang bam du byung ba'o//gang zhig byang chub mchog 'dod pa//rtag tu bdag nyid mchod par bya//mchod pa kun gyis rtag tu mchod//yid bzhin rin chen la sogs rnams//sangs rgyas 'dod na bdag nyid mtshad//
138a3(kha): sangs rgyas zhing khams thams cad du//bdog cag kun gyi lha yin pas//kun gyi mchod pas bdag la mchod//sems can thams cad bsad nas ni//rgyal ba mnyes te sras su skye//'dod chags 'dod pas rtag tu sbyin//'di ni gsang ba'i
138a4(kha): byang chub sems//sangs rgyas nyid du chud mi zo//de ni sangs rgyas dngos grub yin//dngos grub bdag la yod pa'i phyir//yid du 'ong ba'i bdag nyid bsgom//mkhas pas mchod pa bdag las byung //bdag las byung ba'i mchod pa rnams//bdag nyid bdag la mchod
138a5(kha): bar bya//mkhas pas bdag gi khu ba dag/sbyor ba'i don gyis rtag tu dbyung //'di ni sangs rgyas bdag nyid yin//spyod pa mkha' dang mtshungs pa so//'di ni rdo rje theg pa'i spyod ba cha du byung ba'o//chos chen po rmad du byung ba las/rmad du byung ba bstan pa le'u ste ba
138a6(kha): gcig pa'o///bdag les byu ba'i khu ba yis//bdag gi lus la rab brgyan nas//sangs rgyas mthong bas yid du 'ong //'jig rten khams rnams dbang gyur pa//nga'i lus 'di sngags kyi lha//rtag par mchod na sangs rgyas 'grub//gang gis mchod pas/
138a7(kha): dngos grub thob//ngag dang yid kying phyag rgya yis//spyod pa ci yang sprul pa'i sku//dngos grub mchog rnams bdag las byung //byang chub myur du thob pa yin//lus dang ngag dang gsal ba'i sngags//na mkha' bzhin du mnyam par gnas//sems la dmigs pa bdag
138b1(kha): /myu ma ga su gas/ swA sa / smyag skyed//sems su /rna na na pos tshun ma
138b2(kha): gyi hUM ni yin pas ngos grub pan nas bslad du me sa//dus gsus kyi bde ba gshegs pa gas len po cad do//sems dpa chen no//sa sa rgyas su gyi byang chub sems 'di ni sas rgyas nyid kyi lugs 'byung babyed zhi no// byams sa
138b3(kha): sman gyi grags lnga gnas//rdo rje sems dpa'i shes rab kyis//ye shes pa rol phyin pa'i gnas//dus gsum gcig ste rtag par bzhugs nas nam mata sems dpa' chen mo de bzhin te/rnam par sdang mdzad sa shes ni/bang por gshegs pa'i thugs pa
138b4(kha): gnas/mi gsun pa mi snang yang //sems la gsal bar man pa sdang//del ldan rnams kya rigs mig sa s+ho// sa 'grus chu ba med/mas stong sus gyi nga pa 'jig rted gyi ngas 'di dag
139a1(kha): @cad ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku dri ma med pa nyid yin no//de'i phyir 'di skad bka' stsal to//ye shes 'ba' zhig las byung ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi byang chub sems//sangs rgyas nyid kyis rnyed pa yin//bde bar gshegs pa kun gyi gsang //chos
139a2(kha): chen po rmang du byung ba las/gsang bam du byung ba'i le'u skye bcu gnyis pa'o///sems dpa' chen po nyon cig/bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi rgyud gsang ba'i don rnam pa gsum ste/don dang lus dang sems pa'i gsang ba dang /gsum mo//de don ni
139a3(kha): kri ba'i don ma yin gyi nges pa'i don gtan la phab pa'o//de la bde bar gshegs pa rnams dang /snod bcud na mkhas bsdus pa'i chos//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus lus pa med pa thaMd don gcig ste sku gsung thug su 'duso//sku gsung
139a4(kha): kyi don ni dbyer med de//gang gis mi shigs gang gis mi chod//gang gis mi phyed rdo rje'o//sku gsung thugasu ma gyur pa'i dngos po 'am ma lus pa'i chos gcig kyang med pas sku gsung thugs lhun gyis rdzogs par gtan la phab pa'o//de'i phyir rdo rje chos so//rdo rje theg pa'o//
139a5(kha): lu 'di bsgom pa la shes par bya'o//bsgom pa ni lha dang bdag tu don gcig pas gnyis su med pa la bya'o//shes par bya ba ni chos thams cad kyi don sa nur bar shes pa dang /bdag nyid kyi rang bzhin shes par bya ba dang /rtsu ba ma nor bu shes par bya bya dang /rdo rjes
139a6(kha): dpa'i thugs gsang ba'i rgyud la ma nor shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i rang bzhin shes par bya ba dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i yon tan dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i rnam par rol pa chen po ma nor ba shes pa dang /rdo rje sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /chos nyid la longs
139a7(kha): spyod chen pos mnga' mdzad pa'i don ma nor bar shes par bya'o//nges pa'i gsang ba'i man ngag ni rma du byung ba'i don no//rmadu byung ba gsang ba ni thams cad bdag nyid las byung zhing bdag nyid kyis mdzad//bdag nyid las spros shing bdag nyid de la rol lo//dag las/
139b1(kha): /sprul zhing bdag nyid yongasu sdud//bdag nyid las byung ba'i chos la//bdag nyid longs spyod mdzad/bdag nyid kyi yon tan las bdag nyid che ba ston//bdag nyid las byung ba'i chos rnams la bdag nyid ston par byed/slar yang bdag nyid kyi dbyings 'dus te/bdag
139b2(kha): da pas ma byung bang ma/bdag nying las ma myur ba gcig kyang med de//chos chen po//blo byung ba las gas ban du byung ba'i le'u ste gsum //sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs ni gang /ba ga shegs thams cad 'byung gnas sogs
139b3(kha): rnam par shes pa brgyad ni//gsang ba 'dus pa'i dkyil 'khor ye shes rang las byung ba ye shes kyi lha nyid do//rnam par shes pa'i yul brgyad ni//mchod pa'i lha mo brgyad dang /dngos grub kyi rdzas brgyad do//sems dpa' chen po sgo lnga ste//shes rab zab mo'i sgo dang /
139b4(kha): gsang rgyud mthar mi 'gog pa'i sgo dang //chos kyi 'khor lo mthar bskor ba'i sgo dang /sku gsung thugs nyid du gyur pa'i sgo dang /'bras bu yod pa'i sgo'o//sems dpa' chen po sgo lnga'i rnam par shes pa la//yid kyi dkyil 'khor gsal bar gyur pa ni yi ge 'khor lo
139b5(kha): gsale ba'i dkyil 'khor gsang ba nyid de//rnam par shes pa dang /yul dang dbang po rnams ni rnam par shes pa'i tshogs yin te//mtshan dang dpe byad kun 'das te yid kyi lha'o//de'i phyir ngag ni 'das pa'i sku//lus ni da ltar byung ba'i sku//sems ni ma byung ba'i sku//ngag
139b6(kha): ni 'das pa'i lam/lus ni da ltar bzhugs pa'i lam//sems ni phyis 'byung ba'i phalam nyid do//ngag ni 'das pa'i yul//lus ni ltar bzhugs pa'i yul//sems ni phyis byung ba'i mulo//sems dpa' chen po de'i phyir//sangs rgyas kyi mkhyen pa'i yul//sangs rgyas
139b7(kha): kyi rnal 'byor ro//yang dag bdag me de bzhin nyid//yang dag lam ste yi ge med//'gro ba med pas lam yang med//kun spyod yon tan rnam lnga ni//ye shes rol pa'i rgyan nyid yin//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rnams//sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i yis lam//sku
140a1(kha): gsung thugs kyi byang chub sems/byang chub sems ni thar pa'i lam/chos nyid 'khor ba rgya mtsho mthara' rings la//nyon mongs sems can gnas pa rnams//byang chub sems ni thabs chen gyis//bsad pa 'ba' zhig las su rung //mchod pa chen po bdas
140a2(kha): ches 'dod chags chen po dga' ba'i mchog/mchod pa chen po zhe sdang che//zhe sdang chen po rdzogs sangs rgyas//mchod pa chen po gti mug che//gti mug blo te gti mug che//phrag dog chen po dga' bas dga'//mchod pa chen po nga rgyal che//nga rgyal po rgyal bas rgyal//
140a3(kha): sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thugs ni thugs rje rol pa mdzad pa'i don du byang chub sems rol pa yul las ma gtogs pa/dmyal ba'i gnas ni ye nas med//dmyal ba'i rgyu med de'i dngos kyang med//sems dpa' chen po ma skyes pa'i chos rnams

140b2(kha): smo pa sa//da nas bcom ldan 'das dam par snang ma chen pa'i seng na drang chuban bzugs par sku las/sangs rgyas thams pad 'dus nas/tshig mun par dig ces gsos to//jig rten ma pas pa rdzogs sangs rgyas//gang sheg sog mar byang chub sems/
