146a1(kha): /sgral sogs de bzhin rig par gags pas ni/khung po la sogs 'khor dang cas pamed pa nan gags shing 'dza ga pa'i dag bar ba 'khor ba zhes nis byamed pa'i 'khor ba'i 'kho mo sa ni gags/de la 'khor ba nas ni kun gyis ngos pa'o sa
146a2(kha): bzhin cid dbyangs dang mya ngan 'das pas sus mthong ba nyid 'khor ba ste//sems dang sems las byung ba'i chos mthong bas ni spos ci dgos te 'khorang sa chen po yis//sa bcos thig le'i chos cig cir yang mi snang bas//snang zhing brtags pas chos ni gang nas kyang ni
146a3(kha): btsal du med//de med pa la 'khor ba mi gnas mya ngan 'das bral med//dpyad du med pa'i sa la sus kyang sbrang du med de kun bzang nyid kyis brtags par rab tu dka'o//ye nas bya ma dgos pa'i chos la cir yang mthong du med pas ci yang bsam mi bya//'khor ba yud tsam ma
146a4(kha): grub ma myong bas na mya ngan 'das pa'ang med//mya ngan 'das med pa nyid mi rtog rnal 'byor lam chen no//de nyid g.yo ba med pas ye shes gsal rim dag cing rnam pa'i 'byung 'jug zhing //mi rtog mi g.yo ba de yang dag sangs rgyas nyid//de ni thig le cig gis bsdus pa'i
146a5(kha): chos nyid kun tu bzang po bas/'khor ba'i rlung chen rgya mtsho 'am na mkha' lta bur che ba 'ang //mi rtog mi g.yo'i ngang du bcom ldan 'das//gnyis med thig le byang chub bde ba'i lam chen 'di la la 'phrang med//de phyir byang chub snying po'i 'di nyid las chos rnams gang yang med//
146a6(kha): dmigasu med pa'i chos dang dmigasu pa'i chas rnams ma lus pa//dmigs pa nyid dang mi dmigs pa mnyam pa nyid do//phyi rol gyi ni sul rnams sgyu mar yid ches pas na nang gi sems kyang ma grub pas na rnam par mi rtog pa'o//sgyu mar gnyis kar dbyer med pas na gnyis me
146a7(kha): mnyam pa'i ngang //yid kyi rgyu ba sgo lngar 'byung ba rgyun mi 'chang ba de bzhin du//chos 'di thams cad kyis ni ngo bo med pas grub pa med pa'i sgo rnams mi 'tshol bas//ci la'ang mi sems ma rtog mi dmigs gang la'ang mi gnas par sgo mo//de la dmigs byar med pas mnyam nyid de
146b1(kha): yin te chos rnams ngo bo nyid kyi sku'o//byang chub kyi sems nya mo bag la nyal las 'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa gnyisu med pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das chen po nyid kyi bya byed kyi chos thams cad brjod pa/mnyam
146b2(kha): pa'i rgyam tho bya byed med pa'i drurlung min de cod ni//lus tha mi bcing bar bya ba yi nas yong ma yin//thams cad 'dzin pa'i dbang bsgyur de yis ma gzung med//gzung med pams pad pa sis byas pa med par rig/go ba'i snying po du la gso ba gang sang me
146b3(kha): bzhin 'pho 'gyur med pa'i chos de kun gyi 'bras//snying po'i snying po yin pas snying po kun gyi mchog/'dus byas rnam par dag pa'i lam gyi phugs chen yin//so sor rtog pa'i shes rab kyis kyang de la rtag tu ga la yod/chos dang chos rnams 'khrul pa
146b4(kha): rnams kyis de la gzung ba dang /sus kyang ma mthong mthong med sul de rnam par spangs//chos so chag pa 'khrul pa rnams ni de la lam du 'dod/lam 'dod de la lam mi gnas de lam mtha' ring //rnam grol lam ni sus kyang btsar du med pa'i lam can /
146b5(kha): de la mi rnams la gnas pa'i chos de ye nas yod//rgya so rgya so nga rgyas thams cad rgyus//tha dad min par kun ldan de dang ldan//de ni yongs kyi lam ste de la snod du med//ma gsal dri ma rnam par dag pa rang bzhin chos//g.yo ba'i 'byung 'jug rnam par spas
146b6(kha): bas 'bru shes zad //yang dag sangs rgyas dmigs pa med pa ni//ye shes yongs kyi mi shes snying po yin/mo g.yos spun gyis grub pas thig le chen po nyid//spos med spros dang bral bas byang chub snying po'i sku//pad chen yongs kyi lam de sangs rgyas dang /
146b7(kha): sgyu ma'i sgyu mar gnas te sgyu ma'ang med//sgyu ma brdzun gyi chos rnams sgyu ma 'dra//sgyu mas sgyu mar brtags te sgyu ma 'ang med//skad cig tsam yang sgyu mas sgyu ma ma mthong phyir//chos rnams ma lus de 'dra mthong ba med//'du shes zad pas byang chub snying po
147a1(kha): @mthong //rnam par mi sems rang rig nyid kyi dbang //ci yang mi bsgom pa ni sgom pa'i mchog ces bya//ci yang mi dran pa ni yang dag sangs rgyas dran//rtag tu sangs rgyas dran zhing dmigs pa mi 'byung na//mnyamya snyoms yang dag de bzhin nyid ces brjod//
147a2(kha): ci tsam dmigs pa'i yul med cho su brtags dang bral//yul la mi dmigs yul nyid de bzhin brdzun//brdzun phyir shes pa'ang brdzun te sangs rgyas nyid kyang brdzun//brdzun zhes bdags pa'ang brdzun te brdzun nyid yod ma hong //smig sgyu cho 'phrul pa'ang de 'dra yod//
147a3(kha): 'khrul phyir yang dag nyid kyang skad cig mthong mi srid//snang stong ngo bos dben phyir de bzhin de bzhin nyid//thig le gcig gis bsdus phyir de bzhin yod ma myong //yang dag nyid la yang dag yang dag med//yang dag med phyir chos rnams thaMd med//de med pa la sangs rgyas gnas
147a4(kha): te sangs rgyas nyid kyi dang //sangs rgyas nyid kyang rnam par dag pa'i dbyings te dbyings kyi rgyu//yod med rtag chad gnyi su med pa'i lam//'khor ba mya ngan 'das pa gnyis med cig char ro//gcig med gcig tu 'dzin pa rnam rtog rgyun//'dzin pa de la rtog pa 'dus phyir yang dag
147a5(kha): med/yang dag med par shes pa de 'du shes med pa'i lam//de yis sangs rgyas kun bsdus pho brang chen por gnas//lam gyi phul te lem nad thams cad sel//spros bral thig le thig phran kun gyi mchog/ming tsam nyid du gdagus med mtshon pa'i ying dang bral//mi
147a6(kha): skye mi 'gag cir mi dgongs pa rnam dag les chen min//mi g.yo gso rdul med pas rnam mi tig/mi rtog nyid la gang mi gnas pa de ni thams cad mkhyen//mes spyan mkhyen spyan gyi nang na mchog tu gyur pa'i spyan//mtha' dbus khyab par 'dzin pa'i sangs rgyas spyan zhes des
147a7(kha): gdags//rgyal ba'i rgyal ba kun rgyal ma nyid rgyal//byang chub chen po'i tshul mchog rnam par dag pa nyid//rgyal ba'i rgyal bar gyur pa rgyal mchog go//stong nyid rang bzhin yis chen po'i ngang nyid las//sku gsung thugs phyung de la rab gnas pa'i//de las snying po mchog phyang dkyil
147b1(kha): / shA sgra sa//thAg su sa grus gru su sa b+hU / na rnams stsu
147b2(kha): byo 'grol gas//de la zhes rdzogs pa rjon na rkyen phre na//sgyi gsu ma khrus lus pa meng pa'i rnas bta pas//thig sa chen por sgoms 'dzag ce //dwag ni mardzas sa mangs pa byed pade//'dzig
147b3(kha): gsum brtul med de bzhin nyid du 'ub//chung che'i phyag rgya'i snying pos btsan par btab pa'i rgya/de li ma phyung de las thams cad de bzhin phyung mi phyung //thams cad phyag rgyas btab kyang che ba cig gi dang //che ba lnga ldan snying po med pa'i don nyid ni//spyi 'thun las kyis
147b4(kha): mi sgrib g.yo med rang bzhin gnas//ye shes rnam grangs bsam yas dpag mi lang //ye shes gnyis las gzhan du g.yos pa med//nor bu rin chen tshul du 'byin pa'i rgyu//gang yang mi bskor mi len thig le yongs kyi lam//zla ba lta bur rnam grol dam pa'i sku//phyag rgya
147b5(kha): chen po'i ngang du thams cad btsan par gnas//ye shes 'od gsal stong smon mtshan med gsal//sus kyang ma bcos thig le nyid las nyams pa'ang med//gang yang de yod pa la 'dzin pa'i rtog pa yod phyir byang chub med//de yis de byas byang chub de las nyams te de
147b6(kha): ma yin//de ya de'i skyon chen min te sus kyang bskur yang med//ma bsdus de byas te yod min phyir de yang med/btsal bas nang du gyur te 'gro//de ma thag pa rnams kyis de rtags de las nyam//de la gang po gang spang med de rtsa bye stong //de'i sprin 'bras bu mong pa
147b7(kha): 'gyur sa bon rdul//de gyur pas na thig le nyid med pa ste dpag mi lang //stong pa'i stong pa nyid de ye nas stong //rtag chad khyad por nyams te stong par gnas pa min//de nyid yis gzhi 'bras smin pa'i rgyu snying po'o//de lta yongs kyi bdag nyid thams cad mtshungs
148a1(kha): ba'i du sus kyang ma byin de na rang bzhin thams cad gnas//de nyid 'grong med lam du snying po de ma thag/rgyal ba'i yum de byang chub chen por kun mtshungs grong //rnam par mi rtog rtog med chos kyi sku yang yin//rdzogs chen kun rdzogs khyad par
148a2(kha): gnyis rdzogs rdzogs//'khor ba mya ngan 'das med thig phran yongs kyi lam//bdag med phyogs bral rnam par rtog pa rnar zang //khyad par cig tu mtshungs shing bya byed rnam par dag/byed spyod rnam dpyod thaMng se nas dag pa yin//dus dang rgyu dang byed pas byas pa
148a3(kha): bubri'i mthar lam min//rang rgyud nyid du kun gyi rgyu nyid de rgyur gnas/rgyu'i rgyu de sa bon thams cad rnam par dag pa'i rgyu//rgyu med rgyu dang bral ba rang bzhin snying po'i rgyu//snying po yod pa ma yin do rgyu yi nas med//med pa'i snying po yod pa'i mchog gi sku//'du 'bral yad
148a4(kha): med par thams cad ldan gnas pas//med pa'i snying po de la med pa'i log/yod pa de la med pa'i yod pa yod//de la 'khrul pa yod phyir yod med gnyis ka med//med phyir dbus med mtha' med yang dag nyid kyi mtha'//yang dag nyid kyang snying po nyid de ye nas de bzhin bsog/
148a5(kha): /chos sku sangs rgyas ming btags de lta de bzhin no//ye shes rigasu shar ba gsog phyir blang mi bya//rgyal ba mngon byung mtshan dpes brgyan pa yang //de bzhin gsog phyir rgyal ba'ang mi 'dod//rgyal bas kun bslus rgyal ba nyid kyang bslus//rgyal ba'i rgyal ba med rgyal
148a6(kha): rgyal mi gnas//sgyu 'dra de ni 'khrul gyi sgra dngos 'dra//sgyu ma'i rang bzhin du gang du yang grub phyir//chos rnams yongs med de gsung gsung ba gsog/de ltar thams cad gsog phyir de bzhin nyid kyang gsog/dri bral sangs rgyas yang sangs rgyas pa der mi gnas//de ni
148a7(kha): byang chub snying po sangs rgyas med pa yin//de la sgro btags chos med skur zhing de bzhin nyid//rtog pas bsags pa de las byung phyir rgyas par snang /bag chags rtas pas rtog la zhen byas de lta nyid//gang ltar rtags pa de ltar snang ba rtogs pa'i dang /rtog
148b1(kha): dbang las mi rtog ye shes skye//rnam mi rtog pa'i ngang du thams cad ma lus 'dun//mtshan ma ma lus 'dus phyir chen po nyid kyi dbang //ji ltar mtshan ma de dag yong su rnam par dag 'gyur ba//snang ba nyid ni yid la mi byed pa'o//mtshan ma de bzhin de las ye shes /
148b2(kha): ba byung //de nyid mtshan ma 'dus te mtshan nyid snying po nyid//mi rtog pa'i sems kyis de nyid dbang //jis kyang bslad pas mi slod rin chen gser//gyo mo shing bu gseg mar mi gnas pas//shing bu rtsol bas kun tu zhi ba btags pas//'khrul gyur zhugs na smon
148b3(kha): rlung can dang //ra ro tshul can rnams la mi snang srid 'gyur kyang //rtag pa de la yongs rdzogs gser du snang //de bzhin kun gzhi rnam shes nyid bdun bcas pa'ang //ting 'dzin gyis yongs su sbyong ba yi//rnal 'byor can gyis rtag tu de mthong 'gyur//mi rtog/
148b4(kha): pa nyid yis kun gyi ye shes snying //snyi zhing 'jig pa spre'u 'dra rnams rnam shes te//mi skye mi 'jigs de bzhin pa ni ye shes spyod//ye shes nyid 'khrul rnams la snang phyir ye shes med/ye shes med phyir de bzhin dbyings kyang song mi 'gyur//mi rtog pa nyid tshul
148b5(kha): khrims nyid de khrims 'chal min//tshul khrims nyid du tshul khrims gnaso//yongs kyi snying po de la med de 'khrul par snang //'chal pa'i tshul khrims med par gang rtogs pa//gnyis ka tshul khrims log paso yis chen po yin//gnyis med ye shes snying/
148b6(kha): po sangs rgyas kun gyi lam//tshul bzhin thig le yid la byed pa skad cig gis//las dang las kyi rnam smin ma lus rnams//byang chub chen por mtshungs shing khyad par med//mi rtog pa nyid chos rnams thaMd nyid du nges//thams cad dmig su med par mi rtog thig le
148b7(kha): nyid/de nyid mnyam por gnas pas mnyam pa chen po'i ngang //de nyid gang yin mi gnas de nyid song mi gnas//mi gnas btsal phyir mi g.yo bar yang mtshungs//der yang der mi gnas phyir mi rtog snying po yin//de ni thig le snying po yin te thig le nyid du
149a1(kha): su sa dang nga byas su ma pa pa du skyes dag yod/sa la sna nga mchus tu gyi medas myog tu gang/sa par mi gdul mi ga myi na'i ni nga /'dzin pa 'dzin med pa ta na rnaM sa ny-i galas
149a2(kha): la gnas te rtog med ye shes sku//zhi la phra te tsha shes zab pa med phyir lha dka' ba//mtshan mar 'dzin pa'i 'du shes thams cad bcom pas na//lhun grub ye shes snying po'i ting 'dzin//mi rtog pa nyid thams cad mkhyen pa'i sangs rgyas nyid//gang la dmigs pa med
149a3(kha): ra dag par dbang bsgyur ba//zhi ba rnam ldan mtshan nyid med pa'i mtshan nyid ni//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid yin phyir na de nyid do//ye shes snying por sangs rgyas sa rnams ma lus bsdus//che ba'i che ba yang dag don gyi sgo nyid ni//rnam pa'i grangs dang dmigs pa
149a4(kha): gnyis po spang //snang med chos la snang ba med par ye gnas pas//mtshan ma'i rnam rtog theg pa rnams la ga la 'jog/sems can sems can tsam yang mi snang na//sangs rgyas sangs rgyas kun gyis la de bzhin gnas//mi rtog byang chub snying po mi rtog mya ngan 'das
149a5(kha): sna tshogs mtshan mar 'dzin pa'i 'du shes dang bral ba//rgyal ba'i bcom ldan 'das de bcom ldan 'das//theg chen snying po rnam mi rtog pa'i mchog/gang la mi gnas mi rtogs byang chub bo//bya ba dang ni mtshan nyid thams cad ma lus kun//dngos por mya ngan 'dara' bar
149a6(kha): 'gyur ba yin mi srid//rnam rtog mtshan 'dzin rnam par shes pa la//ye zlog mya ngan 'da' ba'i thig le byang chub bo//de bzhin rnam par mi rtog snying pos don mdzad pa//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dgongs pa rnam mi rtog/rtog pa'i dri ma zad phyir cir yang ston//seMn rnam rtog de
149a7(kha): nyis gongs pas na//de banin nyid na don mdzad sems can mgon//bdag dang gzhan dang la gnas pa'i drug la//me rtog byin rlabs rgyun mi gcod pa rtag tu ston//ji ltar yod bzhin sgrub pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po chen po ni/yid bzhin 'dod pa'i reb rdzogs par byed/
149b1(kha): debazhin u par mi rtog pa'i chos nyid kyang //rtog mtha zung ba'i sngon las ma lus rdzogs par snang //mtha' dang byas med gang la'ang mi stod mags gnas pa//mnyam pa'i rgyal po sangs rgyas nyid//tshor ba'i tshor ba de bzhin gshegs pa ni//yang dag don de nyi
149b2(kha): la bsam bya gang yang med//gzhag par bya ba'ang ye nas ci yang med//yang dag nyid la yang dag nyid gnas phyir//don dam yang dag nyid la yod dag lta/yang dag mthong na thams cad rnam par grol//yang dag don chen rnam par dbye ba las//g.yo bar bya ba'i yid kyang mi
149b3(kha): g.yo med pa ste//nyon mongs rnam shes sgrol des sgo lngar dkyus//kun tu nges par bzang ba'i gnas ngan lon//mi zang srid pa'i rgya mtsho 'khor ba'i 'obs na gnas//der btags gnas las dben pas ye shes snying //de lta'i mi rtog byang chub snying po'i lam//yid
149b4(kha): kyi rnam shes gang gi rjes mi 'byung //rnam par mi rtog nyam su des myong klong //de nyid tshor ba nyid de tshor ba'i rgyal po yin//dngos po gang du'ang mi dmigs mi gnas shing //ji ltar bsam pa bsam ma rtog nyid yin te//chos rnams ma lus snang rtog gang bral byed//
149b5(kha): da ni gdas rgyas nyid pa rnams sa chen ldan gyis rtsug//bdag mdo ba ni dag med kun padag da //me+e d+haka mi rtog pA la mi rtag s+ho/rdzus bskul ba la sdug bsngag sa yin//gats+tsha myigad+ha gA sa kA na rmo / s+yA d+has smras rnaMs mas smra
149b6(kha): 'khrul pa'i 'du shes med phyir tshor ba dam par bsngags//byang chub chen po'i lam du de gnas bral mi phod//byang chub kyi sems nya mo bag la nyal las//byang chub sems dpa'i dgongs pa bag la nyal ba dang /kun tu bzang po'i dgongs pa bag la nyal gnyis
149b7(kha): lhag spong du bstan pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos//mi g.yo ba 'dod chags pa'i ting 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/nyid kyis nyid la dmigs bshad pa//'du shes thams cad 'du mi shes
150a1(kha): sa zlog+sas lang gyu byung bdun ga ga sa ga //na las kyil brjo ldan pa / ga sa mi sems su khrims pa bya// rna men ma na dzin na

150b1(kha): rtog rtag chad ma yin pas//yang dag dbu ma'i lam du khyad par bstan pa yin//bla med byang chub lhun gyis grub pa'i ye skuka/mtshan ma rnams dang ye shes de bzhin nyid de 'dres ma yin//mngon par 'du byed mtshan rnams thams cad kyis//sang+yas
150b2(kha): snyun grub mtshan ldan ma yin te/de dag ma lus 'khor los bsgyur yon tan//des ni sangs rgyas mi mthong de bzhin nyid ldan//nye lam bde rdzogs yon tan dpag med//chos rnams ma lus de ze ga cig gi dang du gnas pa la// ba de nyid dag rdo /
150b3(kha): na//yis chen po'i tshogs chen rnam par dag ga lam gyi lam mo tshig mngon du rnyed pa'o//ngo bo nyid kyis rnam par dag la byang chub rlung //bsod nams rlung chen gzugs pa ye shes dag pa'i rgyu//sbyin pa tshul khrims bsgrub pa'i gnas 'dzin pa //ye shes che
150b4(kha): po tshogs chen bskyad ba'i rgyu yin no//'khor gsum yongasu dag pa'i tshul gyis gang spyod pa/'jig rten las 'das pha rol phyin pa'i rgyu mchog/gang yang stong pa mngon du gyur nas bsags pa mthar phyin pa//ye shes kyi tshogs rdzogs par byed pa'i lam gyi
150b5(kha): mchog go//gang gtong ba la le lo snyom pa g.yeng ba med gyur na//ngo bo nyid kyi chos cig de bzhin nyid kyi 'bras bur 'gyur//'dus byas bsod nams tshogs la rtul phod na//'khor los bsgyur rgyal lam de nas ni 'bras bur 'byung ka/ye shes tshogs chen 'bras bu'i
150b6(kha): ma gyur des byed 'gyur//stong la sogs pa bsod nams don du sgrub byed pa//de bzhin gshegs pa zad don du gnyer ba'i bye byed byed //drang med sgyum ma bu btsas nye ba bzhin du nye bar 'gyur//sbyin dang tshul khrims brjod dang brtson 'grus pasam gtan gas rang ldan/rdza
150b7(kha): bo shes rab nyid kyis dbang bsgyur chos kyi sku la sbyor//mar me sgo nas mun khung 'jug cha 'dra bar 'gyur ba yin//bsod nams tshogs kyi don de chu skom 'thungs pa'i tshul du sbyor byed pa//shing gnyis brtsubs pa'i me yis kun bsreg bzhin//nyon mongs ma lus lus
151a1(kha): kha seng pa thugs pa shes da por 'astan sa gras rgya/ sa gsas pa rdzogs na po sa napas ya sngags phyogs sang bldan pa dang /zas kyi tshogs rnags pa dang mi gsogs
151a2(kha): na sa gas gas /de sa das sa ga mas/ na rnas pa da sa ban pa
151a3(kha): ba ni bras bur ston pa nyid kyis byed la shad//chos gcig la ni rims kyis bdenan gnyis rtogs pa//mu stegs kyis ni ku rdzob 'phrul ci'i ban rab gags pa//rnams cad pa ga nga nacom rtog pa bstan pa rjes ni cing //ngas pa dan na rnams pa
151a4(kha): yang 'gyu shes smra pa nyis pa/dus ga gonas pa gapa mabla sa pa sa hA/sos smra bas 'grul ba kyi ma la sa na/lags ci'i na gnyis grub pa dang can/
151a5(kha): thung gi las dang kyim cher bas kun/dus gsum rdzas du 'dod de so so smra sa don dam gzung // la 'phrul brtag pa'i dani gnyis steng du 'dusas byas de ga byed pa yin pa/sems dang 'i u du byed bachu ba ni 'ad ba rnams ni 'gyu
151a6(kha): naM//ngam par phung go dmas dang skye mched cam zhig rdza su 'dong bdon du b+ha/na gas sam du sbyangs thams cad ba na pa gsasu 'du ma khrul pa ni//phyi ri sa phung gi mas dang skye ma chod sems dang mi rdzun rig byed ma yin go//de rnams su 'dod ba rnams ni
151a7(kha): ta sa yid//da la pa tshan nyid gsus kyim bun gags pa na ga tsa pa rjes sa tsu pa zhi da na mas nyi gsus rdzog minas ma da /de da na
151b1(kha): /rjes pani yang chang ma ngam pa rang ba zhen rnam pa pazhi ste nges pa dang ni bi slu ba//dngos ma min dang /gas par dang gang yin pa/dam par 'dod du laphrul gamin la sgra med drug pa med/gzigs nas yang bzhin med par gyur ba 'grub pa yin na du
151b2(kha): ba m+bu tshwam du 'i sa dam por dang gnang pa med//sa rgya sa thos sna ni pa'i ngas kyis zla la 'ad sgyus ma//e ti sa med theg pa'i snying po'i rang zhin 'di//sus kyang dag par nus pa ma yid dan gnyis senas med//
151b3(kha): bden gnyis dod ni 'di na gan yod de la nas med pa yin //bden dang tshad ma gtan tshigs rjesu dpag pa yin//ma mtshan mad kyi chos 'di rtogs par nus pa ga la song ka 'khor ba mya ngan 'das med thig le rten po'i klong //khyar med rlung las 'das pas bya

152a1(kha): gnyams pas smra ba dag la de bzhin long mi gnas//bshang du ye nas med pas bshad pa 'khrul pas te//yang dag nyid la bshad du mtshon pa'i yi ge med/'phung bar sgyu byed rnams ni bshad la mos shing dga' bar 'gyur/yang dag byang chub thob pa/

156'a3(kha): stong sngon mtshan badzin lam mchog bdeb smrab//zhi deng gnas dang lhag mtho dag dang ni//kun gzhinam shes yod pa'i dngos gnyid dang //'jig rten lta ba rnam pani 'das pa'i lha//khams gsum las 'das nor bu na po ma/phung po lnga po sa gso dung chub lam/'dod chags/
156'a4(kha): zla gti mug phrag dag rgyu la//bde ba 'di la gnas pas pad pa'i mchog/rnal 'byor cig d+har 'jug pa'i lam 'di ni//'dug par mi rung zhes ni bskur 'dabs pa//dbang po rtul po ma 'das tha mal pa//gnas ngan len pa bag chags sbyang rung ste//gnyis ya don dpa
156'a5(kha): bun rdzob don dam dngos gnyis gcig/gnyi su mi lta snying po byang chub nyid//ni gnas lhag mthong sbyangs pas brgya byin la sogs bral// na pa'i grong glong wA sbrul dang //dgon pa dben pa'i nags gsang dur khrod d+hams//glen par byed byed bdag bsto gan \u0f7f
156'a6(kha): brnyed dang bkur sti dag la gang chags pa//mkhas pa ma yin shin tu'ang dran pa nyams//chos lnga ldan pas byang chub thob par gsungs//skye ba dag la mos pa'i nyon mongs 'di//sems can gyis tshul khrims nyams te zhig pa yin//de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu
156'b1(kha): /ba dang //lha'i dkyil 'khor nye bar bsgrub pa'i tshe//las gzhan phra mo brtsom pas sgrib mi sgrib//zhi khro'i so sor mchis ma mchis//lha gcig bsgoms pas gzhan dbang 'gyur ram ci//rnal 'byor mi rtog ting 'dzin gsal gyur na//ting 'dzin gzhan la snyoms par
156'b2(kha): 'jug mi 'jug/sngags la 'jug pa'i rnal 'byor dbang phyug gis//mdo sde bsgoms nas gtan tshigs rtsal na kho//'phags pa dag dang mi mnyam skyon ci lags//sgom du med par bzhag tu rung mi rung //lung dang slob dpon mkhas pas sems bcos na//sngags kyi
156'b3(kha): 'phro 'du med par don thob bam//dge ba gzhan gyis rnam smin phyi mar 'byung //sngags spyod tshe 'dir 'grub pam ci las 'gyur//nges pa'i dbang mthob pa'i slob dpon gyis//gzhan la dbang bskur dbang thob pan mi pan//zhes zhus pas/ched du glengs pa/ting nge 'dzin gyis goms pa'i
156'b4(kha): zur ldan na//rang bzhin gzugs brnyan bye brag cung zad med//thams cad sku gsung thug su 'od gsal na//stobs ldan gud na med pas sgrib mi nus//chos skur ro gcig 'gro don byed snyam ste//mos pa'i blo la so so nyid du gsal//ye shes dbyingasu phyag rgya kun gcig phyir//
156'b5(kha): bde gshegs gcig sgoms rgyal bkun gyi dang //ting 'dzin snying po mi rtog goms gyur na//ting 'dzin gzhan phred de la sbyar mi dgos//gcig car 'jug pa goms pa mngon gyur na//tshe 'di 'grub pa gdon mi za bar brtag/ma rig bdag tu bzung nas lha nye bas/sangs rgya
156'b6(kha): sems can 'byed pa srid pa'i rtsa//don dem skye med dngos po med bden pa la/bsgom du med par 'jog pa dam pa nyid//yang dog ma nor gdengs chen ldan pas gang //blo'i gting du shin tu ngoms byis nas//btsal mi dgos pa'i nor bu rin po che//dge sdig gnyis kyi 'dra /
157a1(kha): /so sor nges//sgeg dman gnyis ka'i rgyal zhugs phyir yod//smre sdang la sogs ngan song rgyu nyid nga lta dag pa'i phyir//tshe 'di'i 'bras bu thob pa de nyid mod//dus gsum rgyal ba'i gsang chen nges pa'i dbang //byin nam stan nam mthong bar ma byas na//rdo rje rgyal po
157a2(kha): yon tan dbang med pas//nor bu rgyal sa bskos pa ji bzhin du//dbang bsgyur byas pas bdag gzhan brlag par 'gyur//bkra shis pa rig pa'i khu byugs las//mdor bsdus pa mdo sde'i dgongs pa dang //zhus pa'i lan dang bcas pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o//
157a3(kha): denas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos//btsal ba med pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/ched du brjod pa 'di brjod do//rdo rjer sems na bsung bgrod sbyang ba ma lus de dbang bsgyur//rdo rjer 'du 'bral med de bde la gnas pas rdo rje'i dbang /rdo rje de ni skye
157a4(kha): dang 'gag pa med de yang shing zab//de phyir rdo rje zab mo yangs shing zab pa'i gting ni lta ba rdo rje yin//rdo rje na mkha' lta bu de ni thams cad bskyed rgyu de gzhi ma yin pas che//de phyir rdo rje kun 'byung snying po de ma btsal bzhag nas bsgoms//sems kyi ngang nyid kha dog dngos
157a5(kha): /dngos med ji bzhin na//gang nas ma byon phyi rol gnyi ga min//shes rab tshogs kyi snying rje'i brlan dang ma bral thams cad slengs//log rtog mtshan ma'i 'du shes thaMd de las 'byung //sbyin pa la sogs pha rol phyin pa bcur yang rang bzhin rdzogs/lam 'dus rim pa sa dang 'jug
157a6(kha): lha thams cad grub pa yin//spong len mod pa'i chos 'di sgyu mar gnas pas na//lus ngag yid gsum yongs dag pa nyid ye nas shes pa yin//sangs rgyas chos la gus shing brnyad 'dod dran pa shes rab min//gtong dang tshul khrims chos sems dpal la byang chub 'di stegan
157a7(kha): sku bzhin//thams cad kun tu bzang po'i ngang du gcig par shes par bya//mtha' yas rab tu mtha' yas pa'i sems can thad kun//rang byung rnam dag sku gsung thugs kyi dkyil 'khor du//sangs rgyas la sogs phyag rgya 'bar ba nyid du don 'di snang bas min//snang
157b1(kha): kri kyis rdzogs par mas pa snams kyi zangs//med pa'i skulas yod pa'i dngas por 'od gsapa pa//gdonas dag pa'i dkyil 'byor la songs pa'i tshogs//snre snang sa po mang 'i tshub min na// hrI nas kyang rnam tshanag yod/skye
157b2(kha): tshatshad ma gtan tshigs rje su dpag pa yis//ma mtshan rmad kyi chos 'di rtogs par nus pa ma yin te//rtag par kun rdzob rnam par snang mdzad spu/yid bzhin nor ltar dgos dgu 'byung ba'i gzhi//de bas gsang sngags bdag nyid chen po 'di//dngos grub byin rlabs gtams padz+ri/
157b3(kha): thabs shes can//bsam yas gting dpag dka' ba rgyal po 'dra//khams gsum las 'das byang chub rin po che//stong pas btsal kyang rnyed par ga la 'gyur//thig le chen po kun tu chub gnas pa'i//thabs dang shes rab rnam ldan ye shes snying po nyid//zla gsang thig le
157b4(kha): skye 'gag bdag tu rdzogs//skyes chen thugs la rtag tu gsal ba'i sku//thams cad sangs rgyas pa'i rdzogs chen 'di//ma bkag cing bder gnas pas thabs po che//mi bsgrib gsal bar gnas pas pad+ma can ka gnyis mad thig ler 'dus shing brjod las 'das//bkra shis pa'i
157b5(kha): rig pa khu byug gi rgyud las//thabs dang shes rab gnyisu med pa'i leru ste gsum pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos gsang ba khyad par du bstan pa'i tshe du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod de//phung po lnga po ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i rgyal ba'i tshe/
157b6(kha): /dkar mthong sar po le rgan ljang khu'i mdog/bsal ba bsra dang gnyen pa dro ba dang //gnyis med 'khril ba gzhon tshul chags par zhugs//mthong thos snom pa myong ba dag pa'i dbang //mthong bya mnyan bya ba snam bya myong bya rnams te/mthong byed thos byed snom byed myong byed dang //
157b7(kha): /'das dang da ltar 'byung dang ma byon tshogs//gnyis med 'khril ba rgyal ba yab yum tshul//thar byed reg dang reg byed reg dang //rtag chad bdag dang mtshan mor ma yin rnams//gdod nas dag pas yab yum dkyil 'khor rdzogs//yang dog bden po'i 'bras bu kun rdzob
158a1(kha): ma/lani rten pa dang 'jig rten 'das pa ning /dakhyim bar spyod yul dkyis 'khor gsum du dus/bras bu smin 'i ma khyen 'dus pa'i 'acu//rdorjes pas dang rjod can las gyi mchog/do nas mi skye m-i chi dgos pa dag/ku tu byung bas/
158a2(kha): smram / ma ma thun pa sa rgyas/ can bdag rnams mtshan ma rnam par smras// bar ming ma mi lta sa par bye/ji zhin mi srud bya mad ma par tham byung ba shes 'byung ba 'byung ba ma
158a3(kha): gzhan nas ma yin 'gro kun la gnas//thun mong bde ba byang chub brnyed dka' ba//zhing dang yul yongasu 'khrul pa min//snang med na mkha' lta bur rab 'byor pas//de la mi brtsad de bzhin rnam par bzhag/mchog gi mchog tu gyur pa'i theg pa mchog/thig le
158a4(kha): gcig tu zlums kyang brjod med//de nas bcom ldan 'das bde ba chen pos snying po 'byin pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//rigs lnga sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo la ston to//bton nas ye shes chen po 'od 'bar ba'i sku lngar gyur to//de nas
158a5(kha): bcom ldan 'das bdag po mnyes par byed pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//btsun mo'i tshogs 'di nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las//d+hA tu shwa ri te shwa ra ti/mo ha ra ni/ra ga ra ti/badz+ra ra ti/rnams byung nas mnyes pa chen po'i
158a6(kha): tsun mo lngar gyur to//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnas blor 'joms pa zhes bya ba 'i nyidang 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas/ k+Shed pa'i'i sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje las byung //de byung ma thag tu sgrog 'bar ba bzhir gyur te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis/de nyid mi sdang
158a7(kha): bar bya ra byor/bla med sanas khyabye bas mi 'dzin gtang/mig sa 'oco ma thag cad pas las rnams rigs/mi rtog ma nyam las 'das pas bgags rnams ni/mnyas rdzogs 'arjod du tshad pas snying po gago//gapa gyur migaso ra bzhin pa bar
158b1(kha): /cis kyang khyod nyid la shes gsal ba'i ngang //mi mdor tshig dral lhun rdzogs mnyam pa nyid//dkyil 'khor chen po'i rang bzhin gnas pa'i rgyu/ thams cad se nas dag pas lhag ma med//mi 'gyur snying por gyur bas drang po'i mchog mtha' las mkha' bzhin/
158b2(kha): bzhin gnas pas mi mnyam med//btsal ba med pas gzhan la ltos ma yin//lhun gnas bde chen mtshungs pa'i rgyal po 'di//ming tsam tshig tu 'di zhes btags nas ni//bstan du rdo rje sems dpa'ang ma bstan//thabs dang shes rab rnam par ldan byas nas//snying
158b3(kha): po rgyu ma'i phyag rgya 'dzin 'dod gang //de la 'dzin pa yod phyir skyon yin te//snying po'i rang bzhin gcig tu ma nges pas//ji ltar 'dod pa dpung ming dgos par snang //snang rong phyag rgyar rtsol bas 'di 'dod pa//bla med theg pa'i sgrib shing skyon can yin//yig
158b4(kha): 'brus skyed pa'i phyag rgya bzhi ldan sku//cha lugs bsal bar chu zla bzhin snang ste//snang stong ngo bo nyid bsgom 'gyur yang //bsgom pa nyid kyis spre'u'i spyod yul 'dra ka dkyil 'khor chen po rnam gsum 'od 'bar bas//dbang po mngon du gsal bar gyur na yang
158b5(kha): theg mchog 'di la reg pa yong ma yin//bkra shis pa rig pa khu byug gi rgyud las//ma hA yo ga rdo rje theg pa chen po'i gol sa yin par bstan pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o///de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal kun tu bzang pos//chedu
158b6(kha): brjod pa 'di ched du brjod//tshogs sbyor sgom pa mthong ba mthar phyir lam//tshad med bzhi ni kun nas skye bar nus pa yin//sna gsung thugs las bral bas tshogs rdzogs che ba'i mchog na yam med pas na lem la mi btsal bde ba'i snyun//rgyu
158b7(kha): 'bras mi phyed 'dus pa'i rdo rje yin//ma skyes ye nas gnas pas 'jigs pa med//gdod nas byang chub snying po'i rang bzhin la//btsal ba'i bsam pa yod pas don mi rnyed//kun tu khyab pas rtag pa byams pa'i sku//phyag rgya'i 'byung 'jug
159a1(kha): na/b+h+yo rdzun sgru sas gyung 'jun pa/ sasu ra rgya ma pa so//da pang da 'i gyis snar banyam par gas// ba rgyas mas //
159a2(kha): de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/skad du med pa'i lam lnga po//de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi thar pa'i mdzod//smyung bar bya ba rnam thar lnga nyid kyang //de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi sku'i gsos//'dod yon na
159a3(kha): lnga de bzhin gshegs pa'i brgyan//sangs rgyas dang ni slob dpon phyag rgya sngags//lcam dral tshul du lha rnams yan lag bsam//'di yang byang chub thob pa'i lam po che//ye shes kyis ni tshogs can bsgom pa ni//lam gyi mchog ste nye lam nye rdzogs lam//
159a4(kha): bsod nams tshogs kyang mi sbyor mi rung min//yis dam pa thob pa'i tshogs kyang rung //bsam gtan rab tu gnas pas sangs rgyas nyid dang mtshungs//mang du thob pas byang chub lam reg kyang //mi dmigs mi gnas tshad med stong pa nyid//pha rol phyin drug lam
159a5(kha): gyi gter du gsungs//gdod 'jus rims kyis sbyor ba don thob ldan//dge bcu spyang med chos nyid dbyings kyi rgyan//bkra shis pa rig pa khu byug gi rgyud las//byang chub kyi lam mngon du bstan pa'i le'u ste lnga pa'o////de nas bcom ldan 'das dpal
159a6(kha): kun tu bzang pos/nyid kyi nga rgyal bskyed pa'i phyir/ched du brjod pa 'di ched du brjod do//e ma'o ye nas rnam par rgyal bas las dbyings las 'das//ye shes rnam par dag pas gcig pa'i phyir//grol ba'i las kyi mtshan nyid yod pa min//gcig dang du mi'i
159a7(kha): mtha' las 'das pa na//chos kyi dbyings kyi mi snyam rnam par spangs//gzugs kyi mtshan nyid gang min sangs rgyas pa//thos bosags smon lam btab pas don mi brnyed//chos sku gcig pa'i dam pa ma mthong na//'ja' tshon sgyu mar 'phags pa'i sku spyang
159b1(kha): bas bsod nams rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mthong ba min//mtshan nyid med pa'i chos nyid chen po 'di//chos kyi dbyings las sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid min//chos kyi dbyings las sgyu mar gang bsgrubs kyang //sgyu rtsal rgyal ba'i lam du yongs ma bsngags//
159b2(kha): mi gnas chos sku brjod med las 'das pa//dbu mar brjod med da don dang bral//sgyu ma'i rang bzhin sbyong ba bral ba nad//mkha' bzhi mtha' kun med de phyogs dang bral//zhi dpo zhi med de chos rnams med//ri dang ngogs med mnyam pa'i rgyal po che/
159b3(kha): mtshan med dgongs pa dag pas bsgom mi dgos//byur med lam las 'das pas lam mi rtsol//rmad 'byung sku mchog nyid las skur mi sgom//ra dogs kun bral zhibs rgyas btab pas//phyag rgya bzhi ni sku mchog nyid kyi nad//sku gsum rdzogs pa'i
159b4(kha): kyil 'khor chen po ste//rang byung yi nas ldan pas chos kyi sku//'gyur med lhun gyis rdzogs pas bsod nams sku//'byung 'jug cir yang sna bas sprul pa'i sku//byed spyod kun rdzogs rdzogs longs spyod ba'i btsal med snying po byang chub rdzogs 'dri sku/
159b5(kha): thams cad lhun gyis bdag nyid yin pas nakazin rtsol ngal bas byang chub mchog tshol ba//brnyed pa'i dus med srin las bal skyes tshul//rgyal bar brtag par zhugs pa pa nyid pas//rtag tu bde 'gro thar po'i lam 'dod na//grol ba'i lam chen pa la blta
159b6(kha): bar byos//grol ba'i lam zhugs drang po 'khor sa med//ma phyin 'gro sar phyin pa nye lam mchog/rtag tu rgyal nyid mchod na rgyal ba mnyes//sangs rgyas zhing sbyong mi dgos thaMd rgyas//khyab sku ye nas brdal bas kun gyi lam//yis sna tshogs rgyan
160a1(kha): A b+hin ga bas/'al los bsgyur rgyal nod ltar mtshogrags 'dra//legs thos kyis rgyan pa'i sbyin pa ni/khang pa'i dus myed rnam pa yin cus/ra ga gling sa sa rdzu shAs sa myu rtswas/rgyul la sogs pa
160a2(kha): chul byas pa'ang ka rgya thogs ljang gyis smrangs pani dazad par 'gyur//khu byug dus bsrung gzhan gyis mi 'gyur stor//rang phyug mngar ba rnyes gzhan la rig mi lus//chu thug gis ni gzhan gyi 'di btung ltar /kun rdzogs mchog gi su yis thams cad bsdus/
160a3(kha): sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag nyid mas chen po//zhing khams dag pa'i sangs rgyas kun gyis lnga/ka/dkyil 'khor chen po 'khor lo rdzogs pa'i sku//rgyal kun mchong par 'dod na bdag nyid mchod//dkon mchog gsum mchod 'du shes med pa bdag la gnas pas dbang lnga/
160a4(kha): thob//rdo rje theg pa'i dam tshig mi 'dor ba//nyan mongs lnga po'i las kun spyod pa dang //sems can kun la snying brtse med pa dang //ma byin len dang mi tshangs spyod ba dang //phyag rgyar mi stsol gzhan la mi rtsol ba//rtag par rgyal ba mi 'di dam tshig mchog/rdo rje thig 'i ting
160a5(kha): stan pa ni//de bzhin nyid kyi de bzhin dam pa nyid//na mkha'i khams bzhin rang bzhin mnyam gnas pa//sems la dmigs pa'i bdag nyid med par bsam//lha'i phyag rgyags la pa'i ting 'dzin//ngo bo nyid kyis dmig su med pas ni//rdo rje zag pa'i spyod pa dag pa ni/2
160a6(kha): thub mi bsngags gtsang sbrar mi rtog can//brtul zhugs gsum ldan sems can thams cad gsod//zas mchog gshang gci za zhing rnal 'byor don la brtson//nyon mongs lnga spyod byang chub chen po 'chang //mtshams mad lnga spyod ye shes rol pa rtogs//rdo rje theg pa'i ltar
160a7(kha): rngan la bltab//nas rab 'ab pa'i mtshan 'od gsal bas//byings dang mes gnyis kyang mi 'ang bsregs//dkyil 'khor gsum du mi lho dbazhin nyid mi gdang //snang stong phyag rgyar mi 'dod mnyam nyid ting 'dzin 'das//gang la'ang mi rten gang la'ang grub mang /
160b1(kha): gdod nas mtha' dag de bzhin de bzhin nyid//de'i tshig 'das sangs rgyas ye sangs rgyas//rdo rje theg pa chen po'i 'bras bu bstan pa'o//ma brtsal ma bsgrub rang bzhin gnas pa'i sa//sa rdzogs pad+ma can dang kun tu 'od las 'das//'bras bu lhun grub le nas dag pa'i sa//
160b2(kha): rdo rje theg pa'i lta ba dang //dam tshig dang /spyod pa dang /sgom pa dang /rang bzhin dang /'bras bu kun gcig yin par bstan//lha spyod ting nge 'dzin rgyu 'bras gcig gi dang /gang la spyad kyang 'jug lta 'bras bu rdzogs//rdo rje theg pa'i 'bras bu thob nas yon tan ci 'byung ba bstan
160b3(kha): pa/gab tshe'i nor ltar thams cad kun 'dus shing /na mkha' bzhin du ma g.yos drang gis 'char//bkra shis pa rig ga khu byug gi rgyud las/rdo rje theg pa'i nga rgyal bstan pa dang /rdo rje theg pa'i dbye ba bstan pa'i le'u ste drug pa'o///nga nas
160b4(kha): bcom ldan 'das dpal rdo rje sems dpas/rgyud kyi bdag po la bstod pa//kye bcom ldan 'das/bcom ldan 'das/chen po'i chen po/mchog tu gyur pa'i chen po khyod ni che ba'o//bzang po'i bzang po bdag med phyogs bral dam par gyur pa khyod ni lha'o//kun mkhyen kun
160b5(kha): /gzugs kyi se mkhyen rang rig nyid de sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs so//kun bsdud kun rdzogs kun gyis 'bras bu khyod la mi 'bral rtag phyag 'tshal//sku'i sku ste sna tshogs 'dul ba yi//chos sku dpag med rtag phyag 'tshal//gsung gi gsung sna tshogs 'dus pa'i gsung
160b6(kha): /mchog tu na tu grags pa khyod la'ang rtag phyag 'tshal//thugs kyi thugs ste sna tshogs 'dus pa'i de bzhin nyid de de la dang rtag tu bdag phyag 'tshal//mi 'da' 'da' bar mi mdzad dam tshig rdo rje can gyi sku khyod la'ang rtag phyag 'tshal//kun byas dbang phyug kun gyi sna tshogs
160b7(kha): kun gyi dpal mgon khyod la'ang rtag tu 'dog phyag 'tshal//dkyil 'khor snying po dkyil 'khor ldan pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po khyod la mchod pa 'dul//khyod kyis rang bzhin bsdum gyis ma g.yogs gang yang med pas khyod ni byams pa'i mchog/yangs pa chen po khyod kyis/
161a1(kha): sgro nas 'jug pa'i dba' bo 'jug mkhan de la'ang rtag phyug 'tshal//snying po'i snying por ba lta snyings de la'ang gzhan byug 'am ste//9na bcom ldan 'das/bcom ldan 'das/re mdzes dbang drug khyod ni nar myi gtong bar du /rdorje mchog 'dzin dag gis ga
161a2(kha): phyag 'cha ga bzhin sems pa rnams kyang ga byig 'tshal ba shag/yid dam spyod la phyag 'tshal bani bkra shes mchog kra shis zhes rgyud kyi dag po la bsnyed nas/mchag tulan gdum du phyag 'cha le nas/yang bsno ga rgyud kyi rig po che mchog la/gti du
161a3(kha): dru nas phyag 'jab te/sems pa dag pas khyod la phyag 'tshal bstod//bstod pa'i rgyal po rab tu bstong bas bstod//da ltar stod pas mchog khyod grags nas ni//sangs rgyas kun gyi bstong pa de ltang shog/nas rgyud kyi rgyal po 'i brjod pas//de nas bcom ldan 'das dpa
161a4(kha): kun tu bzang pos//rdo rje 'dzin la rgyud gtad pa/kye rdo rje sems pa rdo rje 'chang ba bo//rgyal ba kun gyi thugs kyi bdag/rgyal bar bstod pa'i lha'i lha khyod la//thugs rje che'i thugs kyi gter chen mdzod//rdo rje theg pa theg pa'i blar gyur pag bstan pa chen po rdo rje 'dzin la bstod//rdo rje
161a5(kha): 'dzin pa khyod ni sngags pa'i phyir//rgyud mchog rgyal po bstan pa bzung du gsol//skyes bu thabs la mkhas pa'i dpal ldan la//rgyal ba skye ba'i gsang mchog gtad do gtad//zhes rdo rje 'dzin pa bos nas/rgyud kyi bdag po chen pos gtad do//de nas rdo rje 'dzin pa chen pos//pus

161b1(kha): /gtad ba dA byA//rdo rje 'dzin pa ma yin sang /skyes bu sang ga'i sems yod cing //rab tu gces dang yid 'thad par//nor gyi dbyig tu gar 'gyur dang //rin chen nor bus gang bkang nas//rin chen nor bu theg pa 'di//gang zhig rgyud 'di 'dzin par byed pa la//dpral du nod dbyig/mi star na/
161b2(kha): de la seng go'i sems med pas//de ni sgyu mkhan dmyal sogs mkhan//'di'i dngos grub des mi thob//de la 'di'i dbang yang med ka bdud kyis bslus pa'i mi de ni//kha bsag g.yo sgyus 'dir gzung bas/ka/rdo rje 'dzin min bdud kyi bu /'di la 'dod pa'i bar chad nas//tshe 'di'i//

162a1(kha): bya/yas pa rmang byung bar grags so//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rang bzhin bdag nyid chen po'i sku//gang yang ma lus par ye nas 'dus pa de/gnyisu med par rtog pas/de nas gnyi su med pa'i bdag nyid chen pos nyid kyis nyid la che du brjod pa 'di che du brjod do//

162a5(kha): se nas sangs rgyas yin//de nas ngo mtshar ba'i rmang du byung ba ste ma rtogs pa la ngo mtshar ba'i tshig 'di brjod do//ma rig log 'khrul rtog pa'i gzung 'dzin gyis//med la yod brtags rten 'brel gnyisu 'khor//bya byed las 'phags rang bzhin nyid la nyams pa med//bdag

162b7(kha): mtshon du med pas gang yang med de rigs rgyud yod ma yin//cis kyang gzhig tu med phyir rdo rje rdo rje nyid//mi 'gyur bde ba'i klong na de bzhin de gnas pa'i//byang chub snying po'i chos 'di mi dmigs byang chub sems mi dmigs//'di ni yang dag nyid de drang srong rnam//

163a2(kha): 'gyur//khra zhing shes dkar drang srong chen po'i lam 'di mi rtog rtog las 'das/brtag par dka' bzhin bstan par dka' la brjod pa'i tha snyad rnams dang bral//cig gis mi phebs gzhan dang byis bkun gyi spyod yul ma yin yang //ston pa nges pa'i lung dang bla ma rnams
163a3(kha): kyi man ngag de nyid don 'dir blta//mngon sum la sogs rgyu yis dngos po 'jog cing grub pa'i mtha' spyod pa//de nyid rtog pa'i rgyu 'bras sa spyod yul tshad mar bzhag nas blo yis 'jig byed do//rtog mtha' med de phyir spyod mtha' med de snying po med na tshad mar gang //de
163a4(kha): phyir 'jig rten tha snyad brtag pa rnal 'byor lam 'dir mi dgas so//phyogs dus mnyam nyid rnam par dag pa la nas yin pas na//sna tshogs chos rnams kun la ji ltar 'dod dbang bsgyur//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten ma 'das thams cad kun//yang dag shes pas rab
163a5(kha): brtags nas rtogs 'gyur na/rang bzhin mnyam pa med las cir yang snang zhing 'byung //ji ltar snang ba nyid na'ang dbyings kyi rang bzhin de bzhin nyid yin te//de ltar rtogs par gyur pa'i rang byung ye shes snying po ni//sna tshogs chos rnams kun la dbang yang bsgyur bas mi nyams

163a7(kha): yin du byed chub kyi semis bsgom pa'i rgyud las//don dam pa'i rang bzhin rtogs par bye ba'i thabs bstan pa dang /tshad mas gzhig tu med par bstan pa'i leru ste gsum pa'o///de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag po chen po da
163b1(kha): nyid la nyid kyis bsgom du med pa'i don brjod pa//yongaso che'o shin tu phra ba'i chos nyid 'di//rgyu 'bras mi 'byed rang gis btsan pas de bzhin nyid//rang bzhin gnas pas bya med bde ba'i klong chen po//rna bas gzhan nas thos par mi rung tshig las 'das//cis kyang

163b3(kha): dmigs na mtshan med mtshan ma 'dra ste rtog/mi rtog pa nyid rtog pa'i rlung ste yis skye//gang yang snang ba med pa'i chos nyid lam mchog la//ma btsal bsam dang bral na bsgom pa'i klung //btsal zhing brtags na lam mchog de las gol//de las de bzhin bde ba mi 'byung /

163b6(kha): de mtshan ma'i rgyu la ming btags pa de ming dang mtshan ma zhes bya'o//rnam par rtog pa gang yang len cing chags na ming sgyu ma mi bde ba'i 'du shes yin//ming dang mtshan ma gang na'ang med par rtogs shing shes na de ming yang dag pa'i ye shes so//ming dang mtshan ma ci yang rang
163b7(kha): rig yid las ma skyes pa ni de bzhin nyid ces bshad//theg pa chen po gang na bden pa bzhin du rnam par shes pa mi g.yo zhing ni sems kyi rgyud nyid skye ba med pa'o//rnam par rtog pa med pa gang na da ltar gyi ni rnam par shes pa mi skye ba'o//sangs rgyas spyod pa ji lta bu na sems

166b1(kha): mnyam pa'i rgyal po bya ba yin pas spyod las 'das pa yin//de ltar ye nas gnas pa de la su'ang mi 'chos ngo bo de nyid de bzhin 'jog pa byang chub che//de kho kun rdzogs rang bzhin chos kyi snying po lta spyod rdzogs pa yin//chos kyi snying po 'di la lta zhing spyod de sgrub par
166b2(kha): byed pa de pha snying po ring //de yis snying po ma mthong spyod dang bcas snying po nyams//'di ni rtsol dang ye nas bral bas btsal du ye nas med phyir snying po'i mchog/dus gsum rgyal bas kyang ni ma bsgrubs ma sbyangs ma btsal bzhag pas mthong //mthong ma thag tu sbyad//
166b3(kha): btsal 'gyod de sbyang ma dgos par rtogs//rtogs pa'i ngang de la ni dus gsum rgyal ba'i sangs rgyas rdul snyed gnas//sangs rgyas bgrod phyir rtsol ba'i lam kun bor bas gdod sangs rgyas//rtsol bgrod lam la gnas tshe 'phags pa'i sar tshud tsam//de phyir
166b4(kha): de lam bde rdzogs sku gsum rdo rje 'chang ba'i sa zhes snying po 'di la bya//ma bgrod gnasu phyin par rang rgyud dag pas phyis pa'i sa yang yin//phyi nang sems kyi spyod yul bag chags rgyun dang bcas par lta ba sems tsam yin//sems rgyun bag chags rtog pa de la snying

166b7(kha): bde gshegs byang chub med phyir de'i rdzu 'phrul sgyu 'dra 'khrul la snang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes de dag shin tu dge ba'i rgyun nyid dang //rdo rje gnas med phyir na mnyam gnas rang bzhin 'drar gyur nas//chos dbyings rdo rje'i rtse mo de dag phyogs nyid med pas skad cig min//dag
167a1(kha): pa'i dag pa gzugs brnyan med pas 'jig rten yod mi 'gyur//de phyir byang chub ma chub mtshan nyid med par gcig pas blang dor med//don de'i tshul gyi don dam rnam grangs skyes 'gag med dang mnyam nyid dang //gnyis med bsam 'das stong nyid chos dbyings brjod dang
167a2(kha): bral la sogs//bstan pa de kun tha snyad yin de don med cing kun sgrib med//don dam tshul gyis lam nyid 'di 'dra zhes pa de nyid kun sgrib yin//the tshom yod med chos ni gang la'ang spong zhing gnas par mi byed do//bsgom pa dang ni chos dbyings med phyir som nyi med la'ang dam par lta ba med//

167a4(kha): nyid 'khrul pa yin par rab rtogs te ka/drug gis bstan pa'i bdud kyis las kyang mi 'dor ngan du mi rtog go//shes rab thabs kyi spyod pa la'ang mi gnas phyir na bdud kyi 'dra bar spyod//rang gi shes pa dam par byas shing kun las rgyal te de snyems nas//'dod chags zhe sdang skyes nas rtsod

177a4(kha): gti mug 'thibs shing sgrib dbang gis/byol song gnasu lhung 'gyur yang /sdug bsngal dngos la mi gnas pas/de dag rig pa'i 'khor lor rdzogs/ser sna chags bcings 'bras bu yis/zas dang longs spyod 'phongs pa dang /'dod pa yid du mi 'ong ba/dngos la de

177b1(kha): gcig po/lta ba klongs yangs mnyam pa'i mdzod/zin chags bral ba'i bdud rtsi la/glang chen chur zhugs lta bu yi/bar chod re dog bral ba la/spong len bral ba spyod pa'i mdzod/skur med ring thob dbang gi mchog/lam dang sa la mi bgrod de ka khyung drug sgo bskyas ji
177b2(kha): bzhin pa/rgyal thabs spyi blugs dbang gi mdzod/byar med gdod nas byas zin la/byung med 'ang gsal phrin las 'di/kun tu ma spangs rang gnas pas/phrin las lhun gyis grub pa'i mdzod/rang gsal ma bcos dkyil 'khor la/sa skyes gdod nas sku gsung thugs rang
177b3(kha): gsal mi 'dzin ting 'dzin lang /skyes med dkyil 'khor chen po mdzod//mkhyen pa'i ye shes rang 'char la/shes rab gsum gyi tshul gyis 'jug/las gsang ba'i gter 'dzin pas/ma bcos rang gsal yis mdzod/dgur snang gcig rdzogs khyab che/rin chen

178a3(kha): de nas rdo rje sems dpa' chen pos 'di skad ces gsungs so//kye sems dpa' chen po/na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam sbyor ba'o//namakha' rgyas pa'i mtha' bzhin du/mtha' dbus med pa'i snying po la/ji ltar bsam pa de bzhin 'byung /gsang ba'i rgya mtsho rin chen dbyig
178a4(kha): thabs sbyor rang gsal bsam mi khyab/rang gsal 'od la rtag tu bzhugs/rin chen 'phrul gyi dbyig la brtag/rdo rje lung dang mi ldan par/rang gsal rig pa'i 'khor lo la/bskal par brtags kyang mi rnyed/snyan rgyud thugs kyi rin chen la/rigs sbyor thugs
178a5(kha): kyi rnal 'byor pas/bla ma rdo rje srog tu 'dzin/yo byad snying pos mnyes byas te/sgo gsum bran du khas blangs nas/rin chen dbyig dang phrad byas nas/bda' don grol ba'i rim pa yis/rgyal thabs rig pa tshangs dbang gis/skad cig sangs rgyas pa dang sprad khang

178b1(kha): dbar lha bzhin du ni bsgom dang bral ba'i dbyingasu rdzogs/'phrul dga' longs spyod tshul de ni/rang gsal sprul cing 'dod dgur bsgyur/rgya mtsho'i gzings kyi bya rog ltar/bskor zhing rang gi dbyings la thugs/thub dbang nor bu de tsi wA rA/gang ltar bstan pa de ltar ro//
178b2(kha): spyi gtsug nor bu ji bzhin du kam brtsal rang gsal dbyings la gnas/bcud cing rin chen gter bzhin du/spro bsdu g.yo med ji bzhin gsal/dbyings la gsal ba'i 'od bzhin du/ma 'dres rang gsal 'du shes bcad/rgyal ba'i spyi gtsug ji bzhin du/bltas

178b4(kha): gser gyi sa gzhi ltar/cir bsal cir g.yo rdzas bral dbyings/rgya mtsho 'du ba'i snying po ltar/sna tshogs sgo ni gcig du mnyam/nyi zla 'bar ba'i 'od bzhin du/phyogs ris mdo rang gsal dang /na mkha'i ngo bo ji bzhin du/brtag cing gzung ba'i mtha' dang bral//rang /

178b6(kha): /na rin chen gsar gling ltar/kun tu snang yang gcig la rdzogs/rang shar rig pa cir mi dgongs/rin chen khang pa'i sgo mo la/kun nas kun tu mnyam sbyor bas/gang ltar phyin kyang rin chen gcig/de yi dbyig de thob par 'gyur/rgyal sras nus pa'i skal
178b7(kha): ldan gyis/rang gsal kun gyi 'od la bzhugs/zhe sdang kun 'joms ting nge 'dzin/gti mug chen po de bzhin te/'dod chags kun sbyor de nyid/nga rgyal zhe sdang gsum ldan pa dang /phrag dog mi chung don lnga yang /mang gas la 'od la 'dus te bzhugs/
179a1(kha): //gcig gi don la thar snang gdaga' ba ma ma mas rgyu dang d+hU/ sbyong bral ba'i cos gyis pa/'dugs pa rig pa'i 'ang gig dag sa zla rnas pa'i snying gca'abral sanyas pa'i mi jun 'khod byeng sma
179a2(kha): mi ge med pa'i don du mthong //mtshan ma sgra yi rjes 'brangs nas//spos bral rig pa'i don dang 'gal//ma btsal rang 'byung 'od gsal bas//mtha' dbus med pa'i dbyings la tham//gsang dang gsang tshan rab gsang ba/reng gsang spros gnang gcig la 'dus
179a3(kha): ye shes rang gsal rig pa'i 'od//phyogs ris mi mnga' yongs la snyoms//mnyam nyid rig pa'i ngo bo nyid//gang yang yin no rnam kun pas//rang rig 'pho 'gyur med pa la//ji ltar gsal yang 'di zhes med//kun gsal me long 'od po ci//yaMs rdzogs pa'i ngo bo la/

179a7(kha): drag zad rgyud la sakhas gyur yang /na nyi ga shing 'gyur sti bral na/ngas par dam 'a rji tu byung /'phrogs 'gros mamams 'das 'dra/'khor ba btid du srung 'gyur da/das pade las ston pa pa/thar ba'i na nam sri dang /rngogs chedan krung
179b1(kha): ga la//tha mi'i ma ga mi ldan pa pa//ma tog shes par snang ba ni//ngas par byung 'gyur te chos med//rang bzhin ngan pa'i mi rnamag sa//thos chung sba la goms med dang //theg pa dman pa'i skye bo dang //dorje slob dpon slong ba dang //don spun la lazung dag

179b5(kha): sems dpa' rdo rje des//rdo rje sems dpa' chen po la 'di skad ces gsol to//dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//ye shes chen por rang shar bdag/'phrul tshig sgra yis mtshon pa'i phyir//brjod bral rig pa'i ngo bo la//sngon byas gdam ngag rim pa yis//gsal
179b6(kha): bar nges par gsung du gsol//zhes gsungaso//de nas rdo rje sems dpa' chen pos 'di skad ces gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po/na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud/rdzogs chen thugs kyi klong gi bcud//dmigs dang thabs gsol gnang ba 'di/skyon yon rim pas
179b7(kha): brtag pa gces//na mkha'i dbyings kyis gter 'chang zhing //rang rig gsal ba'i mi g.yo thugs//ma bcos 'od gsal 'od la bzhugs//mi gnas rtog bral cir mi dgongs//bsam bral spyod med don mnga' zhing //rig pa ma bcos dbyings la 'jug bsam med rig pa'i
180a1(kha): @#//rdeng gis gnas//bsam med sbyang med pa la ba ka dmigs med don la rtag tu bzhugs//rgyal thabs tshangs pa'i man ngag gis//'khor ba ma gnasu s+phar//gtang 'dzin bral ba'i blo yis ni//rnam rtog yis snying por mkhyen//sgrib pa bsal ba'i ye shes la//

180a3(kha): 'khor la//bcos med rig pa'i sems kyi gnas//byar med lhun grub phrin las las//bya rtsal med par rang bzhin gnas//de bzhin nyid kyi dbyings nyid la//ma bgrod lam kun rdzogs par gsal//'phrul tshig pa'i sgrom ma yis//bsam pa'i bye brag ji bzhin gsal ka/dbang dang

180a6(kha): 8//rdul brug ga nga cu dang //sprog dang 'a byang gisang pa na rang dang //thams ca dko mi gcig pa yi/des bcas co ba rgyad spyang byas nos/mag na pa rang gas pa thugs ma shing /'angags pa pa pa man sa duganas//sgos yang na sa gcig tug rdzags can gis
180a7(kha): jug tu byingab nyi lag meg pa pan ma 'i byig la yang rig pa gsung zhi ni 'dag pa ri pa dgas pa mdza 'ang po dgas pa rtogs pa ga sa la sa rin ba bu bya dag pa 'a bad 'a sa ldan cig grong dug pa khyog pa ma ci/
180b1(kha): /'das pa la//gsang ba'i yas bshad ma brjod//rdzogs chen rna rgyud 'di bstan na//bul ba'i ra'i 'bru bzhin du//de ma thag tu brlag pa dang //mkha' 'gro dam na 'dus 'gyur nas//dur khrod 'bar ba'i ro gling du//gcan gzan 'dus nas gtong bzhin 'gyur//rdo rje bla ma long ba nyid//nges par ya yis

180b5(kha): gcig la gnas//gnas med rang gsal yegs pas//bab chu 'dra ba'i man dag skyong //saM gnas drag pa'i grong nyid la//glang chen bstod pa'i man dag sbyin//dro yang mi gnas 'gro ba la//khwa chen byal ba sbyin par bya//'dzin med lal pa'i gang zag la//gzugs ldan rigs pa'i mnapa/
180b6(kha): sbyin//thabs dang shes rab rtag 'chang bas//kha sbyar ba ru'i man dgra sbyin//skal med dbang po dman pa la// shin tu gdams pa'i man ngag sbyin//phyi nang gsang ba'i gang zag la//bgrang dang gzung dang brtag pa dang //thun mong rlung gis man ngag sbyin//blo ldan rtogs pa'i gang zag la//'phrul gyi me lod/
180b7(kha): mon dag sbyin/chag zhan 'dod pa'i rig can la//rin chen bde bskyil man ngag sbyin//rtag 'dzin da nyid rig can la/ngo bo rtag pa'i san ngag sbyin//shes ldan dbang po dul ba la//brdang ldan rig pi'i man dag sbyin//yul la zhen pa'i gang zag la/dbang po brtul ba'i thabs kyi bstan/
181a1(kha): @#//tshig la dga' ba'i gang zag la//sgra la rtag pa'i man ngag sbyin//rgyun tu mnyam bzhog 'dri ba la//spyod lam rgyun gyi man ngag phyog blo ldan skal ldan yang rab la//chu sras lha'i man ngag sbyin//'dzin rtsol bral ba'i rig pa la//'jug med rig pa de ltar blta//'od gsal
181a2(kha): ma bcos dang du bzhag/rjes mi 'brang zhing yul mi 'tshol//rang gsal skyon bral cis mi 'khrug/rang la mi bsam dbyings mi 'tsho laka 'dod shes bral gsal ba'i 'od /phyogs bral kun khyab da migasu med//re dog bsam spyad mtha' las grol ka phyogs bral nyi 'od 'bar ba ltar/khyin

181a4(kha): de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje sems dpa' 'di skad ces gsol to//dus gsum rgyal ba thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs kyi khyab pa'i sku//rnam par rtog pa mi mnga' ba//mtshan ma ye shes nyid sbyangs nas/ngo bo rang gsal rig pa yis//nyams rtag nus pa dam pa rnams//rang gsal nyid du rtog bya'i phyir/
181a5(kha): mgon pos bdag la bstan du gsol //zhes gsol ba btab bo//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgyes pa'i rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsungs so//kye sems dpa' chen po/ji ltar snang ba thams cad kun//rang rig yis dkyil 'khor la//rtag pa'i tha snyad kun dang bral//phyogs bral mnyam pa'i

181b1(kha): ma chag dag pa rig pa'i gnas//'od gsal kun gyi gzhal yas la//tshad dang dpags pa'i tshul las 'dasaka/mi bsam mi spyad rang dgar mchog/rig pa mi 'gyur dag pa'i skuka brjod rang gsal khyab gdal gsum//yon tan 'phrin las rtsal du med//bya bral brjod med chos kyi dbyings//kun la gsal/

181b4(kha): kyis gnas//gang du'ang mi gsal gcig tu gsal//gcig pu rang gsal ye shes dang //rig pa spyi blugs rang 'char bas//yul dang yul can tshul mi gnas//rab la mi gsal gzhan la gsal//gnyi ga ma yin mtha' bral lung ka sgro skur gnyis bral dag pa la//bslad med rang 'byung dang la

181b6(kha): chas dbyings laka/sdug bsngal drag po mi bzad dang //bde ba'i longs spyod ci byung ba//gnyer med rlung rang gsal la//yas rol pa nyid yini pas//rang gsal gnyis med dang du gsal//rang byung ma skyes shes rab kyis ka rig pa rang dbang rgyal po des//gnyen bas ma med pas rdul du byung //gzhan gyis
181b7(kha): shas rgyung thob bya nug//gnyis med las rten pag/gnyis lnga ni dag pa rjes mi 'jug 'dags da'ang mchogs yin pag na tog cad pa'i chos mi gnas/mya ngan 'das la phyogs shing /gakhor bar smras pa'i byesas mi gdag pa mtshams ca 'i nyi na/yo /
182a1(kha): @#/blo yis gsal bar gnas//yengs med 'du 'bral mi gnas pas//bcos mad snying por lhun gyis grub//nus pa'i bye brag mtha' yas te//rdo rje rin chen 'bar ba ltar//mus zil gnon cig bur ro//nyi ma'i snying po 'od bzhin du ka/'khor ba yis rang gsal dang //gser rdzas kha dog
182a2(kha): dbyibs bzhin du//dge sdig las kyis bcos min no//'dab chag na mkha' gnas pa ltar/'khor ba'i gtsang rong lam du bde/ka/sgyu ma'i cho 'phrul ji bzhin du ka rang gis dngang las g.yos med gnas//'gro kun 'dren pa'i mthu yis ni//ngan par mi ltung byin gyis brlobs//rang bas la nus mthu bsam
182a3(kha): khyab ye shes rab gsal 'gro 'ong med//thugs rje rang gos la phyogs ris med//ting nge 'dzin gsal dmigs dang bral//rango gas la nyid la nyid mi 'jug 'ching grol med pa'i rang gsal la//rnam rtog spyong rlung mi gnas//gsal ba mar med tshul nyid la//'du shes med pa'i sems mi 'jug/
182a4(kha): cir yang mi bsam cir mi dgos//gang yang ma yin de ltar yin//mtha' bral na mkha' rdo rje la//dmigs gtad 'jog pa nor ba'i lam//ye shes snying po nyi ma shar//rab snang rig pa gter la rdzogs//mtha' dang dbus dang phyogs bral ba ka de dag mnyam pa'i sems mi 'jug 'jug tu med pa'i 'od
182a5(kha): gsal la//gnyis med rig pa gcig gis 'jug/ji ltar chu la chu bzhag ltar//gnyis med gnas tshul de ltar bzhag/'khor ba ye shes snying por gsal/'khor ba sal ba'i nyi ma la//mtha'i mun pa gdod nas stong //yis gsal ba'i nyi ma ni//kun tu gdal la dmigs dang bra le/ci yang ma yin thaMd
182a6(kha): yin//phyogs ris med pa'i thugs rje ni//na mkha'i mtha' ltar kun tu gdal//ye shes rgya mtsho mu mtha' med//grol ba'i dkyil 'khor yongs rdzogs ldan//rang bzhin bcos med snying po'i sku//ma 'phros nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//brtsal med na mkha'i ngang la gnas//ji bzhin na mkha'i dkyil
182a7(kha): 'khor ni//brtsal med namakha'i dang la gnas//bcos med rang bzhin dang la gas la//mngon par mthong ba'i spyi gtsug la/mtha'i lta bas reg tu med/bcos dang ma bcos de nyid mthar//dbus mtha' med pa'i de bzhin nyid//gang la yang mi gnas dbyings na gnas //dngas ni ma bcos zhi rings/
182b1(kha): ga nas zhi ba'i rang bzhin pa//sto nga mi stong 'du shes bral//ma bsam pa nyid pas mya ba'i mtha'//mtha' bus bral ba'i seMs la//mda' ngas yod med spyad mig gas//skyon med segs 'od po sa//gso rang gas la nyid du 'jug kun 'thubyung nas d+hA m+b+ha chung dang //s+yA sr-i ste/
182b2(kha): gas pa'i lam//gang dag ye shes spyir gas la nyid//ye shes gsal ba'i 'khor lo las//ma rig sgrib pa ye nas med//kun bzang rig pa'i 'khor lo ni//ci yang ma yin 'gog pa med/khas ma dang bral ba'i chos nyid la/ba sam zhing spyad pas yul mi spyod//rang bzhin lcug na/
182b3(kha): ting 'dzin la//sems kyi 'phro 'du las mi byed//rang grol klong la ye gnas pas//bcos med byang chub mchog gi ngang //gang ltar 'gyur ba'i rang bzhin ni//rdzas su med de de ltar snang //rang snang sems kyi dkyil 'khor la//thar pa'i dkyil 'khor 'bum phrag sa so//bgras yis dbyings
182b4(kha): la gnas ka mi bsgom rig pa gcig car 'jug/rang sems gsal ba ma yin pas//gnyis med bral ba'i chos mi gnas//rel pa 'gag med cir yang 'byung //rig pa spros pa la dbyings la 'dus/brjod bsku'i 'khor lo can//sgra yis don nyid dbyigs la 'bigs//kun 'dus sems
182b5(kha): kyi me long la//'dzin dral rig pa'i mi dgongs/ rang gsang thabs kyis mchod pa la ka ye shes gsal ba'i don mi snang ka/ces gsung so//na mkha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud chen po las/rig pa rang gsal gyi le'u ste brgyad pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa des
182b6(kha): de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje sems dpa' la 'di skad ces gsol to//kye ma'o sangs rgyas thams cad kyis/rig pa'i ye shes gsal go la//gnyis med ma dag pa'i don mi snang //gang phyir sgrib pa bshad du gsol ka zhes gsol ba las/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdo rje sems dpas 'di/
182b7(kha): skad ces gsungs so//rnam rtog drabs so sar 'acings ka nyid rang sha dang rtog pos//log pa'i grong khyer nyid du lung //les gsus so//kye sems dpa' chen po//sangs rgyas kun gyi bo nyid gi gnyis med lags gas pang dod/nas par mtha' dang mnyam sbyor ba/
183a1(kha): @#/ye shes kun snad me long ni//'khor min mya ngan 'das pa min ka ye shes 'od gsal rig pa'i mkha'//sangs rgyas sems can thams cad la//rang gsal zhes ni//kun la khyad par ye nas byung //gang yang ma yinam de bzhin nyid//chos nyid kun bral
183a2(kha): 'gyur dang bral ka rtog bral ye shes snying por gsalaka/dper na chu ru gcig pa la//skye dang 'phrad dang ma 'brad kyi/nus pa so sor snang ba ltar//rgyal ba'i yis rang gsal la/rtog dang ma rtogs khyad par de/ka/dngos la de dag mi dmigs kyang //bsam pas 'dul ba'i ngos
183a3(kha): grags//rang gsal rig pa'i mthu med pas//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pa'i//lte ba'i khyad par bsam mi khyab/ka/dngos la 'od gsal rig par 'dus//ji bzhin rigs pa'i mthu med pas//bdag dang byed pa'i dbang phyug dang /ka/rang bzhin dus dang blo bur tshur//yod med
183a4(kha): rtog pa log pa'i chos//des la de dag kun dang bral//dge dang mi dge rnam phye nas//blang dor byas pas mthon por 'joms//gong 'og tshul gyis rim pa can//thob 'dod byas pa ngal ba'i rgyu ka bden bzhi bcu drug lam brten nas//nyon mongs rnam par spangs byas te//rgyun du
183a5(kha): /la sogs rim thob pa//byis pa dam ba don med yin//na 'brel dgag bi'i rgyur byas nas//srid pa'i 'khor lo dgag ces pa//dgag bsgrub ma pa'i don dang 'gal//gnyis mang don la sogs pa //bskal pa glags med gsum nyid du kas bsku las lja nga rab d+hi m+b+ha
183a6(kha): skye zhing gnyis rtog spong lan can//rangs thob kyang thar ma phyin//gnyis mdo 'dogasal don las nyams//chos dbyings ye nas gcig pa la//dag pa nyid dang ma dag pa//spong lan yis gzung 'dzin gyis//rdo rje 'dzin thob 'bras mthar byin//'od gsal kun mkhyen don ma rig
183a7(kha): yigs gsang ba'i don ma rtog/rgyal thabs rig pa'i dbang ma thob//gnyis med rang gsal nyag gcig la//nges med gnyis kyi cha 'dzin gyis//'dod la ngas pa'i 'bras bu gcig/'dod pa 'khrul pa rig pa can//gnyis kyi gnyis med ji ltar rnyed ka/'ching bus gser bum mi
183b1(kha): gcig dang //nges pa med pas kho bor 'dra//ye shes gsal ba'i don la sgrib/byin rlabs sna ba cho ga dbus//dkyil 'khor bskyad dang bzlas pa dang //ting 'dzin gsal ba'i rim pa yis//stug pos bkod pa'i sa 'dod pa//rang gsal don la rtog pas sgribs/rang
183b2(kha): byung nyid la rgyu yis bskyed//ye shes skye 'gag tshul du brtags//yeng shes rang snang don dang 'gal ka skyed pa rnal 'byor chen po yis//bskyed rdzogs bzhi yis mnyam sbyar nas/ka/rig pa 'dzin pa'i lam la gnas//dbang bsgyur yis dkyil 'khor gsal//sku gsung thu Ta
183b3(kha): gzhal yas khang ka spyan drangs bzlas bstod mchod pa yis//rim pas rnam rtog kun sbyangs nas ka 'bras bu 'dod pa don mi d+ha da//rig pa'i ye shes gcig po la//ting 'dzin gsum gyi mdud pas bcings//mtshams med 'ong gsal dkyil 'khor la//zhi dang khro bor su yI
183b4(kha): bye/yis kun bdag gdal ba la//khyon dang ma byon 'khrul pa'i chos//khams gsum ye shes rang gsal la//'pho 'dus sgrib pa sbyangs mi dgongs/ka/ma skyed byang chub snying po la//skye ba bzhi ba'i lhar skye 'khrul//dus gcig ye shes rang gsal la//rim skye 'de bas/
183b5(kha): mtshon du med//rlung bzung dgag dang bgrang ba dang //thun mongs nyid kyis sems sbyangs nas//rdzogs pa'i sems la mnyam sbyor bas//yis rang gsal don mi rnyed//yis 'dus pa'i rtsa ba gsumaka/me long bzhi rtseg 'brel ba las//'byed 'dzums drag tu bskul ba yis//nam
183b6(kha): mthar dkyil 'khor 'ben la 'og/ya shes 'bar ba'i 'ong nyid kyi mtha thug mdo pa'i kun bkug nas//yas thig le gcig tu 'dus//rin chen 'phrul gyi lu gu rgyud//gal stir dag pa'i thig la can//khams gsum 'od kyi khyab gong pa nas//slar yod mo shes snying po
183b7(kha): sa ngan cid ngamams ga nag khadang phrag kyi myong ga zhas yar 'dud ci myo pa'i gzung car 'dagapa sa tad de'i pang /'ding 'ang mams lagapa lag ga yang par myo kyi'o la sugadus mo'i rgya na
184a1(kha): @/gnas/dasteng sa rin chen 'khor lo ni//me long cag pa'i tshul du gnas//sum bcu gnyis gnyis ldan pa yis//dbus su bsil pa'i sa bon can//shi pi 'khor ga rnam par bskor /gnyis pa 'dang brgyad pas mang bcwan/chos dgyis sas rang rgyud dA/
184a2(kha): yon tan gsum gsum re ro//'od zer sbrum dang su tri au/za 'og gur phus tshul du'o//'thi ga gal sgong brgyus tshul la//sbrul mig lta bu a d+hI d+hu/dra mar nag zla kham gzhu'i dbyibs/khre du oM du shes par bya//ma togs 'gro drug sems can la//rtog pas sku gsung thugs nyi du/
184a3(kha): gnyis med bsres pas ye shes yin//rnam bzhi shes pa brgyad kyi rten/sgyu ma'i tshul gyis shes par bya//snying po sa bon 'od 'bar nas//mi shig thig le snying por gnas//dbug sog rang bral gyur pa'i sa//ye shes gang la khyab sa la gnas//bcu gsum gnyis kyi dkyil 'khor nas/
184a4(kha): ye shes gsal ba'i dkyil 'khor spros//bcu drug 'khor lo mtshon sbyar nas//thur du phro zhing gyen du 'khyil//bcu dang gnyis bzhi chos kun ni//sngar gsal ye shes ro nyid do//gzungs ldan chos kun phyag rgya la//byin rlabs dbang bskur cha rdzogs laka 'khyud sbyor rig pa'i
184a5(kha): bde ba'i ro//'byung dang 'jug pa gnyis kyi cha//brgyad gnyis bzhi gnyis bcu drug gi nyas//rang gsal lhan cig skyes pa de//'dod chags bde ba'i ting 'dzin bde//yaMs rang gsal rgyan bral lung //rkyen bral ba'i don mi rnyed//rgyan dang cha'i tshul bzhin 'phags//rje su rnal 'byor/
184a6(kha): gyis ni/ka/dbyings dang ye shes rang bzhin gyis//rgyu 'bras dag pa'i tshul du blta//'on gyi ting 'dzin stobs kyis ni//rdo rje sems dpa' stsa la 'gro/rangs 'bras bu mngon thob kyang //mthar thug 'bras bu de la med//'di kun 'jig rten la ma yin de//rtsol bas rang gsal don/
184a7(kha): ma rnyed//gnyis med ngang la gnyis par bzung //rang gsal rang las mdun mar 'khyams//rgyu rkyen bral las rgyu 'bras lhas//ma bcos dang las 'dzin pas bcol//bgrod med ngang las lam gyi bgrod//gcig la bdag gzhan gnyis kyis ba rung //'di kun las don//
184b1(kha): la smig//khams gsum 'khor bar de yis ltung //kun 'dus rang gsal nyagacig la//cir lhas rang gsal dbyings la thugs//gza' gtad 'dzin bral chos kyi dbyings//rang la mi rtog de mi rtog/'dug med rig pa cig car skyes//kun gsal mtshan ma rig pa rdzogs/
184b2(kha): gang du mi gnas kun la son//gan pa ma bachis dpyas gsal bas//'khor ba la sangs rgyas par ma khyed//snying rje 'dugs yul med la//gdung med rang bzhin bya pa rigs pa//yul dang shes pa 'gyur 'dug sad//ngags pa seMs me yang ga/dmigs 'dzin du
184b3(kha): pa'i las kyis//dus gcig tug phrad nyid du 'jug/gang la mi gnas rang la gnas//rang yang dmigs yul rnam bcad nas//rig pa sgra la rten mi 'cha'//dbang po yul gyi rjes mi 'brang //'di ltar gsal bar ma gtogs na//gang ltar brtags pa da ltar min//
184b4(kha): zhes gsung so//na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud chen po las//gang gis gang la sgribs pa'i le'u ste dgu pa'o////de nas yang sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa des//'di skad ces gsol to//kha rtsal bral yis snying po lnga/phun sum tshogs par
184b5(kha): ma 'dres gsal ba tu//mgon pos bdag la bshed du gsol ka zhes zhus so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje sems dpas 'di skad ces gsung so//kye sems dpa' chen po/chos nyid rab byung byang chub sems/rang byung rang rig dkyil 'khor la//gnyis med
184b6(kha): yis chen po la//rnam g.ya bsdu ba mi gnas na//rig par don la 'jug pa'i phyir//dngos dang bsam pa phye ba yis//gang ltar mtshams pa de ltar bstan//ngo bo mthong tshu la gnyis kyi so//rang rig yis ma yin pa/ka/rnal 'byor bcad pa'i chos mi gnasaka/sku gsum rig
184b7(kha): bi'i grong du rdzogs//gnas ni spro bral reg pa ste//bstan pa rang gas la mi long yin/'khor ni rig pa'i tshe 'phrul te//bstan par ngags pa mi lcong yin/dus ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa//bsdus pa kun 'dus rig pa'i gtab/ra gas pa rang lasas ldan ga/dus
185a1(kha): @gsum tshogs pa mi gnas te//rig pa 'pho 'gyur med pa'i gter//bsam gyis mi khyab dus na shes//kye sems dpa' chen po//ston pa'i chos sku'i zhing khams ni//na mkha' gar khyab chos kyi gterag/chos nyid sems kyi dbyer med pa//chos sku'i
185a2(kha): zhing khams nyid du bshad//gnas ni rig pa'i 'og min te//spros bral 'bar ba'i gzhal yas khang //mtha' dang dbus med yis khang //sems can sangs rgyas kun gyis gnas//ston pa rang byung 'od gsal la//rdo rje sems dpa' chos kyi rje/brjod med
185a3(kha): rig pa'i ngo bo yis//'gro 'od med pa'i chos kyang ston//spros med byin rlabs phyogs bcur gdal/ka/rig pa rang gsal ngo bo ni ka ma gsungs byin gyis brlabs kyis bshadag/rang gsal chen po'i ye su ka rang gis rig bshugs kyi go/'khor ni sangs rgyas nyid
185a4(kha): yin pas//gzhan rnams gnyis pa'i chos mi gnas//'od gsal rig pa'i gdengs gis 'khor//bkod pa med pas 'dus te gnas//thaMd ye shes snying po las//nyid 'chang cing nyid kyis 'grel/ye shes snying por kun 'dus pas//de nyid byang chub sems kyi 'khor
185a5(kha): bsdud pa'ang gsal gnyis ming klong //kun kyang rig pa'i 'od la 'dus//gnyis med gsal ba'i rang bzhin las//ma 'dus pa'i chos mi gnas ka dus ni tha~Md mnyam pa'i dus//rang gsal kho bo'i thog mtha' med//rang byang chub sems nyid lan/dus sum khyad par
185a6(kha): gcig du 'dus//bstan pa chos kun rang gsal la//ma bcos ma smad 'gyur med klong //gnyis med rig pa'i ye shes nyid//rang snang tshul gyis gtan la 'bebs//ma bcos rang bzhin gzhag pas go/nad pa gnyis gsum bstan pa sgrogs//cig car 'jug
185a7(kha): tshul gyis rtogs//gang yang mi mkhyen gsal mkhyen pas//ses rang snang gsal bar mkhyen//ston pa rdzogs longs spyod pa dang //longs spyod namakha'i mtha' mnyam ste//sku gsung thugs kyang mu mtha' med//zhing khams de ltar khyab pa'o//gnas ni 'og min
185b1(kha): chen po ste//sgros bral rig pa'i byin rlabs las//yis rgyun gyis gzhal yas khang ka phyi dang nang med ye shes gsal//ston pa rdzogs longs spyod pa yis//rang byung mtshan dpe rang la chags//bde ba kun la mnga' mdzad cing /seMs dmigs pa'i nga tshul/
185b2(kha): can//'khor ni rang rig nyid 'jog pas//sa bcu dbang phyug 'khor nyid la//kun kyang mtshan ma mnyam pa'i tshul//rang gsal don la 'jog pa'o//bsdud pa gsang bdag rdo rje 'chang ka rgyal ba kun gyis dbang bskur ba//rig pa'i ye shes dkyil 'khor du/
185b3(kha): sgra bral bstan pa rgya mtsho bsdud//dus ni mnyam gzhag rig pa'i dus//rang gsal dang las ma g.yos par//mnyam nyid rang grol rig pa'i 'dus//'di zhes nges pa rab bstan du med//bstan pa phyi ba'i gsang sngags gsum//dgongs par byin
185b4(kha): rlabs rgyud pas bshad//ting 'dzin gsal bas nyan cing go//rang gri ngo bo bstan par bshad//longs spyod rdzogs pa'i bstan par bshad//thugs rje'i rang bzhin sprul par bshad/ka/zhing khams sprul pa'i na mkha'i khams//yang na stong gsum 'jig rten
185b5(kha): te/'dul ba'i ngo la de ltar grags//gnas ni nges medado mtha' yas te//rig drug rang thad de nyid la//rdo godan sogs mtha' yas par//grangs med par gnas pa de nyid do//thug pa'i dbang po grangs las 'das//mdor bsdus snang ba ye shes drug/'dra
185b6(kha): dbang longs spyod spangs tshul gyis//'gro ba 'dran mdzad sgron ma'o//'khor ni lam nyes thob pa dang //'gro drug sems can de dag dang //dge slong la sogs sna tshogs gzugs//sna tshogs 'dus pa'i 'khor tshogaso//sde snod gsum gyi chu lnga
185b7(kha): bzung nas//mos pa tha dad dbang gis na ka theg pa chung ngu rims pa yis//gang ltar 'tshams pa de ltar bstan//kun kyang byang chub sems/nyid//bcos med rang byung 'od la 'dus//ye shes gsang ba'i ngo bo la//ji ltar snang ba de ltar yin/
186a1(kha): @/gsang ba'i me long gsal ba'i mdzod//nyi ma'i snying po'i 'od bzhin du//gsal lo yul dang rnam par bral / thams cad dang las gzhan ma yin//rang nyid bsam brjod yul las 'das//ma rtogs thugs rje mkha' ltar
186a2(kha): gang la//na mkhas 'dzin pa'i 'du shes med/ka/ston pa pa la bstan pa dang //nyan pa'i tshogs nga bsdud pa nga //rang gsal nyid la rang nyid gnas ka gang yang ma yin 'od gsal yin//ces gsung so//na mkha'i mtha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud chen po lnga/
186a3(kha): phun sum tshogs pa'i le'u ste bcu pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa des 'di skad ces gsol to//bdag cag 'dus pa rig pa'i tshogs//ye shes gsal ba ji ltar brtag/rang byung rig pa'i 'khor lo las ka/smad byung
186a4(kha): rol pa ji ltar 'char ka ye shes nyi ma bcom ldan 'das ka rang byung don la ba ga du gsol//zhes zhus so//de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pas ka 'di skad ces gsungs te//kye sems dpa' chen po/rang byung ye shes
186a5(kha): rgyu rkyen bral//gdod nas rang snang 'od kyi mchog/gang yang ma ya ni kun la gsal//phyogs med thugs rje kun 'jug le//phyogs 'dzin mi mnga' rtog mi mngar//mkha' gsal nyi zla'i dkyil 'khor bzhin ka thA dad bsam pas 'dul thabs kus/
186a6(kha): rang byung rig pa sems la bzhugs//chos dbyings bya bral rdzogs pa'i lung //gsang mdzod rig pa thugs kyi gter//sku gsung thugs kyi badre ba'i mchog/kun gdal rig pa'i klong du son//lha med yis rang gsal ni//yul med spros bral ji bzhin nyid/
186a7(kha): rang stong rig pa'i 'khor lo lo//yul med rong shar reg pas mthong ka bas ma bral rig pa cir mi dgos//gzanga' brel dbus ma'i lam po cig /ji ltar ma yin de bzhin nyid//de ltar rig pa nyid kyis gas ga/yas kyang sa bcos de bzhin nyid du
186b1(kha): phra rags 'dzin pa'i rnam rtog la//mtshan ma rnam grangs ci skyes kyang //de nyid 'od gsal dag pa'i mkha'//gnyis med rang gsal cig la son//mthong ba'i skabs na don mi mthong //mi mthong ba yis don mthong ste/ka/gnyis ka ma bco la rang rig la/
186b2(kha): mthong dang mi mthong rjes mi spyod//thams cad byang chub sems nyid la//log rtog brtsol ba chos mi gnas//rang byung mtshan ma mi 'jug pas//gang ltar gsal dang dmigs dang bral ka 'byung 'jug med pa rnal 'byor gyis ka ji bzhin nyid kyis
186b3(kha): don la rtog/lha bral gzigs med ye shes spyan ka chos rnams byang chub semasu gsal//byang chub sems nyid 'od bsal bas//mtha' dbus med pas gcig tu gnas//'dod lnga yul lnga chags snang lnga/ci snang sems las gzhan med pas/
186b4(kha): de nyid ma yin ga zhe na ma yin//'khor ba yi sa 'od du 'bar//rang gsal 'jug med rig pa yis//mthong 'jug med pa'i ngo bor brtag/phyogs mdagasal ba'i yeng shes kyis//phyogasu blta ba zil gyis mnan//dang po sems la rgyu ba med//
186b5(kha): tha mar skye 'jig mtha' las 'das ka rang byung rdo rje skye 'gog med ka rus gcig spyi rdzogs par rim skye meng //skad cig med de rgyan dang bral/rig pa rang gsal mngon sum la//rjes dpag rim pa rdzun gyis bslus//de kho na nyid rang gsal lung ka rgyu rgyo ni byang chub
186b6(kha): sems nyid las//ye shes 'khyil ba'i 'khor lo tsa na//cir yang 'phrul lo thams cad gsal ka namakho' rang gsal dbyings la gnas//snang srid bde sdug 'khrul 'khor la//rig pa yis 'khor la zhugs//rang byung dang la gzhan pa men//'du 'bral med pa rang la
186b7(kha): thugs//khyab gdal rig pa kun du bzang ka gang ltar gsal ba de ltar yin ka klong yang chu bo 'du 'gro zhing //ma 'dres yongs rdzogs gcig tu gsal//nag par gsal ba'i gzhal la su//rig pa gsal ba'i 'od gas la nas//rig pa rang bsal mchog tu/

192b3(kha): chub sems kyis bsgyur//dus mtha' lal mngon par gshegs//lung ldan rdo rje bla ma yis//snod ldan gus pa'i blo ma la//rgyal babs skye blugs dbang bskur nas//dus mtha' shes ba skad cig la//rangags pa chos sku rang gnas bstan//bab chu gnas rje/
192b4(kha): ra ni kyig/col meng 'od gas pa ba pa rang gzhan gnas par 'byung ba rgyur/ nga tha~M pang bag hU~M//rgyal pa'i tshulas nga du gyu/ngags pa rig pa 'od dus pa/rig pa rang sang rgyal po rngog/ rje sa nga na@#
192b5(kha): dad las kyi bag chags kyis//gos par mi 'gyur ba ltar//na mkha' dag pa'i klong bzhin du ka 'dzi ni med ye shes chen por nges//rang gas la rig pa'i slong bzugs nas/'od zer nyi bzhin pa ga la 'char//zhes gsung //ram yang mtha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud
192b6(kha): chen po las//rgyal thabs spyi blugs yis ngos ston pa'i le'u ste bcu bzhi pa'o///de nas sems dpa' rdo rje dam pa des//'di skad ces gsol to//dus gsum rgyal ba'i sku gsung thugs//se nas rang gsal 'das med la//bsrung mod yis/
192b7(kha): rig pa yis//mnyam du 'jog cing ye shes gsal//mgon po khyod la gsol ba ni//bsrung du mang pa'i dam tshig ni//gang ltar bsrung dang mi 'gal don//mgon pos bdag la bshad du gsol//zhes gsol to//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rdo rje
193a1(kha): /sems dpa' chen po des 'di skad ces gsungs to//kye sems dpa' chen po ka bsrung bral rig pa'i dam tshig la//bsrungs shing bsal bas log pa'i lam//bsrung med rtog pa'i dam tshig bzhi//yis gsal bas mchog la rdzogs//gnyis med
193a2(kha): rig pa'i rtsol bral yud/bcos med pa mchog gi mkha' ka 'du 'bral med pa'i rig pa'i gter//rang gisal 'od la 'da' ba med//phyogs bral mnyam pa'i dam tshig la//gcig ste 'od gsal khyab pa che//yongs la bsal bas phyogs ris med//gang yang ma
193a3(kha): yin khyab pa'i dbyings//ye shes rig pa mchog la sbyor//gnyis bral rig pa dam tshig la//'dod pa'i bsam pa bsrung du med//phyogs bral mnyam pa'i dam tshig la//phyogs kyi bsam pa cir mi 'jug/bsam bral gnyis med rigau pa la//bsam cing spyod pa'i
193a4(kha): yul mi gnas//rang dag lhun rdzogs dam tshig lAkaM/brtsal cing bsgrub pa'i ming mi gnas//tsI yang ma yin 'od ma yin//rang gsal rdo rje thabs kyi lung //brjod bral rig pa'i ngo bor 'dzin//rig bA rang gsal yis la//bsrung dang mi bsrung phyogs ris med//mnyam nyid rig pa'i/
193a5(kha): gter la rdzogs//bcol mid rigs pa'i dbyings la rdzogs//phyogs bral mnyam pa'i sems kyi 'jug/yaMs gsang ba'i dam tshig ni mi bskyod rdo rje na mkha'i lung //brjod bral sgra 'di dam tshig la//ma yengs rig pa mchog gi gau/yi ge med par gsal ba'i rgyud/nyan
193a6(kha): bas ma med par rang rig gsal//dgongs pa mo bcos rnal du phebs//ji bzhin rig pa bsam bral lung //gsang ba rkyen bral cir mi gsal//rang gsal rig pa mchog la gnas//na mkha' rang byung khyad pa'i ngang //mtshan mar ma yin mtshan ma med//rdo rje gsang ba'i thabs lung ni/
193a7(kha): gnyis med rang shar mnyam rdzogs so//mra bcos gsal ba'i dam tshig la//bcos pa ma 'di lta du na mug/gnyis med rig pa mchog nyid la//yul dang yul can bsrung ba log/grangs 'das chod med rang gsal la//grangs tshang nam kha'i mthar 'tshal 'dra/bsrung ma mi/
193b1(kha): nas grub zin bkang sdong bsrung bas thug pa med/ka bsrung dang mi bsrung tshig gi mtha' ye shes rig pa tshig mtha' bral//phyogs bral 'od gsal dam tshig la//phyogs kyi log pa bsrung ba'i lam//rtsal bral lhun rdzogs dam tshig la//brtsal ba mi rnyed don la mad//bya bral
193b2(kha): rdzogs lung dam pa la ka 'jug lha'i tshig gi gang rtso kha pa/ mtshan ma'o rtog pa'i rgyun gyis khrid//mtshan ma rtsol ba log pa'i chos//ye shes rang shar nyi ma la//mtha'i zhen pa mun pas bsgribs//mi rtag mun pa'i thibs kyis 'gag/snang gsal rig pa'i 'od dang bral//rang gsal
193b3(kha): yes me long la//rang byung rig pa'i rtsa la 'gag nas//ci yang mi mthong chad lta le//mnar med rdo rje khab tu ltung //rtogs bral gnyis med yis la//phyogs ris med par kun gsal bas//ci yang yin no gang yang rung //kun brtags yod med mtha' dang bral//yid dang med pa/
193b4(kha): kun brtags te//mtha' bral yis nyid du brtags//rang gsal lung la kun gnas pas//kun brtags ye shes gnyis kar gsal ka phyogs ros med rig pa ci yang min//phyogs 'dzin log pa'i grong khyer la//mtha' bral mnyam pa nyid la gnas//med pa phyal ba gcig pu
193b5(kha): dang lhun rdzogs 'ang rig chen po la//ngo bo ma bcos chos gcig laka bsam pa'i 'du shes tshul gnas phye//phyogs med ris med 'od gsal mkha'//rtog med rang gsal ye sangs rgyas//ci yang med pas cir yang gsal//rig pa rang gsal dbyings la bzhugs//yis/
193b6(kha): pa mchog gi sku//ci yang med do /khyab par gdal//rkyen bral mi gsal cir mi dgongs ka gnyis med bad chen phyogs lhung bsal//rtog med rang gsal bde ba'i lung //bde med rig pa'i chos nyid lu//'di zhes rig pa'i sems mi 'jug/gnyen po myed pas 'jigs dang bral
193b7(kha): rig pa 'od gsal mchog la bzhugs//rang las gzhan pa'o chos med pas//med pa'i dam tshig 'da' ba med//phyogs bral rig pa'i ye shes//'di dang 'di zhes bsrung du med//phyogs bral chen po'i skyes bu des//phyogs gcig bral bas phyogs kun bral//rang rig

194a3(kha): dang du ma yongs kyis med//rig pa yis gnyi ga las ka mtha' dbus med pa'i gnasu gsal//ye shes gcig gi don la bzhugs//ye shes rgya mtsho mu mtha' mA da//bdag las gzhan gsal thams cad gsal//kun gsal rig pa'i 'khor mtha' med//kun bzang mchog gi 'khor lo
194a4(kha): ya//gcig dang gnyis la bgrang du med//ye nas rig pa ji bzhin gnas//tshig tshogs bstan pa rgya mtsho'i klongs//gang ltar phye yang gcig las med//theg le gcig las dam tshig bral//de ltar nyid du so sor grags//'dul ba'i thabs ldan rang rig lung //rig pa mchog gi 'khor lo bskor
194a5(kha): rang grol brjod bral don la nyan//yangs pa med pa'i don gyis go//de nyid dgu ldan sems nyid gzhi/rin chen 'bar ba'i 'od kyi lung //btsal med bde bas nyam su blang //rang gsal gcig pu'i dam tshig thob//rang byung lhun grub dam tshig la//bsam yas 'bras bu gter rdzogs
194a6(kha): ga kha tsal med sbyin grub yi sangs rgyas//rin chen 'bar ba'i sa gzhi ltar/'khor ba ye shes snying por gsal kA brgya byin spyi ga tsug nor bu ltar//kha sags ming gdod nas spyan gyis rdzogs//nyi mir snying po'i 'od bzhin dukA rim+b+ha pa med par dus gcig gsal//lhun rdzogs bstan

204a3(kha): gtan la phebs pa yin//sangs rgyas kun gyi rtsa ba ni//rdo rje sems dpa' nyid yin no//sa dang lam gyi rtsa ba ni//'od gsal rig pa mchog gi sa//don 'di rtogs pas bgrod mi dgos//sa 'di spyad ni sbyang mi dgos//sangs rgyas kun+da dang de'i sgras//kun+d+ha kyang rtsa ba byung ba
204a4(kha): sems//btsam med ri 'i don nas/rang gsal sas 'od la bcugs/'gro rje 'dod lnga rigs drug dang //nyon mongs lnga por snang ba sad+ma ka rtsa ba rang gsal byang chub sems//rig pa rang shar di na yi gnas /mig so mchog tsor mi dkris/'jig rten 'di kyi rgyo/
204a5(kha): bo g+ha ra//kun gsal snying po gcig tu 'dus//'od gsal bde ba rnam rtog lung //gsal dang me gsal chos mi gnas//tha snyed bral ba'i rig par gsal//rim chen 'bar ba'i gser gling ltar//de las gzhan pa'i chos med la/ka/ming dang dbyings la che chung dang ka khas
204b1(kha): dngadartag pa tha dad de//ngo bo cig la gnas pa med//gser la gsar du mthong ba bzhin//rang gas la rig pa de ltar lnga//rang shar don la gnyis med//gser gyi rang bzhin mi 'gyur gyur/rang gas pa rkyen gyis 'gyur ba med//rang la mi bas ma gas pargya ljang //mi 'dzin ra
204b2(kha): gsal bsam bral lung //gdod nas bcos dag pa ltar//'od gsal rang bzhin ye nas dag/cir yang 'gyur zhing snang ba ltar//'od gsal rig pa'i dkyil 'khor la//dbang po mos pa'i khyad par gyis//ji ltar sdang ba de ltar snang ka cir yang ma yin snying po ni//rang byung rig
204b3(kha): pa'i 'khor lo can//sgro skur the tsom rnam par gcod//rig pa yis rkyen nga/dra bral//rang la rang gi gsal bar sems//kun gyis dgongs pa'i don rnams kun//rtsa ba byang chub sems la 'dus ka zhes gsungs so//na mkha' mtha' dang mnyam pa'i rgyud chen po ltas//

204b5(kha): ni nges gtan la pa'i//nga bzhin dgang sa pa rnaMs sku sang //mnar gtan pa bya pa'o//nga na d+hi n+yA thos kyi dbyi sa//rnam dag spyod gas pa dkyil la la/d+hA gA rAg+h+r-i swA bAr+tha/rA g-i d+yA g+h+r-is smya nA pa ga/b+h+ya h+lA N+Do n+yA spyan pa swI kA ky-is mye//
